### [1] [Convergence and acceleration of a nonlinear fixed-point iteration for computing the Fitness Centrality of general graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28634)
*Nikita Deniskin,Michele Benzi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文建立了非齐次Fitness Centrality算法在一般图上的全局收敛性，推导了不动点迭代的显式收敛界，并通过Anderson加速和Newton方法切换策略大幅提升收敛速度，在多种类型图上通过数值实验验证了该策略的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fitness Centrality算法在实际应用中面临收敛性问题，缺乏对一般图上全局收敛性的理论保证，且收敛速度可能较慢，需要理论分析与加速策略。

Method: 首先推导非齐次Fitness Centrality不动点迭代的显式全局收敛界；然后引入Anderson加速方法提高迭代收敛速率；当迭代逼近不动点时切换至Newton方法以利用其局部二次收敛性，实现快速精化。

Result: 在多种不同类型的图（数值实验）上验证了所提出的加速策略，表明Anderson加速与Newton切换相结合可以显著改善Fitness Centrality算法的收敛性能。

Conclusion: 本文为Fitness Centrality提供了全局收敛的理论保证，并提出了一种结合Anderson加速与Newton方法的高效混合求解策略，兼顾了全局收敛性与局部快速收敛性。

Abstract: We establish the global convergence of the (non-homogeneous) Fitness Centrality algorithm for general graphs, deriving an explicit convergence bound for the corresponding fixed-point iteration. Furthermore, we show how the convergence can be dramatically improved by Anderson acceleration and by switching to Newton's method once a sufficiently good approximation to the fixed point has been found. The efficacy of this strategy is illustrated by numerical experiments on different types of graphs.

</details>


### [2] [A Framework Integrating the Dynamic Stiffness Matrix with Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Solving Eigenvalue Problems and Analysing Dynamic Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28683)
*Yi An,Zhijiang Chen,Zhiqiang Feng,Qian Cheng,Jack C. P. Cheng,Haijiang Li,Dalei Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出DSM-PINN框架，将动态刚度矩阵与物理信息神经网络相结合，通过嵌入物理约束来处理有限数据场景下的结构分析问题。该方法利用精确动态刚度矩阵作为频域谱单元，结合Wittrick-Williams算法解决特征值问题，并扩展至移动载荷与冲击载荷下的结构动力响应分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PINN在处理高频特征值问题时难以收敛到高阶模态，且在复数域动力响应分析中存在局限；同时，面对有限数据集时如何保证模型的鲁棒性和物理一致性是亟待解决的挑战。

Method: 将动态刚度矩阵与PINN相结合：神经网络输出节点位移场，单元形函数是控制偏微分方程的齐次解（非有限元的多项式插值），构成强形式PINN；损失函数通过神经网络与DSM连接实现物理约束嵌入。采用Wittrick-Williams算法求解特征值问题以克服高阶模态收敛困难，频域PINN方法用于移动和冲击载荷下的结构动力响应分析，并讨论了边界条件执行后DSM为不定矩阵时的理论收敛稳定性。

Result: 数值结果验证了所提DSM-PINN方法在有限数据条件下的实用性、鲁棒性和有效性，证明其能够处理高阶特征值问题及复数域动力响应分析。

Conclusion: DSM-PINN通过将精确谱单元理论与深度学习相结合，有效克服了传统PINN在高阶特征值收敛和复数处理方面的局限，为结构动力学分析提供了一种兼具物理精确性和数据效率的新框架。

Abstract: This paper introduces a framework that integrates the dynamic stiffness matrix (DSM) with physics-informed neural networks (PINN). The DSM-PINN embeds physical constraints within the model and demonstrates robustness, particularly when addressing limited datasets across diverse investigations. In this approach, deep neural network outputs approximate the displacement fields of element nodes. Unlike the finite element method (FEM), the element shape functions are homogeneous solutions to the governing partial differential equation, forming the basis of the exact dynamic stiffness matrix, thereby avoiding high-order derivative terms. This matrix also serves as a frequency-domain spectral element, resulting in a strong-form PINN. The loss function is produced by connecting neural networks with dynamic stiffness matrices. We focus on utilising PINNs to resolve eigenvalue problems by employing the Wittrick-Williams algorithm, which overcomes the challenge of neural networks failing to converge to higher-order eigenvalues. Additionally, the frequency domain-PINN method is used to analyse structural dynamic responses under moving and impulsive loads, addressing the limitation of neural networks in handling complex numbers. Theoretical convergence stability of the suggested approach is also analysed even DSM is an indefinite matrix after implementing the boundary condition. The numerical results validate the practicality and efficacy of the recommended approach.

</details>


### [3] [Overcoming the spatial order barrier for nonlinear SPDEs with additive space-time white noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28727)
*Lukas Anzeletti,Máté Gerencsér,Helena Kremp*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对带加性时空白噪声的半线性SPDE的全离散数值格式，打破了以往空间收敛率的阶数障碍，实现了时间上$M^{-1+\epsilon}$、空间上$N^{-3/2+\epsilon}$（任意$\epsilon>0$）的强收敛率，大幅优于文献中标准的$N^{-1/2}$空间误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半线性SPDE的数值求解中，空间收敛率长期受限于$N^{-1/2}$的阶数障碍，这严重制约了高阶空间离散方法的应用效果，亟需一种能突破该障碍的数值格式。

Method: 提出一种全离散（fully discrete）数值格式，结合恰当的时间离散和空间离散策略，专门针对加性时空白噪声驱动的半线性SPDE设计，从而绕过此前空间收敛率的阶数限制。

Result: 该格式在时间方向达到强收敛率$M^{-1+\epsilon}$，在空间方向达到$N^{-3/2+\epsilon}$（任意$\epsilon>0$），显著优于文献中标准空间误差界$N^{-1/2}$。

Conclusion: 该工作首次突破了半线性SPDE空间收敛率$N^{-1/2}$的阶数障碍，将空间收敛阶提升了近一个阶次，为SPDE的高精度数值逼近开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We introduce a fully discrete numerical scheme for semilinear SPDEs with additive space-time white noise that overcomes the previous order barrier for the spatial convergence rate. The scheme achieves a strong convergence rate of $M^{-1+ε}$ in time and $N^{-3/2+ε}$ in space for any $ε>0$, where $M^{-1}$ and $N^{-1}$ are the temporal, respectively the spatial, meshsizes. This substantially improves the standard spatial error bounds of order $N^{-1/2}$ in the literature.

</details>


### [4] [Separable Nonnegative Matrix Factorization Using Powered Ratio-of-Norms Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28799)
*Matthew McCarver,Jing Qin*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于ℓ1^p/ℓ2幂范数比的ℓ1^p/ℓ2正则化的可分离非负矩阵分解（SNMF）模型，通过DCA和ADMM两种算法求解该非凸非光滑优化问题，并在合成数据和手部手势分类任务上验证了其在锚点识别和分类精度方面的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNMF广泛用于非负数据的低秩表示和聚类，其与图聚类和社区检测密切相关。然而现有SNMF方法在增强所学习因子的稀疏性和可辨识性方面仍有不足，需要一种更有效的正则化策略来提升因子分解的质量。

Method: 提出基于幂范数比（powered ratio-of-norms）正则化器的ℓ1^p/ℓ2正则化SNMF模型，该模型非凸且非光滑。分别采用差凸函数算法（DCA）和交替方向乘子法（ADMM）进行求解，将原问题分解为可处理的子问题，利用幂范数项的闭式近端算子。

Result: 在合成数据集和手部手势分类任务上进行了大量数值实验，所提方法在锚点识别和分类精度上相比现有SNMF方法取得具有竞争力或更优的表现，同时保持有竞争力的计算效率。理论方面建立了DCA方案的下降性和极限临界性性质，以及ADMM方案在标准假设下的收敛性。

Conclusion: 所提出的ℓ1^p/ℓ2正则化SNMF模型结合DCA和ADMM算法，能够有效增强因子稀疏性和可辨识性，在锚点识别和分类任务上优于或与现有方法相当，具有实用价值。

Abstract: Separable nonnegative matrix factorization (SNMF) has been widely used for low-rank representation and clustering of nonnegative data, owing to its ability to produce part-based and interpretable decompositions. In particular, SNMF is closely related to graph clustering and community detection. To enhance sparsity and identifiability of the learned factors, we propose an $\ell_1^p/\ell_2$-regularized SNMF model based on a powered ratio-of-norms regularizer. The resulting formulation is nonconvex and nonsmooth, which poses significant challenges for optimization. To address this, we develop efficient algorithms based on the difference-of-convex function algorithm (DCA) and the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM). The proposed methods decompose the original problem into tractable subproblems, leveraging closed-form proximal operators associated with the powered norm terms. We establish descent and limiting criticality properties for the DCA scheme and convergence under standard assumptions for the ADMM scheme. Extensive numerical experiments on synthetic datasets and hand gesture classification tasks demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves competitive or improved performance in anchor identification and classification accuracy compared with existing SNMF methods, while maintaining competitive computational efficiency.

</details>


### [5] [High-Order-Accurate Continuity Enforcing Nyström Discretization of 3D Maxwell Combined Field Integral Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28876)
*Bernd Hofmann,Reza Molavi,Constantine Sideris*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对电场合积分方程（EFIE）Nyström-配点离散中表面散度作用于跨面片不连续的表面密度导致精度和收敛性退化的问题，提出了一种高阶精度的连续性强制方案，适用于直接和间接EFIE、磁场合积分方程（MFIE）以及正则化组合场积分方程（CFIE）。该方法通过Chebyshev基Nyström离散与基于曲率几何描述构建的稀疏映射矩阵实现基变换，在减少未知量总数的同时改善了条件数、减少了迭代次数并提升了散射场精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Nyström-配点离散化EFIE时，表面散度作用在可能跨面片边界不连续的表面密度上，导致精度和收敛性退化。作者指出这不仅影响EFIE，而是影响所有包含该算子的公式/形式（无论是方程本身还是散射场计算中），因此需要一个统一的连续性强制方案来解决这一普遍性问题。

Method: 方案包含两个关键部分：i) 通过Chebyshev基Nyström方案离散方程，该方案支持闭合求积规则；ii) 由于未知量和测试向量基于面片局部曲率坐标基，通过构造仅由曲率几何描述装配的稀疏映射矩阵进行基变换来实现连续性强制。

Result: 对典型和真实几何体的散射数值研究表明，所有考虑到的公式（单独及各组合形式）都从连续性强制中受益：条件数改善、迭代求解器迭代次数减少、散射场精度提升数个有效数字，同时总未知量数量减少。

Conclusion: 所提出的连续性强制方案能恢复EFIE的精度，使其可与MFIE以等权重组合形成CFIE，并全面改善了直接/间接EFIE、MFIE和正则化CFIE的数值性能。该方案在减少未知量的同时提升了精度与效率，是一种有效的统一修复策略。

Abstract: In Nyström-collocation discretizations of the electric field integral equation (EFIE), the surface divergence acts on surface densities that may be discontinuous across patch boundaries, which degrades accuracy and convergence. We show that this not only affects the EFIE but every formulation in which the operator occurs, either in the equation itself or in the scattered field computation, and propose a high-order-accurate continuity-enforcing scheme for smooth surfaces as a remedy for the direct and indirect EFIEs, magnetic field integral equations (MFIEs), and regularized combined field integral equations (CFIEs) alike. The scheme comprises two ingredients: i) We show how to discretize the equations via a Chebyshev-based Nyström scheme, which admits closed quadrature rules. ii) Since unknowns and test vectors are in terms of patch-local curvilinear bases, continuity is enforced by a change of basis: we construct sparse mapping matrices assembled solely from the curvilinear geometry description. In doing so, we restore the accuracy of the EFIE such that it can be combined with the MFIEs with equal weights to form CFIEs. Numerical studies for the scattering from canonical and realistic geometries show that all considered formulations individually and combined benefit from the continuity enforcement in terms of better conditioning, reduced iterations of an iterative solver, and several more digits of accuracy in the scattered fields, despite reducing the total number of unknowns.

</details>


### [6] [A General Superconvergence Result for Cubature on Triangulated Polygonal Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28883)
*Jonah A. Reeger*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对多边形域上的积分配（cubature）规则提出了超收敛（superconvergence）结果：当规则对固定偶数次二元多项式精确时，随着节点间距减小，可实现额外的收敛阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 积分配规则是物理科学中计算方法的重要组成部分，其收敛速度直接影响计算效率。在多边形域上研究如何通过超收敛现象进一步提升数值积分的精度和收敛阶，具有重要的理论与实用价值。

Method: 针对多边形域上的积分配规则，推导了一个超收敛结果。该结果表明，若分配规则对某一固定偶数次的所有二元多项式是精确的，则在节点间距减小的条件下，该规则能够获得额外一阶的收敛速度。

Result: 证明了一个超收敛定理：在多边形域上，精确度高（对固定偶数次多项式精确）的积分配规则，其实际收敛阶高于预期，实现了额外阶数的收敛提升。

Conclusion: 多边形域上具有偶数次精确性的积分配规则存在超收敛现象，这一发现有助于提高数值积分方法的效率，并为设计和分析更优的积分配规则提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Cubature rules, which approximate definite integrals as a linear combination of a set of function values, are ubiquitous and necessary for computational methods in the physical sciences. A superconvergence result for cubature rules on polygonal domains is developed, whereby a rule that is exact for all bivariate polynomials of a fixed even degree realize an extra order of convergence under a decrease in the spacing between nodes.

</details>


### [7] [A GPU-Accelerated Blocked Adaptive Randomized Range Finder Based on an Implicit Householder QR Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28941)
*Carolin Penke,Andreas Herten*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于隐式Householder QR分解的分块自适应随机范围查找器，并在NVIDIA GH200上实现了优化的混合CPU-GPU方案，避免了显式重正交化，在保持正交性和近似精度的同时大幅提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低秩方法（如GaLore）可降低深度神经网络训练的内存和计算需求。随机范围查找器是SVD的理想替代方案，尤其是当所需秩需要根据预设近似容差自适应确定时。然而现有方法在块Gram-Schmidt无重正交化时可能不稳定，且缺乏高效的异构系统实现。

Method: 提出一种分块自适应随机范围查找器，核心基于隐式Householder QR分解，避免显式重正交化；采用分块形式暴露矩阵-矩阵操作，并实现混合CPU-GPU实现，通过重叠CPU面板分解与GPU更新来充分利用异构资源。

Result: 在块Gram-Schmidt无重正交化变得不稳定的场景下，该方法仍能保持正交性和近似精度。在NVIDIA GH200上，最大测试矩阵的运行时间从CPU上的9.91秒降至0.407秒。

Conclusion: 该方法为异构系统上的低秩近似提供了稳定高效的构建模块，在计算科学与工程中具有广阔应用前景。

Abstract: Low-rank methods can reduce the memory and computational requirements of deep neural network training in approaches such as GaLore. Randomized range finders offer an attractive alternative to singular value decompositions, particularly when the required rank is determined adaptively from a prescribed approximation tolerance. We introduce a blocked adaptive randomized range finder based on an implicit Householder QR decomposition and an optimized hybrid CPU--GPU implementation. The proposed method avoids explicit reorthogonalization. Numerical experiments show that it preserves orthogonality and approximation accuracy in regimes where block Gram--Schmidt without reorthogonalization becomes unstable. The blocked formulation exposes matrix--matrix operations and enables overlap of CPU panel factorization with GPU updates. On an NVIDIA GH200, the overlapped implementation reduces the runtime for the largest tested matrix from 9.91 seconds on the CPU to 0.407 seconds. The method provides a stable and efficient building block for low-rank approximation on heterogeneous systems with applications in computational science and engineering.

</details>


### [8] [Minimizing the Arithmetic and Communication Complexity of Jacobi's Method for Eigenvalues and Singular Values: Part Two -- Parallel Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28952)
*James Demmel,Hengrui Luo,Ryan Schneider,Yifu Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了对称特征值问题和SVD的多种并行Jacobi方法，在2D和2.5D处理器网格下实现了最优算术成本，且在带宽和延迟方面匹敌或接近并行矩阵乘法的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在先前的Demmel等人2025年工作的基础上，作者希望开发算术成本最优且通信成本（带宽和延迟）可匹敌并行矩阵乘法下界的并行Jacobi算法，以解决对称特征值问题和SVD在大规模分布式计算中的可扩展性问题。

Method: 论文在标准分布式内存设置下，针对可变处理器布局（2D和2.5D网格）设计了并行Jacobi算法。在2D情况下，利用标准并行Jacobi实现实现算术复杂度O(n^3/P)的完美加速比，同时达到2D矩阵乘法的带宽下界和近最优延迟。在2.5D情况下，通过增加每处理器内存并利用2.5D矩阵乘法进一步降低带宽/延迟，并证明了这些成本无法在所有Jacobi算法中同时匹配并行矩阵乘法的已知最优界。此外，结果扩展到了一侧Jacobi SVD。

Result: 在2D处理器网格下，并行Jacobi实现了算术最优复杂度O(n^3/P)、带宽达到矩阵乘法下界、延迟接近下界；在2.5D网格下进一步降低了带宽和延迟，但证明了带宽和延迟无法同时达到并行矩阵乘法的最佳已知界；结果同样适用于一侧Jacobi SVD。

Conclusion: 并行Jacobi方法能够在分布式内存设置下实现算术最优和接近通信最优的性能，其带宽/延迟可匹敌或近乎匹敌并行矩阵乘法的下界，为对称特征值问题和SVD提供了高效可扩展的分布式并行求解方案。

Abstract: This paper presents several parallel versions of Jacobi's method for the symmetric eigenvalue problem and the SVD. A continuation of [Demmel, Luo, Schneider, & Wang 2025], we develop parallel Jacobi algorithms whose arithmetic cost is optimal and whose bandwidth or latency can match the corresponding lower bounds of parallel matrix multiplication. Our focus is a standard distributed-memory setting with variable processor layouts, including both 2D and 2.5D processor grids. In the 2D case, we demonstrate that a standard implementation of parallel Jacobi achieves a perfect speedup in arithmetic cost -- i.e., complexity $O(n^3/P)$ when done with $P$ processors -- while hitting the 2D matrix-multiplication lower bound for bandwidth and (nearly) the lower bound for latency. By employing a 2.5D processor grid and leveraging 2.5D matrix multiplication, equivalently by increasing the memory per processor, we demonstrate that parallel Jacobi can achieve even lower bandwidth/latency, though we also prove that these costs cannot simultaneously match the best-known bounds for parallel matrix multiplication in any Jacobi algorithm. Finally, we extend our results to one-sided Jacobi SVD.

</details>


### [9] [A Sharp Unitarily Invariant Norm Bound for the Off-Diagonal Block Perturbation of a Hermitian Matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29009)
*Lei-Hong Zhang,Ren-Cang Li*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究分块 Hermitian 矩阵 A 与其去掉非对角块后得到的矩阵 Ã 之间，特征值变化在任意酉不变范数下的界。Li 和 Li (2005) 已证明该界在谱范数下成立且是尖锐的；本文推广此结果，证明了该界在 Q-范数（包含 Schatten p 范数，2≤p≤∞）下成立，且在 rank(E)≤1 时对任意酉不变范数成立，并证明 2×2 矩阵可达到等号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Li 和 Li 建立了分块 Hermitian 矩阵去掉非对角块后特征值扰动的尖锐谱范数界，但该界能否推广到更一般的酉不变范数（如 Frobenius 范数等）尚不明确。本文旨在回答这一推广是否以及在何种条件下成立。

Method: 通过构造 φ(η,ε₂) 这一与两个谱间隙 η 和非对角块谱范数 ε₂ 相关的函数，对分块 Hermitian 矩阵进行规范化分析。作者分别考虑两类情形：(a) 对 Q-范数（酉不变范数的子类，涵盖 Schatten p-范数中 2≤p≤∞ 的情形），利用该范数的特性和谱对进行精细估计；(b) 对 rank(E)≤1 的低秩情形，利用秩为 1 的矩阵结构特性，将结论推广到任意酉不变范数。

Result: 获得了两个主要结果：(a) 该特征值界的推广对任意 Q-范数（涵盖 Schatten p-范数，2≤p≤∞，包括 Frobenius 范数和谱范数）均成立；(b) 当 rank(E)≤1 时，该推广对任意酉不变范数均成立。此外，证明了在 2×2 矩阵上该界可以达到等号，说明结果是尖锐的。

Conclusion: 本文成功将 Li 和 Li 的谱范数特征值扰动界推广到更广泛的酉不变范数体系，给出了该推广成立的充分条件（Q-范数或 rank(E)≤1），并验证了等号可达该界的尖锐性。这一结果丰富了分块 Hermitian 矩阵扰动理论，为特征值稳定性分析提供了更强的工具。

Abstract: Let $$ A=\begin{bmatrix} H_1 & E^* \\ E & H_2 \end{bmatrix} \quad\text{and}\quad \widetilde A=\begin{bmatrix} H_1 & 0 \\ 0 & H_2 \end{bmatrix} $$ be two partitioned Hermitian matrices, where $\widetilde A$ is obtained from $A$ by simply dropping the off-diagonal blocks, and let $η$ be the gap between the spectra ${\rm eig}(H_1)$ of $H_1$ and ${\rm eig}(H_2)$ of $H_2$. Define, for $δ\ge 0$ and $ε\ge 0$, $$ φ(δ,ε)= \begin{cases}
  2ε/(δ+\sqrt{δ^2+4ε^2}), &\quad\mbox{if $(δ,ε)\ne (0,0)$},
  1, &\quad\mbox{if $(δ,ε) = (0,0)$}, \end{cases} $$ and let $V=A-\widetilde A$ and $ε_2=\|E\|_2=\|V\|_2$, the matrix spectral norm. Li and Li [{\em Linear Algebra Appl.}, 395:183--190, 2005] established a sharp spectral-norm bound on the changes in the eigenvalues of $A$: $$ \big\|{\rm diag}\big(\pmbλ(A)-\pmbλ(\widetilde A)\big)\big\|_2 \le φ(η,ε_2)\,\|E\|_2, $$ where $\pmbλ(A)$ is the vector whose components are the eigenvalues of $A$ in descending order and similarly for $\pmbλ(\widetilde A)$. The goal of this paper is to resolve the question: how far an extension of this result in the form $$ \big\|{\rm diag}\big(\pmbλ(A)-\pmbλ(\widetilde A)\big)\big\|_{\rm UI} \le φ(η,ε_2)\,\|A-\widetilde A\|_{\rm UI} $$ remains valid for some or all unitarily invariant norms $\|\cdot\|_{\rm UI}$? Two results are obtained: (a) the extension holds for any $Q$-norm, a subclass of unitarily invariant norms that encompasses the Schatten $p$-norm for $2\le p\le\infty$ (particularly, the Frobenius norm and the spectral norm included), and (b) the extension holds for any unitarily invariant norm if ${\rm rank}(E)\le 1$. It is demonstrated that the equality is attained on the $2\times 2$ matrix $A$.

</details>

Abstract: This paper proposes gappy probabilistic manifold decomposition (Gappy PMD), a nonlinear method for reconstructing high-dimensional fields from extremely sparse measurements. Gappy PMD reconstructs the field on the nonlinear manifold learned by probabilistic manifold decomposition (PMD). We further propose a differentiable point selection method for reduced-order model (ROM)-based field reconstruction (DPS). Using differentiable meshless interpolation within the ROM-based reconstruction framework, DPS makes the full-field reconstruction error differentiable with respect to the sampling locations and directly optimizes these locations. In addition, a theoretical error analysis for Gappy PMD is also given. It splits the squared reconstruction error into two orthogonal parts: one normal to the reconstruction manifold and the other induced by sparse sampling and observation noise. Under a stability condition on the sampling operator, this error vanishes with the PMD approximation error and the noise. The Gappy PMD is evaluated on three numerical test cases: flow past a cylinder, lid-driven cavity flow, and backward-facing step flow. For the same reduced dimension and sampling points, Gappy PMD attains mean relative $L^2$ errors one to two orders of magnitude below Gappy POD. Optimizing the sampling points with DPS further improves reconstruction accuracy and robustness.

Result: 数值实验在多个具有挑战性的PDE问题上验证了方法的有效性，结果表明可学习多重网格比传统多重网格获得更快的收敛率。跨不同问题设置（变源项、变系数、变几何、变网格尺寸）的实验中，通过权重共享和迁移学习策略学习到的参数展现出良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel framework that integrates learnable graph convolutions with the geometric multigrid method for solving partial differential equations (PDEs). The discretization of PDEs is first represented as a graph structure, enabling the application of graph convolutions to enhance the multigrid performance. By incorporating graph convolutions into the multigrid components such as smoothing and inter-grid transfer operators, we develop a learnable multigrid that can adaptively optimize its performance based on the underlying problem characteristics. In this framework, the graph convolutions are embedded directly within the multigrid cycle, effectively transforming the entire multigrid solver into a specialized neural network architecture, rather than combining a classical solver with surrogate models. The learnable multigrid framework is lightweight in terms of parameter count and requires minimal training effort to achieve good performance. Numerical experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach in solving some challenging problems, showing improved convergence rates compared to traditional multigrid methods. The generalizability of the learned parameters across different problem settings, including varying source terms, coefficients, geometries, and mesh sizes, is also investigated with proper weight-sharing and transfer-learning strategies.

Abstract: This paper presents a convergence analysis for a newly developed nonlinear model reduction method: parametric probabilistic manifold decomposition (PPMD)~\cite{guo2026parametric}. In addition, existing analyzes of nonlinear reduced order models typically treat subspace reduction, manifold representation, regression, and nonlinear reconstruction as separate components and often remain at the level of discrete state vectors. To the best of our knowledge, no theory tracks the complete error propagation in a data-dependent model whose basis, residual geometry, spectral coordinates, parameter maps, and lifting operator are all learned from the same numerical solution data.
  We develop a coupled perturbation analysis for the entire PPMD procedure. A trajectory geometry induced by the spatial discretization and temporal quadrature connects discrete trajectory vectors isometrically with the corresponding PDE norm. Population spectral objects are introduced to align the empirical residual coordinates and derive a uniform coordinate error estimate, whose propagation through the Hilbert-valued kernel lifting estimator is then quantified. Combining these results with the full order discretization error, weighted low-rank approximation, parameter regression, and residual representation defect yields deterministic and high-probability trajectory error bounds and consistency in probability in the continuous PDE trajectory space. The theory identifies how the principal errors interact and which components limit the accuracy of the nonlinear reduced order model.

Abstract: The low-rank alternating direction implicit (ADI) method is an efficient numerical technique for solving several types of large-scale matrix equations that admit low-rank solutions. The discrete-time algebraic Riccati equation (DARE) is an important matrix equation with applications in state estimation, controller design, and filter design. In the literature, the low-rank Cholesky factor ADI method for Stein equations has been used within Newton iterations to solve large-scale DAREs. However, no dedicated low-rank ADI solver is available for such DAREs. To address this gap, this paper presents a low-rank ADI solver for large-scale DAREs. We also propose an efficient approach to generate ADI shifts automatically, which makes the proposed solver fully autonomous for solving DAREs. The effectiveness of the proposed solver is compared with MATLAB's \texttt{idare} on a moderate-order problem. Efficiency and accuracy are further demonstrated on large-scale DAREs of order $10^6$. Numerical results confirm that the solver is efficient, accurate, and fully autonomous.

Abstract: We develop a high-order tensor neural network (TNN) method for locally periodic elliptic multiscale problems of the form $-\nabla\cdot(A(x,x/\varepsilon)\nabla u_\varepsilon)=f$. Because the coefficient depends on both the slow variable $x$ and the fast periodic variable $y=x/\varepsilon$, the high-order cell problems and macroscopic corrector equations are more involved than in the classical case $A=A(y)$, and the correctors depend parametrically on $x$. We derive a computable high-order two-scale expansion and prove an $H^1$ convergence estimate for the partial expansion in boundary-layer-free settings, including periodic domains and ideal boundary-matching configurations. The proof uses the recursive compatibility structure of the corrector hierarchy and a zero-mean oscillation estimate in $H^{-1}$. We then construct a TNN framework for the high-dimensional corrector problems. Its tensor-product structure permits deterministic one-dimensional quadrature for the cell problems, homogenized coefficients, macroscopic source terms, and loss functions, avoiding Monte Carlo integration error. The numerical realization assumes that the coefficient entries and assembled data admit finite or controlled tensor-product representations; this computational assumption is separate from the general matrix-valued coefficient class used in the analysis. Experiments with scalar locally periodic coefficients show accurate high-order correctors. The $H^1$ semi-norm errors are consistent with the proved estimate, while point-normalized $L^2$ errors display the nominal high-order behavior predicted by the formal expansion.

Abstract: We develop an entropy-stable and physical-constraint-preserving discontinuous Galerkin spectral element method for symmetry-reduced general-relativistic hydrodynamics on prescribed stationary spacetimes. Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have emerged as a meshless alternative to conventional numerical methods for solving partial differential equations (PDEs). Owing to their highly nonlinear hyperbolic nature, solutions often develop strong discontinuities, making the design of stable and accurate numerical schemes challenging. Although Weighted Essentially Non-Oscillatory (WENO) schemes are widely used for such problems, component-wise WENO reconstruction may produce spurious oscillations near discontinuities. On the other hand, characteristic-wise WENO reconstruction provides accurate non-oscillatory solutions for systems of conservation laws, but it involves the computation of eigenvectors in each cell, which leads to high computational cost. In this work, we intend to develop hybrid schemes which maintain the non-oscillatory feature of characteristic-wise WENO while being less costly. The proposed troubled-cell indicator effectively distinguishes smooth and discontinuous regions, allowing the hybrid schemes to employ inexpensive reconstructions in smooth regions and the characteristic-wise WENO reconstruction only near discontinuities. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed schemes retain the accuracy and robustness of characteristic-wise WENO methods while significantly reducing the computational cost. In particular, the H1-WENO scheme achieves an approximately 30--40% improvement in computational efficiency compared to the standard WENO scheme in 2D test cases.

Abstract: In this paper, we consider structure-preserving model reduction of port-Hamiltonian (pH) systems which extend classical Hamiltonian systems with dissipation and an input-output port. These pH systems are often used in multi-physics systems, as the interconnection of one or more \pH systems results again in a pH system. If particularly the system matrices associated with the interconnection and/or dissipation of a pH system are state-dependent, then the evaluation of standard reduced-order models (ROMs) may depend on the dimension of the original full-order model, resulting in high computational costs. To circumvent these high costs, we propose to use structure-preserving neural networks. In particular, we perform two steps: (1) we use the generalized manifold Galerkin projection to project the pH system onto the reduced space; then (2) we train a neural network to learn the map from the reduced-order state to the reduced-order interconnection and dissipation system matrices. To ensure that the resulting ROM is again a pH system, the architecture of the neural network is chosen such that the skew-symmetry and positive semi-definiteness of the reduced-order systems matrices are maintained. In a numerical example, we consider a nonlinear mass-spring-damper system with state-dependent system matrices. The numerical results show that the proposed method achieves a significant computational speed-up compared to the original \ROM with comparable accuracy.

Abstract: Phylogenetic networks model evolutionary histories that involve reticulate events, but their structural complexity makes them difficult to interpret. Extracting their simple substructures both clarifies the evolutionary pathways and quantifies the complexity of the networks themselves. For a given rooted almost-binary phylogenetic network, the Level Minimization problem asks for a spanning subgraph that has the same root and leaf-set and whose level is minimum, i.e., which is as close to a tree as possible. Networks for which the minimum level is zero are known as tree-based networks and can be recognized in linear time. However, Level Minimization is NP-hard in general. State-of-the-art algorithms rely on exhaustive searches of the solution spaces and hence apply only to networks of limited size. In this paper, we propose two methods for Level Minimization using integer linear programming: an exact formulation for finding such a subgraph of level at most one, and a heuristic formulation for the general case. Computational experiments confirmed the practicality of both formulations. An application to ancestral recombination graphs suggests that the minimum level provides an alternative measure of the topological complexity of an inferred network.

Conclusion: 良好逻辑斯蒂拟合并不能保证推断的logit斜率能很好地近似相对生长优势。在非线性进化竞争中，当logit斜率被用作相对适应度的代理指标时，必须考虑观测延迟的影响。这一发现对使用logit斜率作为相对适应度估计的实证研究具有重要意义。

Abstract: Relative-frequency trajectories are often used to infer selective advantage in competing biological populations. A common empirical approach is to fit a linear function to the logit-transformed frequency of an invading type and interpret the slope as the relative advantage. Here we test how this estimator is affected when the competing types are observed after type-specific delays. We use SARS-CoV-2 variant replacement in the United Kingdom as empirical motivation and study the mechanism with simple two-type models. In an ideal exponential replacement model, fixed or randomly distributed observation delays change the intercept of the observed log-odds trajectory but not its slope, provided that delayed counts are aggregated before frequencies are formed and boundary effects are absent. In nonlinear SIR-type models, where the relative growth is time-dependent, delays can compare the competing variants at different dynamical phases and substantially bias the fitted logit slope. This bias can occur even when the delayed replacement trajectory remains nearly linear on the logit scale. Thus, a good logistic fit to the observed data is not sufficient to guarantee that the inferred logit slope approximates well the relative growth advantage. Observation delays should therefore be accounted for when logit slopes are used as proxies for relative fitness in nonlinear evolutionary competition.

Method: 分析一个具有非互惠相互作用的广义消费者-资源模型；使用腔方法（cavity method）推导适用于广泛资源供给函数的统一稳定性条件；比较自我更新资源与外部供给资源两种情况下的动力学相图。

Conclusion: 资源动力学从根本上重塑了生态系统可实现的动力学行为，这一发现对解释微生物群落实验结果具有重要启示。

Abstract: Ecological interactions are often mediated by feedbacks between organisms and their resource environments. Yet, how resource supply dynamics dictate collective dynamical phases of an ecosystem remains unclear. Here, we analyse a generalised consumer--resource model with non-reciprocal interactions to demonstrate that self-renewing versus externally-supplied resources yield fundamentally different dynamical phase diagrams. As interactions become increasingly non-reciprocal, ecosystems relying on self-renewing resources transition from stable dynamics to chaos and ultimately to infeasibility. By contrast, ecosystems with externally-supplied resources remain stable over a broader parameter range and transition to infeasibility without experiencing an intervening chaotic phase. Using the cavity method, we derive a unified stability condition applicable to a broad class of resource supply functions, explaining why externally-supplied resources can expand the stable region. We show that stability hinges crucially on the susceptibility of resources to perturbations, which depends strongly on their supply. Further, we show that external resource supply suppresses chaos in the unstable region by drastically reducing the susceptibility of resources closest to extinction. Our findings demonstrate that resource dynamics fundamentally reshape the accessible dynamical behaviours of an ecosystem, with implications for interpreting microbial community experiments.

</details>

*Jong-Hyeon Jeong*

TL;DR: 本文从统计角度批判性审视了流式细胞术差异丰度（DA）分析中的成分性（compositionality）问题，通过模拟研究比较了三种逐群体DA方法（edgeR/TMM、CLR+limma、ANCOM-BC2）和一种全局筛选方法（CODAK/PERMANOVA），发现适用于微生物组数据的方法（ANCOM-BC2和CLR+limma）优于edgeR/TMM，CODAK/PERMANOVA可作为整体成分差异的稳健前期筛查工具。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 通过理论对比三种逐群体DA方法（edgeR/TMM、CLR+limma、ANCOM-BC2）的统计基础和解释范围，并设计涵盖七个场景、两种样本量（每组n=50和n=200）的综合模拟研究，评估各方法在样本量、零膨胀、离散度、细胞群体相关性、FDR和敏感性等维度上的表现；同时评估基于Aitchison距离的PERMANOVA实现的CODAK作为全局筛查测试。

Result: 针对流式细胞术数据的逐群体DA分析，源于微生物组数据分析的方法ANCOM-BC2和CLR+limma在综合性能上优于edgeR/TMM，而CODAK/PERMANOVA为整体成分差异提供了稳健的第一阶段筛查方案。

Conclusion: 作者建议流式细胞术DA分析应采用对成分数据更友好、能妥善处理条件性推断的方法（如ANCOM-BC2和CLR+limma），而非直接沿用RNA-seq方法；CODAK/PERMANOVA可作为检测整体成分变化的全局筛查工具。

Abstract: Flow cytometry generates inherently compositional count data: observed cell population counts are constrained to sum to the total number of acquired events, which precludes direct inference about absolute cellular abundance. Despite this constraint, most differential abundance (DA) analyses in cytometry rely on methods originally developed for RNA sequencing, such as edgeR with trimmed mean of M-values (TMM) normalization, without fully acknowledging the conditional nature of the resulting estimates. Here, we offer a critical perspective on the statistical implications of compositionality for flow cytometry DA analysis. We compare the theoretical foundations and interpretational scope of three per-population DA testing methods (edgeR/TMM), centered log-ratio transformation with linear modeling (CLR + limma), and analysis of composition of microbiomes with bias correction-2 (ANCOM-BC2), and evaluate their performance through a comprehensive simulation study spanning seven scenarios at two sample sizes (n = 50 and n = 200 per group). We additionally evaluate compositional data analysis using kernels (CODAK) as a global, omnibus screening test for overall compositional differences, implemented via PERMANOVA on Aitchison distances. Our results show that, considering sample size, zero-inflation, dispersion, and correlation among cell populations in addition to false discovery rate (FDR) and sensitivity, the methods developed for microbiome data analysis, ANCOM-BC2 and CLR + limma, emerge as better-suited methods for per-population DA analysis of flow cytometry data than edgeR/TMM, while CODAK/PERMANOVA offers a robust first-stage screen for overall compositional shifts.

</details>

Method: GDC基函数定义为对数正态增长累积分布函数与指数衰减项的乘积，两者均可追溯到相应的控制微分方程。GDC的每个参数直接对应增长和衰减的时间尺度，可导出无量纲比值和形状描述符，将拟合结果与底层动力学联系起来。

Result: 在模拟微分方程解以及物理、生物和经济数据的应用中，GDC的拟合质量与样条、广义加性模型、径向基函数网络和傅里叶回归相当，同时具有显著更高的简洁性（更少参数）和物理可解释性。

Conclusion: GDC提供了一种兼顾拟合精度与物理解释性的替代方案，在保持与传统柔性回归方法相当表现的同时，大幅降低模型复杂度，并赋予拟合参数直接对应动力学过程的可解释语义。

Abstract: Curve fitting for physical, biological, and engineering data typically forces a choice between interpretable but rigid parametric forms and flexible but physically opaque smoothers. This paper introduces the Growth-Decay Curve (GDC), a physics-informed basis derived as the product of a lognormal growth cumulative distribution function and an exponential decay term, each traceable to a governing differential equation. GDC's parameters correspond directly to growth and decay timescales, yielding dimensionless ratios and shape descriptors that connect the fit back to the underlying dynamics. Across simulated differential-equation solutions and applications spanning physical, biological, and economic data, GDC matches the fit quality of splines, generalized additive models, radial basis function networks, and Fourier regression, while remaining substantially more parsimonious and physically interpretable.

</details>

Abstract: Varmapack is a software package for efficient, exact simulation of VARMA time series without a burn-in period. For stationary models, Varmapack can generate initial states and innovations from their joint stationary distribution. Alternatively, the user can supply initial states, in which case innovations are generated from their conditional distribution. The core C library has interfaces to R, Python, and Matlab. It also offers VARMAX simulation and computation of autocovariances and correlations, spectral radii, and impulse responses. Varmapack automatically selects between vector-Yule-Walker and state-space methods for covariance computation and uses level-3 BLAS operations for efficient generation of multiple replicates. Benchmarks on several platforms show substantial performance gains over existing simulation software, ranging from several-fold to more than three orders of magnitude for the packages and models considered. Varmapack is open source and publicly available through GitHub.

### [48] [Rescheduled, not redefined: The moving plateau of old-age mortality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29452)
*Silvio C. Patricio,Trifon I. Missov*

TL;DR: 本研究使用12个低死亡率人群的队列数据，发现死亡率减速与平台期的起始年龄在19世纪中叶至20世纪初出生的队列中持续向后推移。虽然各队列均支持平台期存在，但最年轻、尚未完全观测的队列证据较弱——减速始于约100岁，拟合平台期始于约108岁。过往研究因聚焦不同队列和固定年龄范围，实际是在采样同一动态变化的不同阶段，表明死亡率平台期并无固定年龄，而是被"重新安排"了。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 死亡率在极端高龄下是持续攀升还是趋于平缓，是百年来人口学与老年学领域长期争论未决的核心问题。作者认为这一冲突的根源在于研究设计差异而非现象本身的矛盾：不同研究使用不同队列与年龄窗口，恰好捕捉到同一个动态变化的不同阶段。

Method: 采用12个低死亡率人群的出生队列数据，覆盖从19世纪中叶至20世纪初出生的队列，系统地构建队列水平的死亡率轨迹，检验死亡率减速（deceleration）与平台期（plateau）起始年龄随出生队列的变化趋势，并对最年轻、未完全观测的队列进行外推拟合。

Result: 发现死亡率减速与平台期起始年龄随出生年代递进而持续推迟；所有完整观测的队列均支持平台期存在，但最年轻队列的证据较弱；最年轻队列中减速约在100岁出现，拟合平台期约在108岁之后开始。过去研究因聚焦不同队列和固定年龄区间，采样了同一动态变化的不同阶段。

Conclusion: 死亡率平台期并非固定不变的生命现象，而是随队列推移被"重新安排"（rescheduled）到更晚年龄。此前的学术分歧源于研究设计的时间视角差异，而非生物学规则本身的矛盾，未来研究应采用动态、队列匹配的框架来理解极端寿命下的死亡风险。

Abstract: Whether the risk of death keeps climbing at extreme ages or levels off has divided researchers for a century. We show this conflict reflects a moving target. Using cohort data from twelve low-mortality populations, we find that mortality deceleration and plateau onset shift steadily later across cohorts born from the mid-19th to the early-20th century. The data support a plateau across the full cohort range, though evidence weakens for the youngest, incompletely observed cohorts. In these younger cohorts, deceleration begins near age 100, and the fitted plateau begins beyond age 108. Because past studies focused on different cohorts and fixed age ranges, they sampled different phases of the exact same shift. The mortality plateau has no fixed age: it has been rescheduled.

</details>

*Kanghyun Wi,Jaewoo Park,Saumya Bhatnagar,Won Chang*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 提出SC-DS，一种完全基于神经网络的序贯校准方法，利用条件扩散模型模拟南极冰盖厚度场（含冰覆盖二元模式与连续厚度），并通过Siamese网络实现免似然校准，在保持精度的同时大幅降低计算成本并支持全分辨率。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 南极冰盖是未来海平面预测不确定性的主要来源，其物理模拟器（如PSU3D-ICE）校准面临挑战：输出和观测为高维、空间相关、半连续场（在冰覆盖区域存在零处质量集中），这使得传统高斯过程仿真和基于似然的校准在全分辨率下计算不可行且不适用。

Conclusion: SC-DS证明了深度学习框架（扩散模型+Siamese网络）能够有效克服传统统计校准方法在高维、半连续、空间相关地球物理模拟器校准中的计算瓶颈，为全分辨率冰盖模型校准提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The Antarctic ice sheet is a major source of uncertainty in future sea-level projections, and physical simulators such as the PSU3D-ICE model are essential for studying its evolution. Calibrating them against observations is challenging: the simulator outputs and observed ice-thickness fields are high-dimensional, spatially dependent, and semi-continuous, with a large point mass at zero denoting ice-free regions. These features make conventional Gaussian-process emulation and likelihood-based calibration ill-suited and computationally infeasible at full resolution. We propose the sequential calibration method guided by a diffusion model and a Siamese network (SC-DS), a fully neural framework. For emulation, we develop a single conditional diffusion model that jointly generates the binary ice presence--absence pattern and the continuous thickness field, using global and local conditioning to represent how the input parameters shape the output. Because the emulator induces an intractable likelihood, our likelihood-free calibration replaces the hand-chosen distance and tolerance of approximate Bayesian computation with a probabilistic acceptance rule learned by an iteratively retrained Siamese network, together with a data--model discrepancy adjustment. Applied to the West Antarctic Ice Sheet, SC-DS matches the accuracy of state-of-the-art Gaussian-process calibration at a fraction of its computational cost and scales to the full-resolution domain, where existing methods become intractable.

</details>

This work focuses on classifying customer letters as cancellations and identifying the respective reason, if available. The outlined approach can also be applied to other business transactions and reasons. We discuss data acquisition and preparation, present alternatives, and explain the reasons for the chosen approach. A successful implementation of such a tool can lead to a better understanding of customer cancellation behavior by the insurer, enabling more targeted actions in certain situations.

### [51] [Decarbonising price formation: unit-level evidence on battery storage and the imbalance price in the GB Balancing Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29818)
*Robert Dalton,Aidan O'Sullivan*

TL;DR: 本研究基于2023至2025年间英国平衡机制中50,684个半小时结算周期的数据，重构了价格排序的边际投标/报价栈边缘，并归因于各平衡机制单元。电池在投标侧边际性从0.8%升至36.2%，在报价侧从3.2%升至26.9%，分别取代了联合循环燃气和抽水蓄能。容量归一化的边际捕获率双侧上升；增加100MW平衡机制活跃容量与季度投标侧和报价侧边际份额分别提高0.55和0.48个百分点相关。至2025年，电池行动比同阶梯的非电池替代方案每MWh更有利约9-10英镑，但在最高价5%的短系统时段中，电池代表性不足12.9个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可再生能源在英国发电结构中已占主导，但很少处于边际定价位置。作者希望弄清究竟是哪些灵活技术将高可再生能源系统转化为实时价格形成机制，即探究边际价格制定者在可再生主导电力系统中的演变。

Method: 重构英国平衡机制中符合条件的投标/报价栈的价格排序边缘，并归因至单个平衡机制单元；区分长系统投标活跃（bid-active）与短系统报价活跃（offer-active）条件，保留共同边际（co-marginal）联系；采用容量归一化边际捕获率指标，并利用单元-季度固定效应回归估计活跃容量对边际份额的影响。

Result: 电池在投标侧边际占比从0.8%增至36.2%，取代联合循环燃气；在报价侧从3.2%增至26.9%，取代抽水蓄能。100MW额外平衡机制活跃容量与投标侧及报价侧季度边际份额分别提升0.55和0.48个百分点相关。2025年电池行动较同阶梯非电池替代每MWh更有利约9-10英镑，但在最高价5%短系统时段代表性不足12.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 个体电池行为与价格接受者一致，但电池集群整体已内生于日常平衡价格形成过程。这意味着电池已成为英国实时边际定价的核心参与者，但其在高价格极端时段的参与仍存在缺口。

Abstract: Renewables now dominate Great Britain's generation mix but rarely occupy the marginal price-setting position, which raises the question of which flexible technologies translate a renewable-rich system into real-time price formation. This study reconstructs the price-ranked edge of the eligible bid or offer stack in the GB Balancing Mechanism across 50,684 half-hourly Settlement Periods from 2023 to 2025 and attributes it to individual Balancing Mechanism Units, separating long-system bid-active from short-system offer-active conditions and retaining co-marginal ties. Batteries rose from 0.8% to 36.2% of bid-active and from 3.2% to 26.9% of offer-active marginality, displacing combined-cycle gas on the bid side and pumped storage on the offer side. Capacity-normalised marginal capture rose on both sides, and unit-quarter fixed effects show that an additional 100 MW of Balancing Mechanism active capacity was associated with a 0.55 and 0.48 percentage-point increase in quarterly bid- and offer-side marginal share. By 2025, battery actions were around £9 to 10/MWh more favourable than volume-matched non-battery alternatives drawn from the same ladder, yet batteries were under-represented by 12.9 percentage points in the highest-priced 5% of short-system periods. Individual batteries remain consistent with price-taking, while the fleet has become endogenous to routine balancing price formation.

TL;DR: 本文比较了6种纵向统计模型（从传统线性混合效应模型到灵活的非线性模型）用于刻画肺部疾病康复过程中FEV1p的非线性变化轨迹，并使用肺结核2期试验数据验证，发现灵活的非线性模型能够揭示传统线性模型遗漏的特定时期治疗差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺部疾病康复后的肺功能纵向恢复轨迹通常非线性的，但现有研究缺乏对建模非线性纵向轨迹的统计方法的系统评估，导致可能产生有偏或误导的治疗效应估计。

Method: 使用肺结核2期随机试验数据，在180天随访期内比较6种纵向模型：(1)线性混合效应模型、(2)分段线性混合效应模型、(3)二次样条和(4)自然三次样条混合效应模型、(5)指数恢复函数非线性混合效应模型、(6)广义加性混合模型；评估各处理组与对照组间FEV1p重复测量的平均差异，并讨论了各方法的假设、临床解释及治疗效应估计。

Result: 传统线性混合效应模型在随访期内仅提供有限的治疗差异证据，而多种灵活模型（如分段线性、样条和广义加性混合模型）在特定恢复时间段识别出了显著的治疗差异。

Conclusion: 灵活的纵向模型可作为传统线性混合效应模型的补充工具，能够揭示单一线性趋势假设下可能被掩盖的特定随访期处理效应；非线性建模策略的选择应基于科学目标、可用数据以及临床可解释性与灵活性的平衡。

Abstract: Introduction: Longitudinal lung function recovery after pulmonary disease commonly follows nonlinear trajectories, and failure to adequately model these trajectories can lead to biased or misleading estimates of treatment effects. However, an important methodological gap remains as there is limited assessment of statistical methods for modeling nonlinear lung function trajectories.
  Methods: We compared several longitudinal modeling approaches for characterizing recovery in percent predicted forced expiratory volume in one second (FEV1p) using data from a phase 2 randomized trial for pulmonary tuberculosis (TB). We estimate the differences in repeated mean FEV1p between each treatment arm and control arm over a 180-days follow-up period. We compared 6 different statistical models: (1) linear mixed-effects model, (2) a piecewise linear mixed-effects model, (3) quadratic and (4) natural cubic spline mixed-effects models, (5) a nonlinear mixed-effects model with exponential recovery function, and (6) a generalized additive mixed model. We discussed the assumptions, clinical interpretations, and resulting treatment-effect estimates across these approaches.
  Results: The results from the TB trial analyses showed that the conventional linear mixed-effects model provided limited evidence of treatment differences over follow-up, whereas several flexible models identified significant differences during specific periods of recovery.
  Conclusion: Flexible longitudinal models can complement conventional linear mixed-effects models by revealing treatment differences at certain periods of follow-up that may be obscured by assuming a single linear trend. The choice of nonlinear modeling strategy should be guided by the scientific objective, available data, and the desired balance between clinical interpretability and flexibility.

</details>

Result: 所有市场均检测到长程依赖；分数阶积分模型对大多数市场提供最佳拟合，但SARIMA对某些市场仍然更优。

Conclusion: 长记忆的统计证据并不一定意味着分数阶积分模型是最优实证选择，模型选择应基于数据驱动的方式，而非仅凭长记忆检验结果。

Abstract: Agricultural commodity prices often exhibit strong temporal persistence, which may limit the performance of conventional time series models. This study investigates long memory in logarithmic monthly white maize prices from six major markets in Togo between January 2001 and June 2022. Long memory is examined using the Geweke--Porter--Hudak, Local Whittle, Exact Local Whittle, and wavelet log-regression estimators. SARIMA, ARFIMA, and SARFIMA models are subsequently compared using the Bayesian Information Criterion and residual diagnostics. Long-range dependence is found across all markets. Fractionally integrated models provide the best fit for most markets, although SARIMA remains preferable for some. The results demonstrate that evidence of long memory does not necessarily imply that a fractionally integrated model provides the best empirical fit, emphasizing the importance of data-driven model selection.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出一套完整的公交AVL数据清洗方法论，并开发一个基于开放数据标准的开源R包来实现整个工作流并重建车辆轨迹。该方法包括数据清洗、轨迹重建、微观性能指标计算等步骤，并使用交叉验证来量化不同轮询频率下重建轨迹的误差。

Result: 在使用印第安纳波利斯3000多次行程的大型AVL数据集上验证表明：（1）方法高效，大数据集仅需约3分钟处理；（2）位置估计误差低，15秒轮询频率下RMSE低于10米；（3）估计的信号性能指标可用于评估走廊的信号编程。

Conclusion: 本文作为实用指南和配套工具箱，帮助从业人员利用公交AVL数据构建车辆停走循环的详细视图，填补了开源工具方面的空白，并为信号性能评估提供了可行手段。

Abstract: Automatic vehicle location (AVL) data produced by transit vehicles is invaluable in performance studies, but turning raw AVL points into a detailed view of vehicle stop-and-gos is burdensome: the datasets are sparse, noisy, and prone to blunders. While recent research has explored methods of reconstructing trajectories describing the position of vehicles over time, the common techniques can be complex, and no open-source tools exist to help practitioners process the raw AVL. We fill this gap by proposing a thorough methodology for cleaning transit AVL data and providing an open-source R package, built on open data standards, to implement the workflow and reconstruct vehicle trajectories, allowing practitioners to easily formulate custom microscopic performance metrics. Using a large AVL dataset with over 3,000 trips from Indianapolis, Indiana, we demonstrate the workflow, evaluate the package's efficiency, and demonstrate the utility of the trajectories by estimating various traffic signal performance metrics. Finally, we use cross-validation to quantify the error in reconstructed trajectories at various polling frequencies. We find that the proposed data processing methodology and tool are efficient, requiring roughly 3 minutes of processing time on the large dataset, and error in position estimates is low (root mean square error under 10 meters at polling frequencies of 15 seconds). The estimated signal performance metrics can inform future evaluations of signal programming along the corridor. In sum, this paper serves as a practical guide and complementary toolbox for practitioners seeking to use transit AVL data to build a detailed view of vehicle stop-and-go cycles.

</details>

### [55] [Bellman Search in Arbitrary Finite Dimension: A Self-Similar Cell Theorem and Effective Computability of Planar Shoreline Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29032)
*Florentin Koch*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文研究了海岸线搜索问题：从原点出发的路径需与一条未知仿射线相遇，且不知道其法线方向或距离。通过建立自相似约化定理、单元编码与量化消元等技术，证明了确定性平面海岸线搜索的最优值C_2*是可计算实数——对任意有理数ε>0，存在一个算法能够终止并输出包含C_2*的宽度不超过ε的有理区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海岸线搜索是经典的最优搜索问题的一个变体，目标是从原点出发找到一条与未知仿射线相遇的路径。由于线的法线和距离均未知，这是一个高度非确定性的几何搜索问题。此前缺乏有效的理论框架来刻画最优搜索路径，更不用说计算最优值。

Method: 1) 建立自相似约化定理：针对历史信息为记录剖面（通过逐点最大值更新）的齐次搜索问题，用两个准回归归一化状态界定一个能自我更新的块，再以短连接符闭合为单元；2) 将状态空间有限编码，可计算地界定近最优单元的尺度因子和归一化长度；3) 对平面海岸线搜索，利用凸包支撑函数给出精确的单元泛函；4) 通过单侧多边形化将问题归约为可计算数量的顶点，再用量化消元判断是否存在低于有理阈值的多边形单元。

Result: 证明了确定性平面海岸线搜索的最优值C_2*是可计算实数：对于任意有理数ε>0，算法会终止并输出一个宽度不超过ε且包含C_2*的有理区间。另外还提供了滑动记忆、Bellman转移、截止时间、几何滤波和相对平衡等辅助结果作为工具箱，但未在可计算性证明中使用。

Conclusion: 本文第一次给出了海岸线搜索最优值的可计算性保证，将无限维的几何搜索最优化问题转化为有限的可计算问题。自相似约化定理与有限编码方法为更广泛的齐次搜索问题提供了通用框架，而辅助工具则服务于螺旋刚度的研究与认证计算。

Abstract: A shoreline-search path starts at the origin and must meet an unknown affine line, without knowing either its normal or its distance. We first establish a self-similar reduction theorem for homogeneous search problems whose historical information is a record profile updated by pointwise maximum. Two quasi-returns of the normalized state delimit a block that renews the required profile by itself; a short connector closes this block into a cell. Every finite-ratio path can therefore be approximated, with arbitrarily small loss, by repetitions of a single cell at all scales.
  The main chain is then made effective. A finite coding of the state space computably bounds the scale factor and normalized length of a nearly optimal cell. For planar Shoreline search, the support function of the convex hull gives an exact cell functional. A one-sided polygonalization then reduces the problem to a computable number of vertices, after which quantifier elimination decides whether a polygonal cell exists below a rational threshold.
  It follows that the optimal deterministic planar Shoreline value $C_2^*$ is a computable real: for every rational $ε>0$, an algorithm terminates with a rational interval of width at most $ε$ containing $C_2^*$. Additional results---sliding memory, Bellman transitions, deadlines, geometric filters, and relative equilibria---are presented separately as a toolbox for certified computation and for the study of spiral rigidity; they are not used in the computability proof.

</details>


### [56] [Bellman--Shoreline Search in Arbitrary Dimension: Exponential Vector Oscillators, Active Memory, Precession, and Effective Computability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29060)
*Florentin Koch*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文研究在R^D中在线搜索未知仿射超平面的问题，通过自相似单元归约和支持函数方法，系统分析了当法空间从S^0扩展到S^{D-1}时搜索机制的变化。在D=1得到等波纹原理和精确常数9，在D=2得到对数螺线的相对平衡，并通过N-COMP定理证明C_D^*对任意固定有限维都是可计算的实数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在理解在线仿射超平面搜索问题在高维空间中的结构与机制。从一维到更高维度，随着法空间的增长，搜索策略、最优常数以及几何结构如何变化是一个自然的理论问题。作者希望建立统一的理论框架，将低维情形（D=1,2）中得出的精确结论推广到任意有限维空间。

Method: 论文提出了一种多维方法体系，包括：(1) 自相似单元归约与支持函数公式化处理；(2) 对指数轨道Γ(σ)=e^{κσ}ω(σ)发展了对数方向几何、指数折扣记忆、度量（gauges）和递归超球面参数化；(3) 在规则室中推导精确的变分、切触、螺距和年龄公式；(4) 对奇数维障碍、反对映子类和调和塔给出了约束条件和显式候选族；(5) 通过N-COMP定理证明C_D^*的可计算性以及代数多边形ε-最优单元的合成可行性。

Result: D=1时获得了等波纹原理和精确静止常数9；D=2时得到瓶颈弦线施加切触条件并选择螺距的对数螺旋；证明瓶颈证书最多由D个历史供应商支持，全局最差相位下当前点位于活跃面上；在D=3中推导出延迟系统恒等式；N-COMP定理表明C_D^*对任意固定有限D是可计算实数，且原则上可合成代数多边形ε-最优单元；数值筛选扩展到D=10但独立于已证明结果。

Conclusion: 该论文为任意固定有限维空间中在线仿射超平面搜索问题建立了系统的理论框架，从低维精确解逐步推广到高维的结构性结论。虽然奇数维障碍和全局最优性尚未完全解决，但N-COMP定理确保了逐步逼近的可计算性，将数值结果与严格证明明确分离，体现了结果的严谨性。

Abstract: We study online search for an unknown affine hyperplane in $\mathbb{R}^D$, for arbitrary fixed finite dimension. Building on a companion self-similar cell reduction and support-function formulation, we ask how the mechanism changes as the normal space grows from $\mathbb{S}^0$ to $\mathbb{S}^{D-1}$. In $D=1$, alternation and productivity yield an equal-ripple principle and the exact stationary constant $9$. In $D=2$, the analogous relative equilibrium is a logarithmic spiral whose bottleneck chord imposes tangency and selects the pitch.
  For exponential orbits $Γ(σ)=e^{κσ}ω(σ)$, we develop log-directional geometry, exponentially discounted memory, gauges, and recursive hyperspherical parametrizations. Without a shape ansatz, the bottleneck admits a certificate supported by at most $D$ historical suppliers, and at globally worst phases the current point lies on the active face. Within regular chambers we derive exact variation, tangency, pitch, age, and, in $D=3$, delay-system identities. Odd-dimensional obstructions, antipodal subclasses, and harmonic towers provide constraints and explicit candidate families but are not claimed globally optimal.
  Finally, the N-COMP theorem shows that $C_D^*$ is a computable real for every fixed finite $D$ and that algebraic polygonal $\varepsilon$-optimal cells can in principle be synthesized. Numerical screening through $D=10$ is kept separate from the proved results.

</details>


### [57] [Combinatorial maps for hierarchical splines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29545)
*Caleb B. Goates,Kendrick M. Shepherd,Derek C. Thomas*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种为分层样条（如分层和截断分层B样条）的Bézier网格构建拓扑数据结构（组合映射）的算法，该拓扑信息可在下游应用中实现高达一个数量级的计算时间缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层样条在多重尺度和自适应等几何分析中至关重要，其Bézier网格是定义和多个关键算法（如自适应细化和Bézier提取）的基础。然而，典型的分层样条公式只存储元素列表，不计算拓扑数据（如邻接信息），这限制了相关算法和下游应用的性能。缺少拓扑数据结构是提升这些算法效率的主要瓶颈。

Method: 本文提出了构建组合映射（combinatorial map）作为分层样条Bézier网格的拓扑数据结构的算法，适用于细化层级具有一致性Bézier网格的立方体单元复合体。方法涵盖了分层B样条、截断分层B样条以及其它分层样条公式的子集。算法构建了包含邻接信息的高效拓扑数据结构，支持自适应细化和Bézier提取等操作。

Result: 作者展示了这些分层组合映射构建算法的性能特征，并通过示例用例证明拓扑信息能在下游样条应用中提供高达一个数量级的计算时间缩减。

Conclusion: 通过为分层样条的Bézier网格构建组合映射拓扑数据结构，可以显著提升下游应用的性能（最高一个数量级的加速），为分层样条的高效实现提供了一种可行且有效的拓扑信息基础设施。

Abstract: Hierarchical splines are an important part of multiscale and adaptive isogeometric analysis formulations. The Bézier meshes of these splines are an essential part of their definition and of several important hierarchical spline algorithms, such as adaptive refinement and Bézier extraction. Topological data associated with the Bézier mesh-such as adjacency information-can be used to improve the performance of many of these algorithms as well as downstream applications of the splines, but typical hierarchical spline formulations do not compute the topological data, storing instead just a list of elements. In this work we present algorithms to build a performant topological data structure, namely the combinatorial map, to represent Bézier meshes of hierarchical splines over cubical cell complexes where the refinement levels have conforming Bézier meshes. This includes hierarchical and truncated hierarchical B-splines, as well as subsets of other hierarchical spline formulations. We show the performance characteristics of the construction algorithms of these hierarchical combinatorial maps, as well as an example use case, showing that the topological information can provide up to an order of magnitude reduction in computation time in downstream applications of the splines.

</details>

*Satyan L. Devadoss,Matthew Harvey,David Richter*

TL;DR: 本文发现三个例外4D正多胞体（24-cell、120-cell、600-cell）的显式脊展开图会导致面重叠，从而终结了具有"全网性质"的规则多胞体的完整分类研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究正多胞体的展开图是否能在不重叠的情况下平铺到低维空间（即"全网性质"），此前已完成部分分类，但三个例外4D正多胞体的情况尚待确定。

Abstract: We find explicit ridge unfoldings of the three exceptional 4D polytopes (24-cell, 120-cell, 600-cell) that result in overlaps of their facets. These failures bring an end to the full classification of regular polytopes with the all-net property.

</details>


### [59] [Curves of constant width and Lebesgue's covering problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30538)
*Ujjwal Mishra*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文证明任何凸通用覆盖集（universal cover）的面积至少为0.8344，改进了2005年的0.832和2026年预印本中的0.833。作者使用定宽曲线（圆盘、Reuleaux三角形和Reuleaux五边形）作为测试集，通过解析归约加有限计算的方式给出了严格的证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lebesgue于1914年提出的通用覆盖集最小面积问题至今未解。此前最好的下界来自圆盘加上正三角形和正五边形的组合，分别得出0.832（2005年）和0.833（2026年预印本）。作者希望进一步改进这一下界。

Method: 作者用定宽曲线（圆盘、Reuleaux三角形、Reuleaux五边形）替代正多边形作为测试集。每个Reuleaux多边形都包含其对应的正多边形，因此在相同数量的测试体和相同数量的放置参数下，测试族严格更大。证明由两部分组成：（1）解析归约——通过将每个Reuleaux多边形侵蚀为每个允许放置中固定的集合，一次性限制整个放置盒上的凸包面积下界；（2）一个有限计算——对五维空间进行穷尽式细分，生成包含486,799,600个节点的证书，并由独立于搜索的验证器检查，浮点误差的严格界比验证获得的余量小数千倍。

Result: 证明了每个凸通用覆盖集的面积至少为0.8344，优于此前所有已知下界。同时证明经典配置（圆盘、等边三角形、正五边形）存在一种排列使凸包面积低于0.8336，因此用这三个集合无法通过该论证方法达到本结果。

Conclusion: 通过引入定宽曲线作为测试集并结合解析归约与经过验证的有限计算，成功将凸通用覆盖集面积下界从0.833提升到0.8344。该方法论为Lebesgue通用覆盖问题提供了新的严格证明途径。

Abstract: A universal cover is a convex set in the plane that contains a congruent copy of every planar set of diameter one. Lebesgue asked in 1914 for one of least area, and the value is not known. We prove that every convex universal cover has area at least 0.8344, improving on 0.832, published in 2005, and 0.833, in a 2026 preprint, both of which come from a disc together with an equilateral triangle and a regular pentagon. Our test sets are instead curves of constant width: the disc, the Reuleaux triangle and the Reuleaux pentagon. Each contains the regular polygon it is built on, so the family is strictly larger at the same number of bodies and the same number of placement parameters, and we show that the classical configuration admits an arrangement whose hull has area below 0.8336, so no bound drawn from those three sets by this argument reaches ours. Curves of constant width were proposed for this role, and explored numerically, by Gibbs in 2014; what is added here is a proof. It consists of an analytic reduction followed by one finite computation. The reduction bounds the hull area from below over an entire box of placements at once, by eroding each Reuleaux polygon to a fixed set contained in every placement that box allows. The computation is an exhaustive subdivision of the resulting five-dimensional space, recorded as a certificate of 486,799,600 nodes and checked by a verifier independent of the search, with a rigorous bound on its floating point error some thousands of times smaller than the margin the verification attains.

</details>

Conclusion: 本文通过改进的圆法成功处理了加权射影空间中非对称权重的整点计数问题，得到了渐近公式成立所需的维度条件，并以此推出积分Hasse原理在充分大维数情形下的成立。这一结果推广了经典圆法在对称情形下的相关结论，为加权射影空间中算术问题的研究提供了新的工具和视角。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce several novel approaches utilizing the circle method to obtain the asymptotic formula for the number of integral points of bounded height lying on a hypersurface in a weighted projective space.
  Let $F(\mathbf{x} ; \mathbf{y})$ be a given weighted form of degree $d$ in variables $\mathbf{x} \in \mathbb{R}^{s_1}$ and $\mathbf{y} \in \mathbb{R}^{s_2}$, where variables $\mathbf{x}$ and $\mathbf{y}$ have weights $w_1$ and $w_2$ with $w_1<w_2,\left(w_1, w_2\right)=1$, and $d>w_1 w_2$. Write $$ R_F(P):=\#\left\{(\mathbf{x} ; \mathbf{y}) \in \mathbb{Z}^{s_1+s_2}: F(\mathbf{x} ; \mathbf{y})=0,|\mathbf{x}| \leq P^{w_1 / d},|\mathbf{y}| \leq P^{w_2 / d}\right\} . $$ In particular, we show that whenever $$ s_1+s_2-σ_F>\left(1+\frac{w_2}{w_1}\right) \frac{d}{w_1} 2^{d / w_1}, $$ where $σ_F$ is the dimension of the affine singular locus of $F$, the quantity $R_F(P)$ has the expected asymptotic formula, that is $$ R_F(P)=c P^{s_1 w_1 / d+s_2 w_2 / d-1}+o\left(P^{s_1 w_1 / d+s_2 w_2 / d-1}\right), $$ where $c$ is the product of local densities. Furthermore, the constant $c$ is positive whenever $F(\mathbf{x} ; \mathbf{y})=0$ has a nonsingular solution over $\mathbb{R}$ and $\mathbb{Q}_p$ for every prime $p$. As a corollary, we verify the integral Hasse principle for the quasi-smooth hypersurface defined by $F(\mathbf{x} ; \mathbf{y})=0$ in a weighted projective space of sufficiently large dimensions.

Abstract: Let $p$ be an odd prime and let $K/\mathbb{Q}_p$ be a finite unramified extension. Abstract: We study matrix representations of the Jacobsthal sequence generated by binary 3x3 matrices with determinants 0, 2, and -2, using linear algebraic methods analogous to Fibonacci-type constructions. Abstract: Suppose a set of integers $A\subseteq\{1,\dots,N\}$ has no solutions to $a-a'=n\lfloor\sqrt[3]2n\rfloor,$ for distinct $a,a'\in A,$ and $n\in \mathbb{N}.$ We show that $|A|\ll N^{1-c}$ for some absolute constant $c>0.$ To do this, we prove quantitative bounds on the van der Corput property for certain sets of bracket quadratics. This comes as a consequence of establishing exponential sum estimates for these sets, utilising a theorem of Green and Tao on the quantitative equidistribution of polynomial orbits on nilmanifolds, closely following the approach of Neale who went on to prove a Waring-type result. We also extend our result to differences avoiding a family of bracket polynomials (also known as generalised polynomials).

### [87] [Complete characterization of a class of complete permutation quadrinomials over \(\mathbb{F}_{q^2}\)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30126)
*Yanjun Li,Maosheng Xiong*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文完整刻画了有限域 $\mathbb{F}_{q^2}$ 上形如 $f(x) = c_0 x^{Q+1} + c_1 x^Q \overline{x} + c_2 x \overline{x}^Q + c_3 \overline{x}^{Q+1}$ 的完全置换二次四项式（其中 $Q=2^k$，$1 \le k \le m-1$）。对于 $k=1$ 的情形，证明了 Chan 等人给出的条件是充要且完整的；对于 $k>1$ 的情形，证明了不存在这样的完全置换多项式。从而对所有 $1 \le k \le m-1$ 完成了特征刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完全置换多项式（CPP）在密码学、编码理论和组合设计中具有重要应用。此前 Tu 等人给出了 $k=1$ 情形下此类四次四项式成为 CPP 的充分条件，Chan 等人进一步证明了该条件的必要性并揭示了其与 $x^2\overline{x}$ 的线性等价关系。但 $k>1$ 的情形尚未解决，本文旨在补全这一空白，完成对所有 $k$ 的完整分类。

Method: 本文通过证明对于 $k>1$ 不存在此类完全置换四项式，并确认 $k=1$ 时 Chan 等人的条件是完整充要的，从而将 $1 \le k \le m-1$ 范围内所有 $Q=2^k$ 的情形统一刻画。具体证明方法涉及完全置换多项式的代数和组合性质分析，以及与原函数 $f+x$ 的线性等价关系研究。

Conclusion: 该研究完成了对 $\mathbb{F}_{q^2}$ 上这类完全置换二次四项式的完整分类。结合 Tu 等人和 Chan 等人的前期工作，现在对所有可能的 $k$ 值（$1 \le k \le m-1$）都有明确的刻画：CPP 仅存在于 $k=1$ 的情形，且其结构完全由 Chan 等人的条件所确定。

Abstract: Let $q = 2^m$, $Q = 2^k$, and $1 \leq k \leq m-1$. Write $\overline{x} = x^q$. We study complete permutation quadrinomials over $\mathbb{F}_{q^2}$ of the form \[ f(x) = c_0 x^{Q+1} + c_1 x^Q \overline{x} + c_2 x \overline{x}^Q + c_3 \overline{x}^{Q+1},\qquad c_i \in \mathbb{F}_{q^2}. \]
  When \(k=1\), Tu et al. (Finite Fields Appl. 68: 1-20, 2020) gave a sufficient condition for \(f\) to be a complete permutation polynomial (CPP) over \(\mathbb{F}_{q^2}\). Chan et al. (Finite Fields Appl. 110: 102734, 2026) later proved that this condition is also necessary, and that under this condition \(f\) and \(f+x\) are linearly equivalent to \(x^2\overline{x}\) and \(x^2\overline{x}+γx\), respectively, for some \(γ\in \mathbb{F}_{q^2}^*\) with $\ord(γ^{q-1})=3$. In this paper, we prove that no such CPP exists for \(k>1\), and that the known condition of Chan et al. is complete for \(k=1\). This completes the characterization for all \(Q=2^k\) with $1 \leq k \leq m-1$.

</details>


### [88] [$13$ unknowns over quadratic integer rings and Lucas congruences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30389)
*Geng-Rui Zhang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了对任意二次数域K，可在O_K上给出整数元组的均匀3未知量Diophantine定义（允许有限多个多项式非零条件），由此导出有效的+3转移原理和13未知量的递归可枚举整数关系表示。最终得到绝对次数界D_0，使得对每个二次数域K，不存在算法能判定次数不超过D_0的13元整系数多项式在O_K^13中有无解。核心算术工具是四阶Pell-Lucas同余式，它是范数一Lucas乘法公式的特化，用于获得Lucas商偏离其线性项的精确赋值，以及Lucas-Wieferich和Wall-Sun-Sun素数的偏离判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数域的Diophantine定义与Hilbert第十问题研究中，关键问题是为给定数域K构造整环O_K的Diophantine定义，从而将整数上的不可判定性转移到O_K上。此前对二次数域只有非均匀或需更多未知量的结果。本文旨在对所有二次数域给出均匀且未知量数目最少（3个）的Diophantine定义，进而导出次数有界情形的不可判定性。

Method: 利用四阶Pell-Lucas同余作为核心算术输入。该同余是范数一Lucas乘法公式的特化，通过精确计算Lucas商偏离线性项的赋值来构造Diophantine条件。文中还建立了范数一Lucas序列中二阶修正项的局部满射性和ℓ-adic稠密性，以处理非零条件与解的存在性。基于3未知量定义，应用转移原理将整数上的递归枚举关系编码到O_K上，最终构造13未知量多项式表示。

Result: (1) 对所有二次数域K，建立了O_K上整数元组的均匀3未知量Diophantine定义；(2) 得到有效的+3转移原理；(3) 每个递归可枚举整数关系可用13个未知量在O_K上表示；(4) 存在绝对常数D_0≥1，使得对每个二次数域K，判断13元整系数多项式（次数≤D_0）在O_K^13中是否有解的问题不可判定。

Conclusion: 该结果将二次数域上Hilbert第十问题的不可判定性推广到次数有界的13未知量情形，并给出绝对次数界D_0。所用Pell-Lucas同余技术不仅在Diophantine定义中发挥作用，还为Lucas-Wieferich与Wall-Sun-Sun素数提供了新的偏离判据，展示了Lucas序列算术在数论逻辑中的深层应用。

Abstract: For every quadratic number field $K$, we prove a uniform $3$-unknown Diophantine definition of integer tuples in $\mathcal{O}_K$, allowing finitely many polynomial nonvanishing conditions. This yields an effective $+3$ transfer principle and a $13$-unknown representation of every recursively enumerable integer relation. Consequently, there exists an absolute degree bound $D_0\geq1$ such that for every quadratic number field $K$, there is no algorithm that, given \[ P(Y_1,\ldots,Y_{13})\in\mathbb{Z}[Y_1,\ldots,Y_{13}],\quad \mathrm{deg}\ P\leq D_0, \] decides whether $P=0$ has a solution in $\mathcal{O}_K^{13}$.
  The arithmetic input is a fourth-order Pell--Lucas congruence. It is a specialization of the norm-one Lucas multiplication formula, which yields exact valuations for the deviation of a Lucas quotient from its linear term, together with deviation criteria for Lucas--Wieferich and Wall--Sun--Sun primes. We also establish local surjectivity and $\ell$-adic density for second-order correction terms for norm-one Lucas sequences.

</details>

Result: 实现了对任意1≤d<n的联合等分布：归一化子空间与商的Archimedean Grassmannian像及K线性等距类随高度趋于无穷联合等分布，且对有界连续测试函数全局等分布。

Abstract: For every $1\le d<n$, we prove joint equidistribution, as the height tends to infinity, of $d$-dimensional $K$-subspaces in a rigid adelic space over a number field $K$, together with their archimedean Grassmannian images and the $K$-linear isometry classes of the normalized subspace and quotient. We identify the leading constant, prove no escape of mass from either normalized factor, and obtain global equidistribution against bounded continuous test functions. Over a general number field, the two normalized shapes are coupled by a determinant-class relation; conditional on the determinant class, their limiting law is the product of the Haar-induced probability measures on the corresponding fibers.

### [90] [Unit Indices of Prime-Index Shanks Orders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30637)
*Junyu Lu*

Main category: math.NT

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在解决一类特定的三次数域中，子环的加法指标与单位指标之间的精确关系。这类多项式来自矩阵共轭分类的某个自然族，理解其商环的单位群结构对于完整的整矩阵共轭分类具有重要意义。此前问题的难点在于通项系数t使结构随t变化复杂，需要区分"野分支"与"驯分支"两种不同情形的处理方法。

Method: 证明分为几个关键步骤：(1) 首先证明素数q在O_t中具有唯一素理想P位于其上；(2) 证明R_t=Z+P^2，由此将单位指标的候选集合限制为{1,q}；(3) 在"野分支"情形（即q整除某些特定不变量时），调用极大阶单位定理直接确定指标；(4) 在"驯分支"情形，结合调节子（regulator）下界与同余（congruence）上界来排除或确认指标为q的情形；(5) 最终通过枚举验证仅有的例外情形t=3,q=3及t=5,q=7。

Result: 主要定理给出：当q=[O_t:R_t]为素数时，单位指标[O_t^×:R_t^×]=q当且仅当(t,q)=(3,3)或(5,7)，其余所有情形单位指标均为1。此外还完整确定了Picard核和理想类幺半群的结构，并给出了相应的整矩阵共轭分类中每个纤维的显式描述。

Conclusion: 该工作完整解决了参数族R_t=Z[θ_t]在素数加性指标下的单位指标问题，揭示了仅有两个例外情形的意外结果。所发展的"野/驯分支"分析框架及R_t=Z+P^2的结构刻画，为更一般的子环单位指标研究提供了有效工具，同时Picard核与理想类幺半群的确定补充了该类数域的算术结构信息。

Abstract: For an integer $t\geq-1$, let $θ_t$ be the largest real root of $g_t(X)=X^3-tX^2-(t+3)X-1$, and let $R_t=\mathbb{Z}[θ_t]\subseteq\mathcal{O}_t=\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{Q}(θ_t)}$. We prove that if the additive order index $q=[\mathcal{O}_t:R_t]$ is prime, then $[\mathcal{O}_t^\times:R_t^\times]=q$ exactly for $(t,q)=(3,3)$ and $(5,7)$, and the unit index is $1$ otherwise. The proof shows that $q$ has a unique prime $\mathfrak P$ above it and that $R_t=\mathbb{Z}+\mathfrak P^2$, thereby restricting the unit index to $1$ or $q$. It then invokes a maximal-order unit theorem in the wild branch and combines regulator and congruence bounds in the tame branch. We also determine the Picard kernel, the ideal class monoid, and every fiber of the associated integral matrix-conjugacy classification.

</details>


### [91] [Density of the multidimensional Lagrange spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30735)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维Lagrange谱是数论中的经典对象，具有复杂的结构（离散部分、Hall射线和过渡部分），但在多维情形下此前几乎没有被理解。作者希望理解多维Lagrange谱的整体结构。

Method: 1) 利用丢番图逼近与幺模格空间上动力学的对应关系；2) 引入动力学Lagrange谱作为动力学对应物，利用高秩结构论证其等于[0, 最大值]；3) 通过扩张平行球面平移的等分布定理，将完全动力学谱的密度过渡到丢番图谱的稠密性。

Result: 证明多维Lagrange谱的闭包等于0到其上确界之间的整个区间，这与一维情形形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 多维Lagrange谱的闭包是一个完整的区间，说明多维情形下谱的结构远不如一维情形那样复杂，其本质源于多维设置中的高秩性质。

Abstract: The Lagrange spectrum is a classical object in number theory, defined as the set of values of $\liminf_{q\to\infty} q\, \mathrm{dist}(qα,\mathbb{Z})$ where $α$ runs through irrational numbers. It has a complicated structure, with the discrete part, Hall's ray, and a transitional part in between. One can similarly define Lagrange spectrum in the multidimensional set-up, and until now not much has been understood about it. In this paper we prove that, unlike in the one-dimensional case, the closure of the multidimensional Lagrange spectrum is equal to the interval between $0$ and its supremum. The proof relies on a correspondence between Diophantine approximation and dynamics on the space of unimodular lattices and proceeds by studying a dynamical counterpart of the Lagrange spectrum that we call dynamical Lagrange spectrum. The latter is shown to be equal to the interval between $0$ and its maximum by means of an argument utilizing the higher rank nature of the set-up. A passage from full dynamical spectrum to the density of the Diophantine spectrum is achieved by applying equidistribution of expanding translates of horospheres in the space of lattices.

</details>


### [92] [An Explicit Belyi Map for the Wiman Sextic and Cusp Forms for a Noncongruence Subgroup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30798)
*Madoka Horie,Takuya Yamauchi*

Main category: math.NT

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无界分母猜想（unbounded denominators conjecture）在一般情形的证明由Calegari、Dimitrov和Tang等人完成，但已有方法依赖的是某些特定的技术路线。本文旨在为权2情况下的特定非共余子群Γ_Ẇ提供一个独立的、直接的证明，并通过Wiman六次曲线的几何结构揭示其背后的算术性质。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种全新的、基于代数曲线在有限域上退化几何的证明策略，为无界分母猜想在权2情形下提供了一个具体且自洽的验证实例，同时也展示了Wiman六次曲线与模形式理论之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: In this paper, we explicitly determine an algebraic Belyi function on a unique smooth projective model $\widetilde{W}$ of the Wiman sextic curve $W$ and describe its complex uniformization in terms of modular functions associated with a certain noncongruence subgroup $Γ_{\widetilde{W}} \subset {\rm SL}_2(\mathbb{Z})$. As an application, we give a direct proof of the unbounded denominators conjecture in weight $2$ for $Γ_{\widetilde{W}}$. The conjecture is now known in full generality by the work of Calegari, Dimitrov, and Tang, following earlier progress including work of Dong, Lin, and Ng. Our proof, however, uses a degeneration of $\widetilde{W}$ over $\mathbb{F}_{5}$ together with explicit Puiseux series expansions and is substantially different from the methods employed in their work.

TL;DR: 本文澄清了Rønjom和Sandrib提出的APN提升猜想在Galois环上的表述，指出必须使用规范化（次数小于q的约化代表多项式）才能成立；证明了修正后的提升猜想等价于有限域临界点猜想。利用Janwa–Wilson–Rodier曲面证明了对奇数次d≥5（除Gold和Kasami–Welch指数外）APN多项式的显式不存在性阈值，并通过差分表恒等式给出偶次数推论，最后证明了所有三次置换多项式都有有理临界点从而满足修正后的提升猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: APN（几乎完美非线性）函数在密码学中具有重要地位，其从有限域到Galois环的提升问题关系到密码函数在更高特征环上的安全性分析。原APN提升猜想在原始表述中存在歧义（多项式代表不唯一导致形式导数不同），需要澄清并修正；同时需要给出APN函数在不同次数下的有效不存在性判定阈值。

Result: (1) 证明了未规范化的APN提升猜想是假的，修正后等价于有限域临界点猜想f'(x)≠0对所有x∈F_{2^m}成立；(2) 对每个奇数次d≥5且非Gold/非Kasami–Welch指数，给出了显式计算阈值APNmzero{d}，使得当m≥该阈值时F_{2^m}上不存在次数为d的APN多项式；(3) 对偶次数情形证明ax+g(x²)+c与g具有相同差分一致性；(4) 证明所有三次置换多项式都有有理临界点，满足修正后的提升猜想。

Conclusion: 本文解决了APN提升猜想的歧义性问题，给出了严格的修正版本并建立起与有限域临界点猜想之间的等价关系。通过代数几何工具获得了APN多项式不存在的显式次数-域大小阈值，这是对原有定性结果（Aubry–McGuire–Rodier）的重要定量补充，为APN函数的构造与分类提供了更强的工具。

Abstract: We clarify the APN lifting conjecture over Galois rings of Rønjom and Sandrib (CCDS, 2026). A function on $\F_q$ has many polynomial representatives, whose formal derivatives may differ, so the conjecture must use the unique reduced representative of degree less than $q$; without this normalization, it is false. The standard permutation-polynomial criterion over Galois rings then gives an exact reduction: the reduced representative $f$ of an APN permutation lifts to a permutation of $\GR(2^k,m)$, $k>1$, if and only if $f'(x)\ne0$ for every $x\in\F_{2^m}$. Thus the corrected lifting conjecture is equivalent to a finite-field critical-point conjecture.
  We next use Janwa--Wilson--Rodier surfaces, which encode the APN condition by rational points off the diagonal arrangement, to prove an effective nonexistence result. For every odd degree $d\ge5$ outside the Gold exponents $2^r+1$ and Kasami--Welch exponents $2^{2r}-2^r+1$, results of Hernando--McGuire and Aubry--McGuire--Rodier provide an absolutely irreducible factor in the hyperplane section at infinity. This yields an absolutely irreducible component of the surface, defined over the ground field and not contained in the diagonal arrangement. The explicit Cafure--Matera estimate then gives a computable number $\APNmzero{d}$ such that no polynomial of degree $d$ over $\F_{2^m}$ is APN when $m\ge\APNmzero{d}$. The qualitative eventual non-APN result is due to Aubry--McGuire--Rodier; our contribution is the explicit threshold.
  A direct identity for difference tables also gives an even-degree consequence: if $g$ has such an odd degree, then $ax+g(x^2)+c$, with $a\ne0$, has the same differential uniformity as $g$ and is therefore not APN in the same explicit range. Finally, we prove directly that every cubic permutation polynomial has a rational critical point and hence satisfies the corrected lifting conjecture.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高维整数格点计数问题是数论与几何交叉领域的重要课题。当球心和半径同时趋于无穷大时，格点计数表现出复杂的随机行为，确定其统计极限可以揭示高维几何与数论之间的深层联系，并为相关领域（如格密码学、晶体学等）提供理论基础。

Method: 论文采用概率方法分析均匀随机平移的高维欧氏球内的格点数。具体而言：(1) 识別出临界尺度为4π²R²/(d+2)=log d；(2) 在对数窗口内分析格点数与体积之比的对数的极限分布，利用中心极限定理路径；(3) 在临界尺度固定偏移处分析对数正态极限；(4) 在临界尺度之上分析计数-体积比依测度收敛及归一化误差的标准正态收敛，并推导方差的渐近展开公式。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 在临界尺度的对数窗口中，log(格点数/体积)满足中心极限定理；(2) 在临界尺度固定偏移处，格点数/体积的极限为对数正态分布；(3) 在临界尺度之上，计数-体积比依测度收敛于1，其归一化误差以标准正态分布为极限，且方差满足精确的渐近公式。

Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了高维随机平移球内格点计数在一阶（依测度收敛）、二阶（归一化误差正态收敛）以及临界区域（对数正态极限）三个层次的极限行为，建立了从临界尺度到超临界尺度之间格点计数统计规律的完整图谱，为高维格点计数问题提供了精确的极限定理。

Abstract: Let the center of a $d$-dimensional Euclidean ball be uniformly distributed
  modulo $\mathbb Z^d$. We study the resulting number of integer lattice points
  as the dimension and radius tend to infinity. The critical scale is
  $4π^2R_d^2/(d+2)=\log d$. In a logarithmic window around this scale, the
  logarithm of the lattice-point count divided by the volume satisfies a central
  limit theorem. At a fixed offset from the critical scale this yields a genuine
  lognormal limit. Above the critical scale, the count-to-volume ratio converges
  to $1$ in measure; its normalized error has a standard normal limit, and
  its variance satisfies an asymptotic formula.

</details>

Abstract: In this note we count the number of solutions to a non-degenerate quadratic form in eight prime variables of off-diagonal rank $3$. It is a continuation of work by L. Zhao, who counted solutions to forms in at least nine variables, by B. Green, who counted solutions to 'generic' (which implies off-diagonal rank $4$) forms in eight variables, and by J. Dobrowolski, who counted solutions to forms in eight variables of off-diagonal rank at most $2$.

Result: 在K>(1/2+ε)N时，对所有s>0证明了R_k(α)的s阶矩本质上紧的上下界；在更短的区间上（当s>1+1/k时）也得到相应结果。作为应用，在K>(1/2+ε)N时证明了q^{K/6}阶的L^1均值下界。

Conclusion: 该研究成功将正特征函数域上的自由多项式指数和矩估计做到了本质上最优，并由此获得了Möbius扭曲指数和L^1均值在较长区间上的非平凡下界，为正特征算术中的指数和理论提供了新的工具和结论。

Abstract: We extend recent work of the author over $\mathbb{Z}$ into the positive characteristic setting of $\mathbb{F}_q[t]$. In particular, for a polynomial $F \in \mathbb{F}_q[t]$ of degree $N$, let $R_k(α)$ denote the exponential sum over $k$-free polynomials $f$ with $\text{deg}(f-F)<K$. For all $s>0$, we prove essentially tight upper and lower bounds for the $s$-th moment of $R_k(α)$ whenever $K > (\frac{1}{2}+ε)N$, and in even shorter intervals when $s>1+\frac{1}{k}$. As an application of these results, we prove a lower bound of order $q^{\frac{K}{6}}$ for the $L^1$-mean of the Möbius-twisted exponential sum over $\mathbb{F}_q[t]$ whenever $K >(\frac{1}{2}+ε)N$.

</details>


### [97] [Large zeta sums and zeros of the Riemann zeta function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31060)
*Zikang Dong,Ruihua Wang,Weijia Wang,Hao Zhang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了关于黎曼zeta函数的部分和S(x,t)的无条件逆定理：当|S(x,t)|很大时，zeta函数在高度t附近必有大量零点。该定理推广了Granville和Soundararajan对大型特征和的零强迫机制，并由此得到在多项式范围内对S(x,t)的定量上界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究zeta函数部分和S(x,t)=Σ_{n≤x} n^{it}的大值行为与Riemann zeta函数零点分布之间的深层联系。此前Granville和Soundararajan建立了Dirichlet字符和大值的逆定理，本文旨在将其推广到zeta函数设定中，从而将S(x,t)的大值与zeta零点在高度t附近的分布联系起来。

Method: 将Granville和Soundararajan的零强迫机制从字符和推广到zeta函数部分和。关键技术区别在于：1) 谱高度被t平移；2) 高斯变换中出现来自zeta函数极点1+s的留数项。作者证明在考虑的范围内（T≤|t|≤2T，exp(√log T)≤x≤√T），该极点留数项指数小，可以忽略。通过将S(x,t)的大值与圆盘区域内zeta零点计数联系起来，建立逆定理。

Result: 证明了一个无条件逆定理：若|S(x,t)|=x/N且N≤(log x)^{1/100}，则在高度φ≈t的圆盘内（半径与N同阶），对每个cN^6≤L≤(log x)/2，存在至少L/360个零点。作为推论，如果在一族任意薄且固定宽度的窗口内（位于Re s=1左侧）对零点有定量限制，则在多项式范围内x有S(x,t)≪x/(log x)^{1/100}成立。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在无条件条件下建立了zeta函数部分和的大值与Riemann zeta零点在相应高度上的分布之间的定量等价关系，为理解S(x,t)的增长行为和zeta零点结构提供了新的工具。证明中处理极点留数的技术也为类似问题的进一步研究提供了方法基础。

Abstract: For real $t$ and $x\ge 1$, set \[ S(x,t)=\sum_{n\le x} n^{\ii t}. \] We prove an unconditional inverse theorem relating large values of $S(x,t)$, with $|t|$ large, to zeros of the Riemann zeta function near height $t$. More precisely, if $T\le |t|\le 2T$, $\exp(\sqrt{\log T})\le x\le \sqrt T$, and $|S(x,t)|=x/N$ with $N\le (\log x)^{1/100}$, then for every $cN^6\le L\le (\log x)/2$ a disk centered at $1+\iiφ$, where $|φ-t|\ll N$, contains at least $L/360$ zeros of $ζ(s)$. As a consequence, a quantitative restriction on zeros in a short family of arbitrarily thin fixed windows to the left of the line $\Ree s=1$ yields $S(x,t)\ll x/(\log x)^{1/100}$ in polynomial ranges of $x$. The proof adapts the zero-forcing mechanism of Granville and Soundararajan for large character sums. In the zeta setting the spectral height is shifted by $t$, and an additional residue from the pole of $ζ(s)$ appears in the Gaussian transform; in the range considered here that residue is exponentially small.

</details>
