Result: 获得了改进的界H₁≤240，将原有246的界进一步降低了6。

Conclusion: 通过结合Bombieri-Vinogradov定理与新的光滑模等分布估计成功地改进了素数间隙的界，为最终解决孪生素数猜想的H₁逼近提供了进一步的进展。

Abstract: Polymath8b proved that $H_1 = \liminf (p_{n+1}-p_n) \leq 246$. In this paper we show how the Bombieri-Vinogradov theorem can be combined with newer equidistribution estimates for smooth moduli to obtain the improved bound $H_1 \leq 240$.

</details>

### [99] [A Pythia8 Tune for Open Charm and Beauty Production in Fixed-Target Collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29076)
*Matei Climescu,Didar Dobur,Kirill Skovpen*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出FTFT（Fixed-Target Fragmentation Tune），一组为固定靶碰撞（质心能量约20-42 GeV）中粲和 beauty 强子产生而优化的Pythia8参数，分两阶段推导：先拟合D介子的Feynman-x、横向动量平方和电荷/领头粒子产生不对称性分布，再从数据中提取K因子来缩放截面。该调优对射束倾倒实验（如SHiP）的中微子通量和隐藏粒子产额预测至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定靶碰撞中夸克（粲和底）强子的精确产生率是预测射束倾倒实验（如SHiP）中微子通量和隐藏粒子产额的关键。然而现有Pythia8默认参数多为LHC调优所得，不适用于较低质心能量的固定靶碰撞场景，因此需要专门针对该能量区间进行调优。

Method: 方法分两阶段：（1）将D介子的Feynman-x、横向动量平方以及电荷/领头粒子产生不对称性的微分分布拟合到π介子和质子束固定靶实验数据；通过该拟合确定粲碎裂参数和多部分子相互作用参数。（2）从粲全截面测量中提取每种束流的K因子，用于将模拟截面标定到实测数据。

Result: 拟合得到的粲碎裂参数和多部分子相互作用参数显著偏离标准LHC调优的默认值，这与先前固定靶研究的发现一致，表明固定靶能区的动力学机制与高能对撞机存在系统性差异。

Conclusion: FTFT提供了一套适用于固定靶碰撞能区（sqrt(s)约20-42 GeV）的Pythia8参数，能够更可靠地预测粲和 beauty 强子产生，为SHiP等射束倾倒实验的中微子通量和隐藏粒子产额计算提供更准确的输入，从而提升此类实验的物理预期能力。

Abstract: We present FTFT (Fixed-Target Fragmentation Tune), a set of Pythia8 parameters optimised for the production of charm and beauty hadrons in fixed-target collisions at centre-of-mass energies $\sqrt{s} \simeq 20-42$ GeV. Accurate heavy-flavour production in this regime is needed to predict neutrino fluxes and hidden-particle yields at beam-dump experiments such as SHiP. The tune is derived in two stages. First, differential distributions in Feynman-$x$ ($x_F$), transverse momentum squared ($p_T^2$), and charge or leading-particle production asymmetry of $D$ mesons are fitted to data from pion- and proton-beam fixed-target experiments. Second, $K$-factors for each beam are extracted from inclusive charm cross-section measurements to scale the simulated cross section to the measured data. The fitted charm-fragmentation and multiparton-interaction parameters depart from standard LHC-tuned defaults, in line with earlier fixed-target studies.

</details>


### [100] [Sensitivity of a Missing-Mass Search for a Light Dark Photon with a Positron Beam on a Hydrogen Target](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29159)
*Weizhi Xiong,Ashot Gasparian,Bogdan Wojtsekhowski*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一项基于Geant4模拟的轻暗光子A'灵敏度研究，利用正电子束轰击液氢靶并通过湮灭飞行过程e⁺e⁻→γA'中的缺失质量技术进行探测，采用高分辨率前向电磁量能器重建末态光子。该搜索独立于A'的衰变模式，在500 MeV束流能量下可覆盖7-19 MeV的A'质量范围，对ε²的预期灵敏度在m_A'=19 MeV时可达4×10⁻⁸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究由杰斐逊实验室低能正电子设施的研发工作驱动，旨在独立于A'衰变模式地搜索轻暗光子，弥补现有实验对低质量区间暗光子灵敏度不足的问题。

Method: 采用Geant4模拟正电子束入射薄液氢靶，利用湮灭飞行过程e⁺e⁻→γA'的缺失质量技术进行暗光子搜索，通过高分辨率前向电磁量能器重建末态光子，并评估标准QED湮灭和轫致辐射过程的背景贡献。

Result: 500 MeV束流实验可覆盖A'质量范围7-19 MeV，投影的动力学混合参数灵敏度在m_A'=19 MeV时达到ε²=4×10⁻⁸。

Conclusion: 该实验方案通过缺失质量技术提供了一种对A'衰变模式不敏感的有效搜索途径，在7-19 MeV质量区间展现出良好的灵敏度，为低能正电子设施的暗光子搜索提供了定量可行性依据。

Abstract: We present a Geant4-based simulation study of the sensitivity to a light dark photon, $A'$, using a positron beam incident on a thin liquid-hydrogen target and a high-resolution forward electromagnetic calorimeter for final-state photon reconstruction. This study is motivated by ongoing research and development toward a low-energy positron facility at Jefferson Lab. The proposed search employs the missing-mass technique in the annihilation-in-flight process $e^+e^- \to γA'$, providing sensitivity to the $A'$ independently of its decay mode. We evaluate the expected backgrounds from standard QED annihilation and bremsstrahlung processes and present the projected sensitivity to the kinetic-mixing parameter $ε^2$ as a function of the $A'$ mass. The experiment with 500~MeV beam will provide the data for $A'$ mass range 7-19 MeV. The projected sensitivity on $ε^2$ is $4\times10^{-8}$ at $m_{A'}=19$~MeV.

</details>


### [101] [Evidence for the dead-cone effect in bottom quark initiated jets produced in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29412)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: CMS实验利用13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据（59.8 fb⁻¹），通过测量底夸克喷注的发射密度（作为迭代去聚团子喷注间角分离的函数），首次直接观测到底夸克在部分子簇射中的"死锥效应"，并以4.3倍标准差的显著性排除了不含死锥效应的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 死锥效应是夸克质量效应在部分子簇射中的主要表现形式，即质量夸克抑制共线胶子发射。此前该效应虽在理论上被广泛预期，但缺乏对底夸克喷注直接的实验测量验证。该研究旨在通过精确的微分测量提供对底夸克喷注簇射动力学的严格检验。

Method: 分析CMS探测器在√s=13 TeV记录的总亮度59.8 fb⁻¹的质子-质子对撞数据，选取源于top夸克对产生、横动量介于40-200 GeV的底夸克起始喷注，通过迭代去聚团算法定义子喷注，测量发射密度作为子喷注间角分离的函数，并与包含/不包含死锥效应的部分子簇射蒙特卡洛模型进行比较。

Result: 与数据相比，包含死锥效应的部分子簇射蒙特卡洛模型被支持；不含死锥效应的模型被排弃，统计显著性为4.3倍标准差。

Conclusion: 该测量首次以高显著性在实验上证实了质量夸克（底夸克）部分子簇射中的死锥效应，验证了量子色动力学在重夸克簇射领域的理论预期，为改进部分子簇射模拟提供了重要实验约束。

Abstract: The suppression of collinear gluon emissions from a massive quark, or the ``dead-cone effect,'' is the primary manifestation of quark mass effects in parton showers. A proton-proton collision data set recorded by the CMS experiment at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV with an integrated luminosity of 59.8 fb$^{-1}$ is analyzed. A differential measurement is presented of the emission density of bottom quark jets, studied as a function of the angular separation between iteratively declustered subjets. The measurement is performed in bottom quark initiated jets with transverse momenta between 40 and 200 GeV originating from top quark pair production. Compared to data, a parton shower Monte Carlo model that includes the dead-cone effect is favored, while a model without the dead-cone effect is disfavored with a significance of 4.3 standard deviations.

</details>

Zhu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: XENONnT首次在暗物质实验中通过弹性中微子-电子散射测量低能太阳中微子，实现迄今最低的能量阈值（1 keV电子反冲能量，对太阳中微子灵敏度低至17 keV），以5.0σ显著性排除背景假说，测得太阳pp中微子通量为(10.2±2.0)×10^10 cm^-2 s^-1，与Borexino测量结果在1.9σ内一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索暗物质探测器在中微子物理中的应用潜力，建立中微子探测的最低能量阈值，推动下一代多功能观测站的发展。

Method: 利用XENONnT前两次科学运行共2.46 t·yr的数据，通过弹性中微子-电子散射过程进行测量，覆盖1-140 keV的电子反冲能量范围，对能量低至17 keV的太阳中微子具有灵敏度。

Result: 以5.0σ统计显著性排除背景-only假说；测得太阳pp中微子通量为(10.2±2.0)×10^10 cm^-2 s^-1，比Borexino之前的测量值更大，但统计上一致（1.9σ）。

Conclusion: 研究结果证明了液氙时间投影室在中微子物理中从keV能级出发的日益增长的潜力，是迈向下一代多用途观测站的重要里程碑。

Abstract: We report on the first measurement of low-energy solar neutrinos through elastic neutrino-electron scattering in a dark matter experiment, establishing the lowest energy threshold for any neutrino detection to date. The measurement utilizes data from the first two science runs of XENONnT, corresponding to an exposure of 2.46 t $\cdot$ y, and covers electron recoil energies between 1 keV and 140 keV, providing sensitivity to solar neutrinos with energies down to 17 keV. We reject the background-only hypothesis with a statistical significance of $5.0σ$ and measure a solar $pp$ neutrino flux of $(10.2 \pm 2.0) \times 10^{10}$ cm$^{-2}$ s$^{-1}$. The result is larger, but statistically consistent with the previous measurement by Borexino at $1.9σ$. Together with recent observations of coherent elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering of $^8$B solar neutrinos in XENONnT and other liquid-xenon time projection chambers, these results demonstrate the growing potential of liquid xenon detectors for neutrino physics down to the keV-scale and represent an important milestone towards a next-generation multipurpose observatory.

</details>


### [103] [From a Long-standing Prediction to a New Family of Hadrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29502)
*Xining Wang,Kai Yi*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了全粲四夸克态从理论预言到实验发现的完整发展历程，重点介绍了CMS实验中南京师范大学和清华大学团队的贡献，包括全粲四夸克家族的发现、共振态间干涉效应的确立以及量子数的首次确定，标志着奇异强子研究从单个共振态探索进入新强子家族研究阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全粲四夸克态从长期的理论预言发展为强子谱学中最活跃的前沿领域之一，需要对这一快速发展领域进行系统性回顾，总结从早期理论研究和实验搜索到大型强子对撞机近期突破的完整脉络。

Method: 本文采用综述性研究方法，系统梳理全粲四夸克态在理论预测、实验搜索和实验发现三个阶段的进展，并重点聚焦CMS实验中NNU和THU团队在共振态发现、干涉效应建立和量子数确定方面的具体贡献。

Result: 成功总结了全粲四夸克家族的发现历程，确立了共振态之间的干涉效应，并首次确定了其量子数，将单奇异共振态搜寻转化为新强子家族的系统研究。

Conclusion: 全粲四夸克态的研究进展开辟了探索量子色动力学非微扰动力学的新途径，标志着该领域从发现阶段进入系统研究新强子家族的新阶段。

Abstract: Fully charmed tetraquarks have evolved from a long-standing theoretical prediction into one of the most active frontiers in hadron spectroscopy. This article reviews their development from early theoretical studies and experimental searches to the recent breakthroughs at the Large Hadron Collider, with particular emphasis on the contributions of the Nanjing Normal University (NNU) and Tsinghua University (THU) teams at the CMS experiment. We summarize the discovery of the fully charmed tetraquark family, the establishment of interference among resonances, and the first determination of their quantum numbers. These advances have transformed the search for a single exotic resonance into the study of a new family of hadrons and opened a promising avenue for exploring the nonperturbative dynamics of Quantum Chromodynamics.

</details>


### [104] [Low-energy Muon-Nucleon scattering experiment: LUNE (White Paper)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29509)
*Chenlei An,Dong Bai,Ziyu Bai,Kai Chen,Liangwen Chen,Xiang Chen,Jianqiao Deng,Yanxin Dou,Yicheng Feng,Zekai Feng,Lu Gao,Chang Gong,Aiqiang Guo,Liang Han,Qundong Han,Defu Hou,Ruiwen Hou,Huigang Hu,Chen Ji,Xiangdong Ji,Vijay Kumar,Dikai Li,Jiuzhao Li,Liang Li,Qite Li,Xin-Qiang Li,Yuan Li,Qiming Liang,Yutie Liang,Dong Liu,Duanqing Liu,Langtian Liu,Weijie Liu,Zejia Lu,Maowu Nie,Ziwen Pan,Hua Pei,Jinkang Peng,Shusu Shi,Qintao Song,Xiaocheng Song,Xiangming Sun,Yichen Sun,Zhiyu Sun,Enke Wang,Fei Wang,Hulin Wang,Jike Wang,Xiang-Peng Wang,Xiao Wang,Xin-Nian Wang,Yangxi Wang,Yaping Wang,Zeren Simon Wang,Yanbing Wei,Yuehong Xie,Weizhi Xiong,Nu Xu,Yu Xu,Siqi Yang,Yadong Yang,Hang Yin,Xing-Bo Yuan,Dongliang Zhang,Ranyu Zhang,Ruitian Zhang,Xueheng Zhang,Yu Zhang,Yuxiang Zhao,Zihan Zhao,Shuai Zhou,XiaoKang Zhou,Xiaoyu Zhu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本白皮书介绍了基于HIAF高强高质μ子束流（动量0.5-7.5 GeV/c）的LUNE实验合作组，该实验旨在通过μ子弹性散射精确测量质子电荷半径（精度约1.0%）、系统研究质子电磁形状因子、双光子交换效应，并扩展至TMD、引力形状因子、核电荷半径等领域，同时检验量子电动力学和探测超越标准模型的新物理信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HIAF提供的μ子束能量范围（0.5-7.5 GeV/c）填补了低能电子设施与未来高能轻子-离子对撞机之间的能量空白，为精密μ子散射测量提供了独特机遇。实验旨在回答核物理和粒子物理中的基础问题，包括质子电荷半径之谜、核子电磁结构和量子电动力学与强相互作用的动力学。

Method: LUNE项目分两阶段推进：从弹性散射出发，延伸至核子结构及超越标准模型的搜寻。实验利用正负μ子束在同运动学范围内进行精密测量，通过弹性μ子-质子散射确定质子电荷半径，系统测量质子电磁形状因子以研究双光子交换效应和检验量子电动力学，并进一步研究TMD、引力形状因子和核电荷半径。

Result: 预期以约1.0%的精度确定质子电荷半径，实现对质子电磁形状因子的系统测量，并覆盖库仑畸变修正、核介质效应及超越标准模型物理可能信号等多项研究内容。

Conclusion: LUNE实验将补充JLab、EicC和EIC等现有电子散射设施的测量，为核子三维结构、核电荷半径及基础相互作用研究提供新视角，其科学动机、探测器概念、预期性能与长期策略已在本白皮书中系统阐述。

Abstract: The HIAF will provide high-intensity, high-quality muon beams with momenta from 0.5 to 7.5 GeV/c. This energy range is uniquely suited for precision muon scattering, bridging the gap between low-energy electron facilities and future high-energy lepton-ion colliders. In particular, HIAF will enable precision measurements with both positive and negative muon beams over a broad kinematic range, complementing existing electron-scattering facilities such as JLab, EicC and EIC.
  Based on HIAF muon source, the LUNE Collaboration has been established to address several fundamental questions in nuclear and particle physics, including the proton charge radius puzzle, nucleon electromagnetic structure, and the dynamics of quantum electrodynamics and hadronic interactions. The program proceeds in two phases, from elastic scattering to nucleon structure and beyond-Standard-Model searches.
  The experiment is expected to determine the proton charge radius with a precision of approximately 1.0\% using elastic muon-proton scattering. It will also perform systematic measurements of the proton electromagnetic form factors with both $μ^+$ and $μ^-$ beams, enabling precise studies of two-photon exchange effects and stringent tests of quantum electrodynamics. Beyond elastic scattering, LUNE will investigate TMD, gravitational form factors, and nuclear charge radii, providing new insights into the 3D structure of nucleons and nuclei. The experiment will further address important topics including Coulomb-distortion corrections, nuclear medium effects, and possible signatures of physics beyond the Standard Model.
  This white paper presents the scientific motivation, detector concept, expected performance, and long-term strategy of LUNE.

</details>

Yu,T. Yuan,S. Yun-C{á}rcamo,A. Zander Jurowitzki,A. Zegarelli,S. Zhang,Z. Zhang,P. Zhelnin,P. Zilberman,C. Zilleruelo Ca{ñ}as*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该研究利用IceCube中微子天文台10.7年的上行缪子中微子数据（0.5–100 TeV），对低尺度引力理论施加了约束。在额外维度模型下，将最大额外维度的紧致化半径限制为R≲0.17μm（90%置信水平）；在多个标准模型副本场景中，获得N≳O(400)的下限，部分参数区域的结果为文献中最强约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等级问题（hierarchy problem）是粒子物理中的悬而未决的问题。许多试图解决该问题的理论会降低引力基本尺度，从而在中微子扇区产生可观测的物理效应。作者旨在利用高能中微子观测来检验这些低尺度引力理论。

Method: 使用IceCube中微子天文台10.7年的上行缪子中微子数据，能量范围0.5–100 TeV，分析能量范围内的谱形畸变。该畸变源于中微子穿过地球传播时与物质的相互作用（物质效应），可用于约束大额外维度模型和多标准模型副本（镜像世界）等理论场景。

Result: 在大额外维度模型中，将最大额外维度紧致化半径约束为R≲0.17μm（90%置信水平），适用于正常和倒置中微子质量排序；在多个标准模型副本场景中，根据最轻中微子质量的不同，获得N≳O(400)的下限。部分参数区域为文献中最强约束，其他区域则探测了此前未探索的参数空间。

Conclusion: 该工作利用高能大气中微子传播的地球物质效应，成功对低尺度引力模型（大额外维度和多标准模型副本）给出了新约束，并在部分参数空间达到文献最强水平，凸显了中微子天文台在超越标准模型物理探测中的价值。

Abstract: The hierarchy problem remains an open question in particle physics. A number of theories that address this problem lower the fundamental scale of gravity, resulting in observable consequences in the neutrino sector. In this work, we place constraints on low-scale gravity scenarios using high-energy neutrinos observed with the IceCube Neutrino Observatory. The analysis is based on 10.7 years of upward-going muon neutrino data in the energy range from 0.5 to 100 TeV. In this energy range, the theories predict characteristic spectral distortions arising from matter effects when neutrinos propagate through Earth. In the context of large extra dimension models, we constrain the compactification radius of the largest extra dimension to $R \lesssim 0.17\,μ\mathrm{m}$ at $90\%$ confidence level for both normal and inverted neutrino mass ordering. For scenarios with multiple Standard Model copies, we obtain lower limits of up to $N \gtrsim \mathcal{O}(400)$, depending on the value of the lightest neutrino mass. In parts of the parameter space, these results constitute the strongest constraints in the literature to our knowledge, while in other regions they probe previously unexplored parameter space.

</details>


### [106] [ConCA: Concentration-Aware Channel Attention for Fine-Grained Visual Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30183)
*Yu-Sheng Liu,Yu-Chen Tung*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出ConCA（Concentration-Aware Channel Attention），将通道均值与基于负激活熵（NegEnt）的空间集中度结合，构成双描述符，再通过参数量与通道数线性相关的深度可分离一维卷积MLP生成逐通道注意力权重，在六个细粒度视觉识别基准上超越无注意力、SE-Net、ECA-Net等基线，并在八个骨干网络上泛化良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轻量级通道注意力机制在细粒度视觉识别（FGVR）中效果有限，主要原因是大多数模块仅通过全局平均池化（GAP）汇总每个通道，只能捕获激活幅度而忽略空间集中度，导致空间分布不同但均值相同的通道获得相同描述符。

Method: ConCA将通道均值与平移不变的负输入熵（NegEnt，通过对负激活做softmax计算）配对，形成同时编码幅度与集中度的双描述符；随后用参数量与通道数成线性的深度可分离一维卷积MLP将描述符映射为逐通道权重。

Result: 在六个细粒度基准上，ConCA在受控的从零训练协议下优于无注意力、SE-Net、ECA-Net基线以及四种更丰富的描述符模块；同时在iNat2021-mini上跨八个骨干网络均表现出良好泛化性。

Conclusion: 通道描述符本身以及将其映射为注意力权重的逐通道门控机制，是轻量级通道注意力在FGVR中被忽视但重要的方面，ConCA验证了这一观点。

Abstract: Lightweight channel attention mechanisms are widely used in image classification, yet their effectiveness in fine-grained visual recognition (FGVR) remains limited. Most modules summarize each channel by global average pooling (GAP), which captures activation magnitude but ignores spatial concentration, so channels with different spatial distributions but identical means receive the same descriptor. We propose Concentration-Aware Channel Attention (ConCA), which pairs the mean with a shift-invariant negative-input entropy (NegEnt), computed via a softmax over the negated activations, forming a dual descriptor that jointly encodes magnitude and concentration. A depthwise 1-D convolutional multi-layer perceptron (MLP), whose parameter count is linear in the number of channels, maps the pair to a per-channel weight. On six fine-grained benchmarks, ConCA improves over attention-free, SE-Net, and ECA-Net baselines as well as four richer descriptor-based modules under a controlled from-scratch protocol, and it generalizes across eight backbones on iNat2021-mini. These results indicate that the channel descriptor, together with the per-channel gating that maps it to attention weights, is an important but underexplored aspect of lightweight channel attention in FGVR.

</details>

Matsumoto,C. Ise,M. Ishitsuka,M. Sugo,M. Wako,K. Yamauchi,Y. Nakano,A. Yankelevich,F. Cormier,R. Gaur,M. Hartz,A. Konaka,X. Li,B. R. Smithers,S. Chen,Y. Wu,A. Q. Zhang,B. Zhang,H. Adhikary,M. Girgus,P. Govindaraj,M. Posiadala-Zezula,Y. S. Prabhu,S. B. Boyd,R. Edwards,D. Hadley,M. O'Flaherty,B. Richards,A. Ali,B. Jamieson,C. Bronner,D. Horiguchi,A. Minamino,Y. Sasaki*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用Super-Kamiokande纯水相401 kton·年的曝光数据搜索质子衰变到正轻子和不可见中性粒子的过程（p→l⁺+X），未发现超出大气中微子本底的显著信号，并在90%置信水平下给出了质子部分寿命的下限：p→e⁺+X为1.72×10³³年，p→μ⁺+X为0.61×10³³年，分别较此前结果改善了2倍和1.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质子衰变是大统一理论（GUT）等超出标准模型物理的重要预言，寻找质子衰变是对这些理论的直接检验。此前已有相关搜索，但灵敏度有限，本研究利用Super-Kamiokande纯水相全部曝光数据，以期提高对质子衰变到正轻子+X这一衰变道的搜索灵敏度。

Method: 使用Super-Kamiokande探测器在纯水相期间累积的401 kton·年曝光数据，通过探测器径迹与能量重建技术，在事件中搜索质子衰变为正电子或正缪子加一个不可见、无质量中性粒子（X）的特征信号，并与预期的大气中微子本底进行比对分析。

Result: 未观察到显著超出大气中微子本底的质子衰变信号。在90%置信水平下，质子部分寿命下限分别为：p→e⁺+X为1.72×10³³年，p→μ⁺+X为0.61×10³³年；两项结果分别较此前最佳限制改善了2倍和1.5倍。

Conclusion: 该研究未发现质子衰变的证据，但利用Super-Kamiokande纯水相全部数据显著提高了对p→l⁺+X衰变道的部分寿命上限约束，为相关超出标准模型理论提供了更严格的实验限制。

Abstract: A search for proton decay via $p\rightarrow l^{+}+X$, where $l^{+}$ is a positively charged lepton and $X$ is an invisible, massless, neutral particle, was performed using a 401~kton$\cdot$years exposure representing the entire pure water phase of Super-Kamiokande. No significant indication of a proton decay was observed beyond the expected atmospheric neutrino background. Lower limits on the partial lifetime of the proton were set to at $1.72\times10^{33}$ years for $p\rightarrow e^{+}+X$ and $0.61\times10^{33}$ years for $p\rightarrow μ^{+}+X$ at the $90\%$ confidence level. These results improve on previous limits by factors of 2 and 1.5, respectively.

</details>

Zhu,X. Zhu,Y. Zhu,V. Zhukov,J. Zhuo,T. Zies,D. Zuliani,X. Zuo*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该论文测量了LHCb实验在固定靶模式下质子-氩(pAr)和质子-质子(pp)碰撞中ψ(2S)与J/ψ产生截面之比，发现pAr系统中存在核效应，为未来更大系统的测量提供基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究粲偶素在核环境中的产生机制，探究核效应对重夸克偶素态的影响，为理解量子色动力学中的核物质效应提供实验依据。

Method: 利用LHCb的SMOG2存储单元注入氩气和氢气，分别产生pAr和pp碰撞（质心系能量√sNN=113 GeV），测量ψ(2S)与J/ψ产生截面比作为横动量pT和质心系快度y*的函数。

Result: 在-2.3<y*<0.0和0<pT<8 GeV/c范围内，pAr碰撞中ψ(2S)-to-J/ψ比值与pp碰撞中的比值之比为0.90±0.04±0.02，表明pAr系统中出现核效应。

Conclusion: 该测量首次在113 GeV固定靶能区给出pAr系统中ψ(2S)产额抑制的证据，为LHCb未来更大核系统的测量提供重要基线参照。

Abstract: A measurement of the $ψ(2S)$-to-$J/ψ$ production cross-section ratio is performed in proton-argon ($p\mathrm{Ar}$) and proton-proton ($pp$) collisions in fixed-target mode at $\sqrt{s_{\mathrm{NN}}}=113\,\mathrm{GeV}$. Data samples were collected by the LHCb experiment during argon and hydrogen gas injections in the SMOG2 storage cell, resulting in $p\mathrm{Ar}$ and $pp$ collisions, respectively. The $ψ(2S)$-to-$J/ψ$ production cross-section ratio is measured as a function of the charmonium transverse momentum, $p_{\mathrm{T}}$, and rapidity in the centre-of-mass system, $y^{*}$. The $ψ(2S)$-to-$J/ψ$ ratio in $p\mathrm{Ar}$ collisions over that in $pp$ collisions is measured to be $0.90 \pm 0.04 \pm 0.02$ for $-2.3<y^{*}<0.0$ and $0<p_{\mathrm{T}}<8\mathrm{GeV}/c$, indicating the emergence of nuclear effects in the $p\mathrm{Ar}$ system. This study acts as a baseline for the interpretation of future measurements with larger systems accessible by the LHCb experiment.

</details>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [The $\mathbb{Z}_2$-Index of a Pair of Pure States and the Topology of Interacting 1D Superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28796)
*Anna Mazhar,Jacob Shapiro*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在单位C*-代数上为局部可比较、宇称不变的纯态定义了相对Z₂指标N(ω₁,ω₂)，证明其良定义性、乘法性、宇称保持自同构不变性和范数拓扑下的局部常值性。对一维自对偶CAR代数，通过半链自同构σ构造多体Majorana数Nσ(ω)，在准自由Hilbert-Schmidt区域与单体Majorana数一致，并证明该指标完全分类对称局部自同构路径的连通分量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Majorana数的定义通常局限于准自由（Gaussian）态，难以覆盖一般多体量子态。作者希望建立一个不依赖准自由结构的、基于C*-代数框架的相对Z₂指标，从而为更广泛的强关联多体系统提供拓扑不变量，并研究其与单体现有形变理论的联系与差异。

Method: 在单位C*-代数上对局部可比较、宇称不变的纯态定义相对Z₂指标；利用自对偶CAR代数的结构，将半链自同构σ作用于态并定义多体Majorana数Nσ(ω):=N(ω,ω∘σ)；引入对称局部自同构路径作为等价关系，证明指标对其构成完全不变量，并建立与单体Majorana数在准自由极限下的一致性。

Result: 证明了该相对Z₂指标满足良定义性、乘法性、宇称保持自同构不变性与范数拓扑下的局部常值性；在准自由Hilbert-Schmidt区域，多体Majorana数与通常的单体Majorana数一致；多体Majorana数完全分类对称局部自同构路径的连通分量，但该等价关系保留体Majorana数而可能丢失相对的零维宇称障碍。

Conclusion: 该工作将Majorana数的概念从准自由态推广到一般宇称不变的σ-局部纯态，建立了基于C*-代数动力学的拓扑分类框架。多体Majorana数作为自同构路径等价关系的完全不变量，深化了对一维拓扑超导体多体相结构的理解，同时也揭示了不同等价关系下拓扑信息的保留与丢失。

Abstract: We define a relative $\mathbb{Z}_2$-index $\mathcal{N}(ω_1,ω_2)$ for locally-comparable, parity-invariant pure states on a unital $C^*$-algebra. We prove that the index is well-defined, multiplicative, invariant under parity-preserving automorphisms, and locally constant in the norm topology. For the one-dimensional self-dual CAR algebra, we apply this construction to the half-chain automorphism $σ$ and define the many-body Majorana number $\mathcal{N}σ(ω):=\mathcal{N}(ω,ω\circσ)$ for parity-invariant $σ$-local pure states. In the quasi-free Hilbert--Schmidt regime, this index agrees with the usual single-body Majorana number. We then introduce symmetric local automorphism paths and prove that $\mathcal{N}_σ$ completely classifies the resulting automorphic-path-components. This automorphic-path equivalence retains the bulk Majorana number but may forget a relative zero-dimensional parity obstruction.

</details>


### [110] [Pseudorandomness and Diffraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28917)
*David Damanik,Nicolae Strungaru*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个具有特定余弦形式的拟随机势能函数 V(n)，证明了其在衍射性质上是拟随机的——即对所有非零参数 λ、无理数 α 及任意 x₁, x₂，其关联的衍射测度是纯绝对连续的。这一结果为该势能在 Schrödinger 算子意义下拟随机（即展示 Anderson 局域化）的著名猜想提供了进一步证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拟随机结构与随机结构在行为上相似但本身并非随机。作者关注的猜想是：函数 V(n) 在 Schrödinger 算子视角下是拟随机的，即势能函数 V 几乎对一切参数显示 Anderson 局域化（纯点谱、指数衰减特征函数），这与随机势的谱特征相同。该猜想极其困难且著名，本文旨在从衍射角度为它提供证据。

Method: 作者从衍射理论的角度分析 V 的拟随机性，具体研究其关联衍射测度的性质。通过证明该衍射测度对所有参数（λ≠0、无理 α、任意 x₁,x₂）都是纯绝对连续的，从而建立与随机势情形相同的衍射特征。这是通过数学分析精确证明衍射测度的绝对连续性实现的。

Result: 成功证明了 V 在衍射意义下是拟随机的：对于所有 λ≠0、所有无理数 α 以及所有 x₁,x₂，V 的衍射测度都是纯绝对连续的，这与均匀随机势的情况一致。该结果对 Schrödinger 情形中的拟随机猜想提供了进一步支持，同时阐明了 Schrödinger 谱测度与衍射测度之间看似对偶的行为。

Conclusion: 本文从衍射性质角度验证了 V 的拟随机性，为 Schrödinger 算子语境下的拟随机猜想提供了新证据，并揭示了谱测度（离散化倾向）与衍射测度（绝对连续倾向）之间的对偶关系，深化了对拟随机结构本质的理解。

Abstract: Pseudorandom structures are structures that behave like random ones, without necessarily being random themselves. It is a prominent, and evidently very difficult, conjecture that $$ V(n) = λ\cos \Big( 2 π\Big( x_1 + n x_2 + \frac{n(n-1)}{2} α\Big) \Big) $$ is pseudorandom from a Schrödinger operator perspective in that the Schrödinger operator in $\ell^2(\mathbb{Z})$ with potential $V$ displays Anderson localization, that is, pure point spectrum with exponentially decaying eigenfunctions for almost all parameter values --- the same spectral features as those produced by random potentials. We show that $V$ is pseudorandom in terms of its diffraction properties, that is, the associated diffraction measure is purely absolutely continuous for all $λ\not= 0$, all irrational $α$, and all $x_1,x_2$ --- which is the case as well for the random case. Our result gives further evidence for the conjecture in the Schrödinger case and it elucidates the apparent dual behavior of Schrödinger spectral measures and diffraction measures.

</details>


### [111] [Simulating passive scalar advection in rough Kraichnan flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29321)
*Long Li,André L. P. Considera,Simon Thalabard*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文对二维粗糙Kraichnan流中的被动标量平流进行了系统性欧拉研究，覆盖全部速度粗糙度指数h∈(0,1)。使用高达2048²分辨率的伪谱方法直接求解平流-扩散方程，恢复了标量与流场之间的对偶性及二阶统计量的标度律，并在整个h范围内测量了四阶平坦度异常，与理论预测一致。此外还研究了标量增量的概率密度函数和高阶统计量，揭示了与高斯性和对数正态性的系统性偏离，并提供了不同h值下模拟参数（特别是分子扩散率）的调整指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kraichnan模型是研究被动标量湍流平流的经典理论框架，其粗糙度指数h控制载体流场的奇异性。以往研究多集中于特定h值或拉格朗日框架，缺乏对全范围h的系统性欧拉数值研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供完整的数值基准，并检验标量场的高阶统计特性以及数值模拟的可靠性。

Method: 采用伪谱方法在高达2048²网格分辨率下直接积分平流-扩散方程。使用与载体流白噪声时间构造一致的冻结噪声龙格-库塔（frozen-noise Runge-Kutta）格式。系统改变速度粗糙度指数h，识别标量场的标度区间，测量二阶与四阶统计量（如平坦度异常）。利用欧拉模拟能直接获取完整标量场的优势，分析标量增量的PDF和高阶统计特性，并考察模拟参数（特别是分子扩散率）如何随h调整。

Result: 模拟恢复了载体流越光滑则标量越粗糙的双重对偶性以及二阶统计量的预测标度律。四阶平坦度异常在整个h范围内与Frisch等人(1999)的拉格朗日估计以及Bernard等人(1998)和Pumir等人(1997)的微扰预测一致。标量增量PDF和高阶统计量表现出对高斯性和对数正态性的系统性偏离，且中间h值偏差最强。偏离理论标度律的残差可定量归因于载体流的有限谱表示。提供了系统的模拟参数调整指南。

Conclusion: 本文首次提供了对粗糙Kraichnan流中被动标量平流全范围h的系统性欧拉数值研究，验证了理论预测并揭示了高阶统计量的非高斯、非对数正态特性。该研究不仅为Kraichnan模型本身提供了可靠数值基准，还为进一步研究更现实的扩展模型（如载体流与高斯乘法混沌耦合的情形，其中理论发展受限）奠定了基础。

Abstract: We present a systematic Eulerian study of passive scalar advection in rough two-dimensional Kraichnan flows, covering the full range of velocity roughness exponent $h\in(0,1)$. The advection--diffusion equation is integrated directly using a pseudo-spectral method at resolutions up to $2048^2$ grid points, with a frozen-noise Runge--Kutta scheme consistent with the white-in-time construction of the carrier flow. The simulations recover the duality between advected scalar and advecting flow---the smoother the carrier, the rougher the scalar---together with the predicted scaling laws for the second-order statistics. Once the scaling range is properly identified, the fourth-order flatness anomaly is measured across the whole range of $h$, in agreement with the Lagrangian estimates of Frisch \textit{et al.} (1999) and with the perturbative predictions of Bernard \textit{et al.} (1998) and Pumir \textit{et al.} (1997). Benefiting from the fact that our Eulerian simulations give direct access to the full scalar field, we also examine the probability density functions of scalar increments and the corresponding higher-order statistics, which show systematic departures from Gaussianity and from log-normality, with the strongest deviations manifesting for intermediate values of $h$. A central outcome of this work is a systematic account of how the simulation parameters, in particular the molecular diffusivity, must be adjusted with $h$, providing practical guidelines for reliable simulations; we further show that the residual deviations from the theoretical scaling laws are quantitatively accounted for by the finite spectral representation of the carrier flow. Our analysis also serve as a numerical baseline for simulating more realistic extensions of the Kraichnan model, where the carrier flow is coupled with a Gaussian multiplicative chaos and theoretical developments are limited.

</details>


### [112] [Sharp Logarithmic Quantum Dynamics for Quasiperiodic Schrödinger Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29339)
*Wencai Liu,Xueyin Wang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于一类具有偶势函数的一频准周期薛定谔算子，在正Lyapunov指数区域中，量子波包的动力学局域化的相位均匀位置矩下界确实呈对数增长，并且对矩阶数的依赖性也是尖锐的，从而确认了已知对数上界的精确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准周期薛定谔算子的动力学局域化要求量子波包的所有位置矩在时间上有界，但该界一般不随相位均匀。在正Lyapunov指数区域，已知最佳相位均匀估计仅在对数尺度上增长，一个自然的问题是这些对数尺度及矩阶数依赖性是否是最优的（即是否有匹配的下界）。

Method: 作者引入了"反射型半均匀局域化本征函数"（reflective version of semi-uniformly localized eigenfunctions）的新技术，该技术适配于完全共振相位迫使产生的两个局域化中心，并在此基础上沿时间序列构造匹配的对数下界。

Result: 对于一类具有偶势的一频准周期薛定谔算子，作者证明了位置矩的对数增长尺度以及其对矩阶数的依赖都是尖锐的（sharp），即下界与已知的上界相匹配。

Conclusion: 本文的结果表明，在正Lyapunov指数区域，准周期薛定谔算子的相位均匀动力学局域化估计中出现的对数增长既不可避免也精确，解决了此前关于该估计最优性的猜想。

Abstract: Dynamical localization requires all position moments of a quantum wavepacket to remain bounded in time, but for quasiperiodic Schrödinger operators such bounds are generally not uniform in phase. In the positive Lyapunov exponent regime, the best known phase-uniform estimates instead grow on a logarithmic scale. We prove that both the logarithmic scale and the dependence on the moment order are sharp for a class of one-frequency quasiperiodic Schrödinger operators with even potentials. Our main ingredient is a reflective version of semi-uniformly localized eigenfunctions, adapted to the two localization centers forced by a completely resonant phase, from which we obtain matching logarithmic lower bounds along sequences of times.

</details>


### [113] [Simultaneous Reconstructions of (x,t) -Dependent Coefficients and Initial Conditions of Parabolic Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29377)
*Michael V. Klibanov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了一般二阶线性抛物型方程中未知的(x,t)依赖系数与未知初始条件的同时重建反问题，获得了稳定性估计和唯一性定理，并首次推导出具有时间反演数据的抛物型方程初始条件的对数稳定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于抛物型方程的反问题研究，要么假设系数仅依赖于x或仅依赖于t，要么假设初始条件已知（仅两篇例外）。本文突破了这些限制，首次同时重建依赖(x,t)的系数和未知初始条件，拓展了反问题的理论边界。

Method: 输入数据依赖(n+1)个变量，属于形式确定的数据。作者通过建立稳定性估计和唯一性定理来刻画该反问题的适定性，并作为副产品推导了时间反演数据下抛物型方程初始条件的对数稳定性估计。

Result: 获得了该反问题的唯一性定理和稳定性估计；首次得到了时间反演数据下抛物方程初始条件的对数稳定性估计。该方法可应用于平均场博弈理论中的公众意见预测以及流行病时空暴发追踪。

Conclusion: 本文在反问题的理论和应用方面均有重要贡献，既填补了形式确定输入数据下同时重建(x,t)依赖系数和未知初始条件这一理论空白，又为公众意见预测和流行病建模提供了新的数学工具。

Abstract: This is the first publication, in which an inverse problem of the simultaneous reconstruction of an unknown (x,t) - dependent coefficient and an unknown initial condition in a general linear parabolic equation of the second order is considered. The input data depend on (n+1) variables and are, therefore, formally determined ones. Stability estimates and uniqueness theorem are obtained for this inverse problem. As a by-product, logarithmic stability estimates for initial conditions of parabolic equations and inequalities with time reversed data are obtained for the first time.
  All known publications about inverse problems for parabolic equations with formally determined input data and unknown coefficients assume that those coefficients depend either only on x or only on t. In addition, the initial condition is assumed to be known, except of two publications cited in the text.
  The inverse problem of this paper has two potential applications. The first one is in forecasting of public opinions in the framework of the Mean Field Games theory. The second one is in tracking spatiotemporal outbreaks of epidemics.

</details>


### [114] [Kubo Formulas and Bulk-Edge Correspondence for Curved Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29524)
*Aren Martinian,Tom Stoiber*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了强拓扑绝缘体中各种整数不变量的等价性问题，利用Roe代数和适用于非可分C*-代数的KK-理论变体，证明了带任意边界的广义体-边对应关系，并给出了广义Kubo公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强拓扑不变量具有多种实空间表达式，近年来对弯曲边界空间的研究提出了这些表达式之间等价性的问题，需要严格的理论框架来解决。

Method: 使用Roe代数和适用于非可分C*-代数的KK-理论变体，建立谱流、Fredholm指标配对与Kubo公式（含半空间投影）之间的等价性；对粗等价于R^d的空间，通过与标准对偶Dirac算子的配对进行显式比较，并利用对称符号函数的拓扑度给出重数。

Result: 证明了带任意边界情形下与一般位置空间Dirac算子的指标配对的广义体-边对应关系；对粗等价于R^d的空间，这些配对在重数意义下等于标准对偶Dirac算子得到的配对；同时证明了用实空间公式计算一般指标配对的广义Kubo公式。

Conclusion: 该工作统一并推广了拓扑绝缘体中整数不变量的多种实空间表达式，为正定边界和任意边界条件下的体-边对应提供了严格的算子代数框架。

Abstract: Strong topological insulators are classified by integer invariants which admit different real-space expressions. Inspired by recent work studying these invariants for spaces with curved boundaries, we revisit the problem of equivalence of the various expressions, including Fredholm index pairings and Kubo formulas involving half-space projections. Using Roe algebras and a variant of KK-theory suitable for non-separable $C^*$-algebras, we prove a general form of bulk-edge correspondence for the index pairing with a general position-space Dirac operator in the presence of arbitrary boundaries. For spaces coarsely equivalent to $\mathbb{R}^d$, we show that up to multiplicity, these pairings are equal to those obtained with the standard dual Dirac operator, with the multiplicity explicitly given as the topological degree of the symbol function. In addition, we prove a generalized Kubo formula which computes the general index pairing by a real-space formula.

</details>


### [115] [A Morse-Family Integrator for Hamilton--Jacobi Dynamics Across Caustics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29645)
*F. Jiménez Alburquerque,M. Leok,C. Sardón,X. Zhao*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于离散Morse族、Lagrangian关系和Tulczyjew三元组离散类比的一种几何框架，用于隐式离散Hamilton系统。核心思想是将定义离散动力学的Lagrangian子流形视为基本几何对象，而非显式辛演化映射，从而自然容纳隐式、受约束和退化离散系统。框架内建立了Type-II离散Hamilton-Jacobi理论，并在投影奇异时通过Morse族描述演化，最后应用于光学波前通过折叠焦散传播的离散模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的离散Hamilton系统通常依赖于显式的辛演化映射（即graph表示），但这种方法在隐式、受约束或退化系统中会遇到困难，特别是在投影到构型空间变得奇异、单值生成函数不存在时。作者希望通过将Lagrangian子流形作为基本几何对象，构建一个统一的几何框架，以同时处理这些复杂情况并建立完整的离散Hamilton-Jacobi理论。

Method: 采用离散Morse族、Lagrangian关系和Tulczyjew三元组的离散类比，构建几何框架。具体而言：(1) 将离散动力学视为Lagrangian子流形的传播；(2) 当子流形可由精确1-形式dW_k和dW_{k+1}局部表示时，得到离散Hamilton-Jacobi方程；(3) 当投影奇异、单值生成函数不存在时，将Type-II离散动力学与Morse族组合，通过复合构造传播Lagrangian子流形的生成族，无需将动力学表示为graph。

Result: 建立了处理隐式、受约束和退化离散系统的统一几何框架；推导了Type-II离散Hamilton-Jacobi理论及其在正则情形下的方程形式；在奇点情形下，通过Morse族与Type-II动力学的复合，成功构造了传播Lagrangian子流形的生成族。应用方面，给出了光学波前通过折叠焦散传播的离散规则，同时提供了离散Hamilton积分器和多值Hamilton-Jacobi解的规则表示。

Conclusion: 该几何框架表明，以Lagrangian子流形为核心而非显式辛映射来构建离散动力学，能够统一处理正则、隐式、受约束及奇异情形。同一几何构造同时提供了离散Hamilton积分器和多值Hamilton-Jacobi解的规则表示，为光学波前传播等问题提供了有力的离散几何工具。

Abstract: We develop a geometric framework for implicit discrete Hamiltonian systems based on discrete Morse families, Lagrangian relations, and discrete analogues of Tulczyjew's triple. The main idea is to regard the Lagrangian submanifold defining the discrete dynamics, rather than an explicit symplectic evolution map, as the fundamental geometric object. This viewpoint naturally accommodates implicit, constrained, and degenerate discrete systems.
  Within this framework, we formulate a Type--II discrete Hamilton--Jacobi theory in terms of the propagation of Lagrangian submanifolds between consecutive discrete steps. When these submanifolds are locally represented by exact one-forms $dW_k$ and $dW_{k+1}$, the resulting equations provide a discrete Hamilton--Jacobi relation between consecutive generating functions. More generally, when the projection onto configuration space becomes singular and a single-valued generating function ceases to exist, we show that the evolution can be described by the composition of Type--II discrete dynamics with Morse families. This yields a generating family for the propagated Lagrangian submanifold without requiring the dynamics to be represented as a graph.
  As an application, we consider the propagation of optical wavefronts through fold caustics. A Type--II discrete Hamiltonian yields a symplectic ray integrator, while a Morse family represents the multivalued wavefront near the caustic. Their composition provides a discrete propagation rule for the complete Lagrangian manifold across the singularity. In this way, the same geometric construction simultaneously provides a discrete Hamiltonian integrator and a regular representation of multivalued Hamilton--Jacobi solutions.

</details>


### [116] [Homogeneous attractive Bose-Einstein condensates with repulsive three-body interactions: the one-dimensional case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29874)
*Dinh-Thi Nguyen*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维均匀玻色气体在吸引两体与排斥三体相互作用下的行为，通过中间Hartree理论严格推导出三次-五次非线性薛定谔泛函作为多体哈密顿量的平均场极限，并重点分析排斥三体项对基态能量估计和质量集中性质的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有吸引两体与排斥三体相互作用的玻色气体的基态行为，尤其是排斥三体相互作用对系统稳定性和质量集中现象的调控作用，为理解多体量子系统的平均场极限提供严格数学基础。

Method: 采用中间Hartree理论（intermediate Hartree theory），对一维均匀玻色气体的多体哈密顿量进行平均场极限推导，得到均匀三次-五次非线性薛定谔（cubic-quintic NLS）能量泛函，并在此基础上分析基态能量的估计与质量集中性质。

Result: 严格推导出均匀三次-五次非线性薛定谔泛函作为平均场极限，揭示了排斥三体项在阻止吸引两体项导致的塌缩（mass concentration）中的作用，并给出相应的基态能量估计结果。

Conclusion: 排斥三体相互作用在吸引玻色气体中起到关键稳定化作用，使得系统在平均场极限下可由三次-五次NLS泛函精确描述，其基态能量与质量集中行为受三体排斥强度显著影响。

Abstract: We investigate the behavior of a homogeneous one-dimensional Bose gas composed of $N$ identical bosons experiencing attractive two-body and repulsive three-body interactions. Utilizing intermediate Hartree theory, we rigorously derive the homogeneous cubic-quintic nonlinear Schrödinger functional as the mean-field limit of the many-body Hamiltonian. Particular attention is given to the impact of the repulsive three-body term on ground state energy estimates and mass concentration properties

</details>


### [117] [Homogeneous attractive Bose-Einstein condensates with repulsive three-body interactions: the two-dimensional case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29875)
*Dinh-Thi Nguyen*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了由N个全同玻色子组成的均匀玻色气体，该气体同时具有吸引的两体相互作用和排斥的三体相互作用。通过中间Hartree理论，严格推导出均匀三次-五次非线性薛定谔泛函作为模型的平均场极限，并聚焦于系统随两体相互作用的行为变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有吸引两体和排斥三体相互作用的二维玻色气体的平均场极限行为，特别是两体相互作用对系统行为的影响。

Method: 采用中间Hartree理论（intermediate Hartree theory），在无限二维空间中严格推导均匀三次-五次非线性薛定谔（cubic-quintic NLS）泛函作为平均场极限。

Result: 成功推导出均匀cubic-quintic非线性薛定谔泛函作为该玻色气体模型的平均场极限，并揭示了系统行为与两体相互作用的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 通过严格的数学推导，建立了具有吸引两体和排斥三体相互作用的二维均匀玻色气体与cubic-quintic非线性薛定谔泛函之间的联系，且系统行为由两体相互作用主导。

Abstract: We consider a homogeneous Bose gas composed of $N$ identical bosons occupying an infinite two-dimensional space. This gas exhibits both an attractive two-body interaction and a repulsive three-body interaction. Via the intermediate Hartree theory, we rigorously derive the homogeneous cubic-quintic nonlinear Schroedinger functional as the mean-field limit of the model. Our investigation focuses on the system's behavior in relation to the two-body interaction.

</details>


### [118] [Local Density Approximation and Other Limit Regimes for a Homogeneous Bose Gas with Repulsive Three-Body Interactions in Low-Dimensional Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29876)
*Thi Anh Thu Doan,Dinh-Thi Nguyen*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在低维空间（d≤2）中研究具有吸引三次非线性与排斥五次非线性的能量泛函极小元，证明固定有效统计参数下极小元的存在性，并在大有效统计参数极限下推导出托马斯-费米型有效模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究针对描述量子玻色气体的能量泛函展开，其中包含两体吸引和三体排斥的非线性项，这类模型在低维空间中的极小元问题尚未被充分理解，特别是在不同极限区域下的有效描述。

Method: 作者在低空间维度d≤2下分析含吸引三次项与排斥五次项的能量泛函，固定有效统计参数证明极小元存在性，并在质量临界非线性（相对于动能）的大有效统计参数极限下推导出托马斯-费米型有效模型，同时研究其他依赖于质量临界非线性的极限区域。

Result: 成功证明了固定有效统计参数下极小元的存在性，在大有效统计参数极限下获得均匀玻色气体的托马斯-费米型有效模型，并刻画了其他质量临界非线性相关的极限行为。

Conclusion: 该工作系统性地建立了低维空间中具有竞争非线性项的玻色气体能量泛函的极小元理论，为理解量子玻色气体在不同统计参数和维度下的有效行为提供了数学基础。

Abstract: In the whole space of low spatial dimensions, namely $d \leq 2$, we study the minimizers of an energy functional with an attractive cubic nonlinearity and a repulsive quintic nonlinearity, which describes a quantum Bose gas with a two-body attraction and a three-body repulsion. We prove the existence of minimizers at fixed effective statistics parameter. In the limit of a large effective statistics parameter of the mass-critical nonlinearity (with respect to the kinetic energy), we derive an effective Thomas-Fermi-like model for the homogeneous Bose gas. We also consider other limit regimes depending on the mass-critical nonlinearity.

</details>


### [119] [Multiple Nonlinear Waves by (Quantum) Neural Networks: Checking the AI supremacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29894)
*Luigi Martina,Riccardo Caricato,Riccardo Della Torre*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了量子物理信息神经网络（QPINN）技术求解演化偏微分方程的潜力，以具有周期性边界条件的Korteweg-de Vries方程中弱非线性介质的多次波传播问题为测试案例。通过提供一系列性能指标并进行数值实验，评估QPINN在该场景下的成熟度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然该问题存在解析解，但高亏格解对数值方法而言是极具挑战性的测试基准。作者希望检验QPINN是否已足够成熟，能够应用于这一复杂的演化偏微分方程求解场景。

Method: 以Korteweg-de Vries方程（周期边界条件下的弱非线性多次波传播）作为测试问题，设计一组性能指标（indicators of merit），并开展多项数值实验对QPINN进行评估。

Result: 通过数值实验提供了一套关于QPINN在求解该挑战性PDE问题上性能的评估结果（具体实验结果需详见原文）。

Conclusion: 该工作旨在判断QPINN技术是否已足够成熟以应对高亏格解这类具有挑战性的演化PDE问题，为其实际应用提供依据。

Abstract: To test the potential of so-called quantum physics-informed neural network (QPINN) technology for solving evolutionary partial differential equations, we consider the problem of multiple wave propagation in a weakly nonlinear medium described by the Korteweg-de Vries equation with periodic boundary conditions. Although this problem is solvable analytically, high-genus solutions could represent a rather challenging testbed for numerical methods. Therefore, our idea is to test whether a QPINN is sufficiently mature for application in this context by providing a set of indicators of merit and performing several numerical experiments.

</details>


### [120] [Uniqueness of universal quantum dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30012)
*M. Y. Avetisyan,R. L. Mkrtchyan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了在若干假设下，普适量子维度公式的唯一性。首先展示了经典代数的量子维度具有Vogel意义下的普适表达形式（即q-维度因子的乘积/比值，参数为aα+bβ+cγ，其中c=0,1,2且a,b为整数），并基于此推导出简单唯一性判据，证明所有已知的普适量子维度公式都是唯一的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普适量子维度公式在表示论和量子代数中具有重要地位，但此前缺乏对其唯一性的严格证明。该研究旨在解决这一基础性问题，为已知公式的正当性提供理论支撑。

Method: 首先证明经典代数的量子维度具有可普适表示的特定形式（以aα+bβ+cγ为参数的q-维度因子乘积/比），然后基于该结构推导出唯一性判据，并据此验证所有已知普适量子维度公式的唯一性。

Result: 推导出了简单可行的唯一性判据，并证明所有已知的普适量子维度公式在该判据下都是唯一的。

Conclusion: 在特定假设下，普适量子维度公式的唯一性得到严格证明，且这一结果为既有公式提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: We prove, under some assumptions, the uniqueness of universal quantum dimension formulae. We first show that the quantum dimensions of classical algebras have a specific form that can be represented universally, in Vogel's sense, as the product/ratio of q-dimension factors with arguments $aα+bβ+cγ$ where $c=0, 1, 2$ and $a, b$ are integers. On this basis, we derive simple uniqueness criteria, according to which all known universal quantum dimensions are unique.

</details>


### [121] [On the equivalence between the Polchinski flow and the Connes-Kreimer approaches to perturbative renormalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31049)
*Yvain Bruned,Paul Laubie,Aurélien Minguella*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了Polchinski流与Connes-Kreimer两种微扰重整化方法之间的对等性，即前者产生与后者相同的重整化结果。作者提出一种基于装饰图（费曼图）的ansatz，配合组合重整化程序求解Polchinski方程，该结果适用于非常一般的欧几里得量子场论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微扰重整化存在多种等价但其数学结构差异显著的表述方式，其中Polchinski的精确重整化群方法（连续场论视角）与Connes-Kreimer的Hopf代数方法（组合/代数视角）是两大主流范式。理清两者之间的深层对应关系，对于统一理解重整化结构、简化技术推导以及发现新的重整化工具具有重要的理论意义。

Method: 核心方法包括：(1) 构造以装饰图（费曼图）为基础的ansatz，并配以组合重整化程序；(2) 利用关于重整化的多重态射（multiple morphism）性质作为核心证明工具；(3) 提出一个新的装饰图森林对偶公式（duality formula for forests of decorated graphs），该公式是证明的关键技术环节。

Result: 成功证明了Polchinski方程的解可通过基于装饰图的组合重整化程序获得，从而建立了Polchinski流与Connes-Kreimer方法的重整化一致性。此外，由此推导出的重整化势形式被认为是当前文献中最一般的结果。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了Polchinski精确重整化群方法与Connes-Kreimer组合重整化方法之间在数学结构上的深层等价性，为欧几里得量子场论的重整化理论提供了统一视角，并为更一般量子场论模型的重整化提供了新的技术手段。

Abstract: We prove a correspondence between the Polchinski flow and the Connes-Kreimer approaches to perturbative renormalisation, in the sense that the first yields the same renormalisation as the latter. More precisely, we show that an ansatz based on decorated graphs (Feynman diagrams),with a combinatorial renormalisation procedure solves the Polchinski equation. This result holds for very general Euclidean quantum field theories. We are able to derive from this the form of the renormalised potential which is, to the best of our knowledge, the most general one in the literature. The main ingredients of the proof are multiple morphism properties with respect to the renormalisation, as well as a novel duality formula for forests of decorated graphs.

</details>


### [122] [Boundary Quantum Knizhnik-Zamolodchikov Equations and Integrability of Quantum Field Theories with Time-Dependent Bulk and Boundary Coupling Strengths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31080)
*Parameshwar R. Pasnoori*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将广义Bethe ansatz框架从周期边界条件扩展到开放边界条件，处理具有时间依赖耦合强度和时间依赖边界条件的量子多体系统，证明可积性条件可由BqKZ方程给出，且静态模型的RG不变量等同于时间依赖模型中的动力学不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前广义Bethe ansatz框架仅适用于周期性边界条件下时间依赖耦合强度的量子多体系统的精确求解。开放边界条件（尤其当边界条件本身随时间变化时）是更一般且更具挑战性的情形，作者希望将该统一框架推广至此，从而为更广泛的时间依赖可积系统提供精确解方法。

Method: 将广义Bethe ansatz框架从周期边界条件推广到开放边界条件：1) 假设体区耦合强度满足可积的时间依赖条件，推导出与可积性兼容的时间依赖边界条件；2) 将时间依赖Schrödinger方程化简为一组矩阵差分方程，即边界量子Knizhnik-Zamolodchikov (BqKZ)方程；3) 求解BqKZ方程获得精确波函数的显式形式。

Result: 证明在可积的时间依赖体区耦合强度下，广义Bethe ansatz框架能够提供与可积性相容的时间依赖边界条件，并将时间依赖Schrödinger方程约化为BqKZ方程；BqKZ方程的解给出精确波函数的显式形式。此外，发现对应静态模型中的RG不变量与时间依赖模型中的动力学不变量完全一致。

Conclusion: 该工作将广义Bethe ansatz统一框架成功扩展到时间依赖开放边界条件的情形，建立了时间依赖可积模型的精确解方法，并揭示了静态RG不变量与时间依赖动力学不变量之间的深层对应关系。

Abstract: The generalized Bethe ansatz framework formulated in [P. R. Pasnoori, Phys. Rev. B 112, L060409 (2025)] provides a unified framework to find exact solutions to quantum many-body systems with time-dependent coupling strengths with periodic boundary conditions. In this work we extend this framework to the case of open boundary conditions where in addition to the time-dependent interactions in the bulk, the boundary conditions are explicitly time-dependent. We show that for integrable time-dependent bulk coupling strengths, the generalized Bethe ansatz framework provides the time-dependent boundary conditions compatible with integrability and reduces the time-dependent Schrodinger equation to a set of matrix difference equations called the boundary quantum Knizhnik-Zamolodchikov (BqKZ) equations. The solution to the BqKZ equations provides the explicit form of the exact wavefunction. We further show that the RG invariants of the corresponding static model identify with the dynamical invariants in the time-dependent model.

</details>


### [123] [Rotating-wave approximation for spin-boson models with structured fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31090)
*Aitor Balmaseda,Davide Lonigro,Juan Manuel Pérez-Pardo*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对无界哈密顿量生成的两个量子演化，推导了依赖于态的状态依赖界限，通过在半双线性形式层面上执行第二次分部积分（而非算子层面），消除了对公共不变算子域的需求。估计涉及两个生成元时间积分差值的范数而非其范数的积分，因此对快速振荡项的平均效应敏感。作为应用，在一般测度空间上具有任意有质量色散关系及适当形状因子的结构化玻色子场的自旋-玻色子模型中，给出了旋转波近似的定量界，该界在稠密子空间上成立、完全显式，且所有常数仅依赖模型参数而非频率尺度，故在大频率极限下近似变为精确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子演化的比较通常需要公共的不变算子域，这在无界哈密顿量情形下难以满足；此外，传统估计往往是对生成元范数进行积分，无法捕捉快速振荡项的平均效应。作者旨在克服这两个局限，获得对高频行为敏感的、无需公共不变域的定量误差界。

Method: 核心技术是在半双线性形式（sesquilinear form）层面进行第二次分部积分，而非在算子层面操作，从而避免了公共不变算子域的要求。得到的误差估计是时间积分后的生成元差值的范数（时间积分差值范数），而非差值范数的时间积分，这使得估计对快速振荡引起的平均效应敏感。在旋转波近似的应用中，作者仔细分析了高频标度行为，并给出完全显式的常数。

Result: 得到了两个量子演化之间的状态依赖界限，适用于共享共同形式域（而非算子域）的无界哈密顿量；并在自旋-玻色子模型（具有一般测度空间上的任意有质量色散关系和适当的形状因子类）中给出了旋转波近似的定量界，该界在稠密子空间上成立、完全显式、所有常数仅依赖于模型参数而不依赖于频率尺度，从而在大频率极限下趋于精确。

Conclusion: 本文通过形式层面的二次分部积分技术建立了比传统算子层面方法更广泛适用的量子演化误差界，其优势在于无需公共不变算子域且能捕捉高频平均效应；旋转波近似的定量结果表明该界在实际模型中具有有效性和实用性。

Abstract: We derive state-dependent bounds on the difference between two quantum evolutions generated by unbounded Hamiltonians sharing a common form domain. The main technical tool is a second integration by parts, performed at the level of sesquilinear forms rather than at the operator level, which removes the need for a common invariant operator domain. The resulting estimate involves the norm of the time-integrated difference of the two generators, rather than the integral of its norm, and is therefore sensitive to the averaging effects produced by fast-oscillating terms. As an application we prove a quantitative bound on the rotating-wave approximation for spin-boson models with a structured boson field, described by an arbitrary massive dispersion relation on a general measure space and by a suitable class of form factors. The proof involves a careful analysis of the high-frequency scaling. The bound holds on a dense subspace of states, is fully explicit, and all the constants entering it depend only on the parameters of the model and not on the frequency scale, so that the approximation becomes exact in the limit of large frequency.

</details>


### [124] [The Dunkl two-photon/Schrödinger algebras and their applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31104)
*Francisco J. Herranz,Danilo Latini*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了Dunkl双光子代数和Dunkl-薛定谔代数，作为标准六维双光子代数与薛定谔代数的反射扩展对应物，证明了二者同构，推导了三次Casimir不变量及若干子代数的二次Casimir不变量，并构造了Fock空间表示和全纯实现，最终通过向量场实现导出了包含Dunkl热-薛定谔方程在内的六个非平凡Dunkl微分-差分方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将反射（reflection）这一额外的生成元纳入标准代数结构，构建反射扩展的双光子代数与薛定谔代数，从而推广现有的代数框架，为研究Dunkl型微分方程提供新的代数对称性基础。

Method: 通过在标准六维双光子与薛定谔代数中加入对合反射生成元进行扩展；证明两代数同构；利用代数方法推导三次Casimir不变量与子代数二次Casimir不变量；在一维Dunkl谐振子Fock空间上构造表示并转化为Dunkl-Fock-Bargmann全纯实现；最后基于Dunkl空间导数构造向量场实现。

Result: 建立了Dunkl双光子代数与Dunkl-薛定谔代数并证明同构；获得各结构的Casimir不变量；构造了Fock空间表示与全纯实现；由子代数Casimir不变量导出六个Dunkl微分-差分方程，其中包含Dunkl版热-薛定谔方程。

Conclusion: 本文系统地构建了反射扩展的双光子与薛定谔代数体系，其表示理论与微分实现为Dunkl型微分方程提供了新的代数工具，特别是导出了Dunkl热-薛定谔方程这一定性新结果。

Abstract: The work introduces the novel Dunkl two-photon and Dunkl-Schrödinger algebras as reflection-extended counterparts of the standard six-dimensional two-photon and Schrödinger algebras. These extensions are realized through the presence of an involutive generator representing a reflection, and they reduce to the undeformed two-photon and Schrödinger algebras in the absence of this additional generator in an appropriate limit. For both algebraic structures, which are shown to be isomorphic, we derive the associated cubic polynomial Casimir invariant. Furthermore, we identify several structurally significant subalgebras and compute their corresponding quadratic Casimir invariants. Representations of the Dunkl two-photon algebra are constructed on the Fock space of the one-dimensional Dunkl harmonic oscillator and subsequently translated into holomorphic realizations within the associated Dunkl-Fock-Bargmann representation. Finally, for the Dunkl-Schrödinger algebra, we present an explicit vector field realization in terms of the Dunkl spatial derivative, while keeping time continuous. When applied to the Casimir invariants of the aforementioned subalgebras, this realization yields a family of six nontrivial Dunkl differential-difference equations in $1+1$ dimensions, including, in particular, a Dunkl version of the well-known heat-Schrödinger equation.

</details>


### [125] [Exactly flat bands beyond the chiral limit in Bistritzer--MacDonald Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31147)
*Zhen Huang,Kevin D. Stubbs*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了扭转双层石墨烯BM模型在手性极限之外的精确平坦带问题。作者构造了一族非手性隧穿势，使得哈密顿量在每个布洛赫动量处和所有非手性耦合实数下都有至少两个零能态，从而否定了Zworski调查中的开放问题3；反之，证明了所有具备此性质的可容许势都属于该族，并给出了完整刻画。同时证明标准BM模型（一阶谐波隧穿势）在魔术角处除可能的离散非手性耦合强度集合（无有限聚点）外不存在精确平坦带。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性极限下BM模型在魔术角处存在精确平坦带，但这一性质在手性极限之外（即存在非同寻常的非手性隧穿势时）是否能够保持，此前是一个悬而未决的问题。Zworski在调查中将其列为开放问题3，本文旨在解答此问题，并揭示能维持精确平坦带的非手性势的完整刻画。

Method: 作者构造了一族特定的非手性隧穿势，证明其哈密顿量在每个布洛赫动量处对所有实数非手性耦合强度均有至少两个零能态；同时提出一个显式的可逆性判据，要求投影微扰在某些布洛赫动量处具有非零标量系数，用以排除一般非手性隧穿势下精确平坦带的存在；通过局部泰勒展开验证了该判据对标准BM势成立。

Result: 构造出一族非手性隧穿势，其精确平坦带在手性极限之外对所有实数耦合强度均保持，构成Zworski开放问题3的反例族；证明了该族是唯一满足此性质的可容许势族（完整表征）；证明了标准BM模型在魔术角处仅在至多可数个无有限聚点的非手性耦合强度值处可能存在精确平坦带。

Conclusion: 精确平坦带的保持对手性极限的偏离高度敏感：标准BM模型几乎无法维持精确平坦带，但存在特殊构造的非手性隧穿势族可以完全保持此性质。这给出了对该问题的一个完整数学刻画，并回答了Zworski调查中的开放问题3。

Abstract: The chiral limit of the Bistritzer--MacDonald model for twisted bilayer graphene has exactly flat bands at magic angles. We show that exact flatness can persist beyond the chiral limit with nonchiral tunnelling potentials in the usual symmetry class. We construct a family of nonchiral tunnelling potentials for which the Hamiltonian has at least two zero-energy states at every Bloch momentum and for every real value of the nonchiral coupling. This gives a family of counterexamples to Open Problem~3 of Zworski's survey \cite{ZworskiSurvey}. Conversely, we prove that every admissible potential with this property belongs to this family, thereby obtaining a complete characterization. By contrast, we show that the standard BM model, namely, BM Hamiltonians with first harmonic tunnelling potentials, does not admit exactly flat bands at the magic angle except possibly at a discrete set of nonchiral coupling strengths, with no finite accumulation point. To prove this, we introduce an explicit invertibility criterion. The criterion requires the projected perturbation to have a nonzero scalar coefficient at some Bloch momentum, obstructing exact flatness for general nonchiral tunnelling. A local Taylor expansion argument verifies the criterion for the standard BM potentials. For the family constructed above, however, the projected perturbation vanishes at every Bloch momentum, so the invertibility criterion fails.

</details>


### [126] [Lattice Green's function of the hyperkagome lattice: modular uniformization at level 30 from an orthogonal differential Galois group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28141)
*Bryan Nasr,Jean-Marie Maillard*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次将超kagome晶格（由自旋液体候选材料Na4Ir3O8的铱亚晶格实现）的格点格林函数与模性联系起来。利用精确晶格矩得到了湮灭格林函数的三阶Picard-Fuchs微分算子，证明了其微分伽罗瓦群为正交群，并通过二阶微分交织子使其投影地成为对称平方。核心结果：该底层二阶算子是γ0(30)+模曲线的均匀化方程（Lian-Yau表中的一行），文中通过先验极点度界而非数值检查证明了Schwarzian恒等式。超kagome格林函数因此是level-30模函数，其权重2周期被显式给出为一个深度1的准模形式，且证明其在任何权重下都不是模形式乘代数函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超kagome晶格的格林函数长期以来无法像立方晶格那样获得闭式解。Varma和Monien将其态密度归约为三重积分但无法精确求解。作者的目标是找到该格林函数的精确闭式形式，并理解其背后的数学结构。

Method: 从精确晶格矩出发构造三阶线性微分（Picard-Fuchs）算子；分析其微分伽罗瓦群（证明为正交群）；利用二阶微分交织子展示投影对称平方结构；将底层二阶算子识别为γ0(30)+模曲线的均匀化方程（Lian-Yau表的一行）；用先验极点度界（a priori pole-degree bound）而非数值检验证明Schwarzian恒等式；对权重2周期给出深度1准模形式的显式表达式并证明其非模性。

Result: 超kagome晶格格林函数是level-30的模函数，提供了Varma和Monien所求的闭式形式。权重2周期被显式给出为深度1的准模形式，并严格证明了该准模形式在任何权重下都不是模形式与代数函数之积。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了超kagome格点格林函数研究的空白，建立了它与level-thirty模性的深刻联系；同时通过严格的Schwarzian证明也验证了Lian-Yau表中相应条目的正确性，展示了格点格林函数与模曲线理论之间新的桥梁。

Abstract: The lattice Green's functions of the cubic lattices are classical: each is a symmetric square of a second-order operator, with a closed form in complete elliptic integrals. The hyperkagome lattice, realized by the iridium sublattice of the spin-liquid candidate Na4Ir3O8, has resisted such a treatment: Varma and Monien reduced its density of states to a threefold integral they found no way to solve exactly. From exact lattice moments we obtain an irreducible third-order linear differential (Picard-Fuchs) operator annihilating the Green's function. It is not a literal symmetric square, yet its symmetric square carries a rational solution: the differential Galois group is orthogonal, and an order-two differential intertwiner makes the operator projectively a symmetric square. Our main result is that the underlying second-order operator is the uniformizing equation of the genus-zero modular curve of Gamma_0(30)+, the level-thirty group generated by Gamma_0(30) and all its Atkin-Lehner involutions, an explicit eta quotient parametrizing the natural variable. That uniformizing equation is itself not new - it is a row of the Lian-Yau table of genus-zero groups - so what is new here is the identification of a lattice Green's function with it, together with a proof: the Schwarzian identity is established by an a priori pole-degree bound rather than checked numerically, which also proves the tabulated entry. The hyperkagome Green's function is therefore modular at level thirty, supplying the closed form Varma and Monien sought. Its weight-two period is obtained explicitly: a depth-one quasimodular form which we prove is not a modular form times an algebraic function at any weight.

</details>

### [127] [Modeling of Network Constraints in Large-scale Capacity Expansion Optimization of Power Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28893)
*Yu Weng,Lara Booth,Priya L. Donti,Ruaridh Macdonald*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文扩展了GenX容量扩展模型，通过基于不动点定理的网络约束嵌入ACOPF考量，在保持运输模型可解性的同时大幅降低计算成本，并在ISO New England电网案例中进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统容量扩展模型为可解性而省略非线性、非凸的ACOPF约束，使用线性化或运输公式，但这限制了理解电网物理特性对投资决策的影响。直接嵌入完整ACOPF在大型规划问题中计算代价过高，亟需一种既能反映物理网络约束又不显著牺牲求解效率的方法。

Method: 扩展GenX容量扩展模型，引入基于不动点定理的网络约束来嵌入ACOPF相关考量，保持与运输公式几乎相同的维度（不明增加大量变量和约束），仅产生适度的运行时间增加。与原始的运输式GenX模型和完整ACOPF非线性非凸版本进行对比。

Result: 提出的方法在保持规划模型可解性的同时嵌入了ACOPF考量，维度几乎与运输模型一致，运行时间仅适度增加，比直接嵌入ACOPF便宜得多，适用于大规模容量规划问题。

Conclusion: 基于不动点定理的网络约束方法为大规模容量扩展规划提供了一种兼顾物理网络精度与计算可行性的折中方案，相比直接嵌入ACOPF实现了显著的计算成本节约，适用于实际大型电网规划。

Abstract: Capacity expansion modeling plays a critical role in optimizing the deployment of new generation, storage, and transmission, typically at national and regional levels. To support long-term planning, these models consider a large set of energy technologies and policies, along with decades of weather and demand data. Realistic capacity expansion models thus become high-dimensional optimization problems, with hundreds of millions of variables and constraints, which are challenging to solve. A common strategy to address this complexity is to omit non-linear, non-convex AC optimal power flow (ACOPF) constraints and instead use linearized power balance equations or transport formulations. While these simplifications improve tractability, they limit our understanding of how power flow and the physical properties of power networks impact investment decisions across generation, storage, and transmission infrastructure. This paper addresses this gap by extending the GenX capacity expansion model to incorporate fixed point theorem-based network constraints. These embed ACOPF-based considerations while maintaining the tractability of the planning model, nearly preserving the dimensionality of the transport formulation and incurring only modest runtime increases. This approach is much cheaper than embedding ACOPF directly, making it appropriate for large-scale capacity planning problems. We compare our approach to the original transport-based GenX model as well as a non-linear, non-convex version that incorporates the full ACOPF constraints, for a case study of the ISO New England grid.

</details>


### [128] [Differentiating Minimal-Norm Solutions to Parametric Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28899)
*Baptiste Plaquevent-Jourdain,Jalal Fadili,Antonio Silveti-Falls*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了在参数优化问题非良定（存在多解）情况下，通过放宽隐函数定理的可逆性条件为更弱的均匀值域条件，利用极限Tikhonov正则化和保守集值场理论，证明了最小范数解映射的广义导数存在性，并将非光滑隐式微分扩展到不适定场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐式微分在双层规划和元学习中至关重要，但传统方法依赖隐函数定理，需要最优性条件的偏雅可比矩阵可逆，这在优化问题存在多个解时会失效。作者旨在解决这一局限，使隐式微分能够应用于非良定（ill-posed）的优化场景。

Method: 作者将可逆性条件松弛为严格更弱的均匀值域条件（uniform range condition），在此条件下利用极限Tikhonov正则化参数逼近和保守集值场理论来推导最小范数解映射的广义导数；进一步通过控制广义Hessian矩阵特征值，证明了伪逆公式的合理性。该框架针对光滑凸目标函数建立，并扩展到非光滑复合问题，覆盖了最小二乘、Huber回归和LASSO等典型问题。

Result: 在最小二乘、Huber回归和LASSO问题上验证了所提假设的有效性，并在数据投毒（data poisoning）和数据超清洗（data hypercleaning）问题上通过实验验证了该方法在实际任务中的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作为非良定参数优化问题提供了理论保证下的隐式微分方法，将非光滑隐式微分从良定问题推广到不适定场景，拓宽了双层规划和元学习在实际应用中的适用范围。

Abstract: Differentiating through parametric optimization problems is central to bilevel programming and meta-learning, often accomplished using approximate implicit differentiation. The implicit function theorem requires inverting a partial Jacobian of the optimality condition, which fails when there are many solutions. Nonetheless, in such cases it is possible to relax invertibility to a strictly weaker uniform range condition, under which it is shown that the minimal-norm solution mapping admits generalized derivatives by using a limiting Tikhonov regularization argument and conservative set-valued field theory. With additional control on the eigenvalues of the generalized Hessians, a pseudoinverse formula is justified. This is established for a class of smooth convex objectives and extended to nonsmooth composite problems. These assumptions are verified for Least-Squares, Huber regression and LASSO. The resulting extension of nonsmooth implicit differentiation to ill-posed settings is examined experimentally on data poisoning and data hypercleaning problems.

</details>


### [129] [Calculus of the Facial Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28971)
*Javier Pena,Elias Wirth*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文开发了若干微积分法则，用于计算或下界估计多胞形的面距离，并在多种常见多胞形上进行了验证，特别地给出了Birkhoff多胞形面距离的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多胞形的面距离是度量几何与组合优化中的重要概念，但目前缺乏系统化的计算工具。作者旨在建立一套微积分规则，使得面距离的计算或下界估计变得更加系统化和可操作。

Method: 作者提出了若干微积分法则（calculus rules），用于计算或下界估计多胞形的面距离（facial distance），并通过将这些法则应用于多种流行的多胞形实例来验证其有效性，特别针对Birkhoff多胞形给出了面距离的下界。

Result: 作者在多种典型多胞形上展示了所提法则的应用，并成功为Birkhoff多胞形的面距离建立了下界。

Conclusion: 所提出的微积分法则为多胞形面距离的估计提供了有效的工具，并能推广应用于更广泛的多胞形类。

Abstract: We develop a few calculus rules to compute or lower bound the facial distance of a polytope. We illustrate our calculus rules on various popular polytopes. In particular, we provide a provide a lower bound on the facial distance of the Birkhoff polytope.

</details>


### [130] [B$^3$-PWL: GPU-Batched Branch-and-Bound for Piecewise-Linear Optimization with SOS2 Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28988)
*Yilin Guan,Shuqing Luo,Pingzhi Li,Tianlong Chen,Kaidi Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出B³-PWL，一个GPU-centric的批量分支定界框架，用于具有SOS2约束的分段线性优化问题，通过批次LP松弛子问题的并行求解和统一可行性搜索模块，在43个PWL-MIP实例上相比NVIDIA cuOpt取得9.25倍几何平均加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分段线性优化问题的全局求解计算代价高昂，因为分支定界需要反复求解LP松弛子问题。现有求解器以CPU为中心，未能充分利用现代GPU的可扩展性；已有的GPU加速分支定界方法要么只针对神经网络场景不适用于一般PWL优化，要么仅在CPU-centric MIP求解器中加速强分支启发式等辅助子程序。

Method: B³-PWL采用GPU-centric的批量分支定界框架：1) 在GPU上并发求解批量LP松弛子问题，使用一阶原始-对偶求解器，并配备专门设计的批量分块稀疏矩阵核；2) 引入统一的可行性搜索模块，结合SOS2修复原始启发式与批量可行性泵，快速获得可行解并提升剪枝效率。

Result: 在43个PWL-MIP实例的基准测试中，B³-PWL相比NVIDIA cuOpt取得9.25倍的几何平均加速，并在每个测试实例上都能获得高质量的可行解。在公开的阀门点机组组合基准上，B³-PWL进一步优于NVIDIA cuOpt以及开源CPU求解器SCIP和HiGHS。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了一阶LP方法作为GPU加速分支定界核心引擎的潜力，为一般性分段线性优化问题提供了一条GPU-centric的高效求解路径。

Abstract: Piecewise-linear (PWL) optimization problems arise in many mixed-integer programming (MIP) optimization applications, including portfolio optimization, workforce scheduling, and resource allocation. But solving them to global optimality remains computationally expensive because branch-and-bound repeatedly solves LP relaxation subproblems. Existing solvers are largely CPU-centric, leaving the scalability of modern GPUs underutilized. Few prior GPU-accelerated branch-and-bound either targets neural network which is not suitable for general PWL optimization, or accelerates only auxiliary subroutines such as strong branching heuristics within CPU-centric MIP solvers. To bridge this gap, we propose B$^3$-PWL, a GPU-centric batched branch-and-bound framework for piecewise-linear optimization with Special Ordered Set of type 2 (SOS2) constraints. Our method solves batches of LP relaxation subproblems concurrently on the GPU using a first-order primal-dual solver, enabled by a specialized batched block-tiled sparse matrix kernel. To complement bound computation, we further introduce a unified feasibility search module that combines an SOS2 repair primal heuristic with a batched feasibility pump to rapidly obtain feasible incumbents and improve pruning efficiency. On a benchmark of 43 PWL-MIP instances, B$^3$-PWL achieves a 9.25x geometric-mean speedup over NVIDIA cuOpt while reaching high-quality feasible incumbents on every tested instance. On a public valve-point unit-commitment benchmark, it further outperforms NVIDIA cuOpt and the open-source CPU solvers SCIP and HiGHS, demonstrating the potential of first-order LP methods as the central engine of GPU-accelerated branch-and-bound.

</details>


### [131] [A New Primal-Dual Algorithm with Two Convex Combinations and Linesearch for General Convex-Concave Saddle-Point Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28993)
*Zexian Liu,Shuning Liu,Jialong Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对凸-凹鞍点问题提出了基于双重凸组合策略的新型原始-对偶算法（NPDAL-n），该算法通过两个凸组合的设计，解决了理论分析与数值实验对凸组合参数要求不一致的难题，并建立了全局收敛性和O(1/N)的次线性遍历收敛率；当原始函数强凸时，加速版本达到最优的O(1/N²)收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凸-凹鞍点问题广泛存在于机器学习、图像处理、经济学和均衡问题等领域。虽然凸组合是原始-对偶算法中关键的加速技术，但凸组合参数在理论分析与数值实验中的要求往往不一致（理论要求参数小，数值实验倾向于大参数），这种不一致性阻碍了算法的进一步发展和实际应用。

Method: 提出一种基于两个凸组合的新策略，将其整合到原始-对偶框架中，设计了一个带线搜索的新原始-对偶算法NPDAL-n。通过双凸组合设计，使得凸组合参数的允许范围主要由理论考量决定，而无需过多担心数值性能。算法采用Lyapunov能量下降分析进行理论收敛性证明。

Result: 在标准假设下，证明了NPDAL-n的全局收敛性和O(1/N)的次线性遍历收敛率。当原始函数为强凸时，开发了加速版本，达到最优的O(1/N²)收敛率。

Conclusion: 提出的双凸组合策略有效调和了理论分析与数值实验间关于凸组合参数的矛盾，为带凸组合的原始-对偶算法提供了新的理论进展，同时兼顾了算法的数值性能保障。

Abstract: Convex-concave saddle-point problems are ubiquitous across diverse domains, including machine learning, image processing, economics, and equilibrium problems. Primal-dual algorithms provide a highly effective and powerful framework for convex-concave saddle-point problems. Convex combination has become a crucial acceleration technique for primal-dual algorithms, and the integration of this technique has recently made these algorithms a highly active research topic. The choice of the convex combination parameter often has a significant impact on both the theoretical analysis and the numerical performance of the corresponding algorithms. However, the requirements on this parameter imposed by theory are sometimes inconsistent with those suggested by numerical experiments. For instance, theoretical analysis often requires the parameter to be small, while numerical experiments tend sometimes to favor larger values. To address this inconsistency and further advance primal-dual algorithms with convex combination, we develop a novel strategy based on two convex combinations, integrate it into a primal-dual framework, and propose a new primal-dual algorithm with linesearch, termed NPDAL-n, for general convex-concave saddle-point problems. The proposed two convex combinations in NPDAL-n ensure that the permissible range of the convex combination parameters is mainly determined by theoretical considerations, with little regard for numerical performance. Through rigorous Lyapunov energy descent analysis, we establish the global convergence and a sublinear ergodic convergence rate of $\mathcal{O}(1/N)$ for NPDAL-n under standard assumptions. When the primal function is strongly convex, we develop an accelerated version of NPDAL-n that achieves an optimal $\mathcal{O}(1/N^2)$ rate.

</details>


### [132] [The Stochastic Team Orienteering Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29119)
*Alberto Guastalla,Jean-François Côté,Roberto Aringhieri*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究随机团队定向问题（STOP），将旅行时间建模为随机变量，以最大化期望收集利润为目标。采用两阶段随机整数规划建模，提出基于整数L-shaped方法的精确求解算法，引入非线性机会约束确保路由可靠性，并提出了新的最优性割、有效不等式和约束提升来强化模型。在标准TOP基准数据集上的计算实验验证了算法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 团队定向问题（TOP）在实际应用中常常面临旅行时间不确定性的挑战，而传统确定性模型无法处理此类随机性。本文旨在填补STOP这一随机变体在精确求解方法上的空白，通过两阶段随机规划框架来更真实地反映实际场景中的不确定性。

Method: 将STOP建模为两阶段随机整数规划：第一阶段选择路由集合，第二阶段通过补偿（recourse）评估所选路由的期望利润。求解方法基于整数L-shaped方法，并做了以下改进：(1) 引入非线性机会约束以限制搜索范围至高可靠性路由；(2) 提出新的最优性割；(3) 通过有效不等式和约束提升来强化公式。

Result: 在文献中标准的TOP基准数据集上进行了全面的计算实验，验证了所提出的精确算法和强化策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为随机团队定向问题提供了一种有效的精确求解框架，通过两阶段随机整数规划建模和整数L-shaped方法改进，能够在考虑旅行时间不确定性的同时获得高质量的解，并为后续研究提供了可复用的理论和方法基础。

Abstract: This paper analyses the Stochastic Team Orienteering Problem (STOP), a stochastic variant of the Team Orienteering Problem (TOP). In the STOP, travel times are represented by random variables. The objective is to determine a set of routes that maximises the expected collected profit. We model the problem as a two-stage stochastic integer program and propose an exact solution method based on the Integer L-shaped method. We also consider a set of non-linear chance constraints to restrict the search to highly reliable routes. In the first-stage, a set of routes is selected, while in the second-stage the recourse evaluates the expected profit of the selected routes. We present a new set of optimality cuts and strengthen the formulation by considering valid inequalities and constraints liftings. Computational results provide a comprehensive analysis of the algorithm using the standard TOP benchmark dataset from the literature.

</details>


### [133] [Regularized extragradient method for structured bilevel optimization in continuous and discrete time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29181)
*Radu Ioan Bot,Enis Chenchene,David Alexander Hulett*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在实Hilbert空间中研究双层优化问题，目标是最小化外层凸函数，约束是使得内层极大单调算子取零值。在光滑情形下提出了基于动态正则化算子的单调流一阶动力学系统，在非光滑情形下提出了正则化近端外梯度算法，并在弱Attouch-Czarnecki型可积性条件或锐度假设下，建立了外层和内层残差的末次迭代收敛率，以及轨迹/迭代序列到最优解的弱收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层优化问题在机器学习、信号处理和变分分析等领域有广泛应用。当内层问题是极大单调算子的零点问题时，如何高效、可证明地求解相应的双层优化问题仍然是一个挑战。本文旨在发展统一的动力学系统和算法框架，在光滑与非光滑两种设置下同时保证内层和外层目标的最优性与收敛率。

Method: 本文提出两种方法：(1) 光滑设置下，构造将外层凸可微目标与内层连续单调单值算子结合的单调流动力学系统，通过动态正则化算子将双层问题转化为单层单调流问题；(2) 光滑+非光滑设置下，外层目标中加入真凸下半连续函数，内层算子加入其次微分，提出正则化近端外梯度算法，前向步和后向步分别基于动态正则化算子和函数执行。两种方法均在几何条件（弱Attouch-Czarnecki型可积性条件或锐度假设）下进行收敛性分析。

Result: 在光滑设置下，建立了外层和内层残差的末次迭代收敛率，以及轨迹到双层问题最优解的弱收敛性。在光滑+非光滑设置下，建立了正则化近端外梯度算法在内外层残差上的末次迭代收敛率，以及迭代序列的弱收敛性。

Conclusion: 本文为求解极大单调算子零点约束下的双层凸优化问题提供了统一的动力学与算法框架，覆盖了光滑与非光滑情形，并在合理的几何条件下给出了完整的收敛率保证，为该类问题的高效数值求解奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: In a real Hilbert space, we study a bilevel optimization problem that consists in minimizing an outer convex function over the zero set of a maximally monotone operator. In the smooth setting, where the outer objective is convex and Fréchet differentiable and the inner operator is single-valued, continuous and monotone, we associate with the problem a first-order dynamical system that can be viewed as a monotone flow applied to a dynamically regularized operator. Under suitable geometric conditions on the inner problem --- either a weak Attouch-Czarnecki-type integrability condition or the stronger assumption of sharpness --- we establish last-iterate convergence rates for both the outer and inner residuals, together with weak convergence of the trajectories to optimal solutions of the bilevel problem. In the smooth+nonsmooth setting, we enrich the outer objective with a proper, convex, and lower semicontinuous function, while the inner operator is augmented by the subdifferential of a function with the same properties. We propose a regularized proximal-extragradient algorithm in which both the forward and backward steps are performed with respect to dynamically regularized operators and functions, respectively. Under geometric assumptions on the inner problem analogous to those in the smooth setting, we establish last-iterate convergence rates for both the outer and inner residuals, together with weak convergence of the iterates to optimal solutions of the bilevel problem.

</details>


### [134] [Risk-averse design optimization with CVaR constraints via multifidelity tail-region correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29222)
*Gyeolhee Lee,Dongjin Lee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多重保真度（MF）尾部区域校正的CVaR约束设计优化方法，使用维度分解广义多项式混沌展开（DD-GPCE）作为全局代理模型，将有限的高保真（HF）评估预算集中在最影响CVaR的响应上，实现高精度且HF样本高效的CVaR约束优化设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在CVaR约束下的风险规避设计优化中，需要准确的尾部估计，但反复进行高保真评估成本高昂。传统方法在尾部区域的预测精度不足，需要在有限计算预算下有效利用HF评估来提升尾部估计精度。

Method: 1）采用DD-GPCE作为全局代理模型；2）利用估计系数的协方差量化有限样本预测不确定性，并据此定义基于置信区间的尾部区域；3）在尾部区域内采用两阶段策略：先探索预测不确定性高的位置，后利用大校正位移的位置；4）使用Tikhonov正则化的残差展开（基于同一多项式基）构建统一的尾部校正代理模型。

Result: 十杆桁架案例中，该方法以比MF重要性采样配置少约72至274倍的HF评估次数获得接近粗蒙特卡洛参考的设计；吸气阀案例中，质量降低1.05%，弯曲和鼓胀CVaR分别降低17.68%和21.53%，同时满足阀门开启要求。

Conclusion: 通过局部化的MF校正，该方法能够实现准确且HF样本高效的CVaR约束设计优化，表明将有限HF预算智能分配到对CVaR影响最大的尾部区域是有效提升设计优化性能的关键。

Abstract: Risk-averse design optimization with conditional value-at-risk (CVaR) constraints requires accurate tail estimates, but repeated high-fidelity (HF) evaluations are costly. We propose a multifidelity (MF) tail-region correction method that uses dimensionally decomposed generalized polynomial chaos expansion (DD-GPCE) as a global surrogate and directs a limited HF budget to responses that most strongly influence CVaR. Covariance of the estimated DD-GPCE coefficients quantifies finite-sample prediction uncertainty and defines a confidence-interval-based tail region. Within this region, a two-stage strategy explores locations with high prediction uncertainty and then exploits locations with large correction-induced shifts. A Tikhonov-regularized residual expansion using the same polynomial basis yields a unified tail-corrected surrogate. For a ten-bar truss, the method produced a design close to the crude-MCS reference with approximately 72 to 274 times fewer HF evaluations than the considered MF importance sampling configurations. For a suction valve, it reduced mass by 1.05% and bending and bulging CVaR by 17.68% and 21.53%, respectively, while satisfying the valve-opening requirement. The results demonstrate accurate, HF-sample-efficient CVaR-constrained design through localized MF correction.

</details>


### [135] [Exploiting Micro-Structure in the Generalized Riccati Recursion for Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29238)
*Louis Callens,Wilm Decré*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在Riccati递归中利用块内微结构的LU分解算法，集成在Fatrop等结构利用求解器中，可将最优控制问题搜索方向的计算时间最多降低20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优控制问题通过多重打靶法离散化后，Riccati递归利用块对角结构求解线性二次子问题，但将一般动力学约束改写成Riccati递归所需结构时，块矩阵内部会引入未被利用的额外微结构。

Method: 提出一种结构利用的LU分解算法，不仅利用块对角结构，还挖掘每个块内部的微结构，并将该算法集成到Riccati递归中。

Result: 在随机线性系统和两个最优控制问题实例上的数值结果表明，搜索方向的计算时间最多可减少20%，最大加速出现在相对零块大小约为0.25且阶段级等式约束雅可比矩阵较大的情况下。

Conclusion: 利用块内微结构能进一步加速求解器性能，尤其在某些特定结构特征（如中等零块比例和大等式约束雅可比矩阵）下效果显著。

Abstract: Optimal Control Problems (OCPs) have been applied to a variety of applications. Such problems are often transcribed into a Nonlinear Program (NLP) for which solution methods typically solve a Linear Quadratic (LQ) subproblem. To efficiently solve this LQ subproblem, the Riccati recursion exploits the block-diagonal structure that arises from discretizing an OCP using a Multiple Shooting method. However, reformulating general dynamics constraints to fit the required problem structure to use the Riccati recursion introduces additional micro-structure within these block matrices that remains unexploited. This work demonstrates how structure-exploiting solvers such as Fatrop can benefit from exploiting not only the block-diagonal structure but also the micro-structure within those blocks. We propose a structure-exploiting LU decomposition algorithm and integrate it within the Riccati recursion. Numerical results on randomized linear systems and two OCP examples demonstrate a reduction in computation time of the search direction of up to 20%, with the largest speedup observed for relative zero block sizes around 0.25 and large stage-wise equality constraint jacobians.

</details>


### [136] [Nechvile-Transformed Spacecraft Dynamics and Propellant Computation in the 3-Body Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29271)
*Michael J. Dixon,Isaac M. Ross*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明在Nechvile框架下的限制性三体问题中，添加外部力并非简单地直接加到加速度方程上，而是必须乘以(1 + e cosθ)^-3的因子。这一发现与质量流率方程结合后产生多个意外结果，并导致火箭方程本身需要修正。通过Birkhoff理论求解地月空间任务算例，表明使用正确的代价泛函可节省80%的推进剂，而常用的二次代价函数所需的推进剂消耗是最小值的两倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者指出，尽管Nechvile框架下限制性三体问题的无控运动方程自1960年代以来已为人熟知，但以往人们错误地认为添加外部力只需简单地在加速度方程中加入"单位质量外力"项。作者旨在纠正这一错误认识，并揭示其引发的对火箭方程和最优燃料消耗问题的连锁影响。

Method: 作者从Nechvile框架下的运动方程出发，通过严格的数学推导证明通用外部力项必须乘以(1 + e cosθ)^-3的因子（其中e为主天体相对偏心率，θ为位于质心的旋转坐标系真近点角）。进一步将该结果与质量流率方程结合，分析二次项与三次项之间的不匹配效应，并采用Birkhoff理论方法对示例地月空间任务进行求解。

Result: 推导得出外部力的正确加权因子为(1 + e cosθ)^-3；该结果导致火箭方程需要相应修改。针对地月空间任务算例的Birkhoff理论求解显示：使用正确代价泛函可比常用方法节省80%的推进剂，而广泛使用的二次代价函数所需的推进剂消耗超过最小值的2倍。

Conclusion: 在限制性三体问题的Nechvile框架中，外部力必须乘以(1 + e cosθ)^-3因子才是正确的处理方式。错误的外部力建模会因二次项与三次项不匹配而严重高估推进剂需求。研究者若希望设计推进剂最优的空间任务，必须采用经修正的代价函数和修正后的火箭方程。

Abstract: The uncontrolled equations of motion in the Nechvile frame for the restricted three-body problem have been well-known since at least the 1960s. It would seem that adding an external force to these equations is quite trivial: simply add an external force per mass term to the acceleration equations. Here we show that the last statement is not true. In fact, we show that the additive generic external force must be multiplied by the inverse of $(1 + e \cosθ)^3$ where $e$ is the relative eccentricity of the primaries and $θ$ is the true anomaly of the rotating frame located at the barycenter. Furthermore, when this result is combined with the mass flow rate equation, it generates several surprising results due to the mismatch between the resulting quadratic term and the cubic term in the equations of motion. This leads to a corresponding modification of the rocket equation itself. A Birkhoff-theoretic solution to an illustrative cislunar space mission problem shows propellent savings of 80% with the use of the correct cost functional. The popular quadratic cost utilizes more than $2X$ the minimum propellant consumption.

</details>


### [137] [Value of Information in Dynamic Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29273)
*Dimitry Shaiderman,Eilon Solan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究有限马尔可夫链状态预测中信息的价值问题。决策者每阶段选择状态并仅观察当前状态是否与选择匹配，利用累积信息对最终阶段状态进行预测。研究发现从平稳分布开始时，最优终值随观测次数非降并以均匀指数速度收敛；对非平稳初始分布，值可能随时域严格递减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在有限马尔可夫链演化预测中，观测信息的获取如何影响最终预测价值，特别是从平稳分布与非平稳初始分布两种情形下的价值行为差异。

Method: 建立决策模型：每阶段决策者选择状态、观测0-1匹配信号、利用信息预测终态。从两个视角分析：平稳初始分布下的单调收敛性分析与指数收敛速度刻画；引入预测学习指数（predictive learning index）刻画有限次观测后能否提取全部信息价值，并证明有限与无限指数均可能出现。

Result: （1）平稳分布下最优终值随观测数非降且均匀指数收敛；（2）预测学习指数可有限也可无限；（3）非平稳初始分布下值可能随时域严格递减。

Conclusion: 信息价值在平稳设置下具有良好的单调收敛性质，但在非平稳条件下可能出现反直觉的价值递减现象，提示初始分布对信息学习过程具有本质影响。

Abstract: We study the value of information in predicting the evolving state of a finite Markov chain. At each stage, a decision maker chooses a state and observes only whether the current state of the chain matches her choice; the resulting information is used to make a prediction on the state at the final stage of the problem. We show that, when the chain starts from an invariant distribution, the optimal terminal value is non-decreasing with the number of observations and converges at a uniform exponential rate. We introduce the predictive learning index, which measures whether all attainable informational value is extracted after finitely many observations, and show that both finite and infinite indices may occur. In contrast, for nonstationary initial distributions, the value may strictly decrease with the horizon.

</details>


### [138] [Regularized Coupling Maps on Matrix Polytopes: From Assignment to Graph Matching and Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29299)
*Binrui Shen,Shengxin Zhu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了线性分配、图匹配和最优传输三类经典问题中正则化耦合映射的几何与算法框架，区分了熵/KL正则化与Frobenius/欧几里得正则化两类方法，并展示了它们在图匹配和最优传输中的互补算法工具（如CSGO、ASM、FRAM、IP-EOT、PSN）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性分配、图匹配和最优传输三类问题共享一个共同的数学结构——在矩阵多面体上寻找最优的矩阵值耦合。作者希望通过统一的视角来理解正则化耦合映射，揭示不同正则化几何如何产生互补的算法工具，从而为图匹配和最优传输提供统一的框架性认识。

Method: 作者将正则化耦合映射分为两类：熵/KL正则化（通过对输入得分矩阵取指数并进行KL投影到相关矩阵多面体）和Frobenius/欧几里得正则化（对缩放后的输入矩阵执行欧几里得投影到同一多面体）。论文追溯了这两种几何在各自任务中的算法体现。

Result: 正则化耦合映射在图匹配中作为方向生成器发挥作用，在最优传输中作为解表示。正则化器决定了几何性质，而外层任务决定了精度标准。文章建立了统一的分析框架，覆盖了图匹配中的CSGO、ASM、FRAM算法和最优传输中的IP-EOT、PSN算法。

Conclusion: 正则化耦合映射为三类经典问题提供了统一的数学框架，熵/KL与Frobenius/欧几里得两种几何路径互补且各具优势，正则化器的选择决定算法的几何性质，外层任务决定精度需求，这一视角有助于指导未来算法的设计与选择。

Abstract: Three classical problems-linear assignment, graph matching, and optimal transport-share a common structure: they seek an optimal matrix-valued coupling on a matrix polytope. We study regularized coupling maps that encompass two families of regularization: entropy/KL regularization, which exponentiates the input score matrix and projects it onto the relevant matrix polytope via the KL-projection; and the Frobenius/Euclidean regularization, which performs the Euclidean projection of the scaled input matrix onto the same polytope. We trace how these two geometries yield complementary algorithmic tools for graph matching (CSGO, ASM, FRAM) and optimal transport (IP-EOT, PSN). The regularized coupling map serves as a direction generator in graph matching and as a solution representation in optimal transport, with the regularizer determining the geometry and the outer task determining the precision standard.

</details>


### [139] [Negative-Curvature-Informed L-BFGS via Minimal Secant Corrections for Finite Minimax Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29300)
*Wenzhe Zhao*

Main category: math.OC
