TL;DR: 本文研究用于无约束有限极小极大问题的限内存拟牛顿方法。通过截断双曲光滑模型处理非光滑max函数，L-BFGS的曲率对仅在检测到真正负曲率时被修正，提出了三种修正规则（以-y_k替换y_k、欧氏最近点问题、B_k^{-1}度量下最近点问题），均保证正的割线曲率，无需特征分解、Krylov负曲率搜索或各向同性位移。证明了正定性保持、充分下降性质、Armijo回退有限终止以及Clarke平稳性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限极小极大问题的非光滑性给拟牛顿方法带来挑战，传统L-BFGS在负曲率存在时可能失效或需要昂贵的修正手段（如特征分解、Krylov搜索等）。作者希望设计一种简单、无需昂贵计算即可保持L-BFGS正定性和收敛性的修正机制，并提供一个可统一比较多种修正策略的框架。

Method: 采用截断双曲光滑函数近似非光滑max；L-BFGS曲率对仅在检测到严格负曲率时修正。提出三种修正规则：规则一直接令y_k=-y_k；规则二通过欧氏最近点问题求解；规则三通过B_k^{-1}度量下的最近点问题求解。三种规则均强制执行s_k^T ỹ_k=|s_k^T y_k|，之后使用标准BFGS更新和标准L-BFGS两循环递归，不需要特征分解、Krylov搜索或各向同性位移。

Result: 证明了每个被接受的曲率对保持正定性；在显式度量和光滑性假设下建立了充分下降性质和Armijo回退的有限终止性；推导了连续化框架下的全局Clarke平稳性结果；并证明在足够强凸邻域内三种修正均不激活，局部退化为普通L-BFGS。

Conclusion: 该工作将负曲率修正的算法框架与具体修正策略分离，允许在相同光滑、线搜索和内存规则下公平比较三种变体。方法保持了L-BFGS的简洁性和效率，同时具备理论保证，是一种实用且可扩展的有限极小极大问题求解方案。

Abstract: We study a limited-memory quasi-Newton method for unconstrained finite minimax problems. The nonsmooth maximum is handled by a truncated hyperbolic smoothing model, while the curvature pairs used by L-BFGS are modified only when a genuine negative-curvature observation is detected. Three correction rules are developed. The first replaces $y_k$ by $-y_k$; the second is obtained from the Euclidean nearest-point problem; and the third is obtained from the nearest-point problem in the $B_k^{-1}$ metric. All three rules enforce the same positive secant curvature $s_k^T\ytilde_k=|s_k^Ty_k|$ on a strict negative-curvature pair. The standard BFGS update and the standard L-BFGS two-loop recursion are then used without any change. In particular, the method does not require an eigendecomposition, a Krylov negative-curvature search, or an isotropic shift $B_k+μ_kI$. We prove that every accepted pair preserves positive definiteness, establish the sufficient-descent property and finite termination of the Armijo backtracking under explicit metric and smoothing assumptions, and derive a global Clarke-stationarity result for the continuation framework. We also show that all three corrections become inactive in a sufficiently strongly convex neighborhood, so the local method reduces to ordinary L-BFGS. The paper separates the common algorithmic framework from the choice of the negative-curvature correction, which allows the three variants to be compared under identical smoothing, line-search, and memory rules.

</details>


### [140] [Strike Price Optimization for ISO New England's Day-Ahead Ancillary Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29366)
*Karl Zhu,Parviz Alivand,Jinye Zhao,Tongxin Zheng,Dimitris Bertsimas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对ISO新英格兰日前辅助服务计划中储备产品的行权价设定问题，提出了一个优化框架：在确保足够资源有激励准备并共同满足储备要求的前提下，选择最高可行行权价。通过推导紧的下界证书（利用条件价格分布和资源价格影响的界），证明每个资源通过单一激励阈值进入优化，任意有限最优行权价出现在这些阈值之一，从而得到基于阈值计算和少量线性可行性检验的精确求解方法。实测表明高热耗率燃气轮机比联合循环资源更可能约束行权价选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ISO-NE将储备产品结算为基于实时能源价格的金融看涨期权，系统级行权价产生了效率-可靠性权衡：提高行权价虽降低竞争性储备报价，但削弱资源承担准备成本和保持实时可用性的激励。现有行权价规则未显式考虑异构资源激励和储备需求，需要系统化的优化方法来解决这一权衡。

Method: 构建优化框架以选择最高可行行权价，约束条件确保足够资源保持准备激励并满足储备需求。由于资源的准备决策可能影响实时价格分布，其激励依赖于不可观测的反事实，作者推导了一个仅使用可用条件价格分布和资源价格影响上界的紧下界证书。证明每个资源通过单一激励阈值进入优化，有限最优行权价必在这些阈值之一，由此将问题简化为阈值计算加少量线性可行性检验。

Result: 基于重构的ISO-NE条件价格分布和代表性燃气资源进行实证，发现高热耗率燃气轮机比联合循环资源更可能约束行权价的选择。

Conclusion: 论文提供了关于ISO-NE储备结算行权价的第一个可计算的精确优化方法，解决了因不可观测反事实导致的激励评估困难，为系统运营方提供了实用的行权价设定工具，并揭示了资源类型（热耗率）对行权价约束的影响规律。

Abstract: ISO New England's (ISO-NE) Day-Ahead Ancillary Services Initiative settles reserve products as financial call options on real-time energy prices. The system-wide strike price creates an efficiency-reliability tradeoff: increasing it lowers competitive reserve offers, but weakens resources' incentives to incur preparation costs and remain available for real-time performance. The existing strike price rule does not explicitly account for heterogeneous resource incentives and reserve requirements. We develop an optimization framework that selects the highest strike price while ensuring that enough resources retain an incentive to prepare and collectively satisfy the reserve requirements. Because a resource's preparation decision may affect the resulting real-time price distribution, its incentive depends on an unobservable counterfactual. To address this, we derive a tight lower-bound certificate using only the available conditional price distribution and a bound on the resource's price impact. We show that each resource enters the optimization through a single incentive threshold and that any finite optimal strike price occurs at one of these thresholds. This yields a tractable exact solution method based on threshold calculations and a small number of linear feasibility checks. Using reconstructed ISO-NE conditional price distributions and representative gas-fired resources, we find that higher heat-rate combustion turbines are more likely than combined-cycle resources to constrain the strike price choice.

</details>


### [141] [The method of ellipcenters with momentum and relaxation for convex quadratic minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29454)
*Roger Behling,Vincent Guigues,Harry Oviedo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了椭中心法（ME）的两种变体：RelaxME和MomME，前者在每个迭代加入松弛步，后者在ME中嵌入动量。两者均被证明在线性收敛意义上收敛，并且当二次型矩阵仅有两个不同特征值时，ME、RelaxME和MomME都能在一轮迭代内收敛。数值实验表明MomME在大多数问题上迭代次数最少，而共轭梯度法和MomME的CPU时间最少且相近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 椭中心法（ME）是一种仅依赖一阶信息、通过构建二维椭圆来捕捉问题内在病态性的无约束极小化新技术，此前已证明在光滑强凸目标函数下线性收敛。本文旨在进一步改进ME：通过引入松弛步和动量机制来加速其收敛性能，并验证其在实际数值优化问题中的竞争力。

Method: 提出两种ME变体：RelaxME在每次迭代中添加一个额外的松弛步骤，MomME将动量嵌入到ME框架中。针对二次目标函数的特殊情形，从理论上证明两者的线性收敛性，并揭示当二次型矩阵只有两个不同特征值时三种方法均可一步收敛。随后在五个优化问题及不同参数组合下进行数值对比实验。

Result: RelaxME和MomME均收敛且为线性收敛；当二次型矩阵仅有两个不同特征值时，ME、RelaxME和MomME均能一步收敛。数值实验表明，在大多数测试实例中MomME迭代次数最少，共轭梯度法与MomME的CPU时间最小且相似。

Conclusion: 带动量的ME（MomME）在迭代次数上表现最优，且与共轭梯度法在计算效率上相当，这为ME在更广泛场景中结合动量机制提供了乐观前景。

Abstract: The method of ellipcenters (ME) is a recent technique developed for unconstrained minimization. Its iteration relies only on first order information and consists of building a suitable two-dimensional ellipse that tries to capture intrinsic ill-conditioning of the problem. The center of the ellipse is taken as the next iterate, which justifies the name of the method. ME was already shown to converge linearly when the objective function is smooth and strongly convex. The special case when the objective is quadratic was studied in the first paper on ME and is also the subject of our work here. In this paper, we propose two variants of ME: RelaxME which adds in ME an additional relaxation step at each iteration and MomME which embeds momentum in ME. We prove linear convergence of RelaxME and MomME and, in particular, we show convergence of RelaxME and MomME (and also of ME) in one iteration when the matrix of the quadratic form has only two distinct eigenvalues. Finally, we provide the results of numerical experiments which compare on five optimization problems (and different combinations of parameters defining these problems) ME, RelaxME, and MomME, with 3 other optimizers: the conjugate gradient method [10], Barzilai and Borwein gradient method with long step [2], and the gradient method with adaptive spectral step length [7]. On most instances, MomME provides the smallest number of iterations and conjugate gradient and MomME provide the smallest CPU times and similar CPU times. This opens optimistic possibilities for ME with momentum in broader settings.

</details>


### [142] [Stochastic Optimal Control of Hawkes Jump-Diffusion Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29473)
*Daria Sakhanda,Joshué Helí Ricalde-Guerrero*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文构建了带环境风险的随机增长模型框架，将灾害建模为标记Hawkes过程（允许过往灾害暂时提高后续冲击概率），在次临界马尔可夫Hawkes设定下证明受控动力学方程的适定性与非爆炸性，并通过小激励缩放证明Hawkes值函数收敛于Poisson对应值函数，从而给出Poisson近似的严格理论支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机增长模型通常使用Poisson点过程建模环境灾害冲击，但现实中灾害发生率往往具有自激效应（短暂提高后续灾害概率）。作者旨在将这一现实特征纳入随机增长模型，并为Hawkes过程框架下的随机控制问题提供严格的理论基础。

Method: 基于arXiv:2511.13568中的Poisson点过程公式，将灾害冲击扩展为带标记的Hawkes过程；采用次临界马尔可夫Hawkes设定，通过增广状态空间纳入自激分量；利用Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程刻画相应的Poisson控制问题，结合小激励缩放机制证明渐近收敛性。

Result: 建立了受控Hawkes动力学的适定性和非爆炸性；得到了Hawkes激励过程的定量估计；在小激励缩放下证明Hawkes值函数收敛到Poisson值函数，量化了自激效应对最优增长的影响。

Conclusion: 该框架为灾害自激效应下的随机增长模型提供了严格的理论保障，证明了在小自激幅度下Hawkes模型可被Poisson模型可靠近似，从而能够量化环境风险自激机制对最优经济增长的影响程度。

Abstract: This paper is devoted to developing a framework for stochastic growth models with environmental risk, in which rare but catastrophic shocks interact with capital accumulation and pollution. Building on the Poisson point process formulation studied in arXiv:2511.13568, we extend the model to disasters driven by a marked Hawkes process, allowing past disasters to temporarily increase the likelihood of subsequent shocks. Our work focuses on a subcritical Markovian Hawkes specification, in which the state space is augmented by the self-excitation component of disaster risk. We establish the well-posedness and nonexplosion of the resulting controlled Hawkes dynamics. Using the Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman characterization of the corresponding Poisson control problem, we obtain quantitative estimates for the Hawkes excitation process and prove, under a small-excitation scaling, that the Hawkes value function converges to its Poisson counterpart as the magnitude of self-excitation vanishes. This provides a rigorous Poisson approximation of the stochastic control problem and quantifies the effect of self-excitation on optimal growth under environmental disaster risk.

</details>


### [143] [Proof of a Conjecture of De Cock and De Moor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29479)
*Jeffrey Humpherys*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在非共振条件下，给出了De Cock和De Moor关于随机子空间辨识中两个不同视角（Lyapunov方程与主夹角/典型相关性）之间猜想的一个直接有限维证明。证明不要求稳定性或可对角化性，核心机制是秩一扰动暴露隐藏的Cauchy矩阵结构，将问题归结为有理插值，并通过密度与连续性论证去除一般谱假设。结果强化了原猜想：特征值按代数重数一致、原表述中的非奇异假设自动成立，且在参数的稠密开集上两个矩阵相似而非仅同谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: De Cock和De Moor提出的猜想连接了随机子空间辨识中两种看似不同的数学视角：一种基于Lyapunov方程，另一种基于主夹角与典型相关性。该猜想被记录为《数学系统与控制理论中的未解问题》中的问题9.1，长期未获证明。本文旨在给出该猜想的直接证明并进一步强化其结论。

Method: 本文采用秩一扰动作为关键机制，揭示问题中隐藏的Cauchy矩阵结构，从而将原问题约化为有理插值问题。在自然的非共振条件下，直接给出有限维证明，无需稳定性或可对角化性假设。随后通过密度和连续性论证，将一般谱假设下的结果推广到更广泛的情形。

Result: 本文在非共振条件下证明了该猜想，并强化了原始表述：特征值按代数重数一致；原表述中的非奇异假设被证明是自动成立的；在参数的稠密开集上，两个矩阵不仅是同谱的，实际上是相似的。文中还提到Gillberg和Löfberg独立给出了一种基于Lyapunov核恒等式和经典AB-BA原理的证明，但本文证明独立开发且采用不同路径。

Conclusion: 本文成功解决了记录于《未解问题》中的猜想9.1，给出的证明方法（秩一扰动、Cauchy矩阵结构、有理插值、密度连续性论证）独立于已有的Lyapunov核方法，并将原猜想从同谱性强化到相似性，是该领域的一个重要理论进展。

Abstract: De Cock and De Moor proposed a conjecture connecting two seemingly different viewpoints in stochastic subspace identification, one based on Lyapunov equations and the other on principal angles and canonical correlations. The conjecture was recorded as Problem 9.1 of \emph{Unsolved Problems in Mathematical Systems and Control Theory}. We give a direct finite-dimensional proof under the natural nonresonance condition, without requiring stability or diagonalizability. The key mechanism is the rank-one perturbation, which exposes a hidden Cauchy-matrix structure and reduces the problem to rational interpolation. A density and continuity argument then removes the generic spectral assumptions. The result strengthens the original statement. The eigenvalues agree with algebraic multiplicity, a nonsingularity assumption of the original formulation becomes automatic, and on a dense open set of parameters the two matrices are similar rather than merely cospectral. While this manuscript was being prepared, Gillberg and Löfberg independently posted a proof based on a Lyapunov-kernel identity and the classical $AB$--$BA$ principle. The proof given here was developed independently and follows a different route.

</details>


### [144] [Online Gate-Driven Flow Control in Resin Transfer Moulding Using a Neural-Network Surrogate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29521)
*Nicholas Wright,Oliver Maclaren,Piaras Kelly,Suresh Advani,Ruanui Nicholson*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合状态估计与压力控制的策略，用于树脂传递模塑（RTM）过程中防止干斑形成。通过迭代扩展卡尔曼滤波（IEKF）利用压力传感器数据估计赛跑效应（race-tracking）强度，同时优化辅助浇口压力，并采用神经网络代理模型结合贝叶斯近似误差框架实现实时计算。在叉形几何上验证表明该框架能显著减少填充结束时的未填充节点数，但改善程度强烈依赖辅助浇口的位置和数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在RTM过程中，必须使纤维预成型体完全饱和才能防止干斑缺陷，但实际中流动前沿因赛跑效应（race-tracking）难以均匀推进，导致树脂过早到达排气口并形成干斑。现有控制方法要么假设赛跑效应已知，要么无法实时处理，因此需要同时估计未知参数并优化控制的联合策略。

Method: (1) 采用迭代扩展卡尔曼滤波从压力传感器数据中在线估计未知的赛跑效应强度；(2) 同时优化辅助浇口压力，防止树脂在完全饱和前到达排气口；(3) 用神经网络代理模型替代昂贵的有限元-控制体积模型以实现实时计算；(4) 利用贝叶斯近似误差框架处理代理模型与原模型之间的偏差；(5) 通过数学证明流动前沿几何仅取决于时间平均浇口压力，从而使代理模型训练在时变控制下可行。

Result: 在代表性叉形几何上的演示表明，联合估计-控制框架相比无控制基线能大幅减少填充结束时的未填充节点数量。但改善程度强烈依赖于辅助浇口的位置和数量。

Conclusion: 提出的联合估计与控制策略在RTM过程中有效降低了干斑风险，但辅助浇口的位置和数量是决定控制效果的关键因素，提示在实际应用中需要合理设计辅助浇口布局。

Abstract: In resin transfer moulding, complete saturation of the fibre preform is necessary before the resin front reaches the outlet vent(s), to prevent dry-spot formation. In practice, the flow front rarely advances uniformly due to race-tracking effects. We propose a combined estimation and control strategy to address this issue. We use pressure-sensor data collected during filling to estimate the unknown race-tracking strengths via an iterated extended Kalman filter, and to simultaneously optimise auxiliary gate pressures to prevent the resin from arriving at the vent before complete saturation occurs. To make this approach feasible in real time, we replace the expensive finite element-control volume model with neural network surrogate models. The Bayesian approximation error framework is used to account for the discrepancy between the surrogates and the original model. To make the training of the surrogates feasible with time-dependent control actions, we prove that the flow-front geometry depends only on the time-averaged gate pressures. We demonstrate the methodology on a representative forked geometry, showing that the combined estimation-and-control framework can substantially reduce the number of unfilled nodes at the end of filling compared with an uncontrolled baseline. However, the level of improvement depends strongly on the location and number of auxiliary gates.

</details>


### [145] [Quiver Semistability and Structured Kalman Decompositions for Networked Linear Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29871)
*Kazuo Murota,Tasuku Soma*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文基于箭图表示论中的σ-半稳定性，提出了网络化线性时不变（LTI）系统新的可控性与可观测性概念，并利用King准则定义了网络化的Kalman分解，同时给出了高效判定算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Kalman分解和可控性/可观测性理论未考虑系统内部互联结构，而网络化LTI系统的局部动力与互联动力需要分别处理，现有方法难以在保持网络拓扑的前提下系统分解系统。

Method: 利用箭图表示中的σ-半稳定性概念及King准则，将网络化LTI系统的结构性可控性/可观测性与网络代数结构联系起来；建立基于该框架的Kalman型分解，并针对带自环的无环箭图设计高效判定算法（在权重σ对所有含自环顶点同号时）。

Result: 提出了网络级别的一般性Kalman分解，系统地分离局部动力与互联动力且尊重底层网络结构；给出了判定所提可控性与可观测性以及求解Kalman型分解的高效算法，在特定权重条件下对σ-半稳定性判定也提出了高效算法。

Conclusion: 该工作将箭图表示论与网络化控制理论相结合，为网络化LTI系统的结构分析与分解提供了新的代数框架和实用算法工具。

Abstract: We introduce new notions of controllability and observability for networked linear time-invariant (LTI) systems based on $σ$-semistability of quiver representations. Utilizing King's criterion for $σ$-semistability, we define a network generalization of the Kalman decomposition for networked LTI systems, which systematically decomposes the local and interconnection dynamics while respecting the underlying network structure. Furthermore, we present efficient algorithms for deciding the proposed controllability and observability of a given networked LTI system and for finding the Kalman-type decomposition. We also show efficient algorithms for deciding the $σ$-semistability of representations of acyclic quivers with self-loops if the weight $σ$ has the same sign for all vertices with self-loops. Such quiver representations and weights arise from networked LTI systems.

</details>


### [146] [Reciprocal-Manifold Annealed KKT Flows for Constrained Optimization: Application to the Nonconvex AC Optimal Power Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29628)
*M Parimi,Aditi Ramteke,Rachit Mehra,Arun Mahindrakar,Navdeep Singh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种连续时间优化框架，通过互惠乘子流形实现平滑约束非线性优化问题在迭代全过程中的严格可行性保持，无需投影算子或二次规划子问题，计算复杂度从立方降至线性，并在IEEE 9节点与57节点AC最优潮流问题中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键型优化应用（如实时电力系统运行）要求在每一步迭代中保持可行性，而非仅收敛时可行。现有方法要么在求解过程中违反约束（如内点法），要么通过每时刻的二次规划子问题强制可行性，但后者具有立方的计算代价和不可控的最坏情况执行时间。因此亟需一种既能全程保持可行性又具有低计算复杂度的优化方法。

Method: 提出基于互惠乘子流形（reciprocal multiplier manifold）的连续时间优化框架。该方法在不等式约束与其对应拉格朗日乘子之间建立显式关系，通过设计连续乘子更新律使流形保持前向不变，所得动力学等价于连续时间对数障碍梯度下降。框架自然扩展到多不等式约束、等式约束、非凸可行集和不可行初始条件，并通过增广Uzawa流消除经典原始-对偶鞍点动力学中的振荡瞬态。

Result: 在IEEE 9节点和IEEE 57节点系统的AC最优潮流问题上，数值结果显示收敛到基准最优解的0.4%以内，同时严格保持所有约束的可行性。计算复杂度分析表明，所提动力学将每步计算代价从立方降至线性。时变工况下的动态跟踪研究验证了可靠性的可行性保持能力。

Conclusion: 该连续时间优化框架在保持全程严格可行性的同时大幅降低了计算复杂度，为安全关键实时优化应用提供了有效且高效的解决方案。未来可进一步扩展到更广泛的工程优化场景。

Abstract: Safety-critical optimization applications, such as real-time power system operation, maintain feasibility at every intermediate step, not merely at convergence. Existing approaches either violate constraints mid-solve (interior-point methods) or enforce feasibility through per-instant quadratic programming subproblems with cubic computational cost and unbounded worst-case execution time. We propose a continuous-time optimization framework for smooth constrained nonlinear problems that preserves feasibility throughout the optimization process without requiring projection operators, quadratic programming subproblems, or other per-iteration optimization routines.
  The method is built around a reciprocal multiplier manifold, which establishes an explicit relationship between inequality constraints and their associated Lagrange multipliers. By designing a continuous multiplier update law, the manifold is shown to remain forward invariant, while the resulting dynamics are equivalent to continuous-time logarithmic barrier gradient descent. The proposed framework naturally extends to multiple inequality constraints, equality constraints, nonconvex feasible sets, and infeasible initial conditions. The method is further enhanced through an augmented Uzawa flow that eliminates oscillatory transients commonly observed in classical primal-dual saddle-point dynamics.
  The effectiveness of the proposed approach is applied to the AC Optimal Power Flow problem of IEEE 9-bus and IEEE 57-bus systems. Numerical results show convergence to solutions within 0.4\% of the benchmark optimum while maintaining strict feasibility of all constraints. A computational complexity analysis shows that the proposed dynamics reduce the per-step computational cost from cubic to linear complexity. Finally, dynamic tracking studies under time-varying operating conditions demonstrate reliable feasibility preservation.

</details>


### [147] [Transformer-Based Flow Shop Scheduling Using MILP-Generated Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29690)
*Roderich Wallrath*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用Transformer模型解决带次级资源的非置换流水车间调度问题。将MILP生成的调度方案分词化为next-token预测任务，通过约束解码完成前缀序列。在8个作业、4台机器、3种次级资源的流水车间上，Transformer的解质量优于遗传算法、NEH启发式和随机搜索，仅逊于MILP和迭代贪婪（IG）启发式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统运筹学方法在解决复杂调度问题时常面临计算瓶颈，而机器学习特别是Transformer模型能够捕捉token序列中的复杂交互，为调度问题提供了新的求解思路。本文旨在探索Transformer作为非置换流水车间调度（含次级资源）求解器的可行性。

Method: 将非置换流水车间调度建模为next-token预测任务，token表示作业-机器-次级资源三元组。使用MILP生成的最优/近优调度方案进行token化作为训练数据，推理阶段随机生成部分token前缀，通过约束解码由训练好的Transformer完成序列生成。

Result: 在8作业、4机器、3次级资源的流水车间实验中，Transformer的makespan优于遗传算法（GA）、NEH启发式和随机搜索，但性能不如MILP模型和迭代贪婪（IG）启发式。

Conclusion: Transformer模型能够在一定程度上从MILP优化的非置换流水车间调度中学习规律，基于Transformer的调度方法在固定、重复作业集场景下是值得进一步探索的研究方向。

Abstract: Advances in machine learning (ML) have created new opportunities to complement traditional operations research (OR) methods. In particular, transformer models can capture complex interactions in token sequences by mapping tokens into a high-dimensional embedding space and propagating contextual information via attention. This makes them a candidate to model non-permutation flow shop scheduling with secondary resources as a next-token prediction task, where tokens represent job-machine-secondary resource tuples. For training, mixed-integer linear programming (MILP)-generated schedules are tokenized and used as next-token prediction data. During inference, partial token sequences (prefixes) are randomly generated and completed by the trained transformer through constrained decoding. A computational study is conducted on a flow shop with 8 jobs, 4 machines, and 3 secondary resources, where jobs are selected from a fixed pool of 20 jobs that is sampled during training and provides the candidates during prefix completion. The transformer achieves better solution quality (smaller makespans) compared to a genetic algorithm (GA), the NEH heuristic, and random search. It is outperformed only by the MILP model and the iterated greedy (IG) heuristic. The study concludes that transformer models can, to some extent, learn patterns from MILP-optimized non-permutation flow shop schedules and that transformer-based scheduling represents an interesting direction for future research, particularly in settings with a fixed, recurring job set.

</details>


### [148] [Successive design of backstepping observers for parabolic PDE-ODE systems and its duality to state feedback stabilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29693)
*Nicole Gehring*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对严格前馈抛物型PDE-ODE系统的连续反推观测器设计方法，利用耦合结构决定误差稳定化顺序，并通过虚拟测量变换和Volterra积分变换将误差动态映射为指数稳定的级联ODE/PDE子系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对严格前馈结构的抛物型PDE-ODE系统，现有观测器设计方法尚不完善，需要一种系统化的设计框架来稳定观测器误差动态。

Method: 采用逐级反推（successive backstepping）方法：首先基于虚拟测量构造变换，稳定距离测量最远的ODE观测器误差子系统并使其与PDE误差状态解耦；随后利用Volterra积分变换稳定PDE误差子系统，将整体误差动态映射为指数稳定的级联ODE和PDE子系统。

Result: 所提出的观测器设计被证明与近期提出的严格反馈形式PDE-ODE系统的多步状态反馈设计互为对偶，从而阐明了该观测器设计的结构合理性。

Conclusion: 该工作通过对偶性将已有状态反馈设计方法推广到观测器设计领域，为严格前馈PDE-ODE系统的状态估计提供了系统化的反推设计框架。

Abstract: The paper introduces a successive backstepping observer design for strictly feedforward parabolic PDE-ODE systems, in which the coupling structure determines the order of error stabilization and the corresponding transformations. First, a transformation based on a virtual measurement stabilizes the ODE observer error subsystem, which is most distal from the measurement, while decoupling it from the PDE error state. Second, a Volterra integral transformation is employed to stabilize the PDE error subsystem and to map the overall error dynamics into a cascade of exponentially stable ODE and PDE subsystems. The design is shown to be dual to a recently proposed multi-step state feedback design for parabolic PDE-ODE systems in strict feedback form, thus explaining the structure of the presented observer design.

</details>


### [149] [A Unified Perspective on Conformal Prediction and Wasserstein Distributionally Robust Optimization for Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29789)
*Kehan Long,Yiqi Zhao,Pol Mestres,Lars Lindemann,Nikolay Atanasov,Jorge Cortés*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文从统一概率视角分析保形预测（CP）和分布鲁棒优化（DRO），将两者视为将有限校准数据转化为数据依赖的分位数估计器的两种方式：CP通过膨胀分位数水平（level-space）校正，DRO通过移动分位数数值（value-space）校正。两者提供相同的校准条件保证，但在分数分布尾部表现不同——CP依赖稀疏的上尾次序统计量，在样本稀疏时可能过度校正，而DRO的分位数值空间校正可避免此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限数据下的不确定性量化对机器学习、优化和自动化系统至关重要。CP和DRO是两种互补的思路，但缺乏统一的理论框架来理解它们的异同。作者希望建立一个统一的概率视角，从而清晰地对比两种方法在校准条件保证、构造方式和尾部行为上的差异，特别是为何DRO在某些尾部场景下可能优于CP。

Method: 作者将CP和DRO统一为数据依赖的分位数估计器。具体地，CP在分位数水平（level）维度上进行校正——通过保形得分排序来膨胀有效覆盖水平；DRO在分位数数值（value）维度上进行校正——通过经验分布周围的模糊半径移动分位数值。两种方法都在同一估计器族中沿两个坐标校正经验分位数。作者进一步分析了两种方法的保证类型（校准条件保证）、构造差异（闭式vs依赖未知分布）以及尾部行为差异。

Result: CP和DRO提供相同的校准条件下覆盖保证（需目标覆盖以高概率在校准样本上成立）。CP使用闭式、分布自由的水平校正；DRO使用值空间校正，其认证半径依赖未知分布的性质，且额外保证在模糊集上均匀覆盖。在分数分布尾部，CP的水平膨胀在样本在目标分位数附近密集时几乎不动，但在样本稀疏时过度调整；而恰当的DRO半径在值空间校正可避免这种过冲。

Conclusion: CP和DRO并非无关的方法，而是同一分位数估计器族中沿两个互补坐标的校正策略。理解这一统一视角有助于在实际问题中根据分数分布尾部特征选择更合适的方法：当尾部样本稀疏时，DRO的值空间校正可能比CP的水平膨胀更稳健。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification from finite data is central to machine learning, optimization, and automation systems, where decisions must remain reliable under limited samples and test-time distribution shift. Conformal prediction (CP) and distributionally robust optimization (DRO) offer two complementary approaches: CP constructs data-dependent prediction sets with distribution-free finite-sample validity under exchangeability, while DRO optimizes worst-case performance over an ambiguity set around an empirical distribution. We develop a unified probabilistic perspective on CP and DRO by viewing both as ways to turn finite calibration data into a data-dependent quantile estimator that a test score falls below with high probability. From this perspective, CP and DRO correct the empirical quantile along two coordinates of the same family of estimators: CP inflates the quantile level, whereas DRO shifts the quantile value through an ambiguity radius. Both methods provide the same calibration-conditional guarantee for the true distribution, requiring the target coverage to hold with high probability over the calibration sample. Their constructions differ, however: CP uses a closed-form, distribution-free level correction, while DRO uses a value-space correction whose certified radius depends on properties of the unknown distribution and additionally guarantees coverage uniformly over the ambiguity set. This distinction emerges in the tails of the score distribution. Because CP relies on sparse upper-tail order statistics of the calibration samples, its level inflation barely moves the estimator when those samples are dense near the target quantile but overshoots when they are sparse, whereas a well-chosen DRO radius corrects in value space and may avoid this overshoot.

</details>


### [150] [Sharp Singularity-Degree Bounds for Equality-Generated SDP-RLT Relaxations of Binary Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29945)
*Hao Hu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了二元集合的Shor松弛添加首层RLT方程后的SDP-RLT松弛的奇异性度，确定了其精确的最坏情况奇异性度：当rank(A)=m且0<m<n时，奇异性度至多为min{m,n-m}且该界可达，最坏情况奇异性度为⌊n/2⌋（n≥2），远小于一般可行SDP系统的尖锐上界n。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异性度是衡量半定规划(SDP)退化程度的重要指标，但通常无法从问题数据中先验获得。为了克服这一困难，作者希望通过在Shor松弛中引入RLT方程，从而能够根据问题的秩与零化度信息预先估计奇异性度上界。

Method: 对非空二元集{x∈{0,1}^n:Ax=b}的Shor松弛，添加由定义线性等式生成的首层RLT方程，构造等式生成的SDP-RLT松弛；然后分析该松弛的奇异性度，通过秩-零化度关系推导其精确最坏情况上界，并构造达到该界的例子。

Result: 当rank(A)=m且0<m<n时，SDP-RLT松弛的奇异性度至多为min{m,n-m}，且对范围内每个可能的秩该界均可达到；因此该类松弛的最坏情况奇异性度为⌊n/2⌋（n≥2），远小于Sturm给出的n+1阶矩阵变量的可行SDP系统的一般尖锐上界n。

Conclusion: 对于个体松弛，问题数据的秩与零化度能提供奇异性度的先验上界，这一结果同样可应用于从约束残差估计到可行性距离的误差界中的Hölder指数估计。

Abstract: Singularity degree is an important measure of semidefinite programming (SDP) degeneracy, but it is generally unavailable a priori from the problem data. We augment the Shor relaxation of nonempty binary sets $\{x\in\{0,1\}^n:Ax=b\}$ with the first-level Reformulation-Linearization Technique (RLT) equations generated by the defining linear equalities. For the resulting equality-generated SDP-RLT relaxation, we determine the exact worst-case singularity degree. If $\operatorname{rank}(A)=m$ and $0<m<n$, then the associated relaxation has singularity degree at most $\min\{m,n-m\}$, and this rank-nullity bound is attained for every possible rank in this range. Consequently, the worst-case singularity degree over this class is $\lfloor n/2\rfloor$ for $n\geq 2$. This is strikingly smaller than the sharp general bound $n$ for feasible SDP systems with matrix variables of order $n+1$ (Sturm, 2000, Example 2). Thus, for individual relaxations, rank and nullity provide an a priori bound on the otherwise inaccessible singularity degree and on the Hölder exponent in error bounds estimating distance to feasibility from constraint residuals.

</details>


### [151] [On the suboptimality of stochastic MPC with varying constraint horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30017)
*Allan Andre Do Nascimento,Andre Bertolace,Antonis Papachristodoulou,Kostas Margellos*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种无终端约束的随机模型预测控制（MPC）方法，其中机会约束仅在较短的约束时域内被强制执行。通过随机松弛动态规划，推导出依赖于预测时域和约束时域的平均期望闭环成本的显式上界，并针对线性二次问题给出确定等价形式，模拟展示了计算量与性能之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在模型预测控制中，在整个预测时域上强制执行随机状态机会约束计算成本高昂。作者希望通过仅在一个较短的约束时域内施加机会约束来降低计算负担，同时保证闭环性能的可量化上界，从而在计算效率和控制性能之间取得平衡。

Method: 采用无终端代价和无终端约束的随机MPC框架，仅在缩短的约束时域内强制施加机会约束。利用随机松弛动态规划（stochastic relaxed dynamic programming）推导平均期望闭环成本的显式上界，该上界同时依赖于预测时域和约束时域的长度。对于具有仿射机会约束和有界均匀扰动的线性二次问题，通过坐标变换和约束收紧（constraint tightening）给出确定性重构方法。

Result: 推导出平均期望闭环成本的显式上界，其为预测时域和约束时域的函数；建立了线性二次问题下确定性等价的重构形式；通过仿真展示了缩短约束时域可以显著降低计算负担，同时性能损失保持在可接受的范围内，体现了计算量与性能之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 在随机MPC中缩短约束时域是一种有效的降低计算复杂度的策略，且通过随机松弛动态规划可以量化由此带来的性能上界。该研究为实际应用中在计算效率与控制性能之间做出折中提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Enforcing stochastic state constraints over the full prediction horizon in Model Predictive Control (MPC) can be computationally demanding. Here we study stochastic MPC without terminal ingredients in which chance constraints are enforced only over a shorter constraint horizon. Using stochastic relaxed dynamic programming, we derive an explicit upper bound on the average expected closed-loop cost that depends on both prediction and constraint horizons. For linear quadratic problems with affine chance constraints and bounded uniform disturbances, we provide a deterministic reformulation via coordinate transformation and constraint tightening. Simulations illustrate the trade-off between computational effort and performance.

</details>


### [152] [Interception Conditions in Multi-Agent Pursuit Games Governed by Linear Dynamics with Gronwall-type Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30123)
*David Terna. Gbande,Abbas Ja'afaru Badakaya,Mehdi Salimi,Aminu Sulaiman Halliru,Jamilu Adamu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体追击问题，追击者在固定时间范围内拦截一个或多个逃逸者，采用了受Gronwall型约束的一阶线性微分方程描述运动，并给出了追击者策略及拦截成功的充分条件，通过数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体协同追击问题在军事、机器人等领域有广泛应用，现有研究在固定时间范围、非线性控制约束下缺乏系统的充分条件分析，需要在更一般的控制约束框架下给出可保证拦截的策略设计。

Method: 使用一阶线性微分方程建模追击者与逃逸者运动，控制输入受Gronwall型约束；构造追击者的可行策略，并利用数学推导给出在逃逸者对抗下仍能保证拦截的充分条件。

Result: 推导出追击成功（至少一个追击者在指定时间内到达每个逃逸者位置）的充分条件，并通过数值示例验证所提策略的有效性和理论条件的正确性。

Conclusion: 本文在Gronwall约束的广义框架下为多对多追逃博弈提供了可验证的拦截保证条件，为后续扩展至更复杂动力学模型或鲁棒追击策略奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: This paper investigates a multi-agent pursuit problem in which a group of pursuers seeks to intercept one or more evaders within a fixed operational time horizon. The motion of both pursuers and evaders is described by first-order linear differential equations, and the control inputs available to the agents are subject to Gronwall-type constraints. Pursuit is considered successful when at least one pursuer reaches the position of each evader at a finite time within the prescribed duration. Within this framework, we construct permissible strategies for the pursuers and derive sufficient conditions under which interception is guaranteed despite the evaders' counteractions. To support the theoretical results, we present numerical examples that demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed strategies and confirm the analytical conditions for successful pursuit.

</details>


### [153] [Analysis of First-Order Linear Pursuit and Evasion Differential Games with Gronwall-Type Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30128)
*David Terna Gbande,Abbas Ja'afaru Badakaya,Jamilu Adamu,Mehdi Salimi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了$\mathbb{R}^{n}$空间中单个追捕者与单个逃逸者的追逃博弈问题，两者运动由一阶线性微分方程描述，控制函数受Gronwall型不等式约束。游戏时长固定为$\vartheta$。追捕完成的判定条件是两者状态重合，否则逃逸可能发生。追捕问题的结果取决于两玩家的可达域，同时给出逃逸可能性的一个充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在追逃博弈中，经典模型通常假设控制函数受限于有界约束（如模有界），而本文引入Gronwall型不等式作为更一般的控制约束条件，拓展了该类问题的适用范围，使模型更具一般性和理论价值。

Method: 采用微分博弈理论框架，将追捕者与逃逸者的运动分别建模为一阶线性微分方程，控制函数满足Gronwall型不等式。通过分析两玩家的可达域（attainability domains）之间的关系来判定追捕是否可完成；同时给出逃逸可能成立的充分条件。

Result: 追捕问题的结论依赖于追捕者与逃逸者可达域的交叠情况；此外，文中给出一个保证逃逸可能性的充分条件。

Conclusion: 在Gronwall型控制约束下，追逃问题的可解性可由可达域结构刻画，追捕成功与否取决于可达域是否相交，同时存在明确的充分条件保证逃逸可行。

Abstract: We examine pursuit and evasion problems in the space $ \mathbb{R}^{n} $ involving a lone pursuer and evader. Motion of each player is governed by a first-order linear differential equation. The players' control functions are subject to the Gronwall-type inequality. The game's duration is fixed and represented by a positive number $\vartheta $. When the state of a pursuer coincides with that of the evader, we then say the pursuit is completed. On the contrary, evasion is possible when throughout the game there is no agreement between the states of pursuer and evader. The result obtained related to the pursuit problem depends on the attainability domains of the two players. On the other hand, one sufficient condition is given for evasion to be possible.

</details>


### [154] [Solution and Optimal Strategies for a Differential Game of Pursuit-Evasion with Point-Wise Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30134)
*Jamilu Adamu,Abbas Ja'afaru Badakaya,Felix Wallace Tarry,Mehdi Salimi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类定义在序列空间l₂中、具有逐点（几何）约束的追逃微分博弈问题。该博弈涉及可数个追捕者（遵循一阶微分方程）与单个逃跑者（遵循二阶微分方程），博弈时长固定为θ。以终局时刻逃跑者与追捕者之间的最小距离作为博弈收益，追捕者最小化该收益而逃跑者最大化之。论文给出了博弈的解，包括双方最优策略的构造以及博弈值的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无限维序列空间l₂中，具有逐点几何约束的追逃微分博弈问题此前尚未得到充分研究。可数个追捕者与单个逃跑者之间的博弈，结合一阶与二阶动力学的不对称性，构成了一个具有理论挑战性的问题，求解其最优策略与博弈值具有重要的数学与博弈论意义。

Method: 本研究将追逃问题形式化为定义在l₂序列空间上的微分博弈，其中追捕者服从一阶微分方程，逃跑者服从二阶微分方程。通过设定固定的博弈时长θ和终局最小距离作为收益函数，利用微分博弈理论框架对博弈进行求解，包括构造双方的最优策略并估计博弈值。

Result: 获得了该追逃微分博弈的解，具体包括：双方玩家（追捕者与逃跑者）最优策略的显式构造，以及对博弈值的估计。

Conclusion: 在l₂序列空间中，具有逐点几何约束的可数追捕者与单逃跑者之间的固定时长追逃微分博弈是可解的，通过构造最优策略可以获得博弈值的估计，从而完整刻画该博弈的均衡解。

Abstract: This work study a differential game of pursuit-evasion involving countably many pursuers $p_{1},p_{2},\cdots,p_{m}$ and a single evader $e$ under point-wise (geometric) constraints in the sequence space $l_{2}.$ The pursuers evolve according to specified differential equations of $1^{st}$ order while the evader follows a $2^{nd}$ order differential equation. The game period is a fixed time interval of length $θ$ unit of time. The minimum distance between evader and the pursuer at the terminal time denotes the game payoff. The pursuers aim is to minimize the payoff, whereas the evader seeks to maximize it. We obtain the solution of the game, including optimal strategies of the players construction, as well as game value estimation.

</details>


### [155] [Multi-Agent Receding Horizon Games Framework for Autonomous Market Participation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30136)
*Parth Brahmbhatt,Styliani Avraamidou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段滚动时域的广义纳什均衡博弈方法，使电化学分布式能源资源（如电解槽和电池储能系统）能够以点对点（P2P）方式联合参与批发电力市场，克服最小投标规模门槛，同时保护各单元私有数据。基于PJM市场的六单元算例显示，该方法较单独参与可带来37%的利润提升、29%的电力成本降低和92%的可再生能源弃电减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电解槽、电池储能等电化学DER消耗大量电力，直接从批发市场购电可显著降低运营成本，但市场要求最小投标规模，中小规模单元难以独立满足。现有解决方案（第三方聚合商）收取佣金且集中式优化可能偏袒某些单元；已有的P2P方法仅处理单轮现货市场，忽略了真实批发市场日前承诺与实时调整的两阶段结构。因此需要一种公平、去中心化的两阶段协同投标机制。

Method: 将各单元联合投标建模为两阶段滚动时域广义纳什均衡（GNE）博弈：每个单元在五分钟间隔内对滚动的一小时视界重新优化其策略，整个群体在日前和实时两个市场阶段满足集体约束；各单元仅交换市场公开的聚合功率数据，不共享私有成本或生产信息，无需中央权威或第三方聚合商。

Result: 在PJM市场的六单元算例中，所提方法相比单独参与实现37%更高的利润、29%更低的电力成本和92%更少的可再生能源弃电量，且所有单元收益均得到改善。

Conclusion: 该两阶段GNE博弈方法为P2P分布式DER集群参与真实两阶段批发市场提供了公平、隐私保护的可行方案，在提升经济效益和可再生能源消纳方面具有显著优势。

Abstract: Electrochemical distributed energy resources (DERs), such as electrolyzers and battery energy storage systems, consume large amounts of electricity. Buying that power directly from wholesale markets can sharply reduce operating costs. Accessing these markets, however, requires a minimum bid size that individual small or medium-scale units struggle to meet. Grouping several units to bid as a single participant clears this barrier while letting each unit use its own low-cost renewable generation. The common remedy is to hire a third-party aggregator, but aggregators charge commissions and solve a centralized optimization that often favors certain units over others. A fairer alternative is peer-to-peer (P2P) participation, where units form a self-governing group and bid jointly with no central authority. Because each self-interested unit is unwilling to share private data, coordination is best posed as a game-theoretic distributed optimization problem. Existing P2P methods, however, address only single-round spot markets and ignore the two-stage structure of real wholesale markets, where participants commit a day ahead and continuously adjust in real time. We close this gap with a two-stage receding-horizon generalized Nash equilibrium (GNE) game. Each unit re-optimizes its strategy every five minutes over a rolling one-hour horizon, while the group satisfies both market stages collectively. Units exchange only publicly visible aggregate power, never private cost or production data. We apply the approach to a six-unit fleet on the PJM market. It delivers 37% higher profit, 29% lower electricity cost, and 92% less renewable curtailment than individual participation, with every unit better off.

</details>


### [156] [Enhancing Interpretability of Stochastic Programming Solutions: A Multiparametric Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30137)
*Parth Brahmbhatt,Styliani Avraamidou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于多参数规划（multiparametric programming）与Benders分解框架的确定性可解释性方法，将两阶段随机规划的补偿子问题重构为多参数线性规划，生成不确定空间的临界区域（CRs）多面体划分，从而将随机场景解析地聚类而非统计聚类。在供应链需求不确定规划中，100个随机场景被映射为6个临界区域簇，实现了对最优产能规划的完全透明解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段随机规划在实际工业应用中，由于大量场景导致最优补偿策略与不确定实现之间的直接联系在计算和认知上难以理解。现有的可解释性方法（如分类树、k-means后置聚类）仅提供解空间的统计近似，无法揭示驱动最优第一阶段决策的补偿问题的底层结构性质。因此需要一种能揭示结构属性的确定性解释方法。

Method: 将两阶段随机规划的补偿子问题重构为多参数线性规划（mp-LP），在Benders分解框架内生成显式的不确定空间临界区域（CRs）映射，即不确定空间的有限多面体划分。基于这些临界区域对随机场景进行解析聚类（而非统计聚类），使每个场景根据其所属的临界区域被归入对应的运营模式簇。

Result: 在供应链需求不确定规划案例中，100个随机场景精确映射为6个临界区域簇。该映射将最优产能规划决策解释为特定运营模式之间的精确权衡，提供了随机解的完全透明解释。

Conclusion: 基于多参数规划的解析聚类方法为两阶段随机规划提供了确定性的可解释性框架，相较于统计聚类方法能够揭示补偿问题的底层结构性质，从而更精确地解释最优第一阶段决策的驱动因素。

Abstract: Stochastic programming (SP) is a powerful framework for decision-making under uncertainty, but its practical adoption in industry is often hindered by the difficulty in understanding the causal relationships that drive optimal solutions. In the two-stage SP, strategic first-stage decisions are coupled with operational second-stage recourse decisions. When the number of scenarios under consideration is large, understanding the direct link between the uncertainty realization and optimal recourse strategy becomes computationally and cognitively demanding. Common approaches to improve interpretability include trained classification trees or scenario reduction, replacing the large scenario set with a representative subset. This is often achieved through post-hoc clustering (e.g., k-means) based on uncertainty realizations or optimal recourse decisions. While useful, these methods only provide a statistical approximation of the solution space and may fail to reveal the underlying structural properties of the recourse problem that drive optimal first-stage decisions. This work introduces a novel, deterministic approach to explainability using multiparametric programming (mp) within a Benders decomposition framework. We reformulate the recourse subproblem as a multiparametric linear program, generating an explicit map of Critical Regions (CRs), which are polyhedral partitions of the uncertainty space. This allows us to cluster scenarios analytically rather than statistically. We demonstrate this methodology on a supply chain planning under demand uncertainty. Our results show that 100 stochastic scenarios map to exactly six critical region clusters. This mapping allows us to explain optimal capacity planning decisions as a precise trade-off between specific operational modes, providing a fully transparent interpretation of the stochastic solution.

</details>


### [157] [Decentralized Strategies for Finite Population LQG Social Control: A Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30244)
*Liangyuan Guo,Bing-Chang Wang,Guangchen Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的无模型算法，用于解决有限总体、含乘性噪声的线性二次高斯（LQG）分散式社会控制问题。该算法无需系统矩阵的先验知识，通过求解两个代数Riccati方程（AREs）实现有限时域和无限时域下的社会最优，并基于Lyapunov型算子的谱性质分析证明了无限时域下算法的收敛性，最后通过数值示例验证了算法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的有限总体LQG分散式社会控制问题通常依赖系统模型的精确知识，且成本函数中的状态和控制权重往往要求半正定。该论文旨在突破这些限制，设计一种无需系统矩阵先验知识的无模型强化学习算法，适用于权重不限于半正定的一般情形。

Method: 针对有限总体LQG分散式社会控制问题，论文分别考虑有限时域和无限时域两种情况，通过求解两个代数Riccati方程获得社会最优解，并设计了无模型（基于强化学习）的算法。在无限时域情形下，利用Lyapunov型算子的谱性质分析来证明算法的收敛性，同时对比了有限时域与无限时域下RL解法的异同。

Result: 通过数值示例验证了所提出无模型算法的有效性，并展示了有限时域与无限时域下强化学习解决方案之间的差异。

Conclusion: 该研究成功将无模型强化学习方法推广到含乘性噪声的有限总体LQG分散式社会控制问题，放宽了对权重矩阵的非负限制，并在无限时域情形下基于算子谱理论给出了收敛性保证。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel model-free algorithm for the finite-population linear quadratic Gaussian (LQG) decentralized social control problem with multiplicative noise. The state and control weights in the cost functional are not limited to be positive semidefinite. For both finite-horizon and infinite-horizon cases, the goal is to obtain a social optimum by solving two algebraic Riccati equations (AREs), without requiring prior knowledge of the system matrices. Then, we complete the design of a model-free algorithm for solving the decentralized social control problem. Especially, in the infinite-horizon case, the algorithm's convergence is based on analyzing the spectral property of the Lyapunov-type operator. The differences of reinforcement learning (RL) solutions between the finite-horizon and infinite-horizon cases are compared. Finally, the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm is demonstrated by a numerical example.

</details>


### [158] [Dec-BFTRL: Squre-Root Regret for Decentralized Online Upper-Linearizable Optimization under Separation Access with Application to Continuous Submodular Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30271)
*Yiyang Lu,Mohammad Pedramfar,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种去中心化在线优化算法Dec-BFTRL，用于求解具有高效分离访问的上线性化收益函数优化问题，并应用于在线连续DR-次模最大化。每个智能体通过近似的规范投影将内部迭代映射到可行动作，仅通信累积的代理梯度对偶状态，实现了期望网络聚合遗憾的Õ(√T)界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化在线优化在分布式系统中具有重要应用，但现有的上线性化收益函数（包括DR-次模最大化）的去中心化算法研究不足。需要一个在通信效率和计算效率上均可行的去中心化算法，能够在网络环境中实现最优遗憾界。

Method: 提出了Decentralized Barrier Follow-the-Regularized-Leader (Dec-BFTRL)算法。每个智能体通过近似规范投影将内部迭代映射到可行动作，仅通信累积的代理梯度对偶状态，并利用局部HybridNewton过程近似最小化通信后的BFTRL势函数。算法采用T轮邻居混合步骤和Õ(T)次分离预言机调用。

Result: 对每个智能体，实现了期望网络聚合遗憾的Õ(√T)界。每个智能体在T轮中使用T次邻居混合步骤和Õ(T)次分离预言机调用。提供了四种包装器实例化，覆盖三个DR-次模最大化问题。

Conclusion: Dec-BFTRL是首个在去中心化设置下针对上线性化收益函数（包括DR-次模最大化）实现Õ(√T)遗憾界的算法，且通信和计算复杂度均高效，为分布式在线次模优化提供了统一的算法框架。

Abstract: We study decentralized online optimization of upper-linearizable payoffs over an action set under efficient separation access, with applications to online continuous diminishing-return (DR) submodular maximization. We propose Decentralized Barrier Follow-the-Regularized-Leader (Dec-BFTRL), and evaluate each agent's played action against the average of all local objectives. Each agent maps an internal iterate to a feasible action through an approximate gauge projection, communicates only a cumulative surrogate-gradient dual state, and invokes the local HybridNewton procedure to approximately minimize its post-communication BFTRL potential. For every agent, we achieve expected network-aggregate regret of $\widetilde O(\sqrt{T})$. Over $T$ rounds, each agent uses $T$ neighbor-mixing steps and $\widetilde O(T)$ separation-oracle calls. We give four wrapper instantiations covering three DR-submodular maximization problems.

</details>


### [159] [Last two pieces of the puzzle for unsolvability of a system of two quadratic (in)equalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30571)
*Min-Chi Wang,Ruey-Lin Sheu,Huu-Quang Nguyen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文解决了非齐次二次系统可行性的两个长期开放难题：非齐次Calabi定理（判定两个等式f(x)=0与g(x)=0无公共解的条件）以及非齐次（严格）Finsler引理（判定f(x)≤0与g(x)=0无公共解的条件），并给出了定理与算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二次函数不等式/等式系统的联合可行性是数学中的基本问题。齐次情形已从1936年Finsler引理到1990年Yuan引理被完整解决（所有无解组合均可由A和B的正定或半正定矩阵束刻画）。非齐次情形的多个案例已被处理，但两个困难案例长期悬而未决：非齐次Calabi定理与严格Finsler引理的非齐次版本。本文旨在填补这两项空白。

Method: 论文通过严格的数学证明给出了两个关键定理：针对{f(x)=0}∩{g(x)=0}=∅的非齐次Calabi定理，以及针对{f(x)≤0}∩{g(x)=0}=∅的非齐次严格Finsler引理，并基于定理设计了相应的算法来判定解的可行性。

Result: 成功解决了非齐次二次系统领域两个长期未解决的开放问题，分别给出了非齐次Calabi定理与非齐次严格Finsler引理的完整表述与证明，并提供了相应算法。

Conclusion: 本文完整回答了非齐次二次系统可行性的两个遗留挑战，进一步完善了从齐次Finsler引理到非齐次情形的理论体系，为相关优化与控制领域提供了重要的理论工具和算法支持。

Abstract: Given two quadratic functions \( f(x) = x^T Ax + 2a^T x + a_0 \) and \( g(x) = x^T Bx + 2b^T x + b_0 ,\) each associated with either the strict inequality ($<0$); non-strict inequality ($\leq 0$); or the equality ($=0$), it is a fundamental question to ask whether or not the joint system has a solution. For homogeneous quadratic systems ($a=b=0,~a_0=b_0=0$), starting from Finsler's lemma in 1936 until Yuan's alternative lemma in 1990, all combinations of the unsolvability for $\{x\in \mathbb{R} ^n\mid x^T Ax \mathbin{\star } 0\}\cap \{x\in \mathbb{R} ^n\mid x^T Bx \mathbin{\#} 0\}\subset \{0\}$, where $\star $ and $\#$ can be any of $\{<,\leq ,=\}$, have been shown to possess either a positive definite or a positive semi-definite matrix pencil of $A$ and $B.$ Extensions to nonhomogeneous quadratic systems $\{x\in \mathbb{R} ^n\mid f(x) \mathbin{\star } 0\}\cap \{x\in \mathbb{R} ^n\mid g(x) \mathbin{\#} 0\}=\emptyset $ have been done for several cases already. Two challenging cases remain open: the nonhomogeneous Calabi Theorem which determines when $\{f(x)=0\}\cap \{g(x)=0\}=\emptyset $; and the nonhomogeneous (strict) Finsler lemma to determine whether $\{f(x)\leq 0\}\cap \{g(x)=0\}=\emptyset .$ The paper provides the answers to both, in theorems and algorithms.

</details>


### [160] [A method for global minimization of nonconvex quadratic functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30611)
*Adil Bagirov,Vivek Laha,Juan Enrique Martínez-Legaz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了带盒约束的非凸二次函数的全局最小化问题，通过Gershgorin定理将矩阵A化为对角形式以获得DC（凸函数之差）表示，进而利用DC分量的ε-次微分设计全局最小化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸二次函数在盒约束下的全局最小化是一个经典的NP难问题，具有广泛的应用背景（如组合优化、统计估计等）。由于目标函数非凸，传统的局部优化方法无法保证找到全局最小值，因此需要设计能够处理非凸性的全局优化算法。

Method: 该方法首先利用Gershgorin定理将主矩阵A简化为其对角形式，从而将原二次函数表示为两个凸函数之差（即DC分解）。随后研究DC分量的ε-次微分性质，并基于这些理论结果设计了一种全局最小化算法。

Result: 通过Gershgorin定理实现的对角化简化了DC分解的构造，基于ε-次微分的分析为设计全局优化算法提供了理论支撑。

Conclusion: 提出了一种结合Gershgorin定理与DC分解的全局优化方法，为解决带盒约束的非凸二次规划问题提供了一个新的理论框架和算法途径。

Abstract: The problem of global minimization of nonconvex quadratic functions subject to box constraints is studied. Applying Gershgorin theorem we reduce the main matrix A to its diagonal form which is used to obtain difference of convex representation of the quadratic function. Then ε-subdifferentials of DC components are studied and used to design a method for minimizing the quadratic function globally.

</details>


### [161] [Computing Equilibria in Simulation-Based Insurance Markets with Discontinuous Demand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30814)
*Yunsoo Ha,Linda Nozick*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究飓风风险下竞争性保险市场中的仿真均衡问题。针对保费决策导致的不连续需求函数和破产约束导致的非凸可行域两大挑战，提出了一种依赖定价的再保险优化算子，将每家的联合响应问题转化为结构化定价问题，并采用信赖域框架优化非光滑非凸目标函数，最后通过阻尼更好响应方案稳定均衡迭代。在北卡罗来纳州飓风保险市场案例中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 竞争性保险市场均衡问题中，保险公司的定价和再保险决策相互耦合，客户购买行为产生不连续需求函数，破产约束导致非凸可行域，使得传统的联合优化方法难以直接求解。

Method: 1）提出定价依赖的再保险优化算子，对每个候选定价向量重新优化再保险，将联合响应问题转化为结构化定价问题；2）在非光滑非凸的简化目标上使用信赖域框架，同时利用平滑目标与原目标并引入直接搜索探索；3）计算得到的近似响应嵌入阻尼更好响应方案以稳定均衡迭代。

Result: 在北卡罗来纳州飓风保险市场案例中，该框架以实际可运行的耗时识别出多个均衡候选点，基于半径的局部Nash检验在测试邻域内未发现有利可图的抽样偏离。

Conclusion: 通过再保险重优化的算子化处理有效规避了非凸联合可行域上的同步优化难题，为带有不连续需求的保险市场均衡计算提供了一种稳健且高效的数值求解方案。

Abstract: We study a simulation-based equilibrium problem arising in competitive insurance markets under hurricane risk. Each insurer seeks to maximize its own profit by selecting regional pricing and reinsurance decisions while satisfying insolvency constraints. The resulting problem is particularly challenging because customer purchase decisions induce discontinuous demand functions, while insolvency constraints create nonconvex feasible regions. To address these challenges, we introduce a pricing-dependent reinsurance optimization operator that reoptimizes reinsurance for each candidate pricing vector, transforming each insurer's joint response problem into a structured pricing problem in which insolvency feasibility is handled through reinsurance reoptimization. This reformulation avoids simultaneous optimization of pricing and reinsurance over the nonconvex joint feasible set. We optimize the resulting nonsmooth and nonconvex reduced objective using a trust-region framework that exploits both the smoothed and original objectives while incorporating direct-search exploration. The computed approximate responses are embedded within a damped better-response scheme to stabilize the equilibrium iterations. In a case study of the North Carolina hurricane insurance market, the framework identifies multiple equilibrium candidates with practical runtimes, while radius-based local Nash tests find no profitable sampled deviations within the tested neighborhoods.

</details>


### [162] [On the real angular stability radius for sectorial matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30843)
*Sei Zhen Khong,Xin Mao,Li Qiu,Axel Ringh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究扇形矩阵（sectorial matrix）的实角稳定半径问题，即寻找使I+MΔ失去秩所需的最小实数扇形扰动Δ的最大相位。研究表明，对某些矩阵，复数与实数角稳定半径相同，但在一般情况下（如对角阵或复对称矩阵）两者不同，并为此推导了依赖于M最大两个相位的闭式公式，且可将n×n问题通过压缩约简为2×2问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实稳定半径问题是矩阵理论中的经典问题，关注最小实数扰动使矩阵降秩。本文提出其"角版本"——用扰动的最⼤相位而非最大奇异值来衡量扰动大小，这对于扇形矩阵具有特殊意义，因为扇形矩阵在控制系统和数值线性代数中广泛出现，且其相位结构提供了比奇异值更有物理意义的稳定性度量。

Method: 论文定义了扇形矩阵的实角稳定半径，分析了复角稳定半径与实角稳定半径的等价条件与差异。对对角矩阵和复对称矩阵两种情形推导了闭式公式，该公式由M的两个最大相位决定。关键技巧是将任意实失稳扰动压缩至由失稳向量的实部和虚部张成的实二维子空间，从而将n×n问题约简为2×2问题。

Result: 证明了对于某些矩阵，复与实角稳定半径相等，但一般而言（如对角矩阵或复对称矩阵）两者不同；对这两种情形给出了依赖于M最大两个相位的闭式公式，并证明了n×n情形可通过压缩约简为2×2情形。

Conclusion: 本文建立了实角稳定半径的理论框架，揭示了其在一般情形下与复角稳定半径的差异，并为重要矩阵类提供了精确计算公式和降维约简结果，为扇形矩阵的相位扰动分析提供了新的工具。

Abstract: The real stability radius problem asks for the smallest real perturbation that makes a given matrix lose rank, and it is a structured counterpart of the corresponding complex stability radius problem. In this paper, we consider an angular version of this question for sectorial matrices, where the size of a perturbation is measured by its largest phase instead of its largest singular value. More precisely, for a sectorial matrix $M$, we investigate the minimum required largest phase of a real and sectorial perturbation $Δ$ for which $I+MΔ$ loses rank. We show that for some matrices, the complex and the real angular stability radius are the same, but that they are in general different, such as when $M$ is diagonal or complex symmetric. For these cases, we derive a formula for the real angular stability radius, and this formula depends on the two largest phases of $M$. Moreover, in these cases we show that the result for $n \times n$ matrices can be reduced to that for $2 \times 2$ matrices by compressing an arbitrary real destabilizing perturbation to the real two-dimensional subspace spanned by the real and imaginary parts of a destabilizing vector.

</details>


### [163] [Provably Safe Decentralized Contingency MPC under State-Only Information and Limited Sensing for Nonlinear Multi-agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30874)
*Max Studt,Georg Schildbach*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于多智能体控制的分散式应急MPC（contingency MPC）框架，在仅状态信息模式、有限传感和即插即用操作条件下，通过引入新颖的安全集更新机制，在保持递推可行性、安全性和Lyapunov型收敛等底层保证的同时，降低局部交互处理的保守性，实现无记忆局部交互和有限感知范围，并在密集多智能体场景中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分散式MPC在多智能体控制中处理局部交互时往往过于保守，且在实际系统中存在有限传感范围和即插即用操作的需求。作者希望设计一种既能保证递归可行性、安全性和收敛性，又能减少保守性、适应有限传感和即插即用场景的分散控制方案。

Method: 框架基于逐智能体回退区域（安全集），在其中始终存在到安全平衡点的可行应急机动。核心创新是提出了一种新的安全集更新机制，该机制在保持底层保证的同时支持更少保守的分散交互，从而无需智能体重构精确的邻居几何结构即可实现无记忆局部交互和有限感知范围。方案保持完全分散，并保留了共享第一输入的应急MPC结构。

Result: 理论保证（递推可行性、安全性、Lyapunov型收敛）得到证明，并在密集多智能体场景中通过仿真验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文表明，通过精心设计的安全集更新机制，可以同时实现低保守性的分散交互、有限传感操作和即插即用能力，而无需牺牲递归可行性、安全性和收敛性等关键保证，为实际多智能体系统的部署提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: This paper considers decentralized contingency MPC for multi-agent control under a state-only information pattern, with particular focus on limited sensing and plug-and-play operation. The objective is to retain recursive feasibility, safety, and Lyapunov-type convergence while reducing conservatism in local interaction handling. The framework relies on agent-wise fallback regions (safe sets) in which a feasible contingency maneuver to a safe equilibrium is always available. A novel safe-set update mechanism is introduced that supports less conservative decentralized interaction while preserving the underlying guarantees. This, in turn, enables memory-free local interaction and finite sensing ranges without requiring agents to reconstruct the exact neighbor geometry. The resulting scheme remains fully decentralized and preserves the shared-first-input contingency MPC structure. Theoretical guarantees and simulation results illustrate the effectiveness of the approach in dense multi-agent scenarios.

</details>


### [164] [Symmetry-dependence in Rounding of a Convex Body](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30876)
*Zikai Xiong,Robert M. Freund*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明任意凸体S都存在基于其对称度sym(S)的\sqrt{n/sym(S)}-轮换（rounding），即存在以原点为中心的超椭球E和中心c，使得E ⊆ S-c ⊆ \sqrt{n/sym(S)}·E。该结果验证了Belloni和Freund在2005年提出的猜想，并推广了已有的n-轮换和\sqrt{n}-轮换结果。文中还给出了多胞形情形的构造性算法，并证明了该因子在维度和对称度上几乎最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凸体的轮换（rounding）问题在凸优化、计算几何和高维算法中具有基础性意义。已有的n-轮换结果虽然通用但因子较大，而\sqrt{n}-轮换仅对对称凸体成立。本文旨在利用Minkowski中心定义的凸体对称度sym(S)，建立更精细的轮换因子\sqrt{n/sym(S)}，从而统一和推广已知结果，并验证2005年Belloni-Freund猜想。

Method: 本文首先定义了Minkowski中心及凸体的对称度sym(S)，证明任意凸体S存在满足\sqrt{n/sym(S)}因子的轮换。对于由凸包给出的多胞形，该轮换通过关于Minkowski中心进行正则化的最小体积外接椭球问题获得；对于由半空间交集给出的多胞形，则通过关于Minkowski中心正则化的最大体积内接椭球问题获得。在下界方面，利用显式构造法证明当(n+1)/(1+sym(S))为整数时因子紧致，一般情形下构造了使得任意轮换因子至少为\sqrt{2/3}·\sqrt{n/sym(S)}的多胞形。

Result: 证明了任意凸体S都容许一个\sqrt{n/sym(S)}-轮换，验证了2005年Belloni-Freund的猜想。作为特例，恢复了已知的n-轮换结果（因为sym(S)≥1/n）以及对称情形（sym(S)=1）下的\sqrt{n}-轮换结果。对于多胞形，给出了基于正则化最小体积外接椭球和最大体积内接椭球的构造性算法。此外，证明了当(n+1)/(1+sym(S))为整数时轮换因子紧致；在一般情形下，构造了多胞形使得轮换因子至少为\sqrt{2/3}·\sqrt{n/sym(S)}，说明上界在常数因子内最优。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了Belloni-Freund猜想，建立了凸体轮换因子与其对称度之间的精确关系。该结果在理论上统一了已有轮换结果，并在实践中提供了基于椭球优化的构造算法。下界结果表明因子\sqrt{n/sym(S)}在维度和对称度的依赖上几乎是紧致的，仅在一般情形下存在常数因子\sqrt{2/3}的差距。

Abstract: The symmetry measure of a convex body $S\subset\mathbb{R}^n$ is given by $$\operatorname{sym}(S):=\max\{α\ge0:\text{ there exists }x\in S\text{ such that }-α(S-x)\subseteq S-x\},$$ where such an $x$ is called a Minkowski center. We prove that every convex body $S$ admits a $\sqrt{\frac{n}{\operatorname{sym}(S)}}$-rounding of $S$, namely, there exists an origin-centered ellipsoid $E$ and a center $c$ such that the following rounding holds: $$E\subseteq S-c\subseteq\sqrt{\frac{n}{\operatorname{sym}(S)}}\,E.$$ This result was conjectured in 2005 by Belloni and Freund. As special cases, this recovers the known result of an $n$-rounding of $S$ (since it always holds that $\operatorname{sym}(S) \ge 1/n$), and also recovers the known result of a $\sqrt{n}$-rounding when $\operatorname{sym}(S) = 1$.
  In the case when $S$ is a polytope given as the convex hull of points, the desired rounding is produced by a regularized minimum volume covering ellipsoid problem where the regularization is with respect to the Minkowski center. Similarly, when $S$ is a polytope given as the intersection of halfspaces, such a rounding is produced by a regularized maximum volume inscribed ellipsoid problem.
  We also show that the factor $\sqrt{\frac{n}{\operatorname{sym}(S)}}$ is nearly tight in its dependence on dimension and symmetry. When $\frac{n+1}{1+\operatorname{sym}(S)}$ is an integer, we show by explicit construction that the factor $\sqrt{\frac{n}{\operatorname{sym}(S)}}$ is tight. In the more general case, for every dimension $n$ and every admissible symmetry value, we construct a polytope $S$ for which every rounding is at least $\sqrt{\frac{2}{3}}\sqrt{\frac{n}{\operatorname{sym}(S)}}$.

</details>


### [165] [Comment on "Event-Triggered Stabilization of Linear Time-Delay Systems via Halanay-Type Inequality"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30885)
*Luke Jonker,Kexue Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了文献[1]中在事件触发镇定分析中起核心作用的引理1，指出了其证明中的技术缺陷，并提供了修正后的论证。修正后的结果保留了原始引理的陈述有效性，但关键改进在于：对指数衰减项的精细处理表明其衰减率必须被纳入最终的收敛估计中，即系统的指数衰减率应由特征衰减率与该项衰减率的最小值共同确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者发现文献[1]中引理1的证明存在技术性缺陷，由于该引理在事件触发镇定分析中具有核心作用，因此有必要修正其证明以保证相关理论分析的严谨性和正确性。

Method: 作者对引理1的原始证明进行了细致的重新审视，重点分析了指数衰减项在收敛估计中的处理方式，指出原证明中对该项衰减率处理的疏漏，并通过保留该项衰减率的精细论证给出了修正后的证明过程。

Result: 修正后的证明表明，原始引理的陈述仍然成立，但指数衰减率应取为系统特征衰减率与指数衰减项衰减率两者的最小值。这一修正使得收敛估计更为严格和精确。

Conclusion: 本文为文献[1]中的核心引理提供了正确的数学证明，虽然引理的最终结论未变，但修正了证明中的技术缺陷，增强了事件触发镇定分析结果的理论可靠性。

Abstract: This comment revisits Lemma 1 in [1], which plays a central role in the event-triggered stabilization analysis developed therein. We identify technical gaps in the proof of the lemma and provide a corrected argument. In particular, careful treatment of the exponentially decaying term shows that its decay rate must be retained in the resulting convergence estimate. The statement of the original lemma, with the exponential decay rate determined by the minimum of the characteristic decay rate and the decay rate of this term, remains valid.

</details>


### [166] [Goldfarb-Idnani Revisited:Invariants, Certificates, and the Limits of Guessing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30933)
*Thomas Schmelzer,Martin Stoll*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文分析了对偶有效集法（Goldfarb-Idnani）从边界约束推广到一般线性不等式约束时，P-矩阵性质丧失导致终止性保证失效的问题。数值实验表明，尽管随机实例中几乎不失败，但重复列可使失败率升至93%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对偶有效集法在边界约束下因其P-矩阵性质而保证有限终止，但当推广到一般线性不等式约束时，工作集矩阵$C_{\mathcal{A}}^\top G^{-1} C_{\mathcal{A}}$仅在候选集满秩时才正定，这一性质取决于猜测而非数据本身。因此需要从理论上揭示这一结构性障碍，并实证评估其对算法实际性能的影响。

Method: 从理论上分析Goldfarb-Idnani对偶有效集法中候选集系统的P-矩阵性质：边界约束对应$G^{-1}$的主子矩阵总为正定，而一般约束$C^\top x \ge b$下工作集系统变为$C_{\mathcal{A}}^\top G^{-1} C_{\mathcal{A}}$，其正定性依赖于候选列$C_{\mathcal{A}}$的秩。文中在600多个随机实例（四类约束族）及真实长持仓投资组合数据上进行数值实验验证。

Result: 600多个随机实例中从未发生失败。然而当$C$中存在重复列时，失败率飙升至93%，认证候选集的百分比从100%降至零。在真实长持仓组合数据中，有效集达到494+1个约束中的442个。

Conclusion: 严格凸性使得KKT条件能对候选集进行认证而非直接信任，保证了方法在大规模实例中的可用性。但P-矩阵性质的丧失是一个真实的结构性风险，尤其在病态或退化数据（如重复列）下会彻底破坏算法可靠性。

Abstract: The dual active-set method of Goldfarb and Idnani solves the strictly convex quadratic program by adding and dropping one constraint at a time, requiring no phase one. Primal--dual active set and block principal pivoting skip the walk altogether: they guess an entire active set at once and repair it from returning signs.
  For bound constraints the guess is safe. The system solved on a candidate set is a principal submatrix of $G^{-1}$, positive definite regardless of the guess; this $P$-matrix property guarantees finite termination. For $C^\top x \ge b$ that hypothesis fails, which is our central focus. The working-set system is $C_{\mathcal{A}}^\top G^{-1} C_{\mathcal{A}}$, positive definite only when $C_{\mathcal{A}}$ has full column rank, a property of the guess, not the data. The $P$-matrix property is lost and the structural obstruction invalidates the guarantee. Strict convexity keeps the method usable: KKT conditions make candidate sets certified rather than trusted.
  Over 600 random instances across four constraint families this failure never occurs. However, duplicating columns of $C$ raises failure rates to 93\%, dropping certified fraction from 100\% to zero. On real long-only portfolio data, the active set reaches 442 of $494 + 1$ constraints.

</details>


### [167] [Efficiency of cooperation incentives in evolutionary population games under payoff-observation errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30982)
*Shengxian Wang,Chengyu Yin,Xiaojie Chen,Ming Cao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了带有收益观测误差的群体博弈中的合作演化动力学，发现误差在某些条件下可以降低激励实施成本，并提出了最优激励协议及优化算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统合作演化动力学研究基于无误差的理想化博弈设置，但现实中个体在观察对手收益时经常出错（如数据记录错误、忽略关键细节、计算错误等）。这种理想模型与现实场景之间的差距导致对收益观测误差如何影响群体博弈演化动力学，特别是合作激励效率的认识不足。

Method: 构建了带收益观测误差的群体博弈研究框架，在带有联合激励的演化囚徒困境博弈中研究误差对合作演化动力学的影响；设计索引函数量化误差存在下激励的实施成本，并利用最优控制理论推导最优激励协议；最后构建优化问题并设计算法求解有无误差情形下最优激励协议成本差异的最小值。

Result: 理论和数值结果表明，与无误差情形相比，收益观测误差可以降低激励成本，并且推导出了这一结果成立的理论条件。此外，通过优化问题与所设计的算法，获得了最小化有/无误差最优激励协议成本差异的数值解。

Conclusion: 收益观测误差并非总是对合作激励有害，在某些条件下反而能降低激励实施成本；本文为理解现实博弈场景下合作激励的效率提供了新视角，并给出了可操作的优化算法。

Abstract: Traditional studies on evolutionary dynamics of cooperation have concentrated on an idealized game setup free of payoff-observation errors. However, in real-world scenarios, individuals frequently encounter errors when observing the payoffs of their opponents during game interactions, resulting from unintentional mistakes, such as data misrecording, overlooking critical details, and miscalculations. This gap between idealized error-free game models and the error-prone real-world game interactions leads to the lack of insight into the impact of payoff-observation errors on the evolutionary dynamics of population games, in particular the efficiency of cooperation incentives. In this paper, we construct a research framework for population games with payoff-observation errors, which enables us to investigate the effects of errors on the evolutionary dynamics of cooperation in the evolutionary Prisoner's Dilemma game with combined incentives. To quantify the implementation costs of incentives in the presence of errors, we devise an index function and employ optimal control theory to derive the optimal incentive protocols. Our theoretical and numerical results reveal that payoff-observation errors can lower the costs compared to error-free cases, and we also derive the theoretical conditions for these results. Finally, we formulate an optimization problem to explore the cost difference between the optimal incentive protocols with and without errors, and further design an algorithm to obtain the numerical solution that minimizes this difference.

</details>


### [168] [Interpolation Conditions for Instant Data Consistency with Port-Hamiltonian Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31092)
*Martina Vanelli,Nima Monshizadeh,Julien M. Hendrickx*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于插值条件的数据驱动框架，用于评估非线性端口哈密顿（pH）系统与观测数据的一致性，无需显式参数化即可给出存在光滑（凸）哈密顿函数的充要条件，并提供半定规划进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将数据驱动方法应用于物理结构化的动力系统时，需要判断观测数据是否与pH系统结构（包括哈密顿函数、互联与耗散结构）兼容，但目前缺乏无需显式参数化的系统化一致性检验方法。

Method: 基于插值条件推导了数据与pH系统瞬时一致性（instant consistency）的充要条件；针对非退化互联与耗散结构，将一致性验证转化为半定规划（SDP）问题。

Result: 给出了存在光滑（凸）哈密顿函数的pH系统与给定数据集一致性的充要判定条件，并通过SDP公式实现非退化结构下的高效验证。

Conclusion: 该框架为评估数据与物理结构的一致性提供了原则性方法，为直接从数据设计控制器奠定了基础。

Abstract: We develop a data-driven framework for nonlinear port-Hamiltonian (pH) systems based on interpolation conditions to characterize consistency between observed data and structured dynamical models. Specifically, we derive necessary and sufficient conditions for the existence of a pH system with a smooth (convex) Hamiltonian instantly consistent with a given dataset, without requiring explicit parametrization. We further provide a semidefinite programming formulation to verify consistency with non-degenerate interconnection and dissipation structures. Our results provide a principled approach to assess instant data consistency with physical structure and pave the way for control design directly from data.

</details>


### [169] [Controllability of time-varying lumped semilinear systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31131)
*Hernán R. Henríquez,Matthieu F. Pinaud*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了由半线性微分方程描述的时变集总控制系统的可控性问题，将半线性系统视为线性系统的扰动，在底层线性系统可控且非线性强迫函数满足与该线性系统相适应的有界性条件的前提下，证明了半线性系统也是近似可控的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究时变集总控制系统中半线性系统在扰动下的可控性，旨在将线性系统的可控性推广到半线性情形，解决非线性项对系统可控性影响的数学理论问题。

Method: 将半线性系统建模为线性系统的扰动；假设底层线性系统可控；对非线性强迫函数施加一个适配于底层线性系统的有界性条件；在此基础上证明半线性系统近似可控性。

Result: 证明了在底层线性系统可控且非线性项满足适配有界性条件下，半线性时变集总控制系统是近似可控的。

Conclusion: 半线性系统的近似可控性可以在对线性系统可控性假设的基础上，通过适当的非线性项有界性条件来保证，从而建立了从线性到非线性系统可控性的推广框架。

Abstract: In this work we are concerned with the controllability of time-varying lumped control systems governed by a semilinear differential equation. We consider the semilinear system as a perturbation of a linear system. Assuming the underlying linear system is controllable, and the nonlinear forcing function satisfies a boundedness condition which is adapted to the underlying linear system, we show that the semilinear system is also approximately controllable

</details>


### [170] [On the problem of assigning multiple interceptors over multiple aerial threats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31143)
*Liang Hong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究多枚同型拦截器以单齐射方式拦截多枚同型空中威胁的分配问题，严格证明了均匀分配策略（策略A）在平均漏防数上优于随机分配策略（策略B），并进一步证明策略A是最优策略，且这些结论在拦截器数量少于威胁数量时同样成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中已有经验证据表明，当拦截器数量不少于威胁数量时，均匀分配策略在平均漏防威胁数方面优于随机分配策略，但此前缺乏严格的数学证明，且策略A是否是最优策略、以及拦截器数量少于威胁数量时结论是否成立，均未得到充分研究。

Method: 本文首先对策略A优于策略B这一经验事实给出严格的数学证明；其次通过数值实验展示策略A相比策略B的显著效率优势；再次证明策略A不仅仅是优于策略B，而是真正的最优策略；最后将上述所有结论推广到拦截器数量少于威胁数量的情形。

Result: 本文给出了策略A优于策略B的严格数学证明；数值结果表明策略A可显著优于策略B；证明策略A为真正的最优策略；并确认所有这些结论在拦截器数量少于威胁数量时依然成立。

Conclusion: 均匀分配拦截器的策略在平均漏防威胁数上既是优于随机分配的高效策略，也是全局最优策略，且该结论不依赖于拦截器与威胁数量的相对大小关系。

Abstract: This article investigates the problem of assigning multiple identical interceptors over multiple identical aerial threats, where all interceptors are launched in a single salvo. For this problem, two strategies have been studied in the literature: (A) to spread all interceptors as evenly as possible over all threats, and (B) to randomly assign all interceptors over the threats. The main contributions of this article are as follows. First, the literature contains empirical evidence that Strategy A is more efficient than Strategy B in terms of the mean number of missed threats, when the number of interceptors is no less than the number of threats. This article gives a rigorous proof of this fact. Secondly, it demonstrates numerically that Strategy~A can be significantly more efficient than Strategy B. Thirdly, it shows that Strategy~A is not only superior to Strategy B, but also the bona fide optimal strategy. Finally, this article establishes that these conclusions also hold when the number of interceptors is less than the number of threats.

</details>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [RedLight-VLA: Models for traffic-rule grounding and behavioral emphasis in driving policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28656)
*Bala Murali Manoghar Sai Sudhakar,Sourab Bapu Sridhar,Sandipan Das,Rahul Ahuja,Meda Lazar,Ashish Garg,Pratik Likhar,Senthil Yogamani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RedLight-VLA，一种针对红绿灯路口规则驱动驾驶场景的VLA训练目标，通过轨迹导出的行为重加权（BR）和并行辅助（AUX）头来提升行为克隆策略在稀有刹车/启动操作及红绿灯和停止线状态感知上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆的VLA驾驶策略在信号控制路口面对稀有的规则驱动操作（如刹车和启动）时表现不佳。主要原因有二：一是刹车/启动样本在平均轨迹损失中贡献极小，导致模型不重视这些关键操作；二是融合表示缺乏对红绿灯和停止线状态的显式监督，使模型难以正确理解场景规则。

Method: RedLight-VLA包含两个核心组件：（1）轨迹导出的行为重加权（BR），利用旋转不变的纵向动力学和保尺度缩减来强调稀有减速和加速样本，禁用时能精确恢复基线；（2）并行辅助（AUX）头，在不使用自回归语言生成也不修改轨迹解码器的情况下，将红绿灯和停止线状态嵌入连续的后融合规则令牌中。两者可独立或组合使用。

Result: 在相同的骨干网络、训练数据、解码器和评估集条件下，RedLight-VLA相比VLA基线将红灯停止线超调从7.3%降至6.8%，停止线速度误差降低12.7%，3秒红绿灯切片的ADE/FDE从0.274/0.964m提升至0.247/0.897m。绿灯误停从3.2%增至3.9%，但BR与AUX组合缓解了AUX单独使用时的更大增幅（4.0%）。组合模型在非红绿灯ADE/FDE上也从0.268/0.956m提升至0.241/0.876m，在所有四个切片位移指标上均优于任一单独机制。

Conclusion: RedLight-VLA通过行为重加权和辅助监督两种正交机制的有机结合，在不增加手动规则标注、不改变解码器架构的前提下，显著改善了VLA策略在信号路口稀有规则操作上的表现，并验证了组合策略相比单一机制的优越性。

Abstract: Behavior-cloned Vision-Language-Action (VLA) driving policies struggle with rare rule-governed maneuvers at signalized intersections. Braking and launching examples contribute little to averaged trajectory loss, while fused representations lack explicit supervision for the governing traffic-light and stop-line state. We present RedLight-VLA, a training objective that uses expert futures and automatically generated perception targets without additional manual rule annotation. First, trajectory-derived behavioral reweighting (BR) emphasizes rare deceleration and acceleration using rotation-invariant longitudinal dynamics and a scale-preserving reduction that exactly recovers the baseline when disabled. Second, parallel auxiliary (AUX) heads ground traffic-light and stop-line state in continuous post-fusion rule tokens, without autoregressive language generation or changes to the trajectory decoder. We evaluate on a curated set of 20 s sequences with a 5 s prediction horizon. Controlled variants share the same backbone, training data, decoder, and evaluation population. Against an otherwise identical VLA baseline, RedLight-VLA reduces red-light stop-line overshoot from 7.3% to6.8%, reduces stop-line velocity error by 12.7%, and improves 3 s trafficlight-sliced ADE/FDE from 0.274/0.964 m to 0.247/0.897 m. Green-light false stops increase from 3.2% to 3.9%; however, combining BR with AUX supervision mitigates the larger increase observed for AUX alone (4.0%). The combined model also improves non-traffic-light ADE/FDE from 0.268/0.956 m to 0.241/0.876 m and outperforms either mechanism alone on all four sliced displacement measures.

</details>


### [172] [The Potential of Haptic Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28664)
*Jianquan Wang,Haiwei Dong,Abdulmotaleb El Saddik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了触觉基础模型（HFMs）在具身AI中的变革潜力与发展路径，针对当前触觉传感因硬件异构和主动数据采集限制而缺乏泛化能力的问题，提出了从被动的大语言模型/视觉语言模型向主动触觉基础模型转变的范式，并系统评估了现有大规模触觉数据集和代表性模型（UniTouch、AnyTouch、T3、Sparsh）在TacBench上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基础模型在语言和视觉领域取得了巨大成功，但其向具身AI的扩展受到缺乏泛化触觉传感能力的瓶颈制约。消费者电子产品（智能手机、可穿戴设备、VR控制器、家用机器人、健康监测设备）需要安全且自适应的物理交互，而当前触觉模型受限于硬件异构性和主动物理数据采集的需求，仍然高度任务特定化，缺乏灵活性。

Method: 本文提出了触觉基础模型（HFMs）的概念框架，阐述了从被动LLM/VLM向主动HFM转变所需的四个核心维度：动作耦合（action coupling）、物理动力学表示空间（physical dynamical representation space）、连续时间序列数据粒度（continuous time-series data granularity）以及基于动作条件化的未来状态预测（action-conditioned future state prediction）。同时综合了现有大规模触觉数据集，并基于TacBench基准对UniTouch、AnyTouch、T3和Sparsh四种模型进行了力估计、滑移检测和相对姿态估计任务的系统性评估。

Result: 本文系统性地对比了四种代表性触觉模型在TacBench上三个关键任务（力估计、滑移检测、相对姿态估计）中的表现，并综合梳理了现有大规模触觉数据集，为触觉基础模型的发展提供了基准参考和量化依据。

Conclusion: 触觉基础模型是具身AI发展的关键方向，需要从感知被动性向交互主动性的范式转变。通过动作耦合、物理动力学表示、连续时间序列建模和未来状态预测四个核心维度的突破，有望克服当前触觉模型任务特定化的局限，实现跨硬件、跨任务的泛化触觉能力，从而支撑消费电子产品中安全自适应的物理交互。

Abstract: Despite the success of foundation models in language and vision, their expansion into embodied AI is bottlenecked by a lack of generalized touch sensing. This limitation is especially relevant to consumer electronics, where smartphones, wearables, VR controllers, home robots, and health monitoring devices require safe and adaptive physical interaction. Constrained by hardware heterogeneity and the necessity of active physical data collection, current haptic models remain rigidly task-specific. To overcome these limitations, this article explores the transformative potential and developmental trajectory of Haptic Foundation Models (HFMs). We detail the paradigm shift required to transition from passive Large Language Models and Vision Language Models into active HFMs across four core dimensions: action coupling, physical dynamical representation space, continuous time-series data granularity, and action-conditioned future state prediction. Furthermore, we synthesize existing large-scale tactile datasets and benchmark UniTouch, AnyTouch, T3, and Sparsh on TacBench for force estimation, slip detection, and relative pose estimation.

</details>


### [173] [Cognitively-Grounded On-Device Runtime Learning for Ground Robots in Unknown Physical Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28677)
*Yihao Cai,Yanbing Mao,Christian Lebiere*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CogRun框架，使安全关键的地面机器人在未知物理环境中仅依靠边缘AI设备进行认知驱动的运行时学习，无需先验地图或感知知识。实验在真实森林四足机器人和仿真越野自动驾驶车辆上验证了其安全性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键的机器人在未知环境中运行时，既需要安全保证又需要持续学习适应能力，但现有方法通常依赖无线通信、云端计算或先验知识，在通信受限或无连接的挑战性环境中无法适用。需要一种完全在边缘端实现的认知驱动运行时学习框架。

Method: CogRun包含三个组件：Learning-Agent（采用认知神经学习架构，包含专用回放缓冲区、认知驱动经验采样、以及actor-critic强化学习与基于实例学习的安全感知动作融合）、Rational-Agent（非学习模块，专门处理安全关键功能）、Coordinator（管理两个Agent间的交互以促进安全高效的运行时学习）。整个自主堆栈（感知、学习、控制）完全在边缘AI设备上运行。

Result: 在真实野外森林中的四足机器人和仿真荒野中的越野自动驾驶车辆上进行的实验表明，CogRun能够实现安全且高效的运行时学习，使机器人在复杂未知环境中安全地持续与环境交互并提升任务性能。

Conclusion: CogRun通过在边缘AI设备上实现完全自主的运行时学习框架，消除了对无线通信的依赖，为无连接或受限连接环境下的安全关键地面机器人提供了可行的学习和适应方案，扩展了其应用范围。

Abstract: This paper presents \ul{CogRun}, a framework that enables safety-critical ground robots to perform cognitively-grounded runtime learning entirely on edge-AI devices in unknown physical environments, without prior maps or perceptual knowledge. CogRun consists of three components: a Learning-Agent, a Rational-Agent, and a Coordinator. The Learning-Agent is novel in cognitive-neural learning architecture, which featurs dedicated replay buffers, cognition-driven experience sampling, and a safety-aware action blending of actor-critic reinforcement learning (RL) with instance-based learning (IBL). The Rational-Agent is a non-learning module that complements the Learning-Agent by exclusively handling safety-critical functions, while the Coordinator manages interactions between the two agents to promote safe and efficient runtime learning. CogRun's full autonomy stack (i.e., perception, learning, and control) on edge-AI devices eliminates dependence on wireless communications, enabling broader applications in challenging environments with limited or no connectivity. Experiments on a quadruped robot in real-world wild forests and on an off-road autonomous vehicle in a simulated wild forest demonstrate that CogRun enables safe and efficient runtime learning, allowing robots to safely and continuously interact with the physical world for enhancing task performance in complex, unknown environments.

</details>


### [174] [RoboGesture: Real-Time Semantic-aligned Co-Speech Gestures Generation for Humanoid Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28693)
*Zifan Wang,Ziang Ren,Pengyang Shi,Zirui Wang,Chenghuai Lin,Tianze Wang,Zekun Qi,Liangliang Zhao,He Wang,Li Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboGesture是一个面向人形机器人的端到端框架，通过协同设计数据、建模与控制，解决机器人语音驱动手势生成中的三大挑战（数据稀缺、模态遮蔽、仿真到现实差距），在Unitree G1上实现了实时、安全且语义匹配的手势响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人需要以同步且语义有意义的手势回应人类语音以实现自然交互，但面临三个关键障碍：语义丰富数据集稀缺、模型在训练中忽视音频线索而依赖运动惯性（模态遮蔽）、以及物理安全性上的仿真到现实差距。

Method: (1) 构建RoboGesture数据集，包含300+手势类别，并开发自动化管线合成大规模无碰撞、机器人特定音频-运动对；(2) 提出层次语义-声学对齐器（Hierarchical Semantic-Acoustic Aligner），直接从原始音频token提取多粒度韵律和语义线索；(3) 基于条件流匹配的扩散Transformer构建流式条件运动生成器；(4) 引入Anti-Inertia CFG Masking，迫使模型主动从音频模态挖掘控制信号，防止塌缩到重复历史模式；(5) 基于MPC的安全滤波器确保物理硬件上的实时无碰撞执行。

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人上进行的实验表明，RoboGesture与最先进基线相比，生成的手势更安全、节奏感更强、语义适配度更高。

Conclusion: RoboGesture通过数据-建模-控制三者的协同设计，系统性地解决了人形机器人语音驱动手势生成的核心挑战，验证了在真实硬件上实现完整交互系统的可行性。

Abstract: Enabling humanoid robots to respond to human speech with synchronized and semantically meaningful gestures is fundamental to natural human-robot interaction. However, this task faces three critical barriers: the scarcity of semantically rich datasets, the "modality eclipse" where models ignore audio cues in favor of kinematic inertia, and the sim-to-real gap regarding physical safety. We propose RoboGesture, a robot-centric framework that co-designs data, modeling, and control to power a complete interactive human-humanoid system in which the robot listens, responds, and gestures in real time. We first establish the RoboGesture dataset featuring over 300 gesture categories and develop an automated pipeline to synthesize large-scale collision-free, robot-specific audio-motion pairs. Our architecture features a Hierarchical Semantic-Acoustic Aligner that extracts multi-granular prosodic and semantic cues directly from raw audio tokens. These cues drive a Streaming Conditional Motion Generator based on a diffusion transformer with conditional flow matching. To ensure high responsiveness, we introduce Anti-Inertia CFG Masking, which prevents the model from collapsing into repetitive historical patterns by compelling it to proactively mine control signals from the audio modality. Finally, an MPC-based safety filter ensures real-time, collision-free execution on physical hardware. Experiments on a Unitree G1 humanoid demonstrate that RoboGesture generates safer, more rhythmic, and more semantically appropriate responses compared to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [175] [Multi-Group Pipe Routing under Permanent Geometric Occupancy: Problem, Benchmark, and Classical Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28697)
*Deng Quan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对增材制造中液压元件内部流体通道设计的特殊问题——多群管道布线（multi-group pipe routing），提出了在永久几何占用约束下的问题形式化定义、3D基准测试套件，以及经典多智能体路径规划算法（CBS、PBS、优先级规划）的适配基线与实验结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增材制造允许液压元件内部流体通道沿自由3D路径布局，与传统的钻孔通道不同。在旋转直驱伺服阀的多组通道布局中，一旦通道放置就永久占用空间，后续通道必须避开已有几何——这与经典多智能体路径规划（MAPF）中智能体移动后释放空间的假设不同，因此需要专门的形式化定义和基准测试。

Method: （1）提出多群管道布线问题的形式化定义，引入几何对偶见证冲突（geometric dual-witness conflicts）；（2）构建可控难度的3D基准：两个走廊生成器×两个障碍物绘制器，附带可行性见证；（3）将CBS、PBS和优先级规划（PP）三种经典算法适配到此问题耦合场景中。

Result: 在固定时间预算下评估成功率。PlaneSlice困难实例上，CBS明显优于PBS和PP；而较简单的单元则验证了整体流程的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文的目标是提供一个可复现的问题定义、基准测试套件和经典基线，作为后续研究的基础，而非提出新的最优MAPF算法。

Abstract: Additive manufacturing (AM) enables compact hydraulic components whose internal fluid channels can follow free-form 3D paths rather than conventionally drilled holes. A representative case is multi-group channel layout in rotary direct-drive servo valves: once a channel is placed it permanently occupies volume, so later channels must clear earlier geometry--unlike classical multi-agent pathfinding (MAPF), where agents free space after moving. We study this setting as multi-group pipe routing under permanent geometric occupancy. Our contributions are a problem formalization with geometric dual-witness conflicts, a constructive 3D benchmark (two corridor generators x two obstacle painters, controlled difficulty, feasibility witnesses), and baseline results for CBS, PBS, and priority planning adapted to this coupling. We evaluate by success rate under a fixed time budget. PlaneSlice hard instances clearly rank CBS above PBS and PP, while easier cells validate the pipeline. The goal is a reproducible problem definition, suite, and classical baselines--not a new optimal MAPF algorithm.

</details>


### [176] [RoboPhys-3D: A Comprehensive Embodied World Model Evaluation via 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28718)
*Tianyi Wang,Jiazhou Chen,Yiming Xu,Xiangyu Li,Tianyi Zeng,Chih-Hsien Chou,Ning Lu,Liang Peng,Junfeng Jiao,Christian Claudel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RoboPhys-3D，一个基于RoboTwin 2.0构建的3D接地具身世界模型（EWM）基准，覆盖50个操作任务、5,000个回合和25,000个多视角地面真值视频。该基准通过统一的3D重建管线处理生成视频和真值视频，以区分重建误差与生成误差，并组织50个互补指标到18个子维度、4个层次进行评估。实验显示Cosmos 3获得最高RoboPhyscore（0.6330，为真值的92.7%），同时揭示感知级和VLM判断无法捕获的状态级与执行级失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身世界模型基准缺乏统一的3D接地协议，无法验证生成视频是否保持底层3D场景状态或能否转化为可执行动作，导致对EWM能力的评估不全面。

Method: RoboPhys-3D基于RoboTwin 2.0构建，包含50个操作任务、5,000个回合和25,000个多视角真值视频。关键设计是生成视频与真值视频经过相同的3D重建管线，从而分离重建误差与生成误差。基准构建了50个互补指标，分为18个子维度、4个层次（像素级保真度、3D几何一致性、状态级理解、任务级完整性），并提出两个聚合分数：Average Full Score（层次化平均所有50个指标）和RoboPhyscore（任务对齐分数，平均与任务成功最相关的指标）。

Result: 在四个代表性视频世界模型中，Cosmos 3取得最高RoboPhyscore（0.6330，为真值的92.7%）。状态级和执行级的接地指标揭示了感知级及视觉语言模型判断无法捕获的大量失败。RoboPhyscore与人类评估高度一致（Pearson r=0.9761，Spearman ρ=0.8962）。

Conclusion: RoboPhys-3D证明了对EWM进行3D接地、执行感知评估的重要性，强调仅靠感知级和VLM判断会遗漏关键的状态一致性和可执行性缺陷。RoboPhyscore与人类评估的高度相关性验证了该评估框架的有效性。

Abstract: Video world models increasingly serve as data engines, action planners, and simulators for embodied AI, but conventional embodied world model (EWM) benchmarks lack a unified 3D-grounded protocol for establishing whether generated rollouts preserve the underlying 3D scene state or translate into executable actions. We introduce RoboPhys-3D, a 3D-grounded EWM benchmark built on RoboTwin 2.0, covering 50 manipulation tasks across four regimes, with 5,000 episodes and 25,000 multi-view ground-truth videos.
