A defining feature of RoboPhys-3D is that generated and ground-truth videos are processed through the same 3D reconstruction pipeline, enabling reconstruction-induced error to be distinguished from generation-induced error. The RoboPhys-3D benchmark organizes 50 complementary metrics into 18 sub-dimensions across four levels: pixel-level fidelity, 3D geometry consistency, state-level understanding, and task-level completeness. We further introduce Average Full Score, a hierarchical score averaging all 50 metrics for comprehensive evaluation, and RoboPhyscore, a compact task-aligned score averaging the metrics most strongly correlated with task success. Among the four representative video world models, Cosmos 3 achieves the highest RoboPhyscore (0.6330, 92.7% of ground truth), while state- and execution-grounded metrics reveal substantial failures that perceptual and vision-language model-based judgments fail to capture. RoboPhyscore further exhibits strong agreement with human evaluation (Pearson r = 0.9761 and Spearman \r{ho} = 0.8962), demonstrating the importance of grounded, execution-aware evaluation for EWM capability.

</details>


### [177] [Vehicle Drift Emergence: Continuous Evolution from Grip Driving to the Handling Limit via Boundary Exploration Learning Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28723)
*Sheng Zhao,Binh-Minh Nguyen,Hangyu Lu,Xiaodong Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过边界探索学习模型预测控制（BE-LMPC）在重复圈速最小化任务中实现了漂移的自主涌现，无需任何显式漂移参考。在摩擦系数0.6时圈速从49.95s降至25.50s，漂移在第11圈首次涌现并达到16.5°侧偏角和0.894后轴利用率；而在高摩擦系数（0.8-1.2）下无漂移发生，说明漂移是极限驾驶性能需求接近轮胎能力时的条件性延续，而非单独预设的运动模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漂移控制器通常需要预设漂移平衡点、侧偏角参考或轨迹，即"如何执行漂移"已被定义，但从抓地驾驶到漂移的连续过渡问题尚未解决。作者希望在不包含任何显式漂移参考的控制器目标和奖励函数中，研究漂移如何在性能优化中自然涌现。

Method: 提出边界探索学习模型预测控制（BE-LMPC）：基于已完成圈宿构建经验安全集和局部平移终端代价，在固定全局速度约束下迭代优化空间速度分配，逐步探索更大的侧偏角和横摆角速度包络，同时保持可恢复性。分析中采用组合滑移Fiala轮胎模型，验证了轮胎从黏附到滑移过渡的连续性条件。

Result: 在摩擦系数0.6条件下，圈速从第3圈的49.95s降至第12圈的25.50s，漂移在第11圈首次涌现，第12圈达到16.5°侧偏角和0.894后轴利用率。摩擦系数0.8-1.2范围内均未检测到漂移；即使在1.2时达到相似峰值速度，后轴利用率仅为0.483。分析表明外力条件平滑变化时，轮胎从黏附到滑移的过渡不会引起轮胎力或车辆状态的突变。

Conclusion: 漂移应被理解为极限操控的条件性延续——当性能需求增加并接近可用轮胎能力时自然出现，而非需要单独预设的运动模式。该工作揭示了漂移涌现的连续物理机制，为自主赛车控制中处理极限驾驶提供了新视角。

Abstract: Automated drift controllers commonly track a prescribed drift equilibrium, sideslip reference, or trajectory. These formulations establish how to execute drift, whereas the continuous transition from grip driving to drift near the handling limit remains unresolved. This paper defines drift emergence in a repetitive lap time minimization task, where neither the controller objective nor the reward contains an explicit drift reference. A boundary exploration learning model predictive controller (BE-LMPC) constructs an empirical safe set and a locally shifted terminal cost from completed laps. By iteratively improving spatial speed allocation under a fixed global speed bound, the controller progressively explores larger sideslip and yaw rate envelopes while preserving recoverability. As lap performance improves, sustained sideslip and pronounced yaw motion emerge while the rear axle approaches saturation. Analysis shows that, when external conditions vary smoothly, the transition from tire adhesion to sliding does not itself cause abrupt changes in tire force or vehicle state. The combined-slip Fiala model satisfies this continuity condition at the transition. At a tire road friction coefficient of 0.6, lap time decreases from 49.95 s on Lap~3 to 25.50 s on Lap~12, with drift first emerging on Lap~11. Lap~12 reaches 16.5$^\circ$ sideslip and 0.894 rear axle utilization. In contrast, no drift is detected for friction coefficients from 0.8 to 1.2; at 1.2, a similar peak speed is achieved with only 0.483 rear axle utilization. These results characterize drift as a conditional continuation of limit handling that emerges when increasing performance demand approaches the available tire capacity, rather than as a separately prescribed motion mode.

</details>


### [178] [Generation of High-Level Concepts in 3D Scene Graphs via Autoregressive Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28733)
*Jose Andres Millan-Romera,Samuel Cognolato,Holger Voos,Jose Luis Sanchez-Lopez,Luciano Serafini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统一的自回归扩散图生成模型，用于从观测到的垂直平面自下而上构建完整的三维室内场景图（3DSG），该模型联合学习图结构和节点特征，适用于任意层级深度的场景图生成，并在多种数据集上超越现有基线。同时提出基于Fused Gromov-Wasserstein距离的图级评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的高层概念生成依赖针对特定概念类别的手工规则，而基于学习的方法需要为图结构和空间节点特征分别训练独立模型，这限制了模型对新型别和更复杂层级结构的可扩展性。需要一种统一的生成方法来解决这一问题。

Method: 提出了一个统一的自回归扩散式图生成模型，联合学习图结构和节点特征，从观测到的垂直平面出发自下而上地构建任意层级深度的完整3DSG。同时提出了Fused Gromov-Wasserstein距离的改编版本，用于对生成的3DSG进行原则性的图级评估。

Result: 该方法在涵盖合成场景、真实建筑平面图和机器人传感器数据的多个3DSG数据集上，一致地超越了所有基于学习的基线和随机基线。在最大层级结构和真实单层数据上，甚至超过了具有目标图规模oracle访问权限的一次性模型。

Conclusion: 统一的自回归扩散生成框架能够有效联合建模图结构和空间特征，突破传统方法在类别和层级深度上的限制，为室内3D场景图的增量构建提供了更具可扩展性的解决方案，同时FGW距离的改编为3DSG生成提供了更可靠的评估指标。

Abstract: Indoor 3D Scene Graphs (3DSGs) represent environments as multi-layer hierarchies that connect observed geometric primitives (e.g., planes) to higher-level metric-semantic concepts (e.g., rooms, floors, buildings), enabling incremental spatial reasoning for robotic perception and SLAM. However, classical high-level concept generation approaches rely on hand-crafted rules for specific concept classes, while learning-based methods require separate models for graph structure and spatial node features (e.g., centroids), which limits scalability to novel classes and more complex hierarchies. We propose a unified autoregressive diffusion-based graph generative model that jointly learns structure and features, constructing complete 3DSGs bottom-up from observed vertical planes across arbitrary hierarchy depths. Our method consistently surpasses all learning-based and random baselines across 3DSG datasets spanning synthetic scenes, real architectural floor plans, and robotic sensor data, with varying layout complexity and hierarchy depth, and surpasses a one-shot model with oracle access to the target graph size on the largest hierarchy and on real single-floor data. Finally, we propose an adaptation of the Fused Gromov--Wasserstein distance for principled graph-level evaluation of generated 3DSGs against ground truth.

</details>


### [179] [Adversarial Calibration Attack on Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28778)
*Liangkai Liu,Qingzhao Zhang,Kang G. Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出对抗校准攻击（ACA），首次实现对自动驾驶相机-LiDAR在线标定系统的物理攻击。通过单个对抗海报同时欺骗误标定检测器触发标定流程，并引导标定估计器产生错误变换，从而在感知、规划到控制的整个链路中传播系统性错误。实验表明该攻击在各基准数据集、CARLA模拟器及真实Husky机器人上均能有效诱导标定误差甚至引发碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆依赖相机-LiDAR标定实现多模态融合，但标定会因振动、温度变化或传感器轻微位移等因素产生漂移，因此需要在线标定算法在运行时检测并纠正失准。然而，现有自动驾驶攻击大多假设标定正确，忽视了在线标定这一潜在攻击面。作者识别出在线标定可被利用为新的攻击平面：一旦标定更新被污染，误差会持续存在于后续融合操作中，从感知传播到规划与控制，造成系统性错误。

Method: ACA的攻击流程分为两个阶段：(1) 首先欺骗误标定检测器以触发标定过程；(2) 随后将标定估计器引导至错误的变换参数。方法的核心是一个统一优化框架，同时设计对抗海报的几何形状与纹理图案，以同时满足两个攻击目标。对抗海报作为物理攻击载体，无需对车辆软件系统进行传统意义上的入侵，即可破坏在线标定流程。

Result: 在KITTI和nuScenes等基准数据集上，ACA可以诱导高达33.9度的平均旋转标定误差，严重降低目标检测性能。在CARLA模拟器中，当攻击者在精心构造的脆弱场景中注入被污染的标定值时，该攻击可导致车辆碰撞。在真实的Husky机器人平台上，打印的对抗海报成功复现了标定误差，验证了攻击在物理世界的可行性。

Conclusion: 在线标定是自动驾驶系统的一个实用且安全关键的攻击面。通过简单的一张对抗海报即可实现从感知到控制的端到端错误传播，凸显了当前在线标定协议在安全性设计上的不足，需要对在线标定机制的鲁棒性和对抗性防护进行重新审视。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles (AVs) rely on accurate camera-LiDAR calibration for multimodal sensor fusion. In practice, calibration can drift due to vibration, temperature variation, or minor sensor displacement, motivating online calibration algorithms that detect and correct misalignment at runtime while allowing the vehicle to continue operating without a factory visit. Existing AV attacks largely assume correct calibration. We instead identify online sensor calibration as a new attack plane. A corrupted calibration update can persist across subsequent fusion operations, causing system-wide errors that propagate from perception to planning and control. We present Adversarial Calibration Attack (ACA), the first physical attack against camera-LiDAR online calibration. Using a single adversarial poster, ACA first spoofs the miscalibration detector to trigger the calibration process and then steers the calibration estimator toward an incorrect transformation. A unified optimization jointly designs the poster's geometry and texture for both objectives. We evaluate ACA across benchmark datasets, simulation, and physical experiments. On benchmark datasets such as KITTI and nuScenes, ACA induces up to 33.9 degrees mean rotational calibration error, thereby severely degrading object detection. In the CARLA simulator, the attack causes a collision when the corrupted calibration is accepted in vulnerable scenarios crafted by the attacker. On a real Husky robot, a printed adversarial poster successfully reproduces the calibration error. These results demonstrate that online calibration is a practical and safety-critical attack surface for AVs.

</details>


### [180] [Dual Park-Ravani Interpolation of Rigid Motions: Acceleration-Field Continuity and Holonomic Hermite Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28826)
*Daniel Condurache*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将Park-Ravani构造（在SO(3)上通过指数化三次典范坐标多项式生成二次连续、框架不变样条）推广到正交对偶张量群（刚体位移的表示），使得对偶展开的递推式可在不改形式的情况下迁移，从而实现指定刚体位姿的插值、体对偶扭转及其一阶导数的连续性，并进一步证明所得曲线具有连续的物理加速度场。此外，论文区分了代数对偶传递与时间微分延拓，分析了超对偶插值的一阶完整缺陷，并通过在对数坐标中的三次或五次Hermite插值加以消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚体位姿插值在机器人学、机构学等领域具有广泛应用。已有的Park-Ravani构造在SO(3)上表现出色，但刚体运动需要同时处理旋转和平移。将对偶张量引入该构造可以统一表示刚体位移，但需要严格证明其在高阶运动学（如物理加速度）层面的正确性，以及解决任意节点数据插值时的完整性问题。

Method: 将Park-Ravani三次样条构造从SO(3)迁移至正交对偶张量群，通过对偶扩展指数映射的右Jacobian及其一阶Fréchet导数来简洁表述递推式。利用对偶空间扭转的高阶刚体运动学证明物理加速度的连续性。同时区分代数对偶传递与时间微分延拓，引入超对偶代数分析其同时一阶使用的行为，定义并分析一阶完整缺陷，最后采用对偶对数坐标下的三次或五次Hermite插值消除该缺陷。

Result: 构造成功迁移至正交对偶张量群，实现了指定刚体位姿的插值以及体对偶扭转及其一阶导数的连续性；证明所得曲线具有连续的物理加速度场。通过非交换三姿态数值示例验证了递推式、所有节点连续性声明以及维度协变性（米制到毫米制）。定义并分析了超对偶插值的一阶完整缺陷，给出精确反例，并通过对偶对数坐标中的Hermite插值成功消除缺陷。

Conclusion: Park-Ravani构造可以自然地迁移到正交对偶张量群上，并保持其形式与连续性质；高阶对偶运动学可以正确反映物理加速度。代数对偶传递与时间微分延拓需要明确区分，任意延拓节点数据的插值不一定是完整的，但通过对偶对数空间中的Hermite插值可以消除完整缺陷，为刚体运动插值提供了更完善的理论框架。

Abstract: The Park-Ravani construction generates a twice continuously differentiable, frame-invariant spline on SO(3) by exponentiating cubic canonical-coordinate polynomials. We show that the construction transfers, without changing form, to the group of orthogonal dual tensors, a representation of rigid displacements. The transferred recurrence is stated compactly through the dual extension of the right Jacobian of the exponential map and its first Fréchet derivative. This yields interpolation of prescribed rigid poses and continuity of the body dual twist and its first derivative. Using the higher-order rigid-body kinematics of dual spatial twists, we then prove that the resulting curve has a continuous physical acceleration field, not merely a continuous quantity obtained by formally differentiating the dual part of a twist. We also distinguish algebraic dual transfer from temporal differential prolongation: their simultaneous first-order use takes place in a hyper-dual algebra, and interpolation of arbitrary prolonged nodal data need not be holonomic. A noncommuting three-pose example verifies the recurrence, all knot continuity statements, and dimensional covariance under a change from meters to millimeters. We define and analyze the first-order holonomy defect of a generic hyper-dual interpolant, exhibit an exact counterexample, and remove the defect by cubic or quintic Hermite interpolation in dual logarithmic coordinates.

</details>


### [181] [Brain-Language-Action (BLA) Models: Language-Conditioned EEG for Robotics Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28967)
*Alexandr Plashchinsky*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出脑-语言-动作（BLA）框架，通过语言条件化将少量可区分的脑状态动态映射到更大的机器人动作空间，在BCI Competition IV 2a数据集上的四类运动想象脑电信号中实现了840种动作映射下90%的逐token准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于EEG的机器人控制被建模为直接分类问题，将神经信号映射到固定离散动作集。然而EEG信号的可分离性有限且噪声高，难以扩展到细粒度的机器人控制空间。核心动机是：通过语言作为桥梁，让少量可靠区分的脑状态能够应用于更大的全局动作空间，从而突破直接分类范式的扩展性瓶颈。

Method: BLA框架采用两阶段训练：(1) 评估多种EEG编码器架构，利用BCI Competition IV 2a数据集进行受试者特定的四类运动想象分类，将250Hz、3.5秒、22通道的EEG样本转换为5个128维的脑token嵌入；(2) 将这些嵌入投影到预训练大语言模型（LLM）的嵌入空间中，与语言指令联合微调，自回归生成结构化的三token无人机动作序列。系统在4种神经状态与7种飞行动作组合之间实现了840种可能的语言定义映射。

Result: 在840种语言定义的映射空间中，BLA系统在评估中实现了90%的逐token准确率，初步证明了语言条件化可以扩展EEG机器人接口的有效控制范围，而无需相应增加可直接区分的神经状态数量。

Conclusion: 语言条件化是扩展EEG机器人控制有效范围的可行途径。通过将EEG脑token嵌入与大语言模型对齐，可以用少量神经状态覆盖更大的动作空间，为未来高维细粒度脑机接口控制提供了新范式。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG)-based robotic control is commonly formulated as a direct classification problem, in which electrical neural signals are mapped to a fixed set of discrete actions. However, the limited separability and high noise of EEG signals make it difficult to scale this approach to fine-grained robotic control spaces. We introduce Brain-Language-Action (BLA) models, a framework in which language conditions the interpretation of neural representations for robotic action generation. In a BLA, a small set of reliably distinguishable brain states can be dynamically associated with different actions through a language-defined control mapping, allowing a small number of neural classes to apply to a larger global action space.
  We develop a proof-of-concept BLA for drone control using motor-imagery EEG from the BCI Competition IV 2a dataset. The system is trained in two stages. First, we evaluate multiple candidate EEG encoder architectures using subject-specific four-class motor-imagery classification, converting 250Hz, 3.5-second, 22-channel EEG samples into five 128-dimensional brain-token embeddings. Second, these embeddings are projected into the embedding space of a pretrained large language model (LLM) and jointly fine-tuned with language instructions to autoregressively generate structured three-token drone actions. Across 840 possible language-defined mappings between four neural states and seven flight action combinations, the resulting BLA achieves 90% per-token accuracy during evaluation. These results provide an initial demonstration that language conditioning can expand the effective control range of EEG-based robotic interfaces without requiring a corresponding increase in the number of directly distinguishable neural states.

</details>


### [182] [Hydra: A Navigation World Action Model with Discrete Latent Planning and Continuous Flow-Matching Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28995)
*Mohammad Nazeri,Alexandyr Card,Samira Huber,Anuj Pokhrel,Yujun Wang,Ruben Hammele,Daeun Song,Sören Pirk,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Hydra是一种离散世界动作模型（Discrete World Action Model），通过将规划器（采样器和评估器）嵌入生成模型内部，在统一潜空间上建立视觉状态、物理姿态和控制动作的离散词汇表，实现无需解码回像素空间的离散潜规划（DLP），并结合条件流匹配（Flow Matching）生成连续控制轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型让机器人能够想象可能的未来，但在实时控制中受限于表示对齐问题：生成模型与规划器在解耦的流形上运行，规划器每次候选评估都需解码回高维像素空间，这一解码步骤成为实时控制的主要瓶颈。

Method: Hydra建立覆盖视觉状态、物理姿态和控制动作的统一潜流形，通过模态特定的量化瓶颈（Vector-Quantized bottlenecks）压缩为动力学意图（kinodynamic intents）和视觉状态的离散词汇表。规划直接在离散空间内通过Kinematic-Perceptual Cost进行候选排序，无需像素解码；随后用条件流匹配（Conditional Flow Matching）将选中的离散意图映射为连续执行轨迹。

Result: 在两个物理机器人平台上评估，Hydra在目标导向规划任务中优于最先进的世界模型，同时在闭环执行能力上与领先的反应式基础策略相当或更优。

Conclusion: 通过将规划器迁入模型内部并统一离散潜空间，Hydra解决了世界模型用于实时控制时的表示对齐与解码瓶颈，实现了高效的离散潜规划与连续执行的无缝结合。

Abstract: World models let robots imagine possible futures, but exploiting this capability for real-time control is bottlenecked by a representation misalignment: the generative model and the planner operate on decoupled manifolds, so the planner has no shared structure to search over and must instead decode every candidate back into high-dimensional pixel space to evaluate it. This decoding step is a major obstacle to real-time control on physical hardware. In this paper, we present Hydra, a discrete World Action Model that closes this gap by moving the planner, both the sampler and the evaluator, inside the model. Hydra establishes a unified latent manifold over visual states, physical poses, and control actions, then compresses this manifold through modality-specific Vector-Quantized bottlenecks into discrete vocabularies of kinodynamic intents and visual states. Because candidates are now drawn directly from this shared manifold, sampling is informed by the model's own understanding of the observation rather than proposed blind, and evaluation happens natively within the discrete space: candidates are ranked by a Kinematic-Perceptual Cost, without ever decoding to pixels. We term this Discrete Latent Planning (DLP). Because planning over discrete intents alone cannot supply the smooth, continuous commands physical actuation requires, Hydra pairs DLP with conditional Flow Matching, which maps each selected intent to a continuous trajectory for execution. Evaluated on two physical robotic platforms, Hydra outperforms state-of-the-art world models in goal-directed planning, while matching or exceeding the closed-loop execution capabilities of leading reactive foundation policies.

</details>


### [183] [Coding What Matters: A Semantic-Aware Memory Interface for Energy-Efficient Perception in Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29000)
*Haohua Que,Handong Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MotiMem-Omega是一种语义感知的内存接口编码器，通过按语义重要性分配不同精度层级来降低自动驾驶环视相机帧写入内存时的能量消耗（与位1密度和翻转活动相关），在保留大部分感知性能（检测mAP约90%、VRU召回率91%、占用精度98%以上）的同时，将前摄像头位1密度降低52%，对应的内存接口能量降低约27%-36%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆在感知推理之前会将高分辨率环视相机帧流式写入内存，这一传感器到内存的数据通路能耗取决于位1密度和数据总线上的字节翻转活动，而不是像素语义。现有方法未能针对这一能量源头进行优化，因此需要一种语义感知的编码方案来降低内存接口能耗而不损害感知精度。

Method: MotiMem-Omega包含三个核心组件：(1) 语义重要性场，保护交通参与者（特别是弱势道路使用者），对天空和空旷背景分配较低保真度；(2) 跨数据集的位敏感性扫描确定类别权重，并设有行人、骑行者和摩托车手的安全下限；(3) 每个图像块通过最小化能量-失真联合代价来选择精度层级。当有车辆位姿可用时，使用运动补偿先验在帧间携带受保护区域。通过系数扫描的内存能量模型，从两个实测代理指标（位1密度和翻转活动）推算接口能量减少量。

Result: 在29个检测器、12个驾驶数据集、5个占用模型和5个分割网络上验证：保留约90%的检测mAP、91%的VRU召回率、98%以上的占用精度，并在能耗降低方法中实现最强的分割保留。前摄像头位1密度降低52%，对应建模的内存接口能量降低约36%（文献系数扫描下下限为27%）。相比基线和能量匹配截断，在相同或更低位1密度下获得更高保留率；而传统图像编解码器虽能保留精度但无法降低内存接口能量。

Conclusion: MotiMem-Omega证明语义感知的内存接口编码可以在降低传感器到内存路径能耗的同时保持感知性能，是传统无损或有损图像编解码器的有效替代方案。其性能在广泛的检测器、数据集和感知任务上具有鲁棒性，特别强调了弱势道路使用者的安全性保障。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles stream high-resolution surround-camera frames into memory before perception runs. This sensor-to-memory path consumes energy when cells store ones and adjacent bytes toggle on the data bus, so its cost follows bit-1 density and switching activity rather than pixel semantics. We present MotiMem-Omega, a semantic-aware memory-interface coder that lowers this cost while preserving perception predictions. Its semantic importance field protects traffic participants, especially vulnerable road users, while assigning lower fidelity to sky and empty background. Cross-dataset bit-sensitivity sweeps determine class weights, with a safety floor for pedestrians, cyclists, and motorcyclists. Each image block then selects a precision tier by minimizing a joint energy-distortion cost. When ego pose is available, a motion-compensated prior carries protected regions between frames. We estimate interface-energy reduction from the two measured proxies using a coefficient-swept memory-energy model. Across 29 detectors on 12 driving datasets, 5 occupancy models, and 5 segmentation networks, MotiMem-Omega retains about 90% of detection mean average precision, 91% of vulnerable-road-user recall, over 98% of occupancy accuracy, and the strongest segmentation retention among energy-reducing methods. It reduces front-camera bit-1 density by 52%, corresponding to a modeled memory-interface energy reduction near 36%, with a lower end of 27% under the literature coefficient sweep. It also gives higher retention than the baseline and energy-matched truncation at the same or lower bit-1 density, whereas image codecs preserve accuracy without reducing memory-interface energy.

</details>


### [184] [A Degradation-Tolerance Benchmark for Camera-Only End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29005)
*Haohua Que,Handong Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DriveDegrade是一个针对纯视觉端到端自动驾驶的图像退化鲁棒性基准，包含16种退化类型×5级严重度，覆盖15个策略，并在nuScenes、NAVSIM和CARLA上进行评估，发现不同退化类型对规划能力的影响差异显著，部分策略存在意图捷径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯视觉端到端驾驶模型即将部署，但相机流会被模糊、噪声、低光、天气、帧丢失和内存故障等退化影响。现有鲁棒性基准聚焦检测或鸟瞰感知，未针对直接驱动车辆规划的输出来评估退化容忍度，缺乏对规划输出层面图像退化耐受性的系统研究。

Method: 提出DriveDegrade基准：在图像加载器中实时注入16类退化×5种严重度，应用于15个策略；在nuScenes和NAVSIM上进行开环规划评估，并以CARLA闭环作为锚点。将退化分为"降低图像质量"与"删除图像信息"两类，通过双轴分离策略来区分真实的鲁棒性与对输入不敏感的捷径行为。

Result: 轻度退化对规划影响很小，且造成规划崩溃的退化族在中等严重度存在清晰阈值；鲁棒性因退化类型而异：模糊、JPEG和雨滴对规划损害最大，而天气和比特错误容忍范围较广；平坦曲线具有歧义性，通过双轴分析发现发布的视觉-语言-动作规划器在两条轴上均呈现平坦，遮蔽全部六个相机仅导致11.5%的性能损失。

Conclusion: DriveDegrade揭示了端到端驾驶策略的图像退化容忍度具有退化依赖性和阈值性，同时量化了部分策略的自我状态捷径，表明仅看单一退化曲线可能会将捷径误判为鲁棒性，需要区分信息退化与信息删除两类退化来正确评估规划器的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Camera-only end-to-end (E2E) driving models are nearing deployment, where the camera stream is degraded by blur, noise, low light, weather, frame loss, and memory faults. How much a policy tolerates before its driving breaks is unclear. Corruption-robustness benchmarks target detection or bird's-eye-view perception, not the planning output that drives the car. We present DriveDegrade, a benchmark for image-degradation tolerance in camera-only E2E driving. Sixteen corruption families at five severities are injected on the fly inside the image loader, one operator reaching fifteen policies, and we evaluate open-loop planning on nuScenes and NAVSIM plus a CARLA closed-loop anchor. First, mild degradation barely affects planning, and the families that break it have a clear threshold at mid severity. Second, fragility is corruption-dependent: blur, JPEG, and raindrop damage planning most, while weather and bit error are tolerated far into the range. Third, a flat curve is ambiguous, so we separate corruptions that degrade the image from those that remove it. A planner that reads its camera must lose accuracy when information is deleted, whatever it does under quality loss. On these two axes the planners separate sharply, quantifying the ego-status shortcut without mistaking indifference for robustness. A released vision-language-action planner is flat on both axes, and blinding all six of its cameras costs it only 11.5 percent.

</details>


### [185] [Teaching Robot Policies to Humans Using Erroneous Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29023)
*Rithika Narayan,Suresh Kumaar Jayaraman,Henny Admoni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出使用错误示例（erroneous examples）来教人类理解机器人策略，扩展了现有的策略教学框架。用户研究发现，观看不正确的机器人行为演示并口头表达推理过程能够提高人类对机器人策略的长期记忆保持，并发现使用类似逆强化学习推理方式的参与者表现最好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机协作中，让机器人策略对人类透明至关重要。虽然基于演示的解释已被广泛研究并借鉴教育领域方法，但尚无单一教学方法被证明在各领域、难度、学习者等变量下均有效。受传统课堂中错误示例教学的启发，作者希望探索用错误示例来教授机器人策略的有效性。

Method: 作者扩展了现有的策略教学框架，引入错误示例教学方法：向参与者展示不正确的机器人行为演示，让他们纠正动作以与实际策略对齐。通过用户研究收集参与者的表现数据，评估策略记忆的保持效果，并对参与者进行学习风格分类以分析不同推理方式的表现差异。

Result: 研究发现，观看错误演示并口头表达预测机器人动作的推理过程，能显著提高参与者对策略的长期记忆保持，这与课堂上错误示例的效果一致。此外，参与者可按学习风格分类，其中使用类似逆强化学习推理方式的参与者在策略预测任务上表现最佳。

Conclusion: 错误示例是人机协作中教授机器人策略的有效教学方法，能够提升人类对机器人行为的理解和长期记忆，为机器人如何更好地教育人类理解其策略提供了新方向。

Abstract: Human-robot collaboration describes the process of humans and autonomous agents working together to accomplish common goals. This process is facilitated best when robot policies, or behaviors in different situations, are made transparent to humans. Demonstration-based explanations have been a focus of human-robot collaboration research, and the field has frequently drawn upon literature from education to improve how humans are taught robot policies. However, no single teaching method has been proven effective across domains, difficulties, learners, and other variables; the question of how humans can most effectively be taught robot policies remains open.
  In traditional classrooms, learners are shown erroneous examples, in which they reflect on and correct incorrect responses to understand common pitfalls when learning a concept. We propose using erroneous examples to teach robot policies, extending an existing policy teaching framework. We conduct a user study in which participants view incorrect demonstrations of robot behavior and correct the actions to align with the actual policy. Our findings suggest that viewing these incorrect demonstrations and verbalizing one's reasoning in predicting a robot's actions improves retention of the policy over time, in agreement with the effect of erroneous examples in classrooms. We also categorize participants into distinct learning styles and establish that participants using inverse reinforcement learning-like reasoning perform best on policy prediction tasks. With this work, we aim to advance the methods by which robots educate humans on their policies.

</details>


### [186] [DREAM: Deployment-Time Demonstration Generation via Real-to-Sim for Scalable Policy Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29078)
*Makoto Sato,Tatsuya Matsushima,Yutaka Matsuo,Yusuke Iwasawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DREAM框架能够从捕获的工作空间和自然语言指令中自动生成VLA模型的微调数据，无需任务特定的人类示范，通过工作空间重建、LLM指令翻译、任务与运动规划生成可行轨迹，并在随机化物体配置下增强数据用于模型微调，在真实机器人实验中验证了其对语言条件操作任务的适应性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在语言条件下的机器人操作中取得了显著进展，但将其部署到新工作空间时通常仍需要该环境的动作标注数据进行微调。通过人类遥操作收集此类数据成本高昂，尤其是在每个工作空间、物体排列或任务都可能需要新示范的情况下。因此需要一种无需任务特定人类演示即可自动生成微调数据的可扩展方案。

Method: DREAM框架包含四个关键步骤：(1) 重建捕获的工作空间；(2) 使用大语言模型将语言指令自动翻译为符号化任务目标和成功标准；(3) 使用任务与运动规划(TAMP)生成可行的机器人轨迹；(4) 在随机化物体配置下增强规划轨迹，使用生成的成功标准进行验证，并渲染为图像-动作示例用于VLA微调。

Result: 通过在语言条件操作任务上的真实机器人实验，研究了DREAM能否作为部署工作空间的可扩展数据采集系统，考察了在其自动生成数据上微调相比直接部署是否提升成功率，以及将VLA适配到新工作空间时其数据采集成本与人类遥操作的对比。

Conclusion: DREAM提供了一种无需人类遥操作即可为新工作空间生成VLA微调数据的方法，有望成为VLA模型在新环境中部署时的高效数据采集系统，降低适应新工作空间的成本。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have made strong progress in language-conditioned robot manipulation, but improving their performance in a new workspace still often requires action-labeled data from that environment. Collecting such data by human teleoperation is costly, especially when each workspace, object arrangement, or task may require new demonstrations. We present DREAM, a framework that generates fine-tuning data for a pretrained VLA from a captured workspace and a language instruction, without requiring a task-specific human demonstration. DREAM reconstructs the workspace, automatically translates the instruction into symbolic task goals and success criteria using a large language model, and uses task-and-motion planning to generate feasible robot trajectories. The planned trajectories are augmented across randomized object configurations, verified by the generated success criteria, and rendered into image-action examples for VLA fine-tuning. Through real-robot experiments on language-conditioned manipulation tasks, we study whether DREAM can serve as a scalable data-collection system for the deployment workspace by examining whether fine-tuning on its automatically generated data improves success over direct deployment and how its data-collection cost compares with human teleoperation when adapting a VLA to a new workspace.

</details>


### [187] [GHOST in the Robots: Real-Time Exocentric Dual-Robot VR Teleoperation from Onboard Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29080)
*Yichen Wei,Faisal Zaghloul,Soujanya C Aryal,Aanya K. Agrawal,Chengfan Li,Jason Xinyu Liu,James Tompkin,Stefanie Tellex*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GHOST是一个开源VR遥操作系统，允许单个操作员通过直接低层命令同时控制两个移动机械臂，仅在机载传感器感知下工作。系统通过RGB-D相机点云对齐构建外心3D工作空间，并利用学习补全改善场景覆盖，支持模式切换架构进行单臂或双臂同步控制。实验表明其成功率比平板界面高1.6-4倍，专家任务完成速度平均快1.47倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同时遥操作多个机器人可提供额外视角和协调控制，但现有系统要么需要多个操作员、要么依赖自主性、要么将操作员限制在高层命令层面，无法让单个操作员通过直接低层命令控制多台移动机械臂。

Method: GHOST采用VR界面，通过对齐多个机器人RGB-D相机的实时点云构建外心3D工作空间，并运用学习完成的场景补全提升操作员的空间感知能力；控制方面采用模式切换架构，使操作员可以单独或同时向两台机器人发送低层直接命令。

Result: 15名新手参与者实验中，成功率是现成平板界面的1.6-4倍；专家在9个具有挑战性的双机器人任务中，完成了2个平板界面无法完成的任务，且平均速度快1.47倍。

Conclusion: GHOST证明了单个操作员通过VR低层命令界面能够有效控制多台移动机械臂，在成功率和任务完成速度上均显著优于传统平板界面。

Abstract: Teleoperating multiple robots simultaneously enables additional views and coordinated control. Yet, it poses fundamental challenges: the system must present sensor data cohesively and allow operators to manage multiple robot bases, arms, and cameras while maintaining low latency. Current multi-robot teleoperation systems require multiple operators, rely on autonomy, or restrict operators to high-level commands. We present GHOST: an open-source VR teleoperation system that enables single operator control of two mobile manipulators via direct lowlevel commands using only onboard sensing. GHOST creates an exocentric 3D workspace by aligning real-time point clouds from the robots' RGB-D cameras, where scene coverage is improved through learning-based completion to aid operator spatial awareness. For control, the operator uses a mode-switching architecture to command either robot individually or both robots simultaneously. Experiments with 15 novice participants demonstrate 1.6-4x the success rate of an off-the-shelf tablet interface. For experts across nine challenging dual-robot tasks, our system enabled completion of two tasks that were infeasible with the tablet, and was 1.47x faster on average than the tablet. Website and code: https://h2r.github.io/GHOST/.

</details>


### [188] [Agri-Sim: Agricultural Simulation Platform for Embodied Intelligence Evaluation in Greenhouse Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29100)
*Shuhan Shi,Zhenfeng Xue,Minghao Mei,Chao Zheng,Nan Li,Zhonghua Miao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Agri-Sim，一个基于Unity和ROS2的农业机器人仿真平台，支持温室场景构建、虚拟感知、自主导航、运动规划和操作任务执行的闭环开发与功能评估。实验通过自主温室导航和双臂番茄采摘验证了完整的仿真工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业机器人开发需要能够同时支持真实场景构建、虚拟感知、自主导航、运动规划和操作任务执行的仿真环境，以降低开发成本并支持算法的闭环验证。

Method: Agri-Sim采用Unity和ROS2双层架构：Unity负责场景渲染、刚体动力学、碰撞检测、虚拟传感和任务状态执行；ROS2和MoveIt 2负责定位、导航、碰撞感知运动规划、逆运动学和轨迹生成。平台包含可配置的番茄温室环境、移动双臂采摘机器人、虚拟RGB-D/LiDAR/IMU/关节传感器，以及Unity与ROS2之间的双向通信接口。

Result: 通过自主温室导航和双臂番茄采摘实验，验证了平台支持虚拟传感器发布、ROS2导航、碰撞感知运动规划、移动底座控制、番茄获取、双臂交接和装箱等完整工作流，证明了Agri-Sim支持闭环集成和可重复的功能评估。

Conclusion: Agri-Sim为农业机器人在受控虚拟温室中的导航与操作工作流提供了可靠的闭环集成和可重复评估平台，为后续算法开发和Sim-to-Real迁移研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Agricultural-robot development requires simulation environments that can jointly support realistic scene construction, virtual sensing, autonomous navigation, motion planning, and manipulation-task execution. This paper presents Agri-Sim, a Unity and ROS2-based simulation platform for the closed-loop development and functional evaluation of agricultural robots. The platform contains a configurable tomato-greenhouse environment, a mobile dual-arm harvesting robot, virtual RGB-D, LiDAR, IMU, and joint sensors, and a bidirectional communication interface between Unity and ROS2. Unity is responsible for scene rendering, rigid-body dynamics, collision detection, virtual sensing, and task-state execution, whereas ROS2 and MoveIt 2 provide localization, navigation, collision-aware motion planning, inverse kinematics, and trajectory generation. Autonomous greenhouse navigation and dual-arm tomato harvesting were used to evaluate the complete simulation workflow. The experiments covered virtual sensor publication, ROS2-based navigation, collision-aware motion planning, mobile-base control, tomato acquisition, inter-arm handover, and box placement. The results demonstrate that Agri-Sim supports closed-loop integration and repeatable functional evaluation of navigation and manipulation workflows in a controlled virtual greenhouse, providing a practical foundation for subsequent algorithm development and Sim-to-Real studies.

</details>


### [189] [From Multi-Modal Paths to Executable Trajectories: A Trajectory Planning Framework for 4WIS Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29108)
*Runjiao Bao,Lin Zhang,Yongkang Xu,Shoukun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模态全局轨迹规划框架，结合模式增强的前端搜索与模式一致的逐段轨迹优化，用于四轮独立转向（4WIS）移动机器人。前端将Hybrid A*扩展到四维状态空间并融入模式切换感知代价，后端基于改进的迭代安全走廊进行逐段优化，最终在仿真和实车实验中均取得最佳综合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4WIS移动机器人支持多种运动模式，在狭窄复杂环境中具有高机动性，但现有规划方法未能充分利用这些能力，导致轨迹质量次优。

Method: 前端：将Hybrid A*扩展至包含运动模式的四维状态空间，设计模式切换感知的代价与启发式函数；提出多模态Reeds-Shepp曲线与智能终端连接策略提升搜索效率。后端：基于改进的迭代安全走廊方案，开发逐段轨迹优化框架，将离散多模态路径转化为平滑、运动学可行且模式切换平稳的连续轨迹。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提方法在安全性、到达时间、终端精度和计算时间方面均取得最佳综合性能；物理4WIS机器人的实车实验进一步验证了生成轨迹的实际有效性和可执行性。

Conclusion: 该方法为多模态移动机器人轨迹规划提供了灵活且高性能的解决方案，能够有效发挥4WIS机器人的多模式机动能力。

Abstract: Four-wheel independent steering (4WIS) mobile robots support multiple motion modes, offering high maneuverability in narrow and complex environments. However, existing planning methods often fail to fully exploit these capabilities, leading to suboptimal trajectory quality. To address this limitation, this paper proposes a multi-modal global trajectory planning framework that couples mode-augmented front-end search with mode-consistent segment-wise trajectory optimization. In the front-end stage, Hybrid A* is extended to a four-dimensional state space incorporating motion modes, while mode-switching-aware cost and heuristic functions embed mode decisions into the global search process. Multi-modal Reeds-Shepp curves and an intelligent terminal connection strategy are further designed to improve search efficiency. In the back-end stage, a segment-wise trajectory optimization framework based on an improved iterative safe corridor scheme is developed to convert discrete multi-modal paths into smooth, kinematically feasible trajectories with stationary mode transitions. Experimental results show that the proposed method achieves the best overall performance in safety, arrival time, terminal accuracy and computation time. Real-world experiments on a physical 4WIS robot further validate the practical effectiveness and executability of the generated trajectories, providing a flexible and high-performance solution for multi-modal mobile robot trajectory planning.

</details>


### [190] [CGFM-Nav: Cognitive Graph-Field Memory for Semantic-Guided Lifelong Multimodal Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29114)
*Yuxiang Xiao,Xibei Chen,Xin Zhou,Jie Chen,Yifeng Zhang,Guillaume Sartoretti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CGFM（认知图-场记忆），一种将显式关系记忆（多模态场景图）与连续空间直觉（语义前沿场）耦合的持久多模态场景表示方法，并在此基础上构建CGFM-Nav框架，通过VLM推理与验证反馈形成闭环决策，在GOAT-Bench上显著提升VLN任务的成功率与SPL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言导航（VLN）需要智能体在持续探索未知区域的同时对累积观测进行推理，但现有的环境表示方法难以同时支持显式语义记忆和连续探索引导，二者之间存在能力缺口。

Method: CGFM将物体、空间关系和视觉观测组织为多模态场景图以支持目标检索和长时程推理；当无可信目标匹配时，将图证据投影到目标条件化的语义前沿场中，引导探索朝向语义上有前景的前沿和区域。基于CGFM构建的CGFM-Nav框架整合了任务相关子图选择、VLM推理与验证反馈，形成闭环决策。

Result: 在GOAT-Bench上，使用相同的Qwen3-VL-8B骨干模型，CGFM-Nav将总体成功率从53.2%提升至63.0%，SPL从30.0%提升至39.6%。

Conclusion: 实验表明，将显式语义记忆与语义引导探索相结合能有效提升VLN任务的导航性能，验证了CGFM设计的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) requires agents to reason over accumulated observations while continuously exploring unseen regions. However, existing environment representations often struggle to jointly support explicit semantic memory and continuous exploration guidance. To address this challenge, we propose Cognitive Graph-Field Memory (CGFM), a persistent multimodal scene representation that couples explicit relational memory with continuous spatial intuition. CGFM organizes objects, spatial relations, and visual observations into a multimodal scene graph, enabling target retrieval and long-horizon reasoning across navigation tasks. When no reliable target match is identified, graph-based evidence is projected into a goal-conditioned semantic-frontier field to guide exploration toward semantically promising frontiers and regions. Building upon CGFM, we introduce CGFM-Nav, a foundation-model-based framework for lifelong multimodal navigation that integrates task-relevant subgraph selection, VLM reasoning, and verification feedback into a closed decision loop. Preliminary experiments on GOAT-Bench show that, under the same Qwen3-VL-8B backbone, CGFM-Nav improves the overall success rate from 53.2% to 63.0% and SPL from 30.0% to 39.6%, demonstrating the effectiveness of combining explicit semantic memory with semantic-guided exploration.

</details>


### [191] [Systematic Lightweight Method for Robotics Based on Strain Energy Distribution Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29146)
*Jingchen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于应变能均匀分布准则的系统级轻量化设计方法，通过将系统级优化问题解耦为部件级优化问题，实现服务机器人等复杂机械系统的重量减轻与力学性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务机器人需要轻量化设计以保证安全性、灵活性和能量效率，但机器人作为复杂机械系统包含大量零部件，具有多种工作构型和载荷环境，系统级优化设计面临巨大挑战。

Method: 提出应变能分布优化方法：首先证明最优轻量化机械系统中单位质量应变能应均匀分布；基于该准则将系统级问题解耦，依据应变能为每个部件分配设计目标；各部件可采用尺寸优化、拓扑优化、材料优化等不同方法独立优化。

Result: 以机器人手臂为例验证了方法的有效性，在刚度提升轻量化、多材料、多工况和振动性能等场景下均取得良好效果，实现了系统级优化而计算复杂度保持在部件级。

Conclusion: 该方法能够同时实现重量减轻和力学性能提升，为复杂机械系统的系统级轻量化设计提供了一种高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Service robots work with people and are highly expected to be lightweight for safety, agility and energy conservation. As a complex mechanical system, a robot consists of a large number of components and has various working configurations and load environments. An effective method for achieving system-level optimal robot design is a crucial requirement, but it poses significant challenges. In this study, we introduce a novel approach to optimize the distribution of strain energy, which can significantly improve the effectiveness of systematic optimization in a complex system. First, we present and demonstrate that the strain energy per unit mass should be uniformly distributed in an optimal lightweight mechanical system. Based on this criterion, the system-level problem can be decoupled and the design objective of each part can be assigned based on the strain energy. Then, each part can be optimally designed separately according to its specific circumstances by using different approaches, such as size optimization, topological optimization, and material optimization. In this way, the optimization is at the system level, while the computational complexity is at the part level. Weight reduction and improvements in mechanical properties can be obtained simultaneously. As an example, this method is applied to an arbitrarily designed robotic arm, and its effectiveness is further demonstrated in the cases of lightweight with stiffness improvement, considering multiple materials, multiple working conditions, and vibration performance.

</details>


### [192] [AdaVLA: Adaptive Step Flow Matching for Training-free Acceleration of Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29208)
*Sunghwan Han,Youngtae Han,Youngmin Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaVLA，一种无需训练、在线自适应的推理加速框架，针对基于流匹配的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型。通过从流匹配轨迹曲率推导出的新度量来量化动作生成置信度，实现动态减少推理步数和自适应调整MLP剪枝比例。在LIBERO基准和Jetson AGX Orin设备上，对π0.5和X-VLA分别实现1.87×和2.24×加速，成功率几乎无损，并在真实机器人任务（SmolVLA）上验证了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型基于VLM构建，利用互联网规模知识和多模态推理极大增强机器人能力，但其密集的计算开销限制了设备端部署，难以实时响应环境变化。现有加速技术大多依赖微调或训练数据集（常因隐私和专有性不可用），且针对流匹配VLA的加速工作主要聚焦于VLM推理成本，忽视了流匹配推理中迭代ODE求解过程的开销。

Method: 提出AdaVLA框架：1）设计了一种从流匹配轨迹曲率推导的新度量，用于在推理过程中量化动作生成置信度；2）基于该置信度度量动态减少推理步数；3）通过高效计算的注意力重要性评估，自适应调整MLP剪枝比例；4）整个框架无需训练数据访问，在线运行。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上使用Jetson AGX Orin设备，对π0.5和X-VLA分别实现1.87×和2.24×的加速，成功率几乎无退化。在真实世界机器人任务上使用SmolVLA进一步验证了方法的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: AdaVLA通过利用流匹配轨迹曲率作为动作生成置信度的指示器，实现了无需训练数据的在线自适应加速，在保持动作质量的同时显著降低了推理延迟，为VLA模型的设备端实时部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, built upon Vision-Language Models (VLMs), have significantly enhanced robotic capabilities by leveraging internet-scale knowledge and multimodal reasoning. However, the intensive computational overhead of VLAs constrains on-device deployment, hindering real-time responses to environmental changes. While various acceleration techniques have been proposed, they often rely on fine-tuning or access to training datasets, which are frequently unavailable due to privacy and proprietary concerns. Moreover, although flow-matching-based VLAs have emerged as efficient alternatives to standard diffusion models, current acceleration efforts largely target VLM inference costs, failing to address the iterative ODE solving process inherent in flow matching inference. To address these limitations, we propose AdaVLA, an online, training-free adaptive framework for fast yet accurate flow-matching-based Vision-Language-Action models. We introduce a novel metric derived from the flow matching trajectory curvature to quantify action generation confidence during inference. This metric enables the dynamic reduction of inference steps and the adaptive adjustment of MLP pruning ratios through an efficiently computed importance evaluation, requiring no access to training data. Experimental results on the LIBERO benchmark using a Jetson AGX Orin device demonstrate that our method achieves $1.87\times$ and $2.24\times$ speedups for $π_{0.5}$ and X-VLA, respectively, with negligible degradation in success rates. Furthermore, we validate the robustness of our approach on real-world robotic tasks using SmolVLA.

</details>


### [193] [AnyWorld: Factorized Egocentric World Models for Cross-Embodiment Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29242)
*Cheng Chen,Jerry Bai,Jiacheng Wei,Boyu Chen,Xiaoji Zheng,Fan Wu,Minghao Yang,Tianrun Chen,Ruibo Li,Xiaoyu Yue,Xiaoyang Guo,Yixiao Ge,Guosheng Lin,Fayao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AnyWorld是一个跨具身世界建模框架，将单个人类交互视频分解为动作、相�机、具身三部分，通过独立重组生成多样化的机器人域经验数据，无需成对的人类-机器人演示。在大规模人类交互预训练和混合具身微调后，生成的视频-动作对可提升RoboCasa GR1桌面操作基准和IRON人形机器人的操作性能，并验证了动作校准与视觉重组两者缺一不可。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人的可泛化操作依赖大规模、多样化的接触丰富经验数据，但真实机器人的数据采集成本极高且多样性受限。人类第一人称（egocentric）视频蕴含丰富的物理交互，但单段视频只覆盖单一的躯体、相机轨迹和环境，无法直接用于机器人训练，因此需要一种能将单条人类交互扩展为多样化的机器人原生轨迹的方法。

Method: 提出AnyWorld跨具身世界建模框架：将一次交互因式分解为三个独立因子——动作控制（捕获运动结构）、相机控制（指明视角演化）、目标具身上下文（定义执行主体及其交互几何）。三个因子可独立重组，实现具身/视角/场景的自由组合。训练策略为大规模人类交互预训练+混合具身微调两阶段。

Result: 实验表明：该模型支持跨具身、跨视角、跨场景的可控重组；生成数据可提升RoboCasa GR1桌面操作基准及真实IRON人形机器人的操作性能。受控IRON干预实验纠正了虚假完成先验并建立了语言引导的空间目标选择能力；纯动作消融干预无法可靠习得该能力，证明动作校准与视觉重组缺一不可。

Conclusion: AnyWorld证明了无需配对演示，仅通过将人类单条交互视频进行动作-相机-具身的因式分解与重组，即可规模化生成机器人原生经验数据并有效提升操作性能；同时揭示了动作校准与视觉重组在生成可用的机器人训练数据中均是必要环节。

Abstract: Collecting contact-rich robot experiences at scale remains a major bottleneck for generalizable manipulation. Beyond data quantity, robot learning also requires diverse experiences across embodiments, viewpoints, and scenes. Human egocentric videos provide abundant physical interactions, but each video captures only a narrow slice of experience under a single body, camera trajectory, and environment. We propose AnyWorld, a cross-embodiment world modeling framework that expands a single human interaction into diverse robot-native rollouts without paired human-robot demonstrations. Our model factorizes an interaction into action, camera, and embodiment: action controls capture the motion structure, camera controls specify viewpoint evolution, and the target embodiment context defines the acting body and its interaction geometry. This formulation enables independent recomposition of embodiment, viewpoint, and scene factors, allowing a single model to generate many robot-domain experiences while preserving the underlying dynamics and object interactions. We train the model with large-scale human interaction pretraining followed by mixed-embodiment fine-tuning. Experiments show that our model supports controllable recomposition across embodiments, viewpoints, and scenes, and we further demonstrate that the generated data can improve manipulation performance on the RoboCasa GR1 tabletop benchmark and a real IRON humanoid robot. Beyond aggregate gains, we test whether unpaired human experience can be recomposed into robot-native video-action pairs that target a policy gap. Controlled IRON interventions correct a spurious completion prior and establish language-grounded spatial target selection; an action-only counterfactual intervention fails to learn the latter reliably, showing that both action calibration and visual recomposition are necessary.

</details>


### [194] [SGE: Semantically-Guided Exploration for Unstructured Environments via Image-Space Waypoint Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29315)
*Christopher Tatsch,Yu Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种语义引导探索框架（SGE），将像素级语义分割集成到基于采样的航点选择和receding-horizon路径优化中，在模拟基准和真实矿山、校园等多平台实验中实现了与传统几何方法相当的体积覆盖率，同时支持语义任务偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统几何探索方法仅基于空间覆盖进行探索，无法感知环境语义信息（如可通行性、障碍物、感兴趣物体），难以满足实际任务中对特定语义区域的探索需求，也不足以应对真实世界中的导航不确定性。

Method: SGE采用模块化框架：在图像空间中使用语义感知效用函数评估候选探索目标（考虑地形可通行性、障碍物距离、感兴趣物体和深度探索奖励）；将采样航点投影到3D并利用实时TSP求解进行路径排序，实现receding-horizon目标选择；引入临时禁忌区域处理导航失败，以及基于图的重新定位策略支持高效回溯。

Result: 在标准化模拟基准中与最先进的探索规划器对比，SGE在体积覆盖率上表现具有竞争力，同时实现了纯几何方法无法实现的语义任务偏置能力；在真实实验中，SGE在室内校园建筑、石灰岩矿和煤矿等多平台、多域环境中均表现出一致性和适应性。

Conclusion: SGE证明了将语义信息融入探索框架的可行性和优势，在保持几何探索性能的同时提供了语义导向的探索能力，并在跨平台、跨域的真实环境中得到验证，为移动机器人智能探索提供了新思路。

Abstract: This work introduces Semantically-Guided Exploration (SGE), a modular exploration framework for ground vehicles that integrates pixel-level semantic segmentation into sampling-based waypoint selection and receding-horizon route optimization. Unlike conventional geometric exploration methods, SGE evaluates candidate exploration goals directly in the image space using a semantic-aware utility function that accounts for terrain traversability, obstacle proximity, objects of interest, and depth-based exploration reward. Sampled waypoints are projected into 3D and ordered through a real-time Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) formulation, enabling receding-horizon goal selection. To address real-world navigation uncertainty, the framework introduces mechanisms, including temporary taboo regions to handle navigation failures and a graph-based relocation strategy for efficient backtracking across explored areas. We evaluate SGE in standardized simulation benchmarks against state-of-the-art exploration planners and demonstrate competitive performance in volumetric coverage, while enabling semantic task biasing that cannot be achieved by purely geometric methods. The framework is further validated through real-world experiments using multiple robotic platforms in indoor campus buildings and in limestone and coal mines. Results show consistent performance and adaptability across platforms and domains.

</details>


### [195] [A Cognitive Architecture for Shared Autonomy in AUV Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29347)
*Niamh Ellis,Thi Tran,Ignacio Carlucho,Yvan R. Petillot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合本体论（Ontology）和多个大语言模型（LLMs）的认知架构，用于在遥控无人潜水器（ROV）操作的各个阶段辅助操作员，提升态势感知能力并降低工作负担。系统在仿真中完成任务可行性评估、任务规划和任务执行，并支持操作员介入。实验比较了Llama3、GPT-OSS和Qwen2.5三种LLM在不同角色中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ROV操作员在任务中常面临态势感知不足和工作负荷过高的问题，这两者都会对操作安全性产生负面影响。现有的辅助系统缺乏将领域相关知识与大语言模型推理能力相结合的手段，因此需要一种能够将领域知识注入并支持操作员各阶段决策的认知架构。

Method: 构建一个由本体论和多个LLM组成的认知架构。本体论为各LLM提供领域特定的知识基线；每个LLM承担简单明确的角色（如任务类型识别、可行性评估、任务规划、任务执行），分工协作覆盖任务全流程。操作员可介入规划和执行阶段。系统在仿真环境中验证全流程，并分别使用Llama3、GPT-OSS和Qwen2.5比较不同模型在各自角色中的适配性。

Result: 实验结果表明：GPT-OSS在可行性评估、任务规划和任务执行方面表现最佳，而Qwen2.5在从自然语言输入识别任务类型的任务中表现最优。

Conclusion: 将本体论与多个LLM结合使用，能够使决策既扎根于领域知识又受益于LLM的推理能力。不同LLM在不同角色中各有优势，应根据任务职能选择合适的模型，从而构建更高效、更安全的ROV操作辅助系统。

Abstract: Operators remain essential to Remotely Operated Vehicle (ROV) operation, yet often suffer from low situational awareness and high workload, both of which negatively affect safety. This paper presents a cognitive architecture consisting of an ontology and multiple Large Language Models (LLMs) to assist the operator at all stages of the mission. Each LLM is grounded with domain-specific information from the ontology and given a simple role to create a system that can support the operator at all stages of an operation. We are aiming to prove that using the two together will allow decisions to be grounded in the relevant domain knowledge, but also benefit from the reasoning capabilities of the LLM. Our framework determines if a mission is possible for a given Unmanned Underwater Vehicle (UUV), performs mission planning, and executes a given mission in simulation. The operator can be involved in planning and execution, ensuring the resulting plan is valid and that the vehicle behaves safely during execution. We compare different LLMs, Llama3, GPT-OSS, and Qwen2.5, to determine which are best suited to the different roles within our framework. We find that GPT-OSS performs best for feasibility assessment, planning, and execution, while Qwen2.5 is best suited to identifying mission types from natural language input.

</details>


### [196] [Bridging Semantics and Physics with Constrained LLMs for Safe and Trustworthy Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29379)
*Wenhao Hong,Lan Wei,Dandan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种语言引导机器人系统，通过将推理-执行边界形式化为类型化契约（typed contract），利用Model Context Protocol（MCP）进行schema验证的工具调用，并结合MoveIt Task Constructor在真实场景中进行运动规划验证，弥合LLM在语言计划与物理执行之间的鸿沟。系统在真实UFactory 850机器人上完成倾倒（液体、颗粒、离散固体）和抓取放置任务，最高达到90%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM能生成看似正确的计划序列，但存在"语言-动作鸿沟"（language-action gap）：语言上合理的计划在运动学和碰撞约束下可能物理上不可行。真实厨房环境具有杂波和感知不完美，需要系统不仅生成正确计划，还要安全执行。

Method: （1）将推理-执行边界形式化为类型化契约；（2）从RGB-D观测中构建显式、碰撞感知的场景模型并对感知物体进行接地（grounding）；（3）通过MCP定义schema验证的工具调用约束语言级决策，在执行前拒绝格式错误的命令；（4）每个验证后的调用在MoveIt Task Constructor管线中确定性接地，在verify-then-act步骤中根据重建的规划场景评估候选运动；（5）仅将通过运动学和碰撞检查的轨迹发送给机器人。

Result: 在物理UFactory 850机器人上，倾倒任务（液体、颗粒介质、离散固体）在每任务10次试验中实现最高80%成功率；抓取放置任务使用相同规划、协议和验证栈达到90%成功率。脚本化策略在最简单任务上略优，但在最难任务上成功率降至10%，而本文方法可达60%。

Conclusion: 基于类型化契约和MCP协议验证的推理-执行分离架构，能在语言引导的真实机器人操作中有效提升安全性与鲁棒性，尤其在困难任务中显著优于脚本化策略，验证了"先验证后执行"（verify-then-act）思想在弥合语言-动作鸿沟中的有效性。

Abstract: A language-guided robot operating in a real kitchen must do more than produce a plan that appears correct. It must also execute that plan safely in cluttered environments under imperfect perception. Large language models (LLM) can decompose instructions into action sequences, yet a language-action gap remains: a plan may appear valid linguistically while being physically infeasible under kinematic and collision constraints. We bridge this gap by formalizing the reasoning-execution boundary as a typed contract. From RGB-D observations, the system grounds perceived objects in an explicit, collision-aware scene model and constrains language-level decisions through schema-validated tool calls defined by the Model Context Protocol (MCP), rejecting malformed commands before they reach the robot. Each validated call is deterministically grounded in a MoveIt Task Constructor pipeline, where candidate motions are evaluated against the reconstructed planning scene in a verify-then-act step. Only trajectories that pass both kinematic and collision checks are sent to the robot. On a physical UFactory 850, the method achieves up to 80% success across ten trials per task on pouring tasks involving liquids, granular media, and discrete solids. It achieves 90% success on a grasp-and-place task using the same planning, protocol, and verification stack. Although a scripted policy slightly outperforms our method on the easiest task, its success rate falls to 10% on the hardest, compared with 60% for our method.

</details>


### [197] [Toward Trustworthy Robot-Assisted Sliding Palpation for Shallow Vessel Localisation with a Calibrated Digital Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29396)
*Piotr Blaszyk,Wen Fan,Kaizhong Deng,Daniel Elson,Dandan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种机器人辅助滑动触诊框架，利用校准的数字孪生生成带标签的触觉序列，减少对真实数据采集的依赖。通过贝叶斯优化域适应校准数字孪生，训练时空图神经网络进行血管分类和局部定位，在四个数据集配置上验证了仿真到真实迁移的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浅层皮下血管的可靠定位对机器人辅助静脉穿刺和血管感知操作至关重要，但在物理硬件上收集多样化的触觉数据成本高昂、耗时，且会损坏基于软视觉的触觉传感器。因此需要减少对真实世界数据的依赖，探索仿真驱动的触觉感知方案。

Method: 提出滑动触诊框架：(1) 构建校准的数字孪生模型，模拟传感器-血管接触；(2) 使用贝叶斯优化域适应对真实触诊轨迹进行校准；(3) 对滑动方向和接触条件进行随机化；(4) 训练时空图神经网络对模拟标记轨迹进行逐节点血管分类；(5) 通过2D-3D-2D几何投影生成人类可验证的俯视局部定位图。评估使用四个数据集配置：Sim→Sim、Sim→Silicone、Sim→Meat和Meat→Silicone。

Result: 校准的数字孪生在四种典型交互中最深接触处实现0.50 mm的模拟到真实标记对齐平均绝对误差。在1 mm俯视网格重投影后，预测血管像素距最近真实血管像素平均1.05-5.49 mm，其中除Sim→Meat外的三个配置为1.05-1.31 mm。Sim→Meat误差较大反映了更大的域偏移和当前仿真迁移的局限性。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了通过校准仿真、可解释定位和透明跨域评估推动可信触觉触诊的进展，代码、模型权重和数据均已公开。

Abstract: Reliable localisation of shallow subsurface vessels is important for safe robot-assisted venous access and vessel-aware manipulation, but collecting diverse tactile data on physical hardware is costly, time-consuming, and can degrade soft vision-based tactile sensors. We present a robot-assisted sliding-palpation framework in which a calibrated digital twin generates labelled tactile sequences, reducing reliance on real-world data. The twin models sensor-vessel contact, is calibrated against real palpation trajectories using Bayesian-optimisation-based domain adaptation, and is randomised over sliding direction and contact conditions. A spatio-temporal graph neural network trained on simulated marker trajectories performs per-node vessel classification and produces a human-verifiable top-view localisation map through 2D-to-3D-to-2D geometric projection. We evaluate three datasets: Sim, Silicone, and Meat, the latter a raw-meat phantom with vessel models at nominal depths of 0 to 30 mm, using four train-to-test configurations: Sim to Sim, Sim to Silicone, Sim to Meat, and Meat to Silicone. The calibrated twin achieves a simulated-to-real marker-alignment mean absolute error of 0.50 mm at deepest contact across four canonical interactions. After reprojection onto a 1 mm top-view grid, predicted vessel pixels lie on average 1.05 to 5.49 mm from the nearest true vessel pixel across the four models, with 1.05 to 1.31 mm for all except Sim to Meat. The larger error for Sim to Meat reflects the greater domain shift and current limit of simulation transfer. These results demonstrate progress toward trustworthy tactile palpation through calibrated simulation, interpretable localisation, and transparent cross-domain evaluation. Code, model weights, and data are publicly available on GitHub and Zenodo.

</details>


### [198] [SMILE: Smooth Motion for Improved Long-Horizon VLA Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29432)
*Jongwoo Park,E-Ro Nguyen,Kanchana Ranasinghe,Cristina Mata,Xiang Li,Michael S Ryoo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SMILE是一种架构保持的接口，通过预测B样条系数并将解码为平滑动作序列来替代原始动作chunk，从而在不改变VLA模型骨干和规模的前提下实现更长的固定推理horizon，在多个基线和真实实验中同时提升准确率与推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型通过每次调用执行多个动作来降低推理成本，但更长的horizon会因原始动作chunk中的抖动（jitter）和离群点（outliers）导致精度下降。作者旨在通过仅改变动作表示来解决长horizon精度退化问题，从而在不增加模型规模的前提下获得既准确又高效的长时间任务执行能力。

Method: 提出SMILE接口，其核心是预测B样条（B-spline）系数，并将系数解码成平滑的动作序列。SMILE只改变动作表示，不改变各基线模型的骨干网络和模型规模，因此可适配任意VLA模型并支持更长的固定horizon。

Result: 在多个基线上取得提升：SMILE-Evo1在LIBERO上达到98.0%准确率并获1.1x加速；SMILE-VPP在CALVIN上达到平均序列长度4.42并获1.5x加速；在匹配执行horizon为10时，SMILE-SmolVLA将非边界加速度降低78.6%、速度符号变化率降低42.3%；真实xArm实验中成功率更高、掉落更少、接触更少。

Conclusion: 实验证明平滑系数空间生成（smooth coefficient-space generation）是实现准确且高效的长horizon VLA执行的有效途径，为VLA动作表示的设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models reduce inference cost by executing multiple actions per call, but longer horizons often degrade accuracy because raw chunks contain jitter and outliers. We introduce SMILE, an architecture-preserving interface that predicts B-spline coefficients and decodes them into smooth action sequences. SMILE changes only the action representation, enabling longer fixed horizons while retaining each baseline's backbone and model scale. We apply SMILE to SmolVLA, Evo1, VPP, and DAWN, improving accuracy and amortized inference efficiency across LIBERO, CALVIN, and real-world experiments. SMILE-Evo1 reaches 98.0% with a 1.1x speedup on LIBERO, while SMILE-VPP reaches an average length of 4.42 with a 1.5x speedup on CALVIN. At a matched execution horizon of 10, SMILE-SmolVLA reduces non-boundary acceleration by 78.6% and velocity sign-change rate by 42.3%. Real-world xArm tests show higher success, fewer drops, and fewer contacts. These results establish smooth coefficient-space generation as a route to accurate, efficient long-horizon VLA execution. Project page: jongwoopark7978.github.io/smilevla

</details>


### [199] [Blind Dexterity: Whole-Body Humanoid Manipulation via Pure Proprioception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29487)
*Aditya Bhatt,Oleg Kaidanov,Puze Liu,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文展示了仅依赖本体感觉（关节编码器）的Unitree G1人形机器人能够在无相机、无外置传感器的情况下完成全身灵巧操作任务（推抗行走、足球拦截、提行李箱、骑滑板），论证了关节编码器在柔顺接触下形成的全身触觉通道是强大且实用的感知基底。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人灵巧操作高度依赖相机、力觉/触觉等外部传感，但这些传感器成本高、鲁棒性受限且在实际部署中存在诸多约束。作者质疑是否需要如此丰富的感知才能完成全身操作，探索仅凭关节编码器这一最基础的本体感觉信号能否支撑复杂任务。

Method: 采用强化学习训练无视觉输入的控制策略，仅依赖关节编码器读取的关节位置/速度信息。针对每个任务训练独立且与策略分离的紧凑状态估计器，利用短时程本体感觉历史解码任务相关物体状态（如位姿），并通过主动产生接触丰富运动来增强信息的可解码性。

Result: 在Unitree G1上实现了四类定性不同的任务：无IMU反馈的抗推双足行走、脚部主动拦截足球、搜寻并提起行李箱把手、骑上随机位置的滑板。状态估计器的预测误差在有效接触后迅速下降，证实了关节编码器读数在柔顺接触时携带丰富的物体状态信息。

Conclusion: 关节编码器结合柔顺驱动器已构成强健、实用的全身灵巧操作感知基底，可在此基础上叠加更丰富的传感信息。这一发现拓展了本体感觉的感知边界，降低了复杂机器人任务的传感依赖门槛。

Abstract: We present blind, whole-body manipulation skills on a Unitree G1 humanoid using only onboard proprioception, without cameras, markers, force-torque, or tactile sensors. Despite this minimal sensing, the trained policies exhibit surprising capability across qualitatively different tasks: push-resilient bipedal walking without IMU feedback, active soccer ball trapping with a foot, seeking and lifting a suitcase by its handle, and mounting a randomly positioned skateboard.
  We argue that these capabilities arise from a key underappreciated signal: the way the joint encoder readouts evolve under purposeful compliant contact, effectively forming a whole-body tactile channel. By generating contact-rich motions, the trained policies actively probe the environment; as a result, task-relevant object state (e.g., pose) becomes increasingly decodable from short proprioceptive histories. We expose this information using compact task-specific state estimators trained alongside, but fully separately from, the policies; their prediction errors decrease rapidly after informative contact.
  Our results indicate that joint encoder-based proprioception, combined with compliant actuation (now widely available on commercial robots and low-cost motors) is already a strong, practical substrate for whole-body dexterous manipulation and interactive perception, and therefore a natural foundation on which richer sensing can be layered.

</details>


### [200] [A Sliding Window Filter on the Galilean Group for Consistent Aided Inertial Navigation with Unknown Measurement Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29514)
*Jonathan Kelly*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究辅助惯性导航中未知恒定延迟的联合估计问题，利用特殊伽利略群建模，并通过滑动窗口滤波器联合处理多个延迟测量以提高导航估计的精度和一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辅助传感器测量常受未知恒定延迟影响，若忽略延迟或单独处理延迟测量，会导致轨迹校正时间错误、导航精度下降，且估计可能不具可观性而产生过度自信和不一致的估计。需要在统一的框架下联合估计延迟与导航状态。

Method: 在特殊伽利略群（special Galilean group）上构建辅助导航的状态空间结构，以统一刻画运动与时间的不确定性；通过可观性分析揭示单延迟测量存在精确对称性（延迟变化可由状态变化补偿）；据此设计滑动窗口滤波器，保留短时导航状态历史并在窗口内联合施加延迟校正。

Result: 仿真表明：若不维持足够的窗口，估计器会迅速变得高度不一致；而即使较短的滑动窗口也能通过提供可观性所需的时间支撑，显著改善估计一致性。

Conclusion: 多时刻联合处理延迟测量是消除可观性退化方向、实现一致导航估计的关键；滑动窗口滤波为实际应用提供了有效且计算可负担的解决方案。

Abstract: We study aided inertial navigation when the aiding sensor measurements are subject to an unknown constant delay. The goal is to estimate the delay and navigation state jointly so that delayed measurements correct the trajectory at the appropriate times, yielding a more accurate navigation solution. We formulate the problem on the special Galilean group, which provides a natural state-space structure for aided navigation with uncertainty in both motion and timing. We then examine the observability of joint delay and state estimation and show that, for a single delayed measurement, the model admits an exact symmetry in which a change in the delay can be compensated by a change in the navigation state, leaving the measurement unchanged. Processing measurements individually allows spurious information to `leak' along the corresponding null direction of the measurement Jacobian, producing overconfident and inconsistent estimates. Applying measurements from multiple times together can eliminate this direction when the trajectory is informative enough. Motivated by this result, we develop a sliding window filter that retains a short history of navigation states and applies delayed aiding corrections jointly across the active window. We conduct a series of simulation studies to characterize estimator accuracy and consistency. The simulations demonstrate that an estimator that does not maintain an adequate window can rapidly become highly inconsistent, whereas even a short sliding window markedly improves consistency by providing the temporal support that observability requires.

</details>


### [201] [Task-Relevant Feature-Dynamics Fidelity Enables Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Transfer for Robotic Ultrasound Scanning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29516)
*Yizhao Qian,Jiayuan Luo,Wanyi Zhu,Yameng Zhang,Max Q. -H. Meng,Yixuan Yuan,Li Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"任务相关特征动态保真度"(TR-FDF)概念，用于评估超声模拟器在零样本迁移中的有效性。通过收缩分析证明TR-FDF失配对探针运动的敏感度影响闭环收缩裕度，据此设计结合结构中间域、轨迹级固定噪声和少步条件流生成的超声模拟器，在幻影实验中实现390/400次零样本部署成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接在B模式图像上训练的机器人超声策略需要大量真实交互数据，而真实机器人数据采集成本高且受安全约束。模拟提供可扩展替代方案，但零样本迁移不仅取决于单帧真实感，还取决于模拟观测是否能复现由探针运动引起的任务相关特征变化。现有模拟器缺乏对此类跨域一致性的理论分析和对齐保障。

Method: 1) 形式化定义TR-FDF并基于局部正则性假设进行收缩分析，证明TR-FDF失配对探针运动的敏感度降低有效闭环收缩裕度，而运动无关误差主要放大残差误差界；2) 据此设计TR-FDF导向的超声模拟器，融合共享结构中间域、轨迹级固定噪声和少步条件流生成三个组件。

Result: 幻影实验中，纯模拟训练的策略在4个目标平面上实现了390/400次零样本部署成功；模拟器FID达29.66，生成频率67.1Hz；受控干预、消融和基线对比表明TR-FDF敏感性在预测零样本迁移性能方面与单帧真实感互补。

Conclusion: TR-FDF是比单帧真实感更全面的模拟器评估维度，任务相关特征动态的保真度对零样本迁移性能具有关键影响，所设计的TR-FDF导向模拟器在部署效率与生成质量上均达到实际可用水平。

Abstract: Robotic ultrasound policies operating directly on B-mode images require extensive interaction data, whereas real-robot data collection is costly and safety-constrained. Simulation provides a scalable alternative, but zero-shot transfer depends not only on single-frame realism but also on whether simulated observations reproduce task-relevant feature changes induced by probe motion. We term this cross-domain consistency task-relevant feature-dynamics fidelity (TR-FDF). Under local regularity assumptions, our contraction analysis shows that greater sensitivity of TR-FDF mismatch to probe motion reduces the effective closed-loop contraction margin, whereas motion-independent errors primarily enlarge the residual error bound. Guided by this analysis, we develop a TR-FDF-oriented ultrasound simulator that combines a shared structural intermediate domain, trajectory-level fixed noise, and few-step conditional flow generation. In phantom experiments, a policy trained exclusively in simulation succeeded in 390 of 400 zero-shot deployments across four target planes. The simulator achieved an FID of 29.66 and generated observations at 67.1 Hz. Controlled interventions, ablations, and baseline comparisons showed that TR-FDF sensitivity complements single-frame realism in predicting zero-shot transfer performance.

</details>


### [202] [AGM: Achievement-Grounded Memory for Closed-Loop Agents with Frozen VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29537)
*Hongbo Gao,Zeyu Ni,Xin Wen,Siyu Xu,Ruifeng Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AGM是一个轻量级闭环框架，通过物理证据验证的子目标进度指针来增强冻结的VLA策略，将其开环执行转换为闭环控制，在RoboMME Counting基准和物理机器人上均显著超越现有记忆增强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略虽然具备广泛的操纵技能，但以开环方式执行动作块，无法跟踪任务进度，因而不能可靠地判断是继续、重试还是终止。外部记忆虽可缓解此问题，但将尝试过的动作视为已完成进度会产生有害影响，把局部执行错误变成持续的任务状态错误。

Method: AGM将任务表示为带有进度指针的子目标序列，仅当当前子目标通过物理证据验证后才推进记忆状态。本体感觉交互线索决定何时验证，而通过冻结基础模型（经单一2.43M参数验证头）的相干点跟踪和语言条件跨视角比较决定实现了什么。由此将开环执行转变为"执行-验证-推进"的闭环，保持策略冻结且无需测试时大模型推理。

Result: 在RoboMME Counting基准上，AGM在PickXTimes和BinFill任务上均达到最优，平均超过最强的记忆增强基线若干个百分点，并在物理机器人上取得同样显著的提升。

Conclusion: 可靠的具身记忆更依赖于纪律性的状态更新而非记忆容量本身。

Abstract: Frozen vision-language-action (VLA) policies offer broad manipulation skills but execute open-loop action chunks without tracking task progress, so the agent cannot reliably decide whether to continue, retry, or terminate. External memory is a natural remedy, yet it can be harmful when attempted actions are treated as completed progress, turning local execution errors into persistent task-state errors. We propose Achievement-Grounded Memory (AGM), a lightweight closed-loop framework for frozen VLA policies that represents a task as a subgoal sequence with a progress pointer and advances this memory only after the current subgoal is verified by physical evidence. Proprioceptive interaction cues decide when to verify, while coherent point tracking and language-conditioned cross-view comparison, sourced from frozen foundation models through a single 2.43M-parameter verification head, decide what was achieved. AGM thereby converts open-loop execution into a closed loop of execution, verification, and progress, keeping the policy frozen without test-time large-model inference. On the RoboMME Counting benchmark, AGM reaches on PickXTimes and on BinFill, surpassing the strongest memory-augmented baseline by points on average, and the framework yields equally decisive gains on a physical robot. Reliable embodied memory thus depends more on disciplined state updates than on memory capacity.

</details>


### [203] [Module Number Adaptive Visual Shape Control for Serial Modular Soft Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29547)
*Kyohei Akamine,Takato Horii,Yusuke Sakaue,Hiroki Ishizuka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模块数量自适应的视觉形状控制方法，仅用单模块驱动形状数据训练控制器，通过将整机相机图像分解为局部模块patch，实现对1至5个模块的串行模块化软体气动机器人的形状控制，无需针对不同构型重新采集控制数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于数据驱动的软体机器人形状控制方法通常针对固定机器人结构开发，当模块数量变化时需要重新训练和采集新的控制数据，缺乏可扩展性和泛化能力。

Method: 1) 将整机相机图像分解为局部模块patch；2) 使用单一的公共模块分割器在多种构型中定位单个模块；3) 对每个提取的patch应用相同的局部控制器；4) 通过几何数据增强提高对下游模块的迁移能力；5) 使用轻量级掩码重建网络重建被合成移除的执行器掩码通道。

Result: 在物理机器人上进行的实验表明，该方法能够在不同模块数量、环境变化和载荷加载条件下实现形状控制，证明了单模块控制学习可以实现可扩展的整机控制，无需针对特定构型采集控制数据。

Conclusion: 仅基于单模块驱动数据训练的控制器即可实现对多模块软体机器人的整机形状控制，验证了模块化软体机器人控制的可扩展性和数据效率。

Abstract: Image based shape control provides a simple means of controlling the whole body configuration of soft robots. However, existing data driven approaches are typically developed for fixed robot structures and require new control data when the number of modules changes. This paper presents a module number adaptive visual shape control method for serial modular soft pneumatic robots. A controller trained only on single module actuation shape data is reused for robots with one to five modules by decomposing whole body camera images into local module patches. A single common module segmenter localizes individual modules across all tested configurations, while the same local controller is applied to every extracted patch. Geometric data augmentation improves transferability to downstream modules, and a lightweight mask reconstruction network reconstructs a synthetically removed actuator mask channel. Experiments on physical robots demonstrate shape control across varying numbers of modules and under environmental changes and payload loading. The results show that single module control learning enables scalable whole body control without configuration specific control data collection.

</details>


### [204] [$\mathcal{N}_0$-Foundation: Towards the Age of Tactile Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29601)
*NeoteAI Team,Fudan TEAI Team*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了N₀-Foundation，一个触觉增强的具身操作范式，涵盖触觉传感硬件、大规模多模态数据（NeoData，超30000小时）、触觉表示模型（NeoForce）和标准化评估基准（NeoReal与NeoSim），并开源了5000小时的OpenNeoData子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有操作数据语料库缺乏触觉模态信息，限制了形变物体操作、精确装配、精细力控制和持续表面交互等任务的能力。

Method: 1) 设计视觉触觉传感器与触觉UMI数据采集基础设施；2) 构建NeoData（30000+小时同步视觉-触觉演示，跨越6种具身、450个任务）；3) 提出NeoForce视觉-触觉表示模型以学习跨传感器可迁移的触觉表示；4) 构建涵盖真实世界NeoReal和仿真NeoSim的标准化评测基准。

Result: 实验表明策略受益于物理接触状态而非触觉信号的设备特定外观；同时发布了数据集、表示模型和基准以支持后续研究。

Conclusion: N₀-Foundation为触觉具身操作提供了从硬件到数据再到模型的完整基础设施，验证了触觉物理接触状态对操作策略提升的关键作用。

Abstract: We present $\mathcal{N}_0$-Foundation, a paradigm for tactile-enabled embodied manipulation, which integrates tactile sensing hardware, large-scale multimodal data, tactile representation learning, and standardized evaluation. First, we engineer the infrastructure for scalable data collection, including a vision-based tactile sensor, a tactile Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI), and a synchronized visuo-tactile data collection system supporting both robot embodiments and UMI-based demonstrations. Leveraging this infrastructure, we construct NeoData, which contains more than 30000 hours of synchronized visual and tactile demonstrations, spanning six embodiments, 450 tasks, and billions of paired RGB and tactile frames collected through a mixture of real-robot teleoperation and UMI-based demonstrations. To facilitate open research, we further release OpenNeoData, a 5000-hour open-source subset of NeoData. The dataset addresses a central limitation of existing manipulation corpora, critical for deformable-object manipulation, precise assembly, delicate force control, and sustained surface interaction. Capitalizing on the large-scale, heterogeneous tactile measurements, we propose NeoForce, a visuo-tactile representation model that learn transferable tactile representations across different sensor designs. To enable systematic evaluation of tactile embodied models built upon our infrastructure, datasets and tactile representations, we further propose a comprehensive benchmark, which combines the real-world NeoReal suite and the simulated NeoSim suite for standardized evaluation. Experiments across both suites show that policies benefit from the physical contact state rather than from the device-specific appearance of the tactile signal. We release the dataset, the representation, and the benchmark, aiming at supporting future work on tactile-enabled embodied manipulation.

</details>


### [205] [VeloBins: Learning Velocity and Its Uncertainty via Bins and Error-Conditioned Gaussian Labels for Aerial Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29720)
*Maulana Bisyir Azhari,Seungwook Lee,Donghun Han,Sung Jun Park,David Hyunchul Shim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VeloBins，将惯性里程计中的速度回归问题重新表述为离散化速度箱的分类问题，从分类分布的期望和方差分别解码速度及其不确定性，消除了独立不确定性解码器的需求。作者还提出一种基于误差条件的高斯标签来显式监督不确定性。在四个空中机器人数据集上，VeloBins相比最强基线在速度、相对轨迹和绝对轨迹误差上分别降低了3-27%、8-40%和6-53%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 惯性里程计对空中机器人至关重要，特别是在激进机动和光照不足的环境下视觉传感器性能下降。现有基于学习的方法通过回归方式预测速度存在困难，而使用独立的解码器和负对数似然（NLL）损失联合估计不确定性会使训练复杂化并导致过度自信的估计。作者希望通过重新设计速度估计的范式来解决这些问题。

Method: VeloBins将速度回归重构为离散化速度箱上的分类问题：从箱分布的期望解码速度，从方差解码不确定性，无需独立的不确定性解码器。同时提出误差条件的高斯标签来显式监督不确定性——标签以真实速度为均值、以速度误差为标准差的高斯分布。这些预测在扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）框架内进行融合。

Result: 在四个空中数据集（包括自由形态激进飞行、27g纳米四旋翼、以及速度超过21m/s的无人机竞速）上，VeloBins在全部四个数据集上取得了最低的平均误差：相比最强基线，速度误差降低3-27%，相对轨迹误差降低8-40%，绝对轨迹误差降低6-53%。该监督方法实现了最低的NLL和最佳的滤波器一致性，尽管从未优化NLL损失。

Conclusion: 将速度估计建模为分类问题并显式监督不确定性，是一种比回归+独立不确定性解码器更有效的方法，能够在不优化NLL损失的情况下实现更好的不确定性校准和滤波一致性。VeloBins在多种复杂空中飞行场景中展现了卓越的泛化性能。

Abstract: Inertial odometry (IO) is critical for aerial robots, where aggressive maneuvers and poor lighting degrade visual sensors. Recent learning-based IO methods improve traditional integration-based approaches by learning motion priors from IMU and platform-specific sensors, then fusing the predictions within an extended Kalman filter. However, learning velocity through regression is difficult, while jointly estimating uncertainty with a separate decoder and negative log-likelihood (NLL) loss further complicates training and can lead to over-confident estimates. We introduce VeloBins, which reformulates velocity regression as classification over discretized velocity bins. We decode both the velocity from the bin distribution's expectation and the uncertainty from its variance, removing the need for a separate uncertainty decoder. We further supervise the uncertainty explicitly using an error-conditioned Gaussian label centered at the ground-truth velocity, with a standard deviation set to the velocity error. We evaluate VeloBins on four aerial datasets, ranging from free-form aggressive flights and a 27 g nano-quadrotor to drone racing at over 21~m/s. VeloBins achieves the lowest average errors on all four datasets, reducing velocity, relative trajectory, and absolute trajectory errors by 3-27%, 8-40%, and 6-53%, respectively, compared with the strongest baseline. Notably, the proposed supervision achieves the lowest NLL and best filter consistency despite never optimizing an NLL loss. The code will be available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [206] [DriftingVLA: Native One-Step Vision-Language-Action Generation via Per-Dimension Temporal Drifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29749)
*Yuxuan Gao,Shiqi Zhang,Yedong Shen,Yifan Duan,Wenhao Yu,Xin Zhang,Siyuan Cao,Jiajun Deng,Yanyong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DriftingVLA 是一种原生单步视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，通过分布漂移目标直接学习从噪声到完整动作块的一步映射，避免了传统流式VLA模型在推理时所需的多次迭代精化，从而大幅降低机器人在线控制的延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于流的VLA模型虽然能生成表达力强的连续动作，但需要在推理时进行多步精化才能生成每个动作块，这显著增加了在线机器人控制的延迟。作者希望设计一种既能保持连续动作表达能力、又能在单步内生成完整动作块的VLA模型。

Method: DriftingVLA 采用分布漂移（distribution-drifting）目标替代流场学习，直接用一次前向传播完成噪声到动作块的映射。此外，提出Per-Dimension Temporal Drifting (PDTD)机制，将每个动作维度的时间轨迹作为独立漂移单元进行细粒度建模，以应对不同动作维度的控制语义和分布差异。PDTD仅用于训练目标，共享VLA模型仍联合生成完整动作块，保留跨维度依赖。

Result: LIBERO达到98.32%成功率，RoboTwin 2.0达到81.09%，六项真实世界单臂和双臂任务达到77.67%成功率，均优于多步流策略和单步VLA基线。原生单步部署带来3.36倍的动作块生成加速，无需迭代精化且不牺牲控制性能。

Conclusion: DriftingVLA通过原生单步动作生成和维度级时间漂移建模，在保持控制性能的同时显著降低了推理延迟，验证了分布漂移目标在VLA模型中的有效性，为机器人实时控制提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Conventional flow-based vision-language-action (VLA) models support expressive continuous action generation but rely on multi-step refinement to produce each action chunk, increasing latency in online robot control. To address this issue, we introduce DriftingVLA, a native one-step VLA that generates a complete action chunk with a single action-expert forward pass. Rather than learning a flow field that requires iterative integration at inference, DriftingVLA uses a distribution-drifting objective to learn a direct noise-to-action-chunk mapping for one-step deployment. Since robot action dimensions carry distinct control semantics and distributional characteristics, we further introduce Per-Dimension Temporal Drifting (PDTD). PDTD treats the complete temporal trajectory of each action dimension as a separate drifting unit, enabling finer-grained modeling and shaping of dimension-specific action distributions. This per-dimension decomposition applies only to the training objective; the shared VLA model still generates the complete action chunk jointly, thereby preserving cross-dimensional dependencies. DriftingVLA achieves 98.32% success on LIBERO, 81.09% on RoboTwin 2.0, and 77.67% across six real-world single- and dual-arm tasks, outperforming the evaluated multi-step flow policy and one-step VLA baselines. Native one-step deployment also delivers a 3.36-fold speedup in action-chunk generation, eliminating iterative refinement without sacrificing control performance.

</details>


### [207] [LARC: Lazy Adaptive Reachability Certification of Robot Manipulator Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29767)
*Yu Feng,Hao Wu,Yuzhe Wang,Jianshu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出懒惰自适应可达性认证（LARC）方法，通过对轨迹区间进行自适应二分检查，在保持与固定细粒度基线一致判定结果的同时大幅降低计算开销。在160条AgileX PIPER轨迹上，LARC仅使用基线24.8%的区间评估量，实现10.28倍的中位加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散轨迹检查可能遗漏采样机器人状态之间的碰撞，而基于可达性（reachability）的认证方法虽能约束状态间的运动，但均匀时间划分在间隙较大区域浪费大量计算资源。

Method: LARC通过只对结论不明确的区间进行二分细化来检查规划轨迹。针对分段三次Hermite关节轨迹，使用由精确分量级速度最大值膨胀的中点胶囊（midpoint capsules）来约束连杆占用。认证覆盖的区间可提供连续时间的外部障碍物间隙信息，满足几何包含、静态障碍物和预设安全余量约束。

Result: 在160条AgileX PIPER（80个起终点对）轨迹上，LARC与深度九的固定细粒度基线判定结果完全一致，仅使用20328次区间评估（基线工作量的24.8%），中位加速比为10.28倍。MoveIt/FCL审计检查了158051个状态，在21个直接插值控制中检测到碰撞，LARC均未认证这些轨迹；但27条采样无碰撞轨迹未被LARC认证，采样审计无法独立证明连续时间安全性。

Conclusion: LARC在共享认证模型下显著减少了计算量，但其保守性导致部分实际无碰撞轨迹未被认证；采样审计方法本身具有局限性，无法替代连续时间安全性的独立证明。

Abstract: Discrete trajectory checks can miss collisions between sampled robot states. Reachability-based certification bounds motion between states, but uniform time partitions waste computation where clearance is large. We present lazy adaptive reachability certification (LARC), which checks a planned trajectory by bisecting only intervals with an inconclusive clearance test. For piecewise-cubic Hermite joint trajectories, the method bounds link occupancy using midpoint capsules inflated by exact componentwise speed maxima. Certified intervals covering the trajectory provide continuous-time external-obstacle clearance, subject to geometric containment, static obstacles, and a prescribed margin. On 160 AgileX PIPER trajectories from 80 start-goal pairs, LARC matched all decisions of the fixed-fine baseline at depth nine. It used 20328 interval evaluations (24.8% of baseline work), with a median paired speedup of 10.28x. A separate MoveIt/FCL audit checked 158051 states and detected collisions in 21 direct-interpolation controls, none of which LARC certified. The method reduced computation under a shared certificate model, but 27 of 139 sampled-clear trajectories remained uncertified. The sampled audit cannot independently prove continuous-time clearance.

</details>


### [208] [SmoothRL: Online Reinforcement Learning During Asynchronous Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29768)
*Guang Gao,Yuxuan Nong,Baifu Huang,Jianan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SmoothRL是一个在线强化学习框架，在异步推理循环中微调预训练机器人策略。它采用值梯度范式直接更新策略参数，并显式建模异步推理过程（将动作块按帧索引划分为committed、execution和discarded三个区域），仅通过execution区域传播梯度，以确保优化与异步执行产生的轨迹分布一致。在需要高精度和高度动态性的真实机器人任务上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界部署机器人策略需要同时满足可靠性和平滑实时执行两个基本要求。然而，当前最先进通用模型存在两方面挑战：一是需要样本高效的在线强化学习来适配预训练模型以满足精度和鲁棒性需求；二是机器人基础模型规模增大导致推理延迟升高。虽然异步推理与动作分块（action chunking）已被用于隐藏延迟，但将异步执行与基于梯度的在线强化学习相结合的研究仍然缺乏。

Method: SmoothRL遵循值梯度（value-gradient）范式，直接使用动作价值函数对策略动作的梯度来更新策略参数。在异步推理下，每个生成的动作块按帧索引分为三个区域：committed区域（由上一推理周期承诺执行的动作）、execution区域（新生成并被机器人执行的动作）和discarded区域（被下一推理周期取代的动作）。训练时仅通过execution区域传播梯度，确保策略优化与异步执行诱导的轨迹分布对齐。

Result: 在需要高精度的真实世界机器人任务以及需要异步执行的高度动态任务上进行了评估，验证了SmoothRL框架的有效性。

Conclusion: SmoothRL通过显式建模异步推理过程中的动作分块结构并仅对实际执行的区域传播梯度，成功将基于梯度的在线强化学习与异步推理框架整合，为真实世界机器人部署中兼顾高精度与实时性提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Deploying robot policies in the physical world requires satisfying two fundamental desiderata: reliability and smooth real-time execution. However, deploying state-of-the-art generalist models presents challenges on both fronts. Achieving the precision and robustness required for real-world deployment necessitates sample-efficient online reinforcement learning (RL) to adapt pretrained models. Meanwhile, the increasing scale of robot foundation models has led to higher inference latency. To satisfy real-time constraints under high latency, modern systems adopt asynchronous inference with action chunking, overlapping policy computation with chunk execution to hide latency and enable smooth control. Despite their complementary roles, integrating asynchronous execution with gradient-based online RL remains underexplored. We present SmoothRL, an online RL framework that fine-tunes a pretrained policy within an asynchronous inference loop. SmoothRL follows a value-gradient paradigm, directly updating policy parameters using gradients of the action-value function with respect to policy actions. To enable correct optimization under asynchronous execution, SmoothRL explicitly models the asynchronous inference process during training. Specifically, each generated action chunk is partitioned by frame index into three regions: a committed region, consisting of actions committed by the previous inference cycle; an execution region, containing newly generated actions executed by the robot; and a discarded region, containing actions superseded by the next inference cycle. Gradients are propagated only through the execution region, ensuring policy optimization aligns with the trajectory distribution induced by asynchronous execution.
