We evaluate SmoothRL on real-world robotic tasks requiring high precision, as well as highly dynamic tasks that necessitate asynchronous execution.

</details>


### [209] [Learning Agile Perceptive Traversal of Sparse 3D Structures for Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29769)
*Efe Ongan,Chong Zhang,Boyang Sun,Andrei Cramariuc,Cesar Cadena,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于强化学习的感知控制系统，使双足机器人借助头戴式固态激光雷达在稀疏3D结构上完成猴杠穿越（跳跃上杠、摆荡、跳下落地）。策略通过注意力编码器加循环记忆直接处理原始激光雷达点云，经相位调度的师生训练流程获得；在硬件上14/15次试验成功率，摆荡速度最高0.5 m/s，且同一感知骨干可迁移至躲避薄障碍物任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在稀疏3D结构中穿行需要感知细薄悬空几何结构，同时执行敏捷且精确的全身运动。现有方法难以同时满足感知与运动控制的需求，猴杠穿越任务为这一挑战提供了理想的测试平台，需要机器人跳跃上杠、稀疏交互摆荡并安全落地。

Method: 采用强化学习感知控制系统：策略通过带注意力机制的编码器与循环记忆直接在原始固态激光雷达点云上提取任务相关几何信息；采用相位调度的师生流水线（phase-scheduled teacher-student pipeline），结合上跳、摆荡、下跳三个特权专家策略；建模了激光雷达噪声、电池电压下降和执行器热限制以促进硬件迁移；使用被动钩式末端执行器增强杠交互鲁棒性。

Result: 硬件实验中，策略在三种杠配置下完成"跳上->摆荡->跳下"完整序列的14/15试验成功率，摆荡速度最高达0.5 m/s。同一感知骨干训练出独立策略后，还能在2 cm截面细薄悬空障碍物下方完成下蹲躲避。

Conclusion: 这项工作验证了基于强化学习的感知控制方法能够让人形机器人在真实稀疏3D结构中完成复杂敏捷动作，且感知骨干具有良好的任务可迁移性。

Abstract: Traversing sparse 3D structures requires humanoid robots to perceive thin, overhanging geometry while executing agile, accurate whole-body motions. We study this problem through monkey-bar traversal, where the robot must jump to the structure, traverse it through sparse bar interactions, and land safely. For this task, we present a reinforcement-learning-based perceptive control system that operates directly on observations from a head-mounted solid-state lidar. To extract task-relevant geometry from the sparse returns, the policy consumes the raw lidar scan through an attention-based encoder with recurrent memory. This policy is obtained by a phase-scheduled teacher- student pipeline that combines privileged experts for jumping up, brachiating, and jumping down. For transfer to hardware, we model lidar noise, battery-voltage sag, and actuator thermal limits, and equip the humanoid with passive hook end-effectors for robust bar interaction. On hardware, the resulting policy completes the full jump-up->brachiation->jump-down sequence in 14 of 15 trials across three bar configurations and reaches brachiation speeds up to 0.5 m/s. Beyond brachiation, the same perception backbone supports a separately trained policy that ducks beneath thin overhead obstacles with 2 cm cross-sections.

</details>


### [210] [Sampling-based Certified Planning with Graphs of Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29770)
*Peng Xie,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次定量测量了基于凸集图的运动规划器中"区域生成器概率性保证"与"规划器信任该保证"之间差距的代价。实验表明，在14自由度双臂操作库上，3.2%的界面样本发生碰撞，但该体积误差导致搜索式GCS规划器产生62%的答案错误（29个任务中18个返回报告中声称成功实则穿过货架的轨迹）。修复库（更严格验收、SOS认证区域、均匀裕度）均不可行，因为它们先破坏了连通性。作者转而构建一个主动认证答案的规划器：通过采样重叠面和共享面、可采纳的知情剪枝、连续无分辨率参数的安全证书球链验证候选解，失败时以局部区域内绕行修复并重新验证凸抛光。在全部29个任务上，该方法零无效答案（参考方法21个），首次认证答案耗时0.11秒（参考方法未验证答案1.59秒），且在参考解物理有效的所有查询上精确复现其最优值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于凸集图（GCS）的规划器假设凸区域无碰撞，但区域生成器只能概率性保证该性质，没有规划器验证这一假设，这导致规划器可能返回碰撞轨迹却报告为成功。作者希望首次量化这一概率保证与实际碰撞之间的差距代价，并构建能够主动认证答案正确性的规划器。

Method: 作者首先测量了概率性区域生成与实际碰撞之间的差距（3.2%碰撞样本导致62%答案错误）。接着验证了修复库的多种方案（更严格验收、SOS认证、均匀裕度）均不可行。最终提出新规划器：(1)采样分解的重叠面和共享面；(2)用可采纳的知情界剪枝；(3)对搜索每轮提出的唯一候选解，通过无分辨率参数的安全证书球链连续验证；(4)失败时用局部区域内绕行修复并重新验证凸抛光阶段。

Result: 在全部29个任务查询上与参考GCS规划器对比：新方法零无效答案（参考方法21个无效答案）；首次认证答案耗时0.11秒（参考方法未验证答案需1.59秒）；在参考答案物理有效的所有查询上精确复现其最优值。这证明认证规划器不仅消除了错误，还在速度和最优性上不逊于乃至超过不验证的参考规划器。

Conclusion: 概率性区域保证与规划器信任之间的鸿沟导致了显著的答案错误（62%），而简单修复不可行。通过构建连续验证每个候选解的规划器，可以在不牺牲速度（更快达到首个认证答案）和最优性（精确复现参考最优值）的情况下实现零无效答案，为GCS类规划器提供了一条迈向可认证安全运动规划的道路。

Abstract: Planners on graphs of convex sets return trajectories that are collision-free by construction, provided the convex regions are collision-free. The region generator only promises that property probabilistically, and no planner in the family verifies it. We report the first measurement of what the gap costs. On a scaled 14-DOF bimanual library, $3.2\%$ of interface samples are in collision, and a search-based GCS planner (\gcsstar) turns that volume error into a $62\%$ answer error: $18$ of $29$ pick-and-place queries return trajectories that drive the arms through the shelves, up to $91$\,mm deep, reported as successes. Repairing the library does not work; a ten times stricter acceptance contract, sums-of-squares certified regions, and uniform margins each destroy the connectivity planning needs before they deliver soundness. We instead build a planner that certifies its answers. It samples the overlaps and shared faces of the decomposition, prunes with an admissible informed bound, and verifies the one candidate each search round proposes, continuously, by a chain of clearance certificate balls with no resolution parameter; failures are repaired with local in-region detours, and the convex polish is re-verified. Head-to-head on all $29$ task queries it delivers zero invalid answers against $21$ for the reference, reaches its first certified answer in $0.11$\,s against $1.59$\,s for the reference's unverified one, and reproduces the reference optimum exactly on every query whose reference answer is physically valid.

</details>


### [211] [Self-Aware Active Learning Enables Continual Improvement in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29772)
*Dong Hu,Chao Huang,Carman K. M. Lee,Dimitrios Kanoulas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAGE是一个用于自动驾驶后训练自适应的主动学习框架。它通过世界模型生成两种内在信号——"恐惧"（估计预测风险与模型不确定性）和"好奇心"（通过预测误差衡量新颖性）——来调节干预阈值。当恐惧超过自适应阈值时，智能体将控制权移交专家或后备策略，并利用接管轨迹进行聚焦模仿学习。该方法在模拟路径迁移、Waymo日志场景、CARLA遮挡危险和真实移动机器人导航中均提升了鲁棒性并减少了安全违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的自动驾驶系统在熟悉条件下可靠，但面对罕见分布偏移和长尾事件时容易突然失效。核心局限在于大多数智能体主要从被动经验中学习，缺乏估计自身能力不足的机制，无法及时寻求帮助，也不能将安全危急场景转化为针对性改进。

Method: SAGE包含三个关键组件：(1) 学习预测世界模型，生成"恐惧"信号（估计短时域预测风险与模型不确定性）和"好奇心"信号（通过预测误差衡量新颖性）；(2) 好奇心自适应校准恐惧的干预阈值，实现情境依赖的风险调节；(3) 当恐惧超过阈值时转移控制权给专家或后备策略，用接管轨迹进行聚焦模仿学习，同时将恐惧作为安全约束集成到策略优化和评估中以减少性能回退。

Result: 在模拟路径迁移任务、Waymo日志驾驶场景、CARLA遮挡危险和真实移动机器人导航测试中，SAGE在新型和危急场景中提升了鲁棒性，减少了安全违规，并保持了与强基线策略相当的任务性能。

Conclusion: 智能体可以通过估计自身能力边界、在需要时请求指导、选择性地从稀有高价值事件中学习，从而在初始训练后持续改进。

Abstract: Learning-based autonomous driving (AD) systems can perform reliably in familiar conditions, yet rare distribution shifts and long-tail events remain a major source of abrupt failure. A central limitation is that most agents learn primarily from passive experience and lack mechanisms to estimate when their competence is insufficient, seek timely assistance, and convert safety-critical encounters into targeted improvement. Here we present self-aware guided exploration (SAGE), an active learning framework for post-training adaptation in AD. SAGE learns a predictive world model that generates two online intrinsic signals: fear, which estimates short-horizon predictive risk and model uncertainty, and curiosity, which measures novelty through prediction error. Curiosity adaptively calibrates the intervention threshold for fear, allowing the agent to regulate risk in a context-dependent manner. When predicted fear exceeds this adaptive threshold, the agent transfers control to an expert or fallback policy and uses the resulting takeover trajectories for focused imitation learning. In parallel, fear is integrated into policy optimization and evaluation as a safety-oriented constraint to reduce performance regressions during adaptation. We evaluate SAGE in simulated route-transfer tasks, Waymo-based logged driving scenarios, CARLA occlusion hazards, and real-world mobile robot navigation tests. Across these settings, SAGE improves robustness in novel and safety-critical scenarios, reduces safety violations, and maintains task performance comparable to strong baseline policies. These results suggest that agents can improve after initial training by estimating the limits of their competence, requesting guidance when needed, and learning selectively from rare high-value events.

</details>


### [212] [EMERGE-Policy: A Robot Mind Emerges Beyond a Single Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29896)
*Zhirui Fang,Qingchi Yu,Ziyang Chen,Longfei Li,Haoran Ma,Keru Zhou,Xinrun Xu,Samith Va,Yuxuan Hu,Peixuan Song,Qiang Du,Bin Qian,Yongkang Deng,Xin Li,Yezhen Wang,Zhe Li,Hao Luo,Shuyan Li,Ziwei Wang,Weijian Deng,Xiu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EMERGE-Policy 提出了一种图结构的智能体框架，通过主智能体与多个角色专属子智能体的协同编排，将感知、推理、预测、执行、验证和记忆等功能模块化组合，无需额外微调即可在公开基准和真实机器人实验中取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人的有效"心智"不必局限于单一策略中，而可以通过多个专业化组件的协同编排涌现。作者希望通过功能子任务的划分与多智能体并发协作来扩展机器人策略的鲁棒性，超越孤立运行的单一策略框架。

Method: EMERGE-Policy 是一个图结构的智能体框架：主智能体在活跃上下文窗口中维护任务级状态，多个角色专属子智能体（感知、执行监控、验证、记忆整合）在隔离上下文中处理各自功能并返回结构化证据；通过角色专属上下文控制信息负载，功能化 Skill 接口将异构后端组合为操作、想象和评估技能；基于准则的验证、文本故障诊断和分支栈恢复提供局部修正，结合 token 感知的外部记忆保留任务状态。

Result: 无需额外微调，在多个具有广泛影响力的公开基准上取得优异表现，并完成了一系列真实机器人实验，证明系统级设计能有效扩展机器人策略的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过多智能体间的功能子任务分工与并发协作，以及将模型视为技能并在框架内调用的技术范式，EMERGE-Policy 能够将鲁棒的机器人策略从孤立运行扩展为系统级涌现行为。

Abstract: A robot's effective ``mind'' need not reside in a single policy. It can emerge when specialized components perceive, reason, predict, act, verify, and remember within a shared orchestration process. EMERGE-Policy turns this perspective into a graph-structured agentic framework that coordinates both capability invocation and information exchange. A Main Agent retains task-level state within an active context window, while role-specific Sub Agents process perception, execution monitoring, verification, and memory consolidation in isolated contexts and return structured, task-relevant evidence. Role-specific contexts control information load by exposing only decision-relevant evidence to the Main Agent, while the functional Skill interface composes heterogeneous backends as Operational, Imagination, and Evaluation Skills. Criterion-grounded verification, textual failure diagnosis, and Branch Stack recovery provide localized correction, with token-aware external memory preserving task-relevant state. Together, their closed-loop interaction realizes the system-level policy captured by the name EMERGE-Policy. Without additional fine-tuning, we achieved outstanding performance on several public benchmark that have had a wide-reaching impact, and conducted a series of real robot experiments. These system-level results suggest that through the division of different functional sub-tasks among multiple agents and their concurrent collaboration, as well as the technical paradigm where the model is regarded as a skill and called within the framework, EMERGE-Policy can extend the robust robot policies beyond isolated runs.

</details>


### [213] [System Identification of Admittance Models for Large Real-World Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29935)
*Nathan I. Baum,Nathaniel G. Luttmer,Mark A. Minor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个完整的物理一致性建模工作流，用于生成大型真实世界物体的模型，该模型能保证惯性和摩擦参数的物理一致性，无需在铰链或轴关节处安装扭矩传感器。方法在门和独轮车两个物体上验证，手柄估计RMS误差低于0.64 N和0.042 Nm，门体估计RMS误差为2.19 Nm，独轮车体估计RMS误差为6.77 Nm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 导纳型模型的仿真需要物理上一致的动态模型，但现成的日常物体很少具备这样的模型，这限制了依赖此类仿真的触觉界面的保真度。因此需要一种能够为大型真实世界物体生成物理一致性模型的工作流。

Method: 提出一个完整的工作流，分两个阶段将物体分离并识别手柄和主体，无需在铰链、轴等约束关节处安装扭矩传感器。针对不同类型的物体采用不同的建模策略：重型闭门器驱动的门采用四杆连杆运动学和基于流体动力学的集总参数模型（包括开启、回拉、摆动和闩锁区域）；独轮车则建模为具有球形轮子且在滚动中无滑动的刚体。

Result: 手柄估计RMS误差在两个物体上均低于0.64 N和0.042 Nm。门体估计RMS误差为2.19 Nm，独轮车体估计RMS误差为6.77 Nm。

Conclusion: 该工作流能够为具有不同约束类型和模型复杂度的大型真实世界物体生成物理一致的动态模型，满足导纳型触觉仿真的需求，为后续高保真触觉界面的开发奠定了基础。

Abstract: Simulation of admittance-type models requires physically consistent dynamic models that are rarely available for off-the-shelf, everyday objects, limiting the fidelity of haptic interfaces that rely on such simulations. This paper presents the first complete workflow for producing physically consistent models of large real-world objects with various constraints and mechanisms, guaranteeing physical consistency of inertia and friction parameters. The workflow separates each object and identifies the handle and body in two stages, requiring no torque sensors at hinges, axles, or other constrained joints. Models are produced for a heavy, closer-actuated door and a wheelbarrow, representing objects of differing constraint types and model complexity. The door is modeled using four-bar linkage kinematics and a fluid dynamics-based lumped parameter model including opening, backcheck, swing, and latch zones. The wheelbarrow is modeled as a rigid body with a spherical wheel and no slip during rolling. Handle estimation RMS errors were below 0.64 N and 0.042 Nm across both objects. Door body estimation had RMS error of 2.19 Nm and wheelbarrow body estimation had RMS error of 6.77 Nm.

</details>


### [214] [Training-Free Action Correction for VLA Model Failures via Language Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29967)
*Owen Kwon,Pablo Ortega-Kral,Arthur Bucker,Jean Oh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CorrectVLA框架，通过任务级别的自然语言修正来调整动作幅度，在不修改策略权重的情况下修正VLA模型在部署中的执行错位故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型虽然具有强大的语义理解能力，但在部署过程中会出现系统性故障。目前对这些故障发生的条件以及能否在不重新训练的情况下被修正的理解还很有限，本文旨在弥补这一研究空白。

Method: CorrectVLA将任务级自然语言修正转化为加性的动作幅度调整，不修改策略权重。人类提供一次任务级修正，该修正统一应用于所有试验轨迹，无需逐情节干预。方法通过在LIBERO-90上建立故障模式分类体系来界定可修正的故障子集。

Result: 在仿真中，CorrectVLA恢复了分布内和分布外任务中的执行错位故障。在真实机器人（UFactory xArm7）环境偏移实验中，基础策略几乎完全失效时，CorrectVLA恢复了近乎完美的成功率，并跨物体位置和身份泛化。通过LIBERO-90故障分类发现，执行错位故障（策略到达正确目标但动作幅度校准错误）是可修正的子集，而语义理解本身的故障则不可用此方法修正。

Conclusion: 该方法在策略具备策略正确性时有效，在根本性理解缺失时失效，为推理时修正建立了实用的操作边界。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models demonstrate strong semantic understanding yet exhibit systematic failures during deployment. The conditions under which these failures occur, and whether they can be corrected without retraining, remain poorly understood. In this paper, we take steps toward addressing this gap. We present CorrectVLA, a framework that translates task-level natural language corrections into additive action magnitude adjustments without modifying policy weights. A human provides a single task-level correction, applied uniformly across all rollouts without per-episode intervention. In simulation, CorrectVLA recovers execution misalignment failures across both in-distribution and OOD tasks. In real-robot experiments on a UFactory xArm7 under environment shift, CorrectVLA restores near-perfect success where the base policy almost entirely breaks down, generalizing across object locations and identities. Through a taxonomy of failure modes on LIBERO-90, we find that execution misalignment failures, where the policy reaches the correct target but miscalibrates action magnitudes, represent the correctable subset, while other failure modes where semantic comprehension itself breaks down are not amenable to this approach. The approach succeeds when policies possess strategic correctness and fails when fundamental comprehension is absent, establishing a practical operational boundary for inference-time correction.

</details>


### [215] [MiBOT: A head-worn robot that modulates cardiovascular responses through human-like soft massage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30055)
*Alice Mylaeus,Stephanie Vogt,Berken Utku Demirel,Marcel Gort,Mirko Meboldt,Manuel Meier,Christian Holz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MiBOT，一种头戴式按摩机器人，通过气动人工肌肉驱动两个软触觉执行器产生模拟人类按摩的触摸动作，实现静音驱动，并在受控实验中验证其能降低受试者收缩压2.8 mmHg、舒张压1.7 mmHg，并使心率平均降低8-10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 按摩疗法对多种疾病（如偏头痛和压力引起的头痛）的康复有益，但现有机器人系统主要聚焦于躯干和四肢的按摩，通过合适的驱动方式在人的头部执行按摩动作仍是一个挑战。

Method: 设计并实现了MiBOT头戴式按摩机器人，采用两个软触觉执行器产生模拟人类按摩的触摸动作。关键设计原理是静音驱动，通过气动人工肌肉结合控制器回路来响应接触压力，从而实现无声操作。

Result: 在受控研究中评估了MiBOT的有效性，监测受试者的血压和心率。结果显示该机械系统产生了与人工按摩相似的积极且具结论性的量化结果：受试者平均收缩压降低2.8 mmHg，舒张压降低1.7 mmHg，心率平均降低8-10%。

Conclusion: MiBOT头戴式按摩机器人在生理指标上产生了与人工按摩相当的积极效果，证明其作为一种静音、有效的头部按摩机器人系统的可行性，为头部按摩机器人领域提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Massage therapy is helpful for the rehabilitation of various diseases, such as headaches caused by migraines and stress. Existing robotic systems have focused on massage therapy on the torso and limbs, but performing massage motions through suitable actuation on a person's head has been a challenge. In this paper, we present MiBOT, a head-worn massage robot that actuates two soft tactors to produce touch motions mimicking human massage. A key design principle behind MiBOT is its silent actuation, which we achieve through pneumatic artificial muscles in conjunction with a controller loop to respond to contact pressure. We evaluated the effectiveness of MiBOT in a controlled study and assessed subjects' blood pressure and heart rate levels while applying MiBOT. We found that our mechanical system generated positive and conclusive quantitative outcomes that are similar to the human-administered massage, decreasing participants' mean systolic and diastolic blood pressure by 2.8 mmHg and 1.7 mmHg, respectively, as well as calming their heart rate by 8-10% on average.

</details>


### [216] [Rethinking Language's Role in Efficient VLA for Autonomous Vehicles: Toward Smarter, Trustworthy Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30144)
*Tongfei Guo,Lili Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文聚焦视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在自动驾驶中的推理成本问题，提出"语言残差"（Language Residue）分类法，将VLA方法按推理时语言使用程度分为L1-L4四个层级，并从延迟、参数、内存、FLOPs、token五个部署维度对代表性方法进行系统分析和基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型通过语言统一感知、推理与控制，从而提升自动驾驶的可解释性和长尾泛化能力。然而语言模态在车载端推理时成本高昂——延迟和内存预算紧张，自回归解码本质上是串行的。由于推理成本在每一帧部署中都会重复产生，而训练成本只需支付一次，因此需要在推理时决策"何时、何地使用语言"，以平衡性能与部署开销。

Method: 提出Language Residue分类法，将推理时利用语言的方式分为四类：L1（仅训练时监督学习）、L2（潜在的、非文本推理）、L3（条件触发式调用）、L4（每帧完整生成）。引入五个部署轴（延迟、参数量、内存、FLOPs、token数）对代表性方法进行标记分析，并在nuScenes、NAVSIM、Bench2Drive等开环和闭环驾驶基准上进行分析。同时追踪NLP/LLM中的高效方法如何被适配到自动驾驶领域，并识别这些适配的约束与动机。

Result: 对VLA方法按推理时语言使用层级进行了系统归类，在多维度部署指标上分析了各方法的成本特征，并通过主流开环/闭环基准揭示了不同L1-L4策略的性能与效率权衡。整理出NLP/LLM高效技术在自动驾驶中的适配路径及背后的约束和动机。

Conclusion: 语言并非自动驾驶中必须每帧使用的昂贵资源，应根据具体场景与部署约束选择合适的推理时语言使用层级。作者将持续维护一个开源仓库，收录相关方法的分类与更新。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are reshaping autonomous driving (AD) by unifying perception, reasoning, and control through language, enabling semantic grounding, interpretable decisions, and better long-tail generalization. But language is expensive onboard: latency and memory budgets are tight, and autoregressive decoding is inherently sequential. This work reframes the central question as when and where language should act at inference, since inference cost recurs at every deployed frame while training cost is paid once. We introduce the Language Residue taxonomy to organize methods by their inference-time use of language: train-time-only supervision (L1), latent non-textual reasoning (L2), conditional invocation (L3), and full per-frame generation (L4). We review representative methods and tag each across five deployment axes (latency, parameters, memory, FLOPs, tokens), analyzing them on major open- and closed-loop driving benchmarks (e.g., nuScenes, NAVSIM, Bench2Drive). We further trace how efficient methods from NLP/LLM are adapted in AD, identifying the constraints and motivations driving these adaptations. A continuously updated repository will be available at Github.

</details>


### [217] [Contrast-Free Autonomous Navigation of Untethered Endovascular Microrobots Using Single-Plane Fluoroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30220)
*Husnu Halid Alabay,Tuan-Anh Le,Ping Wang,Hakan Ceylan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VISTA是一种数字孪生框架，可在单平面X射线透视下实现无造影剂的磁驱动无系绳微型机器人自主3D导航，通过重建血管3D数字孪生、离散中心线里程碑并生成磁驱动指令，在离体血管模型和活体大鼠下腔静脉中验证，较传统人工控制减少62%导航时间、98%矫正指令和57%辐射暴露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁驱动无系绳微型机器人的3D导航是临床转化的主要障碍。单平面荧光透视仅提供2D投影，丢失深度信息，而通过双平面成像或重复造影获取深度会增加程序复杂度、辐射暴露和造影剂负担，亟需一种不依赖造影剂的3D导航方法。

Method: VISTA框架包含三个核心步骤：(1)将血管解剖结构重建为3D数字孪生；(2)将血管中心线离散化为导航里程碑；(3)将检测到的2D机器人位置分配给最近的投影里程碑。连续的里程碑定义局部血管方向，生成磁驱动指令，将单平面荧光观察转换为拓扑约束的导航状态，无需在导航过程中注射造影剂。

Result: 在解剖结构不同的血管模型（连续流动条件下）和活体大鼠下腔静脉中进行了验证。与传统荧光透视人工控制相比，VISTA将导航时间减少最多62%，矫正驱动指令减少最多98%，辐射暴露减少最多57%。

Conclusion: VISTA被确立为一种数字孪生引导的框架，利用广泛可用的单平面荧光透视实现无系绳血管内微型机器人的无造影剂自主导航，为临床转化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Reliable three-dimensional (3D) navigation of magnetically actuated untethered microrobots remains a major barrier to clinical translation. X-ray fluoroscopy is the standard real-time imaging modality for endovascular procedures, but single-plane fluoroscopy provides only a two-dimensional (2D) projection, eliminating depth information and complicating autonomous navigation. Recovering this information through biplane imaging or repeated contrast-enhanced angiography increases procedural complexity, radiation exposure, or contrast burden. Here, we introduce VISTA (Virtual Integration for Spatial Tracking and Autonomy), a digital twin framework enabling contrast-free autonomous navigation under single-plane fluoroscopy. VISTA reconstructs vascular anatomy as a 3D digital twin, discretizes the vessel centerline into navigation milestones, and assigns the detected 2D robot position to the nearest projected milestone. Consecutive milestones define the local vessel orientation used to generate magnetic actuation commands, converting single-plane fluoroscopic observations into topology-constrained navigation states without requiring contrast injection during navigation. VISTA is demonstrated across anatomically distinct vascular phantoms under continuous flow and within the inferior vena cava of a live rat in vivo. Compared with conventional fluoroscopic human-in-the-loop control, VISTA reduced navigation time by up to 62%, corrective actuation commands by up to 98%, and radiation exposure by up to 57%. These results establish VISTA as a digital twin-guided framework for contrast-free autonomous navigation of untethered endovascular microrobots using widely available single-plane fluoroscopy.

</details>


### [218] [Optimized Modular Design and Development of a Tilt-Rotor Bicopter Drone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30222)
*Saideep Verma,Nimisha Tatapudi,Akshay Arjun,Danwada Sharanya Sukka,Abhishek Sarkar,Joyjit Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对倾转旋翼双旋翼无人机的模块化设计展开研究，通过结构分析、气动模拟、重心与中性点位置控制、推力功率分析等步骤完成设计，并进行了飞行测试验证仿真结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合动力系统（如倾转旋翼双旋翼无人机）兼具固定翼和旋翼技术的优势，可实现长航时和垂直起降能力，但需要优化设计以确保静态和动态稳定性。

Method: 采用系统性分析和仿真方法：1）结构分析验证不同飞行模式下的结构强度；2）控制重心（CG）与中性点（NP）的相对位置以确保悬停和前飞稳定性；3）推力与功率分析评估飞行性能和续航能力；4）基于分析结果制作样机并进行飞行试验。

Result: 经过仿真分析和实际制造后，使用基础飞控进行飞行测试，验证了仿真中获得的关键性能指标。

Conclusion: 该模块化双旋翼无人机设计通过仿真驱动的分析流程完成了从设计到验证的闭环，证明了所提出设计方法的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Hybrid systems like tilt-rotor bicopter drones combine the beneficial characteristics of both fixed-wing and rotary-wing technology, enabling long endurance and VTOL capability. However, such drones also require an optimum design to ensure both static and dynamic stability. The modular design of a traditional bicopter is developed in this paper based on extensive analysis and in-depth structural and aerodynamic simulations. The structural analysis has been performed to ensure that the aircraft's structure withstands the stresses encountered during different flight modes. Controlling the relative positions of the Center of Gravity (CG) and Neutral Point (NP) is an essential aspect of the design, ensuring stability during hover and positive stability during forward flight. The thrust and power analyses have been conducted to assess the flight performance and endurance. After analysis, the drone has been developed, and flight tests with a basic flight controller were conducted to validate the performance metrics obtained in the simulation.

</details>


### [219] [Motus2: A Self-Evolving General World Model for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30237)
*Hongzhe Bi,Zihao Zhou,Yihang Tang,Jingrui Pang,Shuhe Huang,Haitian Liu,Runqing Wang,Shuai Huang,Yichen Wang,Yiming Cheng,Ruowen Zhao,Zhenghua Li,Hengkai Tan,Xiaolong Liu,Jinhui Wan,Jiabao Liu,Min Zhao,Fan Bao,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Motus2是一种面向灵巧操作的自进化通用世界模型，通过模型缩放和数据缩放两条主线推进。模型上，单一共享权重模型同时暴露策略、模拟器和评估器三个控制接口，形成闭环决策-学习回路用于策略改进；数据上，从大规模单目自我中心数据逐步扩展到同步立体数据、机器人域适配数据及人机对齐数据，并加入触觉反馈与混合记忆扩展，最终在完全仿生平台上实例化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用具身智能体需要在统一系统中感知、预测、行动、评估和改进。现有世界模型通常只是在世界模拟器上附加动作输出头，没有将决策和学习耦合为闭环回路，无法实现策略的自我改进。

Method: Motus2在模型侧采用单一共享权重的模型同时实现三种控制接口：策略（世界-动作模型）、模拟器（动作条件世界模型）和评估器（价值模型）。策略提出候选动作块，模拟器预测其视觉后果，评估器评估预测结果，三者耦合形成闭环决策-学习回路。数据侧从单目自我中心数据逐步扩展到立体自我中心数据，再通过机器人轨迹和互补的人-机对齐数据进行域适配。此外还研究了全局自回归和混合记忆的上下文扩展、以及触觉反馈用于接触感知控制。

Result: Motus2在完全仿生平台上实例化，该平台具备立体视觉、双臂、双灵巧手和触觉感知。通过自我中心数据扩展和闭环通用世界模型扩展的结合，为自我进化的灵巧操作提供了一条通用路径。

Conclusion: 自我中心数据扩展与闭环通用世界模型扩展相结合，是通往自我进化灵巧操作的一条通用路径。数据中的次优交互和失败轨迹为动力学建模和价值学习提供重要证据，而模型中的闭环耦合保证了策略可以持续自主改进。

Abstract: General embodied agents should perceive, predict, act, evaluate, and improve within a unified system. World models have shown great promise in building such agents, yet existing models typically append an action output head to a world simulator, without coupling them into a closed decision-and-learning loop for policy improvement. We present Motus2, a self-evolving general world model for dexterous manipulation. Motus2 advances world modeling through model scaling and data scaling. For model scaling, a single model with shared weights exposes three control interfaces: a policy (world-action model), a simulator (action-conditioned world model), and an evaluator (value model). The policy proposes candidate action chunks, the simulator predicts their visual consequences, and the evaluator assesses the predicted outcomes. Their coupling forms a closed decision-and-learning loop for policy improvement. This formulation uses curated expert demonstrations for action learning, while failed and suboptimal interactions provide valuable evidence for dynamics modeling and value learning. For data scaling, Motus2 progresses from large-scale monocular egocentric data to synchronized stereo egocentric data, followed by robot-domain adaptation with robot trajectories and supplementary human-robot alignment data. Motus2 further studies global-autoregressive and hybrid-memory extensions of its sliding-window context, adds tactile feedback for contact-aware control, and is instantiated on a fully biomimetic platform with stereo vision, dual arms, dual dexterous hands, and tactile sensing. Together, egocentric data scaling and closed-loop general world model scaling provide a general path toward self-evolving dexterous manipulation.

</details>


### [220] [CanonNav: Disentangling Navigation Behavior from Camera Geometry in Cross-Platform Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30242)
*Dong-Wook Kim,Ji-Hoon Hwang,E-In Son,Mintaek Oh,Seung-Woo Seo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CanonNav框架，通过相机几何标准化解耦导航行为与相机几何，并引入安全性和局部进程的规划监督信号，提升跨平台演示学习的视觉导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉导航的模仿学习虽已利用跨平台演示数据取得进展，但仍面临两大挑战：一是直接从图像-轨迹对学习会纠缠导航行为与平台相关的相机几何，迫使策略隐式推断相机几何（病态问题）；二是演示轨迹只捕获专家的最终动作，未显式包含决策背后的中间规划推理。

Method: 提出CanonNav框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 相机几何标准化（camera geometry canonicalization），将视觉观测和轨迹变换到相机一致的表示空间，解耦导航行为与相机几何；(2) 利用离线可通行性估计器生成的伪标签，引入安全监督（惩罚不安全轨迹）和局部进程监督（引导机器人前进方向）两种互补的规划监督信号。

Result: 在多种相机配置和环境中进行实验，CanonNav在推理时仅使用RGB输入，即可一致地优于基于RGB的基线方法，并在挑战性场景中超越基于RGB-D的方法。

Conclusion: 通过解耦相机几何并引入规划级监督，CanonNav有效解决了跨平台演示学习中的视觉-几何纠缠和决策隐式化问题，显著提升了视觉导航的性能与泛化能力。

Abstract: While visual navigation has advanced through imitation learning from cross-platform demonstrations, fully leveraging such data remains challenging. First, directly learning from image-trajectory pairs entangles navigation behavior with platform-dependent camera geometry. This hinders consistent learning by forcing the policy to implicitly infer camera geometry from visual observations, an inherently ill-posed problem. Second, imitation learning from demonstrated trajectories captures the expert's chosen motion but leaves the intermediate decisions underlying that motion implicit. To address these issues, we propose CanonNav, a visual navigation framework that disentangles navigation behavior from camera geometry and incorporates complementary planning supervision into learning from cross-platform demonstrations. CanonNav introduces camera geometry canonicalization, which transforms visual observations and trajectories into a camera-consistent representation space. Building on this representation, we derive safety and local-progress supervision using pseudo-labels from an offline traversability estimator. Safety supervision penalizes unsafe trajectories, while local-progress supervision guides where the robot should advance. Experiments across diverse camera configurations and environments show that, despite using only RGB at inference, CanonNav consistently outperforms RGB-based baselines and even surpasses RGB-D-based methods in challenging scenarios.

</details>


### [221] [CometVLA: Co-Training on an Embodied Data Pyramid towards Physical Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30289)
*Hanwen Wan,Dafeng Chi,Linbo Zhai,Tianao Shen,Yuzheng Zhuang,Tianle Zhang,Peidong Liu,Liang Lin,Xiaoqiang Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CometVLA，通过构建与机器人动作数据严格对齐的具身物理VQA语料库CometData和基准CometBench，并引入Global Action Prior (GAP)标记作为可学习的紧凑瓶颈，将物理常识注入动作头而不破坏预训练VLM主干，从而证明物理理解预训练能真正提升下游操作任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在处理需要物理常识的操作任务时表现脆弱。当前物理VQA数据通常是非具身的，且与机器人动作领域不对齐；第一视角视频仅作为辅助预训练使用。此外，尚不清楚改进的VLM物理理解是否真正有利于下游动作生成。本文旨在弥合具身物理理解与动作生成之间的鸿沟。

Method: 1) 构建CometData和CometBench——与机器人动作数据和具身形态严格对齐的具身物理VQA语料库与评测基准；2) 引入Global Action Prior (GAP)标记，作为紧凑的可学习瓶颈，隔离任务无关的运动规律，使动作头能够消费物理常识而不破坏预训练VLM主干；3) 在具身数据金字塔（遥操作、仿真、第一视角轨迹、VQA层）上联合训练CometVLA。

Result: 在真实世界操作任务和RoboTwin仿真中，CometVLA持续优于强VLA基线。相关性分析表明，在CometBench上VLM性能越强，VLA任务成功率越高。

Conclusion: 物理理解预训练确实有益于下游操作任务，CometVLA通过具身对齐的物理VQA数据和GAP标记设计，成功验证了物理常识与动作生成之间的正相关关系，为该领域提供了标准化的评测基准和方法框架。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models remain brittle in manipulation tasks that require physical commonsense. Current physical VQA data is typically disembodied and misaligned with robot action domains. Egocentric videos are used only as auxiliary pre-training. It remains unclear whether improved VLM physical understanding actually benefits downstream action generation. Therefore, we present CometVLA to close this gap. We construct CometData and CometBench, an embodied physical VQA corpus and benchmark strictly aligned with the robot's action data and embodiment. We introduce Global Action Prior (GAP) tokens, a compact learnable bottleneck that isolates task-agnostic motion regularities and lets the action head consume physical commonsense without corrupting the pre-trained VLM backbone. We co-train CometVLA across the embodied data pyramid, spanning teleoperation, simulation, egocentric trajectories, and VQA layers. On real-world manipulation tasks and RoboTwin simulation, CometVLA consistently outperforms strong VLA baselines. Correlation analysis shows that stronger VLM performance on CometBench indicates higher VLA success rates. Results demonstrate that physical understanding pre-training genuinely benefits downstream manipulation.

</details>


### [222] [Data-Centric Neuromotor Interfaces for Portable Human-Machine Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30301)
*Jiaxuan Li,Di Wu,Jianhua Liu,Yuxin Zhao,Jinnuo Li,Xiao Zhang,Zhenzhi Ying,Changsheng Dai,Xiang Li,Liming Shu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以数据为中心的范式，通过优化生理特征的可分性来构建轻量级表面肌电（sEMG）神经运动接口。仅用2,210个参数模型即可在34个手势上达到94.36%的准确率，突破了千参数级别的灵巧解码基准，并可在边缘设备上快速部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧的人机交互需要直观且易部署于资源受限边缘设备的接口。尽管柔性电子皮肤可实现高保真可穿戴数据采集，但实际部署中计算资源与便携性之间存在固有矛盾，亟需一种兼顾高识别性能与低模型复杂度的解决方案。

Method: 采用数据中心范式，核心思想是通过生理特征提取实现数据的内在可分性，从而降低对模型容量的需求。具体包括：开发无线高带宽电生理信号采集系统，结合肌肉特异性电极构建sEMG接口；利用高可分性数据训练仅2,210个参数的轻量级模型，并阐明数据-算法交互机制。

Result: 所提出的2,210参数模型在34个手势识别中达到94.36%的准确率，可在边缘设备上快速部署，建立了千参数级别的灵巧解码新基准，并在真实场景中验证了数据中心范式的可行性。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一条有原则且经过验证的路径，为神经运动接口在实际场景中的可靠部署奠定了基础，展示了数据质量主导、模型轻量化的新思路。

Abstract: Dexterous human-machine interaction requires intuitive and expressive interfaces that can be efficiently deployed on constrained edge devices. Flexible material-based neuromotor interfaces hold considerable promise, as they decode human movement intention into natural control. Although emerging flexible electronic skins enable wearable high-fidelity data acquisition, practical deployment inevitably involves trade-offs between computational resources and portability. We present a data-centric paradigm where physiological features yield fundamental separability, providing sufficient discriminative cues for recognition. A wireless, high-bandwidth system developed for collecting various electrophysiological signals, when integrated with muscle-specific electrodes, forms a surface electromyography-based interface. Exploiting highly separable data, a 2,210-parameter model achieves 94.36% accuracy across 34 gestures and can be rapidly deployed on edge devices, establishing a new thousand-parameter benchmark for dexterous decoding. The underlying data-algorithm interactions in the data-centric paradigm are further clarified, demonstrating its feasibility in real-world scenarios. This study provides a principled and validated pathway for practical deployment of reliable neuromotor interfaces.

</details>


### [223] [SpectraTac: A Compact Camera-Free Optical Tactile Sensor with Distributed Color Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30368)
*Hao Wu,Haotian Guo,Yu Feng,Yutong Wang,Yanzhe Wang,Jianshu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SpectraTac，一种紧凑、无相机的光学触觉传感器，通过主动RGB照明和分布式颜色传感捕获低维时空光谱特征，同时估计3D力和接触区域，成本低于5美元，直径19.2mm，力预测MAE约0.16-0.43N，九区域接触分类准确率达99.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉传感对机器人与人机系统的物理交互至关重要，但现有方案难以同时兼顾丰富的触觉信息与紧凑硬件、低成本、低计算开销。基于相机的触觉传感器虽信息丰富，但体积大、成本高、图像处理负载重。

Method: SpectraTac采用无相机的光学设计：接触形变柔顺透明弹性体并调制内部光场，通过主动RGB照明与三个分布式颜色传感器捕获空间分辨的颜色和强度变化，生成低维时空光谱特征。采用数据驱动的解码框架，从光测量中同时估计3D力和接触区域。

Result: 3D力预测在x、y、z轴上的MAE分别为0.161、0.164和0.429 N；九区域接触分类准确率达到99.9%；通过实时3D力跟踪和基于接触区域的人机交互控制任务验证了实用性。

Conclusion: 分布式颜色分辨光学传感为基于相机的触觉传感提供了一种紧凑、低成本的替代方案，适用于机器人、可穿戴传感和交互系统。

Abstract: Tactile sensing is essential for physical interaction in robotics and human--machine systems. However, combining rich tactile information with compact hardware, low cost, and low computational overhead remains challenging. This work presents SpectraTac, a compact, camera-free optical tactile sensor that combines active red--green--blue (RGB) illumination with spatially distributed color sensing. Contact deforms a compliant transparent elastomer and modulates its internal light field, producing spatially differentiated changes in color and intensity. Three distributed color sensors capture these responses as low-dimensional spatio-spectral features, avoiding cameras, imaging optics, and high-dimensional image processing. The device measures 19.2 mm in diameter and 4 mm in height, with a material cost below USD~5. A data-driven decoding framework simultaneously estimates three-dimensional (3D) force and the contact region from the optical measurements. For 3D force prediction, the sensor achieved mean absolute errors (MAEs) of 0.161, 0.164, and 0.429 N along the x-, y-, and z-axes, respectively. The nine-region contact-classification accuracy was 99.9%. We further evaluated real-time 3D force tracking and contact-region-based human--machine interaction through an interactive control task. These results indicate that distributed color-resolved optical sensing offers a compact, low-cost alternative to camera-based tactile sensing for robotics, wearable sensing, and interactive systems.

</details>


### [224] [PAVE: Predictive Alignment and Value-Guided Evolution for World-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30378)
*Botong Zhao,Fang Yu,Tim,Senhua Zhu,Xinyuan Chen,Yue Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种直接世界-动作策略（direct world-action policy），通过结合与结果无关的预测性学习（多尺度JEPA目标）和与结果相关的策略改进（基于优势值的文本条件流匹配），在不改变在线直接动作生成路径的前提下，显著提升了机器人操作策略的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的直接视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略通过行为克隆生成连续动作，但存在两个互补的缺陷：(1) 其表示未被明确要求描述场景在多时间尺度上的演化；(2) 部署轨迹质量参差不齐，直接复用未区分有效动力学与不良行为。

Method: Method包含两个核心组件：(1) 预测性学习：保留局部固定偏移的JEPA目标，并增加轨迹相对的多样本视界过渡对齐（在剩余回合的25%、50%、75%、100%处），仅作为训练目标，不向动作头提供显式未来token；(2) 策略改进：训练独立的分位数价值critic于累积部署轨迹上，计算与动作块对齐的N步优势值，并将其转换为正/负/空文本条件，用于流匹配actor的条件生成。

Result: 在三个仿真基准任务上，该方法取得了最强的综合性能，同时保持了直接actor的在线执行路径不变（多视界预测器和critic均不参与在线执行，仅从当前观测、语言指令和本体感受直接生成动作）。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过训练时引入多时间尺度预测目标与基于优势值的条件机制，可以在不改变在线推理结构的前提下，既让有效轨迹教授物理动态，又让actor仅在相对更优动作的条件下部署，从而兼顾表征质量与行为质量。

Abstract: Direct vision-language-action policies generate continuous robot actions efficiently, but standard behavior cloning leaves two complementary gaps: their representations are not explicitly required to describe how the scene evolves over multiple time scales, and deployment trajectories of unequal quality are often reused without separating useful dynamics from undesirable behavior. We introduce \method, a direct world-action policy that combines outcome-agnostic predictive learning with outcome-aware policy improvement. \method first retains a local fixed-offset JEPA objective and adds trajectory-relative multi-horizon transition alignment at 25%, 50%, 75%, and 100% of the remaining episode. These training-only targets require the current policy representation to preserve both local physical changes and longer-range task progress, without supplying explicit future tokens to the action head. \method then trains an independent distributional value critic on cumulative deployment trajectories, computes action-chunk-aligned $N$-step advantages, and converts them into positive, negative, or null text conditions for a flow-matching actor. Thus, every valid trajectory can teach what physically happened, while the actor is deployed only under the condition associated with relatively better actions. The multi-horizon predictor and critic are removed from online execution, preserving direct action generation from the current observation, language instruction, and proprioception. \redclaim{Across the three simulation benchmarks, \method achieves the strongest overall performance while preserving the direct actor's online execution path.}

</details>


### [225] [A Hybrid PEM-GP Framework for Uncertainty-Aware System Identification of Quadcopters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30433)
*Abdallah Ghoul,Ismail Khalil Bousserhane,Kadri Boufeldja*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合物理建模与数据驱动的四旋翼无人机动力学建模框架（PEM-GP），先用预测误差法（PEM）捕获系统主要结构，再用高斯过程（GP）学习残差动态，在Duckiedrone实验平台上验证，预测精度与LSTM相当，同时提供校准的不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典系统辨识方法（如PEM）虽然可解释性强，但难以捕捉小型四旋翼平台中显著的非线性效应；而纯数据驱动方法虽能更好建模复杂非线性，却缺乏可解释性和校准的不确定性估计。现有方法难以兼顾精度、可解释性和不确定性量化。

Method: 提出一个物理-数据混合建模框架：首先使用预测误差法（PEM）辨识物理基模型以捕获系统主要动力学结构，然后利用高斯过程（GP）对剩余残差动态进行学习建模。通过将已知物理效应与未建模动态分离，显式量化不确定性。

Result: 在类Duckiedrone的实验平台上验证，PEM-GP模型的预测精度与LSTM网络相当，同时具备校准的不确定性估计能力，提升了模型的可靠性和不确定性感知决策能力。

Conclusion: 将物理基模型与GP残差学习相结合是四旋翼动力学建模的有效方案，能够在保持可解释性的同时获得接近深度学习方法（如LSTM）的预测精度，并提供可靠的不确定性量化，为安全关键的控制应用奠定基础。

Abstract: Accurate dynamic models play a central role in achieving reliable control of quadcopters. Classical system identification methods remain widely used, mainly because of their interpretability. However, they often fail to capture important nonlinear effects, especially in small-scale aerial platforms where such effects become more pronounced.
  Data-driven approaches offer a different perspective. They can represent complex nonlinear dynamics more effectively, but this comes at the cost of reduced interpretability and the absence of well-calibrated uncertainty estimates.
  In this work, we propose a framework that combines physics-based modeling with data-driven learning, while explicitly accounting for uncertainty. A physics-based model is first identified using the Prediction Error Method (PEM), which captures the main structure of the system. The remaining dynamics are then modeled using a Gaussian Process (GP), allowing the residual behavior to be learned directly from data. This separation makes it possible to distinguish between known physical effects and unmodeled dynamics.
  The proposed framework is validated on a Duckiedrone-like experimental setup. The results show that the PEM-GP model achieves prediction accuracy comparable to that of a Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) network, while additionally providing calibrated uncertainty estimates. This combination improves model reliability and supports uncertainty-aware decision-making.

</details>


### [226] [Anomaly Detection on Small Industrial Components via Vision-Based Tactile Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30506)
*G. F. Preziosa,M. Casiglia,M. Faroni,A. M. Zanchettin,P. Rocco*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了基于视觉触觉传感（GelSight Mini）的工业异常检测方法，在真实工业部件数据集上比较了SPADE、PaDiM、FAPM和InReaCh四种特征嵌入方法，并从最小名义接触次数、跨位置泛化和分辨率成本效益三个实际部署角度进行了验证，为视觉触觉传感在工业质检中的应用提供了实用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型工业部件的缺陷是几何驱动的，且尺寸在亚厘米级，标准光学相机难以有效分辨。视觉触觉传感器虽能通过接触压痕生成高分辨率图像数据并与深度学习兼容，但其在工业异常检测中的有效应用尚未被充分探索。

Method: 在协作机器人上搭载GelSight Mini传感器，采集五个真实工业部件构建触觉数据集。对四种特征嵌入方法（SPADE、PaDiM、FAPM、InReaCh）进行无监督AD比较，并设计了三种贴合接触式传感部署约束的验证方案：Good Fraction分析最小名义接触次数（受凝胶磨损限制）；跨位置评估检测相同表面图案在不同接触位置的泛化能力；低分辨率与高分辨率的成本效益对比。

Result: 完成了对四种无监督异常检测方法在真实触觉数据集上的系统性基准测试，并通过三种针对接触式传感部署约束设计的验证方案，全面评估了各方法的性能表现及实际部署可行性。

Conclusion: 该基准研究为研究人员和从业者在工业AD中采用视觉触觉传感提供了实用指导，表明该模态可作为工业质量控制任务的有效替代方案。

Abstract: Automated inspection of small industrial components, including sub-centimetre-scale parts where defects are geometry-driven and poorly resolved by standard optical cameras, calls for sensing modalities that can directly capture fine surface geometry. Vision-based tactile sensors address this need by converting contact imprints into high-resolution image-like data compatible with existing deep-learning pipelines, yet their effective use for industrial anomaly detection (AD) remains largely unexplored. This work systematically evaluates unsupervised AD methods on a real tactile dataset covering five genuine industrial components acquired with a GelSight Mini sensor mounted on a collaborative robot. Four feature-embedding methods, SPADE, PaDiM, FAPM, and InReaCh, are compared under three validations explicitly motivated by the deployment constraints of contact-based sensing: a Good Fraction analysis establishing the minimum number of nominal contacts for stable performance, directly bounded by gel wear since every acquisition degrades the soft interface; a cross-position evaluation assessing generalization across different contact locations observing the same recurring surface pattern; and a low- versus high-resolution comparison evaluating the cost-benefit of higher-resolution tactile acquisition. Overall, this systematic benchmarking study provides practical guidance for researchers and practitioners adopting vision-based tactile sensing for industrial AD and shows how this modality can serve as a viable alternative for industrial quality-control tasks.

</details>


### [227] [Behavior-Skill: A Fine-Grained Benchmark for Evaluating Vision-Language-Action Policies in Long-Horizon Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30536)
*Chunyun Ma,Lun Luo,Xingjian Luo,Xiexing Feng,Hang Zhang,Wei Liu,Feng Qiao,Yaonan Wang,Huimin Lu,Xieyuanli Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Behavior-Skill是一个围绕可执行组成技能重构长时域移动操作任务学习与评估的基准，包含50个家庭任务、10000条示范中的235,492个技能实例，覆盖34个语义技能类别。该基准为每个技能实例提供指令-观测-动作对齐片段、可恢复的中间状态和技能成功条件，支持独立评估。实验表明技能间失败分布高度不均匀，接触丰富的操作技能是持续瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域移动操作任务的可靠执行极具挑战性，因为整体任务成功依赖于多个组成技能的成功完成。然而现有基准仍主要依赖完整任务回放和聚合任务级指标，导致中间环节的失败难以被观察和分析，阻碍了对策略能力的深入诊断与改进。

Method: 将长时域任务重构为可执行组成技能（skills），构建包含235,492个技能实例的大规模基准。每个实例包含技能指令与对齐的观测-动作片段，并关联可恢复的中间状态和技能成功条件，使每个技能能在有效前提条件下被独立评估。同时提出轨迹级和技能级指标以刻画策略能力。

Result: 在50个任务的完整基准上对代表性VLA策略（pi0.5和GR00T）进行广泛实验，发现失败在技能间高度不均匀分布，接触丰富的操作技能构成持续瓶颈，证明该基准能通过暴露中间能力画像来补充完整任务评估的不足。

Conclusion: Behavior-Skill通过引入细粒度的技能级评估框架，弥补了现有全任务级评估在中间失败诊断上的盲区，为分析和改进长时域VLA策略提供了有效工具，基准已公开。

Abstract: Reliable execution of long-horizon mobile manipulation tasks remains challenging because overall task success depends on the successful completion of multiple constituent skills. Existing benchmarks, however, still rely primarily on full-task rollouts and aggregate task-level metrics, making intermediate failures difficult to observe and analyze. We present Behavior-Skill, a benchmark that reformulates the learning and evaluation of long-horizon tasks around executable constituent skills. It contains 235,492 skill instances from 10,000 demonstrations across 50 household tasks and 34 semantic skill categories. Each instance pairs a skill instruction with an aligned observation-action segment, and is further associated with a restorable intermediate state and a skill success condition to enable independent evaluation under valid preconditions. We further introduce trajectory-level and skill-level metrics to characterize policy capability beyond aggregate task success. Extensive experiments across representative VLA policies including pi0.5 and GR00T on the complete 50-task benchmark show that failures are highly non-uniform across skills, with contact-rich manipulation skills forming persistent bottlenecks. These results demonstrate that Behavior-Skill complements full-task evaluation by exposing intermediate capability profiles for analyzing and improving long-horizon VLA policies. Behavior-Skill is publicly available at https://github.com/nubot-nudt/Behavior-Skill.

</details>


### [228] [Temporal Forcing: 4D Representation Alignment for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30643)
*Xingyu Ding,Yuzhong Zhao,Chunhai Zhao,Yinghuan Shi,Chaoyang Zhao,Yifan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Temporal Forcing，一种用于视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的4D表示对齐方法。通过引入历史通路使VLA模型总结观察历史，并与预训练的4D基础模型提取的几何特征进行对齐，从而捕获动态环境中随时间的3D世界演变，解决长 horizon 操作和视觉相似状态下的观察混叠问题。在LIBERO上达到98.8%的成功率，在物理隐藏放置任务中全任务成功率达43.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA方法通过将表示与3D场景几何对齐来提升操作性能，但仅捕捉当前状态而非随时间演化的过程，缺乏时间信息，导致在长 horizon 操作和视觉上相似状态（观察混叠）下表现不佳。

Method: 1) 引入历史通路（history pathway），使vanilla VLA模型能够将观察历史总结为时间感知的潜在表示；2) 将这些潜在表示与预训练4D基础模型提取的几何特征对齐，该4D模型通过时间一致的几何表示捕捉演化的3D世界。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上达到98.8%的成功率，超越基座模型2.2个百分点；在物理隐藏放置任务中，全任务成功率从20.0%提升至43.3%。

Conclusion: 通过4D时间对齐，Temporal Forcing有效增强了VLA模型对动态环境的时间理解能力，显著提升长 horizon 操作性能，代码将公开。

Abstract: Recent vision-language-action (VLA) methods improve manipulation performance by aligning their representations with 3D scene geometry. However, these methods often struggle with long-horizon manipulation and observation aliasing between visually similar states due to a lack of temporal information: the 3D scene geometry captures only the current state, rather than how it has evolved over time. To resolve this, we present Temporal Forcing, a 4D representation alignment method for VLA models. Specifically, we first introduce a history pathway that enables a vanilla VLA model to summarize observation history into temporally aware latent representations. Then, the latent representations are aligned with the geometric features extracted by a pretrained 4D foundation model, which captures the evolving 3D world through temporally consistent geometric representations, enabling a deeper understanding of dynamic environments. Temporal Forcing reaches 98.8% on LIBERO, outperforming its base model by 2.2 points. On a physical hidden-placement task, it raises full-task success from 20.0% to 43.3%. Code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [229] [CIG-RL: Curiosity-Driven Information-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Source Term Estimation in Uncertain Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30673)
*Junhee Lee,Seunghwan Kim,Hongro Jang,Hyungjin Kim,Hyoungho Park,Changseung Kim,Hyondong Oh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于好奇心驱动的信息引导强化学习方法（CIGRL），用于移动传感器自主估计危险气体泄漏源参数，通过主动探索未充分训练的信念状态转移并引入不确定性自适应主动感知奖励，在高噪声条件下实现鲁棒且高效的源项估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于深度强化学习（DRL）的源项估计（STE）方法在动作选择时依赖随机探索或仅基于信念不确定度降低，缺乏有效的探索策略，导致在噪声环境下策略鲁棒性不足。而信息论方法虽鲁棒但在线动作选择计算开销大，需要一种兼具计算效率和鲁棒性的解决方案。

Method: 提出好奇心驱动的信息引导强化学习框架：1）采用好奇心机制促进对训练中尚未充分探索的新型信念状态转移进行主动探索；2）引入不确定性自适应主动感知奖励，在不确定性条件下引导高效源搜索；3）将两者整合到DRL框架中，代理基于由含噪测量序列更新的信念状态选择动作。

Result: 在高噪声条件下的仿真实验和真实世界实验中均验证了所提框架的鲁棒性和可行性，证明该方法在实际STE问题中具有应用潜力。

Conclusion: 好奇心驱动的好奇心机制与不确定性自适应奖励相结合，能够有效提升DRL在噪声环境下STE任务中的策略鲁棒性和搜索效率，为实际危险气体泄漏源定位提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Source term estimation (STE), which aims to estimate key properties of the gas source, is essential for identifying hazardous gas releases. Information-theoretic approaches have been adopted for autonomous STE using mobile sensors due to robustness in noisy environments, yet their online action selection incurs substantial computational cost. Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) provides a promising alternative with its fast decision-making capability. In DRL-based STE, the agent selects actions based on belief states of the source term updated from noisy measurement sequences. However, existing methods rely on random exploration or solely on belief uncertainty reduction without an effective exploration strategy in DRL, which can limit policy robustness in noisy environments. To address this, we propose a curiosity-driven information-guided reinforcement learning for robust and efficient STE. The proposed method promotes active exploration of novel belief state transitions that have not been sufficiently explored during training. We further introduce an uncertainty-adaptive active perception reward to guide efficient source search under uncertainty. Simulations under high-noise conditions and real-world experiments demonstrate the robustness and feasibility of the proposed framework, highlighting its potential for practical STE problems.

</details>


### [230] [Learning to infer and manipulate through distributed whole-arm interaction in a soft robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30773)
*Chuhan Zhang,Ebrahim Shahabi,Kseniia Khomenko,Wei Pan,Cosimo Della Santina*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"物理智能"框架，通过分布式柔性交互同时完成任务相关信息获取与操纵行为组织，并利用基于记忆的强化学习策略在混合刚柔机械臂上实现盲态全身抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人智能对物理交互的利用十分有限，通常将交互视为需要抑制的干扰或补偿目标错位的手段。然而在自然界中（如大象象鼻、章鱼触腕），柔性附肢与环境的丰富大面积交互是感知与操作不可分割的过程，这一原则尚未被充分转化为机器人系统的设计理念。

Method: 提出物理智能框架，将分布式柔性交互作为同时揭示任务相关信息和组织操纵行为的机制。问题被建模为固有部分可观测的强化学习任务，提出基于记忆的端到端控制策略架构，三个关键创新包括：(i)预训练探索策略提供大范围工作空间探索参考；(ii)探索与抓取目标在单一循环策略中的联合优化；(iii)包含观测映射与策略微调的两阶段仿真到现实适应。系统采用混合刚柔机械臂，柔性结构内嵌入IMU作为唯一本体感知来源。

Result: 学习得到的策略能够自主协调工作空间探索、物体遭遇与定位、抓取相关属性推断以及稳定全身包裹，成功实现多种物体的盲态识别与抓取，仅依赖嵌入式IMU的本体感知。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了物理交互作为感知与操纵统一机制的可行性，为柔性机器人提供了从物理交互中端到端学习智能行为的新范式。

Abstract: In animals such as elephants and octopuses, acquiring non-visual information about an object and physically engaging with it are inseparable processes mediated by rich, large-area interactions between compliant appendages and the environment. Soft robots provide a natural platform for translating this principle into engineered systems. Yet current robotic intelligence makes limited use of physical interaction, treating it primarily as a disturbance to be rejected or, at best, as a means of compensating for object misalignment.
  Here, we introduce a physical intelligence framework in which distributed compliant interactions jointly reveal task-relevant information and organize manipulation behavior. This results in an intrinsically partially observable problem: key task-relevant information is never measured directly, but must instead be inferred from the history of physical interactions. We propose a reinforcement-learning architecture that addresses this challenge by learning a memory-based control policy end-to-end. The key innovations making this possible are (i) a pretrained exploration policy that provides a reference for broad workspace exploration, (ii) joint optimization that integrates exploration and grasping objectives within a single recurrent policy, and (iii) a two-stage sim-to-real adaptation including observation mapping and policy fine-tuning.
  We demonstrate this principle through blind whole-arm grasping with a hybrid rigid-soft robotic arm that we equip with IMUs embedded directly within its compliant structure, providing its only source of proprioceptive sensing. The learned policy successfully identifies and grasps various objects by autonomously coordinating workspace exploration, object encounter and localization, inference of grasp-relevant properties, and stable whole-arm wrapping.

</details>


### [231] [A Dual-Cam Parallel Elastic Actuator with Shared Gas-Spring Compensation for Humanoid Ankles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30832)
*Jingcheng Jiang,Yifang Zhang,Nikos G. Tsagarakis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于人形机器人踝关节的2自由度并联弹性执行器（PEA），通过双凸轮-单气弹簧架构实现俯仰和滚转两个方向的力矩补偿，相比传统多弹性元件方案更紧凑，并建立了耦合2自由度数学模型用于优化设计凸轮轮廓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人踝关节需要提升力矩容量和能量效率，传统多弹性元件补偿方案结构复杂、体积庞大，缺乏系统化的设计方法将任务需求映射到硬件实现。

Method: 提出双凸轮-单气弹簧架构，利用共享弹性元件同时实现俯仰和滚转方向的力矩补偿；建立耦合2自由度数学模型显式刻画共享弹簧导致的补偿单元间相互依赖；基于该模型开发优化设计框架，从给定力矩参考合成定制凸轮轮廓；进行CAD集成、静态FEA和运动学仿真验证。

Result: 静态有限元分析和运动学仿真证实了设计的可行性和力矩缓解效果，验证了该架构在紧凑性和定制化方面的优势。

Conclusion: 所提出的PEA设计是一种紧凑、可定制的人形机器人踝关节2自由度力矩补偿解决方案，建立了从任务需求到硬件实现之间的系统化设计链路。

Abstract: To improve torque capacity and energy efficiency of humanoid ankles, this paper proposes a 2-DoF parallel elastic actuator (PEA). The main novelty of the proposed design lies in its dual-cam, single-gas-spring architecture, which enables torque compensation in both pitch and roll using a shared elastic element, thereby improving structural compactness compared with conventional multi-element compensation schemes. By leveraging parallel gas springs and customized cam modules, the proposed architecture provides dual-axis torque assistance tailored to specific task requirements. The second key contribution is the formulation of a coupled 2-DoF mathematical model that explicitly captures the interdependence between the two compensation units through the shared spring. Based on this model, an optimization-based design framework is developed to synthesize customized cam profiles from prescribed torque references, establishing a systematic link from task requirements to hardware realization. The complete lower-leg CAD integration is presented in detail. Static FEA and kinematic simulations confirm the design's feasibility and torque-relief effectiveness. The results highlight the proposed design as a compact, customizable solution for 2-DoF humanoid ankle torque compensation.

</details>


### [232] [GAFT: Geo-Anchored Fine-Tuning for Hazard Identification from Rare Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30858)
*Yanran Xu,Chuanhang Qiu,Yue Wang,Wenbo Wu,Zhaoxing Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Geo-Anchored Fine-Tuning (GAFT)，一种参数高效的微调方法，利用几何先验引导视觉基础模型的LoRA适配，以识别越野导航中导致不可恢复状态（如高底盘卡滞或困住）的物理结构，显著提升了少样本、跨场景泛化下的失败检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野导航中，物理结构可能导致不可恢复状态（如高底盘卡滞、被困），需要人工干预。识别这些结构至关重要但极具挑战：失败事件稀有且采集成本高，导致训练数据有限；且数据只关联帧与结果，未标注导致失败的具体视觉线索。直接学习易利用场景特定的视觉线索，导致泛化能力差。

Method: GAFT通过几何先验引导LoRA适配：将空间注意力rollout图与几何先验对齐，同时保留预训练表示，实现参数高效的视觉基础模型适配。

Result: 在干预验证的森林危险物基准上，跨10个独立训练的适配，GAFT一致优于冻结DINOv2和监督PEFT基线，将重复留一场景外（LOSO）平均F2从0.0607提升至0.3757（配对分析下统计显著）。最佳GAFT模型在重复LOSO上达到F2=0.570。

Conclusion: GAFT通过注入几何先验有效克服了少样本和模糊标注带来的泛化难题，显著提升了越野导航失败结构的识别性能，验证了在参数高效微调中结合几何领域知识引导视觉基础模型的有效性。

Abstract: Off-road navigation can fail when physical structures induce irrecoverable states such as high-centering or entrapment, requiring human interventions. Identifying these structures is crucial, yet challenging. Such failure events are rare and costly to collect, resulting in limited training data. Moreover, the collected data associate frames with outcomes, but do not indicate the visual cues responsible for the failure. Learning directly from these data can therefore exploit scenario-specific visual cues, leading to poor generalization. We propose \textbf{Geo-Anchored Fine-Tuning (GAFT)}, a parameter-efficient method that adapts a vision foundation model with a geometry-derived prior. It guides LoRA adaptation by aligning a spatial attention-rollout map with the geometry prior, while preserving pretrained representations. On an intervention-verified forest hazard benchmark, across ten independently trained adaptations, GAFT consistently outperforms frozen DINOv2 and supervised PEFT baselines, improving the repeated leave-one-scenario-out mean $F_2$ from 0.0607 to 0.3757 with statistical significance under paired analysis. Within these independently trained models, the best-performing GAFT model achieves a repeated-LOSO $F_2$ of 0.570. Code and benchmark: https://github.com/Xu-Yanran/geo_anchored_fine_tuning

</details>


### [233] [Zeva: In-Context Causal Learning for Generalizable Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30880)
*Fu Chen,Xin Ding,Bingjia Huang,Xiangyu Li,Mingju Wang,Jiawei He,Kun Li,Wei Sun,Yunxin Liu,Hao Wu,Ting Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了Zeva框架，这是首个让机器人在真实部署过程中从自身物理交互经验中进行上下文学习（in-context learning）的框架，同时保持策略模型冻结。Zeva通过Causal Interaction Extractor编码动作及其导致的状态变化为因果交互信号，存储于双时间尺度因果记忆（dual-timescale causal memory）中，并在后续动作决策时检索相关信号注入冻结的策略模型。实验表明Zeva在模拟和真实操作任务中优于前沿VLA和WAM方法，且无需梯度更新即可实现部署中的自我进化，成功率随经验积累持续提升，获得的交互经验还能跨任务泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用可泛化的具身操作难以仅通过预训练实现，因为真实世界中存在大量未见的物理条件。作者认为机器人需要在真实部署过程中从自身的物理交互中实时学习，并利用这些知识指导后续动作，而非仅仅依赖预训练或固定策略。

Method: Zeva包含三个关键组件：(1) Causal Interaction Extractor，将执行的动作及其引发的状态变化编码为因果交互信号；(2) 双时间尺度因果记忆（dual-timescale causal memory），存储不同类型的交互经验；(3) 检索机制，在后续动作决策时从记忆中检索相关的因果交互信号，作为上下文注入到冻结的策略模型中（如VLA或WAM），从而实现无需梯度更新的上下文学习。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界操作实验中，Zeva取得了优于对比的前沿VLA（Vision-Language-Action）和WAM（World Action Model）方法的性能。更重要的是，Zeva实现了部署过程中的自我进化——随着机器人积累更多交互经验，任务成功率持续提升，且无需进行梯度更新。此外，获得的交互经验可以跨任务泛化。

Conclusion: Zeva证明了让机器人在真实部署中从自身物理交互经验中进行在线上下文学习是可行且有效的。这种方法突破了预训练模型的局限，通过非梯度方式实现自我进化，为通用具身操作提供了一条新路径，其经验的可迁移性进一步增强了框架的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Generalizable embodied manipulation remains difficult to achieve through pretraining alone, due to unseen physical conditions in the real world. We argue that robots need to learn from their own physical interactions on the fly during real-world deployment and use this knowledge to inform subsequent actions. We present Zeva, the first framework that enables in-context learning from a robot's own physical interaction experience while keeping the policy model frozen. Zeva employs a Causal Interaction Extractor to encode an executed action and its induced state change into a causal interaction signal, which is stored in a dual-timescale causal memory. For subsequent actions, relevant causal interaction signals are retrieved from memory and injected into the frozen policy model as context. Experiments in simulation and real-world manipulation demonstrate that Zeva achieves the best performance among the compared frontier VLAs and WAMs and, more importantly, enables self-evolution during deployment without gradient updates. Its success rate continues to improve as the robot accumulates interaction experience. Furthermore, the acquired interaction experience can generalize across tasks.

</details>


### [234] [SleepWalking: Privileged Representation Shaping for End-to-End Blind Locomotion in Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30883)
*Zheng Pan,Tenghui Wang,Peilin Li,Shiyu Zhou,Hao Sun,Yan Ma,Liang Yu,Liang He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SWAQ，一个单阶段端到端框架，通过下一时刻特权物理重建来塑造循环历史表征的信息保持，使部署的actor仅用直接的历史到动作通路即可实现部分可观测足式机器人运动控制，相比DWAQ提升15%的峰值地形水平同时减少44.4%的推理MACs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可观测的运动控制问题中，任务相关信息未被瞬时观测完整提供。现有方法要么显式估计缺失物理变量，要么通过结构化架构处理扩展观测历史。作者认为部分可观测性本质上是信息保持问题，关键在于策略的内部状态是否保留相关信息，而非信息如何进入网络。

Method: 提出SWAQ（SleepWalking for Robot Locomotion）框架，采用单阶段端到端训练。通过next-step特权物理重建作为辅助目标，在策略学习过程中塑造循环历史表征（如RNN）保留的信息内容。部署时actor仅使用直接的历史到动作通路，无需辅助解码器或特权信息输入。同时辅以层间探针分析和特权可恢复性与基于历史/特权策略类之间可达收益差距的理论分析。

Result: 在对齐的训练设置下，SWAQ比最强的非外感受基线DWAQ高出15.0%的峰值平均地形水平，同时每个控制步的推理MACs减少44.4%。层间探针显示重建的物理变量相关信息在策略头中直到动作输出前一层都能保持线性可解码性。

Conclusion: 语义目标可以在不要求部署控制器进行相应架构分解的情况下结构化学习过程。这为部分可观测机器人运动控制提供了一种新的视角：通过语义监督塑造信息保持，而非依赖显式的状态估计或专门架构。

Abstract: Partially observable locomotion requires a policy to act when task-relevant properties of the robot--environment state are not fully specified by instantaneous observations. Existing approaches often address this challenge by explicitly estimating missing physical variables or processing extended observation histories through structured architectures. We take a different view: partial observability is fundamentally an information-retention problem. The decisive question is not how task-relevant information enters the network, but whether the policy's internal state retains it. Guided by this perspective, we propose SleepWalking for Robot Locomotion (SWAQ), a one-stage end-to-end framework that uses next-step privileged physical reconstruction to shape what a recurrent history representation retains during policy learning, while the deployed actor uses only a direct history-to-action pathway. Under aligned training settings, SWAQ achieves a 15.0\% higher peak mean terrain level than DWAQ, the strongest non-exteroceptive baseline, while using 44.4\% fewer inference MACs per control step. Layerwise probes further show that information associated with the reconstructed physical variables remains linearly decodable through the policy head up to the layer preceding the action output. Complementary theoretical analysis relates privileged-variable recoverability to the achievable-return gap between history-based and privileged-information policy classes. These results suggest that semantic objectives can structure learning without requiring a corresponding architectural decomposition of the deployed controller.

</details>


### [235] [LightNav-0: Eliciting VLM Spatial Intelligence for Generalist Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30935)
*Shaoan Wang,Aocheng Luo,Fei Huang,Jingyi Xu,Xiaoyang Wang,Yueyu Wang,Qianli Ma,Fan Yang,Ran Mei,Jia Wei,Jiangpeng Hu,Xuhao Liu,Hongming Chen,Yuanbin Shao,Yiyang Lin,Ziliang Li,Liang Pan,Xinhang Liu,Yuntao Ma,Tingxiang Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LightNav-0，一个紧凑的通用具身导航模型，通过统一Token接口（双通道指向+残差向量量化动作分词器）直接激发预训练VLM的空间智能，无需任务特定的预测头，实现了指令跟随、开放词汇目标导航和视觉追踪的单模型统一，并在10个公开仿真设置和真实世界中达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身导航系统依赖任务或具身特定的组件，将感知、推理和行动割裂开来，泛化能力有限。尽管现代VLM已具备空间先验知识（视觉接地、空间推理、指认），但这些能力很少被直接用于机器人控制。此外，现有导航系统常需要为每个任务设计专门的预测头，缺乏统一的表征和跨任务、跨具身的泛化能力。

Method: LightNav-0采用统一Token接口：双通道指向表达任务、场景和具身无关的空间意图；残差向量量化动作分词器将空间意图映射为具身特定的精确轨迹。结合时间感知的视觉历史压缩、ER（具身推理）中间训练、监督微调和强化学习，在超过2000个场景和4000小时具身导航数据上训练。

Result: LightNav-ER在8个具身推理基准上达到最高完整集平均分；LightNav-0在全部10个公开导航仿真设置中取得SOTA单目成功率；真实世界评估显示跨具身、跨场景、静态和动态目标的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 结果表明紧凑VLM可作为通用且可迁移的通用具身导航骨干网络，统一的分词接口和训练方法使得一个模型即可同时支持多种导航任务，并保持良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Embodied navigation requires agents to translate heterogeneous goals and visual observations into actions across tasks, environments, and robot embodiments. Modern vision-language models (VLMs) already encode spatial priors for visual grounding, spatial reasoning, and pointing, but these capabilities are rarely elicited directly for robot control. Existing navigation systems instead rely on task- or embodiment-specific components, fragmenting perception, reasoning, and action while offering limited generalization. Here we present LightNav-0, a compact generalist embodied navigation model that elicits the spatial intelligence of a pretrained VLM and aligns it with navigation, without task-specific prediction heads. LightNav-0 represents diverse navigation tasks through a unified token interface: dual-channel pointing expresses task-, scene-, and embodiment-agnostic spatial intent, while a residual vector-quantized action tokenizer maps this intent to precise, embodiment-specific trajectories. Together with temporally aware visual history compression, ER mid-training, supervised fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning, this formulation supports instruction following, open-vocabulary object navigation, and visual tracking within a single model. The navigation training corpus spans 2K+ scenes and 4K+ hours of embodied navigation data. LightNav-ER, the embodied-reasoning checkpoint used to initialize LightNav-0, attains the highest complete-set average across 8 embodied-reasoning benchmarks, while LightNav-0 achieves state-of-the-art monocular success rates across all 10 public navigation simulation settings. Real-world evaluations further demonstrate zero-shot generalization across robot embodiments, diverse scenes, and static and dynamic targets. These results establish compact VLMs as a unified and transferable backbone for generalist embodied navigation.

</details>


### [236] [Autonomously Acquiring Robot Manipulation Skills with Language-Driven Quality-Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30983)
*Émiland Garrabé,Mahdi Khoramshahi,Stéphane Doncieux*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自主利用质量-多样性（QD）算法的方法，仅需任务的自然语言描述即可输出多样化的运动基元库。通过LLM驱动的自主探索机制设计适应度和行为描述符（BD）函数，并采用多BD的MAP-Elites成功（MES）算法变体，在4个机器人操作任务上超越了传统QD算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QD算法在机器人学习中日益重要，但传统方法需要专家手动编写成功条件、适应度和多样性指标，限制了机器人的自主性；而现有的LLM奖励塑形技术虽能实现自主学习但只输出单一高性能解，缺乏适应性。因此需要一种既能自主设计Qd指标又能输出多样化解的方法。

Method: 1) 将策略探索建模为函数设计问题，功能空间维度低于完整BD和适应度空间；2) 提出LLM驱动的探索方案，从低维空间采样，无需任务特定提示、微调或专家干预；3) 采用多BD变体的MAP-Elites成功（MES）算法，利用异构BD样本生成多样化运动基元档案。

Result: 在基于genesis模拟器的4个机器人操作任务上，该方法有效生成多样化运动基元档案，性能优于使用推断和手写参数化的经典QD算法。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了仅凭任务自然语言描述即可自主生成多样化运动基元库的目标，消除了对专家设计指标的需求，同时保持了QD算法输出多样性智能体的优势，提升了机器人在部署时的零样本适应能力。

Abstract: Quality-diversity (QD) algorithms have been gaining traction in robot learning, where diverse motion primitive libraries allow robots to adapt zero-shot to constraints at deployment time. However, such methods typically require expert designers to write the success condition, fitness and diversity metrics, and this strongly limits the robot's autonomy. On the other hand, existing LLM-based reward-shaping techniques allow robots to learn autonomously but only output single high-performing solutions, limiting the robot's adaptability. In this paper, we propose an approach designed to output diverse motion primitive archives by autonomously leveraging quality-diversity algorithms, only requiring a free-form description of the task in common language. To address the difficulty of designing relevant fitness and diversity metrics, we propose an autonomous exploration mechanism able to reliably output sets of functionals covering the fitness and behavior descriptor (BD) space. First, we pose policy exploration as a functional design problem, where the functional spaces are lower-dimensional than the full BD and fitness spaces, and propose an LLM-based exploration scheme to sample from these low-dimensional spaces without any task-specific prompts, fine-tuning or expert intervention. We adapt a multi-BD variant of the MAP-Elites success (MES) algorithm, designed to leverage the heterogeneous BD samples. Finally, through experiments based on the genesis simulator, we show that our method effectively generates archives of diverse motion primitives, outperforming classical QD algorithms with inferred and hand-written parametrizations on a set of $4$ robotic manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [237] [DARP: A Calibrated Dual-Arm RGB-D-IR Dataset for Multi-View Robotic Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31002)
*Manish Kansana,Mohammed Yusuf Mujawar,Sudip Mittal,Shahram Rahimi,Noorbakhsh Amiri Golilarz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了DARP（双臂机器人感知）数据集，一个校准的双臂RGB-D-IR数据集，用于以物体为中心的机器人感知。该数据集使用两个独立移动的眼在手式机械臂，分别位于共享桌面工作空间的两侧，每个机械臂携带Intel RealSense传感器连续记录RGB、深度和立体红外数据，同时记录同步的机器人关节状态。数据集包含10个独特的桌面物体，并提供了多视角融合管线验证几何一致性，在224个保留的RGB-D关键帧上取得了2.13mm的中位点对网格距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单视角机器人感知常受自遮挡和不完整表面可见性限制，需要多视角或双臂协作感知来克服这一问题。现有数据集缺乏同时具备双臂运动、多模态传感器（RGB-D-IR）和精确位姿对齐的资源，因此需要构建一个可重复使用的双臂机器人感知数据集。

Method: DARP使用两个独立移动的eye-in-hand机械臂（相对放置于共享工作台两侧），每个臂携带Intel RealSense传感器连续记录RGB、深度和立体红外数据，同时记录同步机器人关节状态。采集流程包括自动定位、跨臂确认、自适应视点生成和连续多模态记录。物体以无固定位姿方式放置。评估方面，实现了一个确定性的多视图融合管线，将校准的RGB-D观测转换为互补的部分点云和实测表面网格，不使用学习或生成式补全方法。

Result: 在224个保留的RGB-D关键帧（包含1,563,466个三维查询点）上评估，中位点对网格距离为2.13mm，RMSE为4.04mm，96.56%的点在实测表面网格10mm以内。

Conclusion: DARP是一个可重复使用的双臂机器人感知资源，适用于多视图重建、协作机器人感知、多模态融合、主动感知以及未来基于学习的部分物体观测推理等研究方向。

Abstract: Robotic perception from a single viewpoint is often limited by self-occlusion and incomplete surface visibility. This paper presents DARP(Dual-Arm Robotic Perception) https://doi.org/10.21227/rmv3-be47, a calibrated dual-arm RGB-D-IR dataset for object-centered robotic perception using two independently moving eye-in-hand manipulators positioned on opposite sides of a shared tabletop workspace. Each arm carries an Intel RealSense sensor that continuously records RGB, depth, and stereo infrared data while synchronized robot joint states are logged for pose recovery. Objects are placed without fixed poses or marked locations, and the acquisition procedure performs automatic localization, cross-arm confirmation, adaptive viewpoint generation, and continuous multimodal recording. DARP contains ten unique tabletop objects and preserves the original sensor recordings, robot-state logs, object-level metadata, and calibration information required to reconstruct camera trajectories in a shared metric frame. To evaluate the geometric consistency of the acquisition, we implement a deterministic multi-view fusion pipeline that converts calibrated RGB-D observations into complementary partial point clouds and measured surface meshes without using learned or generative completion methods. Evaluation on 224 held-out RGB-D keyframes comprising 1,563,466 three-dimensional query points yields a median point-to-mesh distance of 2.13~mm and an RMSE of 4.04~mm, with 96.56\% of points within 10~mm of the measured-surface mesh. DARP is intended as a reusable resource for multi-view reconstruction, collaborative robotic perception, multimodal fusion, active perception, and future learning-based reasoning over partial object observations.

</details>


### [238] [SUN: Persistent Programs For Language-Grounded Control-to-Learning-to-Real Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31167)
*Weiqi Wang,Zhi Li,Yudong Lei,David Martinez,Xiaofeng Gao,Yuxin Jiang,Chenfanfu Jiang,Yingnian Wu,Demetri Terzopoulos,Ran Gong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SUN Programs框架及Kuafu系统，通过将任务语义（几何与接触关系）编译为统一的MPC成本、奖励函数、转移条件等，由大规模视觉语言模型自动生成，实现从模型预测控制到数据驱动策略的无缝衔接。在9个任务中Kuafu达到82.03%宏成功率，并在大规模数据生成和真实机器人部署上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在模型预测控制与学习策略之间缺乏语义一致性的桥梁：控制执行明确目标，学习将行为摊销为反应式策略，但两者间任务语义被丢弃，导致奖励需要手工设计、行为与控制验证的结果产生漂移。

Method: 提出SUN Programs——一种类型化可执行程序，将几何与接触关系定义一次并编译为对齐的MPC代价、满足谓词、RL奖励、转移守卫和诊断信息。在此基础上构建Kuafu系统，利用大型视觉语言模型从语言和场景语义自动合成SUN Programs，通过MPC筛选可行性，并在训练阶段条件化策略时保留语义。

Result: 在9个任务中Kuafu达到82.03%宏成功率，远超稀疏奖励（35.67%）和Stage-BC（24.75%）基线；在8192路规模下，每小时的遥操作时间生成10.57倍的成功轨迹时间；每个任务500条轨迹下，DP3策略在仿真中达到46.0%成功率（对比基线的22.4%），在Franka和Kinova实体机器人上达到34.7%。

Conclusion: 仿真筛选的任务语义能够有效将控制摊销为鲁棒策略，无需人工演示或手动密集奖励，统一了符号规划与数据驱动执行，弥合了模型预测控制与学习策略之间的语义鸿沟。

Abstract: Bridging model-based control and learned policies in long-horizon manipulation has harbored a silent disagreement: control executes specified objectives, learning amortizes that behavior into a reactive policy, yet existing protocols discard task semantics, leaving rewards hand-crafted and behavior drifting from what control verified.We introduce Semantically UNified (SUN) Programs, typed executables where geometric and contact relations are defined once and compiled into aligned Model Predictive Control (MPC) costs, satisfaction predicates, RL rewards, transition guards, and diagnostics. Our system, Kuafu, driven by large vision language systems, automatically synthesizes SUN Programs from language and scene semantics, screens feasibility via MPC, and retains semantics while training stage-conditioned policies. Across nine tasks, Kuafu achieves 82.03% macro-success, outperforming sparse-reward (35.67%) and Stage-BC (24.75%) baselines. At 8192-way scale, it generates 10.57x the successful trajectory time per hour of human teleoperation. With 500 trajectories per task, Kuafu data trains DP3 policies to 46.0% simulation success (vs. 22.4% for alternatives) and 34.7% on physical Franka and Kinova robots. These results establish that simulation-screened task semantics can effectively amortize control into robust policies, without demonstrations or manual dense rewards, unifying symbolic planning and data-driven execution.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [239] [Bounded $t$-structures on the category of strongly bounded objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29031)
*Hongxing Chen,Xiaohu Chen,Jinbi Zhang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究弱可逼近三角范畴中强有界对象的全子范畴何时承认有界t-结构。在有限强有限性维数条件下，证明了这恰好当该子范畴与环境中全子范畴的有界对象一致时成立；此时有界t-结构在等价意义下唯一，且在有界对象全子范畴上的唯一性无条件下成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强有界对象与全局维数密切相关，并在三角增强的唯一性问题中扮演重要角色。探究强有界对象子范畴何时承认有界t-结构，是理解三角范畴内在结构及增强唯一性的关键问题。

Method: 引入有限强有限性维数这一有限性条件，通过比较强有界对象子范畴与环境三角范畴中有界对象全子范畴的关系，刻画有界t-结构的存在性与唯一性。

Result: 主要结果表明，在有限强有限性维数条件下，强有界对象子范畴承认有界t-结构当且仅当该子范畴与有界对象全子范畴一致；此时有界t-结构在等价意义下唯一，且在更一般的设定下有界对象全子范畴上的唯一性无条件成立。

Conclusion: 强有界对象承认有界t-结构的条件可被精确刻画，且所得t-结构具有唯一性，这深化了对三角范畴结构与增强唯一性的理解。

Abstract: Strongly bounded objects in a weakly approximable triangulated category are known to relate closely to global dimension and to play a significant role in the uniqueness problem for triangulated enhancements. In this paper, we investigate when the full subcategory of strongly bounded objects admits a bounded $t$-structure. Our main result, under a finiteness condition termed the finite strong finitistic dimension, states that this happens exactly when the subcategory agrees with the full subcategory of bounded objects in the ambient triangulated category. In that case, the bounded $t$-structure is unique up to equivalence; even more, the uniqueness holds unconditionally on the full subcategory of bounded objects.

</details>


### [240] [Examples of nontrivial presilting objects in a singularity category](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29044)
*Changchang Xi,Jinbi Zhang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文针对有限维代数奇异范畴中除零对象外不存在预倾斜对象的猜想，给出了正特征域上代数情形的反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前有人猜想：域上有限维代数的奇异范畴中不存在非零的预倾斜对象。作者旨在验证或推翻这一猜想。

Method: 通过在正特征域上构造具体的有限维代数，证明其奇异范畴中存在非零预倾斜对象，从而给出该猜想的反例。

Result: 在正特征域上构造出了有限维代数，其奇异范畴中存在非零的预倾斜对象，成功反驳了该猜想。

Conclusion: 该猜想在正特征域的代数情形下不成立，即奇异常范畴中可能存在非零预倾斜对象。

Abstract: It was conjectured that no presilting objects exist in the singularity category of a finite-dimensional algebra over a field except the zero object. In this note, we give counterexamples to this conjecture for algebras over fields of positive characteristic.

</details>


### [241] [A pre-$(n+2)$-angulated category which is not $(n+2)$-angulated](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29221)
*Jian He,Jing He,Panyue Zhou*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个显式的 pre-9-angulated 范畴，它不是 9-angulated 范畴，从而给出了从 pre-(n+2)-angulated 到 (n+2)-angulated 蕴含关系的一个真正的更高阶反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已有 pre-triangulated（即 pre-3-angulated）但不满足三角化公设的反例（Chen-Liu-Lu-Zhang 的工作），但更高阶（n≥3，即 pre-9-angulated 等）的情形是否也存在类似反例尚不清楚。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 底层加性范畴取为预投射代数 Π(A_5) 在特征 2 上的有限生成投射右模范畴。构造方法是对 Chen-Liu-Lu-Zhang 在 pre-triangulated 反例中使用的 twisted complete comparison 取奇数幂，并证明所得 pre-9-angulation 不满足更高阶的 mapping-cone 公理。

Result: 得到一个显式的 pre-9-angulated 范畴，并严格证明了它不满足 9-angulated 所需的更高阶映射锥公理，从而构成一个 genuinely higher 的反例。

Conclusion: 该工作表明 pre-(n+2)-angulated 到 (n+2)-angulated 的蕴含在更高阶情形下同样失效，推进了对高阶三角化范畴理论的深入理解。

Abstract: We construct an explicit pre-$9$-angulated category which is not $9$-angulated, thereby giving a genuinely higher counterexample to the implication from pre-$(n+2)$-angulated to $(n+2)$-angulated. The underlying additive category is the category of finitely generated projective right modules over the preprojective algebra $Π(A_5)$ over $\mathbb F_2$. The construction is obtained by taking an odd power of the twisted complete comparison used by Chen-Liu-Lu-Zhang in their pre-triangulated counterexample and by showing that the resulting pre-$9$-angulation fails the higher mapping-cone axiom.

</details>


### [242] [Model structures on the category of Q-shaped modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29267)
*Yajun Ma,Peiru Yang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在Q-形模范畴Q,AMod上从AMod中的余挠对构造阿贝尔模型结构的方法，并应用于微分模范畴Dif(A)，得到相应的阿贝尔模型结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在表示论与同调代数中，如何在由小预加性范畴Q定义的Q-形模范畴上构造模型结构是一个重要问题，尤其是从基础模范畴中的余挠对自然提升到Q-形模范畴中的模型结构。

Method: 给定AMod中的一个余挠对，在Q,AMod中构造两个余挠对；当Q无环时进一步构造射影模型结构；将该方法应用于微分模范畴Dif(A)（将Dif(A)视为某特定Q的Q-形模范畴），并证明诱导的余挠对是相容的，从而得到阿贝尔模型结构。

Result: 成功从AMod中的余挠对构造出Q,AMod中的两个余挠对；在Q无环条件下构造出射影模型结构；对微分模范畴Dif(A)，证明诱导的余挠对相容，从而在Dif(A)上获得阿贝尔模型结构。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一条从基础模范畴中的余挠对到Q-形模范畴（及微分模范畴）中阿贝尔模型结构的系统构造路径，为相关齐次化与模型范畴理论提供了新工具。

Abstract: We develop a method for constructing abelian model structures on the category Q,AMod of Q-shaped modules from cotorsion pairs in AMod, where Q is a small preadditive category satisfying certain conditions and AMod denotes the category of left A-modules for any ring A. More precisely, we construct two cotorsion pairs in Q,AMod from a given cotorsion pair in AMod. This leads to a construction of projective model structures on Q,AMod under the condition that Q has no cycles. We further apply this method to the category Dif(A) of differential left A-modules, viewed as a category of Q-shaped modules for a suitable choice of Q. In this case, the induced cotorsion pairs are shown to be compatible, thereby giving rise to abelian model structures on Dif(A).

</details>


### [243] [Generalized Jantzen filtration, hyperbolic restriction, and characteristic cycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29312)
*Johannes Droschl*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文给出了局部非阿基米德域F上GL_n(F)抛物诱导表示之间解析交织算子的几何描述，利用Braden的双曲局部化函子，并由此证明了交织算子像的半单性、给出了其极点的下界估计，并将Lapid-Mínguez猜想归结为特征循环的计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交织算子是表示论中的核心对象，理解其像与极点行为对理解抛物诱导表示的结构至关重要。作者旨在通过几何方法（双曲局部化函子和混合Hodge模）揭示交织算子的解析性质，并解决与此相关的猜想。

Method: 作者利用Braden的双曲局部化函子给出交织算子的几何描述，借助反常层的奇异支撑（singular support）来刻画其极点阶数，并运用混合Hodge模理论进行关键证明。通过将Lapid-Mínguez猜想归约为特征循环（characteristic cycles）的计算，建立了表示论与分析几何之间的桥梁。

Result: 证明了交织算子像总是半单的；给出了交织算子极点阶数的下界，并猜想该下界在特定反常层的奇异支撑条件下可达到上界；将Lapid-Mínguez关于诱导表示不可约子表示形状的猜想归约到纯几何的特征循环计算问题。

Conclusion: 本文通过几何方法深化了对GL_n上交织算子的理解，揭示了表示论对象的几何本质，并为Lapid-Mínguez猜想提供了一条可行的几何解决路径。

Abstract: In this paper we give a geometric description of the behavior of analytic intertwining operators between parabolically induced representations of $\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathrm{F})$, where $\mathrm{F}$ is a local non-archimedean field, in terms of hyperbolic localization functors of Braden. As a consequence, we can show that the image of an intertwining operator is always semi-simple and give a lower bound on the order of its pole, which is conjectured to be an equality as well as an upper bound in terms of the singular support of certain perverse sheaves. Moreover, we are able to reduce the conjecture of Lapid and Mínguez on the shape of irreducible subrepresentations of induced representations to a computation of characteristic cycles. The theory of mixed Hodge modules plays a crucial role in the proofs.

</details>


### [244] [Pseudo-spherical representations and types for covers of split tori](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29548)
*Edmund Karasiewicz,Shuichiro Takeda*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文为p-adic域上分裂环面的Brylinski-Deligne（BD）覆盖引入了伪球表示的概念，不假设覆盖是驯顺的。作者枚举了伪球表示并计算其维数；当环面是连通分裂约化群的极大分裂环面时，在温和条件下枚举了其中一族"受尊崇的"（distinguished）伪球表示，预期这些表示为BD覆盖的Borel-Casselman定理提供环面输入。同时证明了覆盖环面的不可约真表示限制到极大紧子群上是重数自由的，并由此刻画了相应的Bushnell-Kutzko型及Hecke代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BD覆盖（即广义Weil表示覆盖）在p-adic表示论中具有重要地位，但此前伪球表示的定义仅适用于驯顺覆盖。本文旨在将伪球表示的概念推广到任意（含非驯顺）BD覆盖，为建立BD覆盖的Borel-Casselman定理奠定基础，同时揭示覆盖环面表示在极大紧子群上的重数自由结构及其类型理论意义。

Method: 1) 对任意BD覆盖（不假设驯顺）定义伪球表示，利用BD数据（根、指数及二乘子）进行系统枚举并计算维数；2) 当环面来自分裂约化群时，在温和的BD数据假设下筛选出受尊崇的伪球表示子集；3) 利用紧诱导与限制技巧证明不可约真表示限制到极大紧子群的重数自由性，进而识别其Bushnell-Kutzko型并描述相应的Hecke代数。
