Result: 完成了任意BD覆盖上伪球表示的完整枚举及维数公式；在分裂约化群情形下确定了受尊崇伪球表示的子集（适用于温和BD数据）；证明了覆盖环面不可约真表示限制到极大紧子群的重数自由性，从而其不可约真紧表示构成Bushnell-Kutzko型，并明确描述了这些型对应的Hecke代数。对于某些非驯顺二重覆盖，所提出的受尊崇伪球表示确实提供了Borel-Casselman定理所需的环面输入（见另文验证）。

Conclusion: 本文建立了任意BD覆盖的伪球表示理论，填补了非驯顺情形的空白；受尊崇伪球表示的引入为BD覆盖的Borel-Casselman定理提供了关键的环面组合输入；重数自由性与类型理论的结论深化了对覆盖环面表示结构及其Hecke代数代数的理解，为进一步研究分裂约化群BD覆盖的不可约表示分类铺平了道路。

Abstract: We introduce a notion of pseudo-spherical representations for any Brylinski-Deligne (BD) cover of a split torus over a $p$-adic field. In particular, we do not assume that the cover is tame. We enumerate the pseudo-spherical representations and compute their dimension.
  When the torus is the maximal split torus of a connected split reductive group $G$, we enumerate a subset of distinguished pseudo-spherical representations, under mild assumptions on the BD datum. We expect these distinguished representations to provide the torus input for a Borel-Casselman Theorem for BD covers of $G$. We have verified this expectation for certain non-tame double covers in a separate work.
  While investigating the pseudo-spherical representations, we also prove that the restriction of irreducible genuine representations of covering tori to their maximal compact subgroup is multiplicity-free. From this we see that the irreducible genuine representations of this maximal compact subgroup are types in the sense of Bushnell-Kutzko. We conclude by describing the Hecke algebras attached to these types.

</details>


### [245] [Auslander-Reiten-Serre duality revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29584)
*Ji-Wei He,Jixing Pan*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了extriangulated范畴中带右Auslander-Reiten-Serre（ARS）对偶的范畴结构，证明了ARS对偶可诱导出相对理论上的右ARS对偶，并进一步证明了τ函子是精确函子且保持几乎分裂exangle，最后给出了应用实例并推广了A. Hubery的近期结果到范畴框架下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Iyama、Nakaoka和Palu引入了extriangulated范畴上右ARS对偶的概念，但此前尚未系统研究该对偶在相对理论（relative theories）下的行为。本文旨在回答：在相对结构中，ARS对偶是否仍保持其性质？τ函子是否保持相应的范畴结构和几乎分裂序列？这些问题的回答有助于统一和推广已知的Auslander-Reiten理论结果。

Method: 本文采用公理化方法，在带右ARS对偶(τ,η)的extriangulated范畴(𝒞,𝔼,𝔰)上建立相对理论，系统验证ARS对偶在相对理论中的保持性。通过分析τ函子在相对结构下的性质，证明其为extriangulated范畴间的精确函子并保持几乎分裂exangle，最后利用这些结论给出具体应用并推广既有结果。

Result: （1）证明了(τ,η)可诱导出相对理论上的右ARS对偶；（2）证明了相对结构下τ是extriangulated范畴间的精确函子且保持几乎分裂exangle；（3）推广了A. Hubery的近期结果到范畴框架，并给出了若干实例。

Conclusion: 本文系统建立了extriangulated范畴中ARS对偶在相对理论下的理论框架，揭示了ARS对偶与相对结构之间的内在兼容性，并将相关结果推广至更一般的范畴设定，为Auslander-Reiten理论在相对范畴理论中的进一步应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Let $(\mathcal{C},\mathbb{E},\mathfrak{s})$ be an extriangulated category with a right Auslander-Reiten-Serre (ARS for short) duality $(τ, η)$ in the sense of Iyama, Nakaoka and Palu. We show $(τ, η)$ induces right ARS dualities on the relative theories of $(\mathcal{C},\mathbb{E},\mathfrak{s})$. Under relative structures, we show that the functor $τ$ is indeed an exact functor between extriangulated categories and preserves almost split exangles. Finally, we give some applications and examples on these results. For instance, we generalize a recent result by A. Hubery to a categorical framework.

</details>


### [246] [Picture groups and green sequences from the perspective of Ringel--Hall algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29960)
*Erlend D. Børve*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文应用Joyce和Bridgeland的Ringel-Hall代数理论，证明了关于picture space和picture group的结果。作者为任意有限维结合k-代数Λ构造了一个忠实的群函子（从τ-簇态射范畴到某个群胚的忠实函子），从而在兼容性条件下证明了picture space是局部CAT(0)的，并推广了Igusa--Todorov关于green序列与picture group中正表达式双射的结果到非遗传代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Joyce和Bridgeland的Ringel-Hall代数框架应用于表示论中的几何与组合问题，目的是在一般有限维结合代数（不限于遗传代数）上建立picture space和picture group的相关性质，推广已有结果。

Method: 利用Joyce和Bridgeland的Ringel-Hall代数工具，构造从τ-簇态射范畴到群胚的忠实群函子；结合Hanson-Igusa的结果证明picture space的局部CAT(0)性质；通过正表达式与green序列的对应关系推广Igusa-Todorov的结果。

Result: (1) 构造了忠实群函子；(2) 在τ-簇态射范畴满足last factors兼容性的条件下，证明了picture space局部CAT(0)；(3) 证明了green序列与picture group中的若干正表达式一一对应，将Igusa--Todorov的结果推广到非遗传k-代数。

Conclusion: Ringel-Hall代数方法为一般有限维代数的picture space和picture group提供了统一而有力的工具，使多个此前仅限于遗传代数的结果得以推广。

Abstract: Let $k$ be an algebraically closed field and let $Λ$ be a finite-dimensional associative $k$-algebra. We apply Joyce and Brideland's notion of Ringel--Hall algebra to prove results about picture spaces and picture groups. For instance, we construct a faithful group functor for $Λ$, i.e. a faithful functor from the $τ$-cluster morphism category of $Λ$ into a groupoid. Consequently, by results of Hanson--Igusa, the picture space of $Λ$ is locally CAT(0) provided that the $τ$-cluster morphism category of $Λ$ admits compatibility of last factors. We also show that green sequences are in bijection with certain positive expressions in the picture group, which generalizes a result of Igusa--Todorov beyond hereditary $k$-algebras.

</details>


### [247] [Transfer of Wakamatsu tilting modules along Frobenius extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30090)
*Wei Ren,Chunxia Zhang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究Frobenius扩张下Wakamatsu倾斜模的提升问题。给定Frobenius扩张$ι: R\to A$和左$R$-模$T$为Wakamatsu倾斜模，给出$A\otimes_R T$仍为Wakamatsu倾斜模的充分条件，并建立分裂中心射影Frobenius扩张的提升-下降结果。通过$\mathrm{Ext}_R^i(T,A\otimes_R T)$对所有$i>0$的消没刻画倾斜情形。此外，若$A\otimes_R T\in\mathrm{add}_R(T)$，则自同态环的自然映射构成Frobenius扩张，且存在$R$-$B$-双模同构$T\otimes_S B\cong A\otimes_R T$。最后讨论了在Brenner-Butler-Miyashita等价及若干具体Frobenius扩张类中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wakamatsu倾斜模是倾斜模理论的重要推广，在表示论和同调代数中具有核心地位。将Wakamatsu倾斜模沿Frobenius扩张进行提升（即考虑$A\otimes_R T$），并探讨提升后模保持Wakamatsu倾斜性质的充分条件，有助于理解模在不同环扩张下的行为，并推广相关等价理论。

Method: 本文采用同调代数与环论相结合的方法：首先分析Wakamatsu倾斜模的定义条件在Frobenius扩张下的保持性，给出充分条件；其次利用分裂中心射影Frobenius扩张的特殊结构建立提升-下降（ascent-descent）对应；最后利用Ext消没条件刻画倾斜情形，并通过自同态环的Frobenius扩张结构构造双模同构。

Result: 获得了$A\otimes_R T$保持Wakamatsu倾斜的充分条件；建立了分裂中心射影Frobenius扩张的提升-下降结果；证明了倾斜情形可由$\mathrm{Ext}_R^i(T,A\otimes_R T)$（$i>0$）的消没来刻画；在$A\otimes_R T\in\mathrm{add}_R(T)$条件下，证明了自同态环映射$S\to B$是Frobenius扩张且$T\otimes_S B\cong A\otimes_R T$。

Conclusion: 本文系统建立了Frobenius扩张下Wakamatsu倾斜模与倾斜模的提升理论，给出了自同态环的Frobenius结构，并将其应用于Brenner-Butler-Miyashita等价及具体Frobenius扩张类中，丰富了倾斜理论在环扩张背景下的研究。

Abstract: Let $ι: R\to A$ be a Frobenius extension and let $T$ be a Wakamatsu tilting left $R$-module. We give sufficient conditions for the induced module $A\otimes_R T$ to remain Wakamatsu tilting and establish an ascent--descent result for split centrally projective Frobenius extensions. We also characterize the tilting case by the vanishing of $\mathrm{Ext}_R^i(T,A\otimes_R T)$ for all $i>0$. Moreover, if $A\otimes_R T\in\mathrm{add}_R(T)$, then the natural map $S=\mathrm{End}_R(T)\to B=\mathrm{End}_A(A\otimes_R T)$ of endomorphism rings is a Frobenius extension and $T\otimes_S B\cong A\otimes_R T$ as $R$-$B$-bimodules. Applications to Brenner--Butler--Miyashita equivalences and some specific classes of Frobenius extensions are also discussed.

</details>


### [248] [Poisson blow-ups and the adjoint quotient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30185)
*Peter Crooks,Iva Halacheva*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文利用Polishchuk的Poisson blow-up准则，将仿射Poisson概形的纤维blow-up构造为一族Poisson概形，并特化到半单李代数的伴随商，证明了该族是平坦、锥形且具有典范Poisson Hamiltonian G-簇结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在代数几何可积系统框架下，需要理解Poisson概形沿其可积系统纤维进行blow-up后是否仍保持Poisson结构，以及这类blow-up是否能统一地组织为一个家族。

Method: 利用Polishchuk的Poisson blow-up准则，对每个仿射Poisson概形X及其可积系统τ，构造沿纤维的blow-up，证明其构成一族Poisson概形X×B̃→B；随后特化到半单李代数g的伴随商τ:g→g//G=c，研究该族的平坦性、锥形性及G-Hamiltonian结构。

Result: 证明了blow-up族X×B̃→B中每个成员都是Poisson概形；在伴随商情形，证明了g×c̃→c是平坦、锥形的，并具典范Poisson Hamiltonian G-簇结构；同时获得了关于该族纤维（即沿正则伴随轨道闭包的blow-up）的Poisson几何结果。

Conclusion: 该工作将Poisson blow-up理论系统应用于代数几何可积系统，特别是伴随商情形，建立了blow-up族的典范几何结构，为深入研究轨道闭包blow-up的Poisson几何奠定了基础。

Abstract: We leverage Polishchuk's Poisson blow-up criterion in the context of algebro-geometric integrable systems. In more detail, one may associate an integrable system $τ:\mathfrak{X}\longrightarrow\mathfrak{B}$ to each affine Poisson scheme $\mathfrak{X}$ over $\mathbb{C}$. We prove that the blow-ups of $\mathfrak{X}$ along fibers of $τ$ are Poisson schemes occurring in a family $\widetilde{\mathfrak{X}\times\mathfrak{B}}\longrightarrow\mathfrak{B}$, where $\widetilde{\mathfrak{X}\times\mathfrak{B}}$ is itself a Poisson scheme. This result is subsequently specialized to the adjoint quotient $τ:\mathfrak{g}\longrightarrow\mathfrak{g}/\!/G=:\mathfrak{c}$ of a finite-dimensional complex semisimple Lie algebra $\mathfrak{g}$ with integrating algebraic group $G$. We show that the family $\widetilde{\mathfrak{g}\times\mathfrak{c}}\longrightarrow\mathfrak{c}$ is flat, conical, and equipped with a canonical Poisson Hamiltonian $G$-variety structure. We also obtain Poisson-geometric results on the fibers of this family, which are blow-ups of $\mathfrak{g}$ along regular adjoint orbit closures.

</details>


### [249] [Classification of Simple Harish-Chandra Modules over the Loop Mirror HeisenbergVirasoro Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30187)
*Haibo Chen,Xiansheng Dai,Yucai Su*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文对loop mirror Heisenberg-Virasoro代数的单Harish-Chandra模进行了完整分类，将其分为最高权模、最低权模和中间系列的求值模三类。作为副产品，分类了截断mirror Heisenberg-Virasoro代数L(n)（n≥2）的所有单Harish-Chandra模，并通过d-参数族中的移位算子给出了Billig-Futorny恒等式的更简洁证明，该方法还可推广到其他李（超）代数的一致有界模的零化子计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: loop mirror Heisenberg-Virasoro代数的嵌入子代数具有丰富的结构，包含Takiff型和bms3型等有趣的截断子代数。对这些代数上的单Harish-Chandra模进行分类是表示论中的核心问题，同时作者希望给出Billig-Futorny恒等式（来自Y. Billig和V. Futorny的经典工作）的更简洁证明，并发展可用于其他代数零化子计算的新方法。

Method: 作者对loop mirror Heisenberg-Virasoro代数的单Harish-Chandra模进行完整分类，利用d-参数族中的移位算子（shift operators）来重新证明关键的Billig-Futorny恒等式，从而给出更简洁的证明框架。该框架随后被推广用于计算其他李（超）代数上一致有界模的零化子。

Result: 给出了loop mirror Heisenberg-Virasoro代数上所有单Harish-Chandra模的完整分类，共分为三类：最高权模、最低权模和中间系列的求值模。同时对截断mirror Heisenberg-Virasoro代数L(n)（n≥2）的所有单Harish-Chandra模进行了分类，并以更简洁的方式重新证明了Billig-Futorny恒等式。

Conclusion: 该工作完整解决了loop mirror Heisenberg-Virasoro代数及其截断子代数的单Harish-Chandra模分类问题，同时提供了一种基于移位算子的更简洁证明方法，该方法具有通用性，可推广到其他李（超）代数的零化子计算中。

Abstract: The loop mirror Heisenberg-Virasoro algebra, an embedded subalgebra of the loop Heisenberg-Virasoro algebra, admits a family of interesting truncated subalgebras including those of Takiff type and \(\mathfrak{bms}_3\) type. We give a complete classification of simple Harish-Chandra modules over the loop mirror Heisenberg-Virasoro algebra, whose simple modules fall into three categories, highest weight modules, lowest weight modules, and evaluation modules of the intermediate series.
  As a by-product, we classify all simple Harish-Chandra modules over the truncated mirror Heisenberg-Virasoro algebras \(\mathcal{L}(n)\) for \(n\geq2\). By virtue of shift operators in the \(d\)-parameter family, we give a more streamlined proof of Theorem 3.3 from the work [Classification of simple $W_n$-modules with finite-dimensional weight spaces, {\it J. Reine Angew. Math.}, {\bf 720} (2016), 199-216] by Y. Billig and V. Futorny, which states the key Billig-Futorny identity. Furthermore, our approach can be extended to the computation of annihilators for uniformly bounded modules over some other Lie (super)algebras.

</details>


### [250] [ABRR Summation Formulas for Relative Extremal Projectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30245)
*Jonas T. Hartwig*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文推广了ABRR递推公式到相对极值投影子（relative extremal projector），该投影子由Conley和Sepanski于2003年引入。当Levi子代数退化为Cartan子代数时，恢复经典的ABRR递推。作者同时给出了递推解的紧凑显式表达式，适用范围涵盖无穷维对合李超代数、Kac-Moody代数、基本经典李超代数以及有限维约化李代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Khoroshkin在2004年证明了极值投影子与动力学扭曲（dynamical twist）等价，而ABRR方程提供了极值投影子系数的递推公式。本文的动机是将这一强大且统一的递推方法推广到相对极值投影子，从而扩展其在表示论和Mickelsson-Zhelobenko约化代数中的应用范围。

Method: 作者将ABRR递推公式从普通极值投影子推广到相对极值投影子。相对极值投影子是针对Levi子代数而非Cartan子代数定义的更一般的投影算子。通过对递推结构进行分析，作者找到了一个紧凑且显式的闭合形式解，使其适用于包括无穷维contragredient李超代数、Kac-Moody代数等多种代数结构。

Result: 成功得到了相对极值投影子的ABRR型递推公式及其显式解表达式。当Levi子代数取为Cartan子代数时，结果退化为经典ABRR递推，验证了推广的正确性。该结果统摄了无穷维contragredient李超代数、Kac-Moody代数、基本经典李超代数和有限维约化李代数等广泛情形。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个统一的相对极值投影子递推框架，将ABRR方法从Cartan情形推广到一般Levi子代数情形，并给出了显式闭合解。这一结果不仅统一了此前分散的研究，也为表示论和Mickelsson-Zhelobenko约化代数中的计算提供了更为强大的工具。

Abstract: In 2004, Khoroshkin proved that the extremal projector is equivalent to the dynamical twist. The Arnaudon-Buffenoir-Ragoucy-Roche (ABRR) equation, satisfied by the dynamical twist, yields a recursive formula for the coefficients in the extremal projector. The resulting summation formula is uniform and powerful in its applications to representation theory and Mickelsson-Zhelobenko reduction algebras. In this paper we generalize this recursive formula to the relative extremal projector, introduced by Conley and Sepanski in 2003. When the Levi subalgebra is the Cartan subalgebra, we recover the usual ABRR recursion. We also find a compact and explicit expression for the solution to the recursion. Our setting includes infinite-dimensional contragredient Lie superalgebras, Kac-Moody algebras, basic classical Lie superalgebras, and finite-dimensional reductive Lie algebras.

</details>


### [251] [GIT for root stacks and 3d mirror symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30350)
*Swapnil Garg,Ruoxi Li,Yuji Okitani*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文定义了根栈构造的范畴化推广，将其解释为3d镜像对称等价（Gammage-Hilburn-Mazel-Gee）B侧的拉回，并利用Ben-Zvi等人的范畴表示论结果（应用到半正交分解）分析该拉回。同时证明该拉回等价于A侧perverse schobers的推前，从而从A侧Lagrangian骨架的简单分解推出周期性。此外将Bodzenta-Donovan的构造推广到新的GIT问题，得到m<n互素时Coh(∛[m]{X/D})到Coh(∛[n]{X/D})的嵌入，并证明所得2项半正交分解的2n周期性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bodzenta-Donovan利用window theory证明了根栈的导出范畴具有2n周期的2项半正交分解。本文旨在将该构造范畴化，并从3d镜像对称的角度理解这一周期性现象，同时将其推广到更一般的GIT设置中。

Method: (1) 定义根栈构造的范畴化推广，解释为3d镜像对称B侧的拉回；(2) 运用Ben-Zvi-Francis-Nadler和Ben-Zvi-Nadler-Preygel的范畴表示论结果分析半正交分解上的拉回；(3) 证明拉回等价于A侧perverse schobers的推前，从A侧Lagrangian骨架的分解推出周期性；(4) 将Bodzenta-Donovan构造适配到新的GIT问题，构造Coh(∛[m]{X/D})到Coh(∛[n]{X/D})的嵌入并证明2n周期性。

Result: 成功给出根栈导出范畴2n周期性的范畴化证明（通过A侧骨架分解），并将Bodzenta-Donovan的构造推广到新的GIT问题，建立了互素指数m<n时对应凝聚层范畴之间的嵌入，同时保持了2n周期的半正交分解结构。

Conclusion: 本文从3d镜像对称的角度统一了根栈构造及其周期性，建立了B侧范畴化拉回与A侧perverse schobers推前之间的对偶，并将已有结果推广到更一般的GIT设置，揭示了根栈导出范畴结构的深层对称性来源。

Abstract: Using window theory, Bodzenta--Donovan showed that the derived category of a root stack $\sqrt[n]{X/D}$ has a $2n$-periodic $2$-term semiorthogonal decomposition. We define a categorical generalization of the root stack construction and interpret this as a pullback on the B-side of the 3d mirror symmetry equivalence of Gammage--Hilburn--Mazel-Gee. We analyze this pullback using categorical representation theoretic results of Ben-Zvi--Francis--Nadler and Ben-Zvi--Nadler--Preygel applied to SODs. We also show that the pullback is equivalent to a pushforward of perverse schobers on the A-side, allowing us to deduce periodicity from a simple decomposition of an A-side Lagrangian skeleton. Additionally, we adapt the construction of Bodzenta--Donovan to a new GIT problem, which yields an embedding of Coh($\sqrt[m]{X/D}$) into Coh($\sqrt[n]{X/D}$) for $m<n$ coprime, and prove $2n$-periodicity of the resulting $2$-term SOD.

</details>


### [252] [Auslander correspondence for higher stable dg categories and cluster Morita theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30740)
*Ryu Tomonaga*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文建立了d-稳定dg范畴的Auslander对应，将其刻画为模范畴上的coherence、弱整体维数与对偶条件，揭示了d-稳定性是(d+1)-Calabi-Yau对偶的扭曲形式。通过构造d-丛代数dg范畴C_{d,dg}(M)，证明了每个d-稳定dg范畴都可作为稳定dg范畴的d-丛倾斜子范畴实现，并发展了cluster Morita理论，最终证明了Amiot猜想的一个Morita理论变体（Calabi-Yau对应）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: d-稳定dg范畴公理化刻画了稳定dg范畴中的d-丛倾斜子范畴，本文旨在建立对应的Auslander对应（即同调刻画），并进一步通过构造d-丛代数dg范畴与cluster Morita理论，证明其可实现性以及Amiot猜想的Morita理论变体。

Method: 本文采用以下方法：(1) 通过coherence、弱整体维数和有限呈现模上的对偶来建立Auslander对应；(2) 在Koszul对偶下将d-稳定性解释为Koszul对偶的平移自内射性条件；(3) 按照Amiot、Guo和Keller的构造引入d-丛dg范畴C_{d,dg}(M)，利用Auslander对应证明其包含M为d-丛倾斜子范畴；(4) 发展cluster Morita理论，证明满足条件的预三角dg范畴拟等价于C_{d,dg}(M)；(5) 在局部有限的情况下建立Calabi-Yau对应。

Result: 本文的主要结果包括：(1) 建立了d-稳定加性连通dg范畴的Auslander对应（同调刻画）；(2) 在Koszul对偶下，d-稳定性对应于平移自内射性；(3) 构造了d-丛dg范畴并证明每个d-稳定dg范畴都可实现为稳定dg范畴的d-丛倾斜子范畴；(4) 建立了cluster Morita理论，说明丛倾斜子范畴的连通dg结构决定其环境范畴的拟等价类；(5) 证明了Amiot猜想的一个Morita理论变体：右(d+1)-Calabi-Yau结构与右d-Calabi-Yau结构之间存在双射。

Conclusion: 本文通过Auslander对应给出了d-稳定性的完整同调刻画，并通过构造d-丛dg范畴和cluster Morita理论，实现了d-稳定dg范畴在稳定dg范畴中的具体嵌入。最终建立的Calabi-Yau对应验证了Amiot猜想在Morita理论框架下的成立。

Abstract: The notion of $d$-stable dg categories axiomatizes $d$-cluster tilting subcategories of stable dg categories. We establish an Auslander correspondence for $d$-stable dg categories: we characterize the $d$-stability of an additive connective dg category in terms of coherence, weak global dimension, and a duality on finitely presented modules. This gives a homological characterization of $d$-stability and reveals it as a twisted form of $(d+1)$-Calabi--Yau duality. For locally finite connective dg algebras, this interpretation becomes particularly transparent under Koszul duality, where $d$-stability corresponds to a shifted self-injectivity condition on the Koszul dual.
  Following the constructions of Amiot, Guo and Keller, for a $d$-stable dg category $M$, we introduce its $d$-cluster dg category $\mathcal C_{d,{\rm dg}}(M):=\operatorname{per}_{\rm dg}M/^\mathbb{L}\mathcal D^b_{\rm fp, dg}(M)$. Using our Auslander correspondence, we show that $\mathcal C_{d,{\rm dg}}(M)$ contains $M$ as a $d$-cluster tilting subcategory. In particular, every $d$-stable dg category can be realized as a $d$-cluster tilting subcategory of a stable dg category. We then develop cluster Morita theory: a pretriangulated dg category equipped with a $d$-cluster tilting subcategory $M$ is quasi-equivalent to $\mathcal C_{d,{\rm dg}}(M)$. Thus the connective dg structure of a cluster tilting subcategory determines its ambient dg category up to quasi-equivalence.
  As an application of cluster Morita theory, we prove a Morita-theoretic variant of Amiot's conjecture. More precisely, we establish a Calabi--Yau correspondence: for a locally finite $d$-stable dg category $M$ over a field, right $(d+1)$-Calabi--Yau structures on $\mathcal D^b_{\rm fp, dg}(M)$ are in bijection with right $d$-Calabi--Yau structures on $\mathcal C_{d,{\rm dg}}(M)$.

</details>


### [253] [Kashiwara--Nakashima tableaux, Gelfand--Tsetlin patterns, and quantum symmetric pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30780)
*Hideya Watanabe*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文通过量子对称对（对应$(\mathfrak{so}_N,\mathfrak{so}_{N-1})$）的表示论，在Kashiwara-Nakashima表格与正交型Gelfand-Tsetlin模式之间建立了自然双射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kashiwara-Nakashima表格和Gelfand-Tsetlin模式都是特殊正交李代数$\mathfrak{so}_N$有限维不可约表示的重要组合模型，但二者分别基于量子群的晶体理论和分支规则构造，缺乏统一的联系。本文旨在通过量子对称对表示论这一桥梁，构建二者之间的自然双射。

Method: 利用对应$(\mathfrak{so}_N,\mathfrak{so}_{N-1})$的量子对称对的表示论，特别是相关晶体或组合结构，在Kashiwara-Nakashima表格与正交型Gelfand-Tsetlin模式之间建立显式的自然双射映射。

Result: 成功构造了Kashiwara-Nakashima表格与正交型Gelfand-Tsetlin模式之间的自然双射，统一了两个此前独立发展的组合模型。

Conclusion: 量子对称对表示论为联系不同组合模型提供了有效的工具，揭示了$\mathfrak{so}_N$表示理论中晶体理论框架与经典分支规则框架之间的深层一致性。

Abstract: Kashiwara--Nakashima tableaux and Gelfand--Tsetlin patterns of orthogonal type are famous as combinatorial models of the finite-dimensional irreducible representations of the special orthogonal Lie algebras $\mathfrak{so}_N$. The former is constructed based on the representation theory of quantum groups, especially the theory of crystals, while the latter based on the branching rule for $(\mathfrak{so}_N,\mathfrak{so}_{N-1})$. In the present paper, we construct a natural bijection between them by means of the representation theory of quantum symmetric pairs corresponding to $(\mathfrak{so}_N,\mathfrak{so}_{N-1})$.

</details>


### [254] [Linear independence of global monomials on positive spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30907)
*Peigen Cao*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了正空间上的全局单项式是线性无关的，该证明基于对Laurent展开的Newton多胞体的研究。该结果可应用于从簇代数（cluster algebras）和许多Laurent现象代数（Laurent phenomenon algebras）中产生的正空间，从而得到Laurent展开的适当Laurent单项式性质以及这类代数的簇单项式的线性无关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正空间上的全局单项式线性无关性是代数和组合数学中一个重要问题。该性质与Laurent现象紧密相关，而Laurent现象在簇代数理论中具有核心地位。作者希望建立一般性的框架来证明这类线性无关性，并将其推广到更广泛的代数类别上。

Method: 本文通过研究Laurent展开的Newton多胞体（Newton polytopes）来建立论证框架。利用Newton多胞体的几何性质来证明正空间上全局单项式的线性无关性，然后将该一般性结果应用于簇代数和Laurent现象代数中产生的具体正空间实例。

Result: 作者证明了正空间上的全局单项式线性无关的一般性定理，并成功将其应用于簇代数及许多Laurent现象代数，得到了这些代数的Laurent展开具有适当Laurent单项式性质（proper Laurent monomial property），以及簇单项式的线性无关性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个基于Newton多胞体的统一方法，不仅证明了正空间上全局单项式的线性无关性，还为簇代数和Laurent现象代数提供了重要的结构性结论（适当Laurent单项式性质和簇单项式线性无关性），这为相关领域的进一步研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove that the global monomials on positive spaces are linearly independent. The approach is based on the study of the Newton polytopes of Laurent expansions. Our general results can be applied to the positive spaces arising from cluster algebras and many Laurent phenomenon algebras, which in turn yield the so-called proper Laurent monomial property of Laurent expansions and the linear independence of cluster monomials for such algebras.

</details>


### [255] [Morita equivalent blocks with an endotrivial source that are not globally isotypic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31083)
*Xin Huang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文研究2-模系统下SU₃(2)的块代数与四元数群代数Q₈的Morita等价关系，指出它们虽Morita等价但非全局同型，因此不满足p-置换等价和splendidly Rickard等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索块代数之间更精细的等价关系（如p-置换等价、splendidly Rickard等价）与Morita等价之间的区分，特别是通过带有特定维数endotrivial source的双模来实现Morita等价的构造。

Method: 利用2-模系统上SU₃(2)的块结构，构造一个与Q₈代数Morita等价的双模，该双模带有3维endotrivial source；并通过分析全局同型性来进一步区分等价关系的类型。

Result: 证明了存在双模使得B与OQ₈ Morita等价，但两者并非全局同型，从而排除它们是p-置换等价或splendidly Rickard等价的可能。

Conclusion: Morita等价并不蕴含p-置换等价或splendidly Rickard等价，说明这些更精细的等价概念严格强于Morita等价，为块代数分类提供了新的区分性例子。

Abstract: Let $(K,\mathcal{O},k)$ be a large enough $2$-modular system. We show that there is a block algebra $B$ of $\mathcal{O}{\rm SU}_3(2)$ which is Morita equivalent to $\mathcal{O} Q_8$ via a bimodule with a $3$-dimensional endotrivial source. We also prove that $B$ and $\mathcal{O} Q_8$ are not globally isotypic. Hence they are not $p$-permutation equivalent and not splendidly Rickard equivalent.

</details>

### [256] [Determinant Quantum-Quantum Monte Carlo: Coherent Auxiliary-Field Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28742)
*Xuepeng Wang,Sagnik Banerjee,Debanjan Chowdhury*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出行列式量子量子蒙特卡洛（DQ²MC）算法，将经典DQMC的核心辅助场采样与平均过程提升到量子计算机上执行。通过行列式预言机在单粒子层面合成DQMC幅值，仅需O(log N_st)个系统量子比特，无需Jordan-Wigner或Bravyi-Kitaev编码；全量子协议消除马尔可夫链与自相关时间，混合协议实现无拒绝的热浴更新。电路深度随空间体积的标度优于经典DQMC，但付出后选择开销。费米符号问题的重加权估计器精确映射为量子弱值，建立指数成本的精确对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典行列式量子蒙特卡洛（DQMC）作为研究强关联费米体系的核心数值方法，受限于两个根本瓶颈：一是辅助场采样的马尔可夫链导致自相关时间，二是费米符号问题导致指数级计算成本。本文旨在借助量子计算的原理优势，从算法层面突破这些瓶颈，将辅助场采样与平均的操作核心理念直接移植到量子计算机上，从而开辟一条新的量子-经典混合计算路径。

Method: DQ²MC算法核心包括：（1）行列式预言机：通过量子奇异值变换（QSVT）从单粒子作用矩阵的分块编码中直接合成DQMC幅值，避免枚举、预计算或存储指数多的Hubbard-Stratonovich权重；（2）全量子协议：将所有可观测量转为干涉幅值，完全消除马尔可夫链；（3）混合量子-经典协议：保留常数大小的活性块，用精确热浴抽取替代Metropolis-Hastings接受步骤，实现任意大小簇更新的无拒绝采样，支持并行回火与分布式执行；（4）理论映射：将费米符号问题的重加权估计器精确映射为量子弱值，刻画后选择开销。

Result: 该算法仅使用O(log N_st)个系统量子比特（N_st为时空体积），不需要Jordan-Wigner或Bravyi-Kitaev编码；电路深度随空间体积的标度优于经典DQMC；后选择开销由最大目标概率精确决定——光滑分布为多项式，尖锐分布为指数；费米符号问题的指数成本与弱值提取的后选择开销建立精确对应。

Conclusion: DQ²MC提供了一种从算法本源上规避经典DQMC两个根本瓶颈（自相关时间与符号问题）的量子方案，并通过弱值映射建立对符号问题复杂度的精确量子力学刻画。这是一个概念上全新的框架，兼具理论深度与实用潜力，为强关联费米系统的量子计算模拟开辟了新方向。

Abstract: We introduce determinant quantum-quantum Monte Carlo (DQ$^2$MC), a quantum algorithm that lifts the auxiliary-field sampling and averaging at the operational core of determinant quantum Monte Carlo onto a quantum computer. A determinant oracle synthesizes the DQMC amplitudes directly from a block encoding of the single-particle action matrix via quantum singular value transformations, so that the exponentially many Hubbard-Stratonovich weights are never enumerated, precomputed, or stored. Since the fermions are free for fixed auxiliary fields, the construction operates entirely at the single-particle level, requiring $O(\log N_{\mathrm{st}})$ system qubits and no Jordan-Wigner or Bravyi-Kitaev encoding, where $N_{\mathrm{st}}$ is the space-time volume. A full-quantum protocol makes observables interference amplitudes, eliminating the Markov chain and its autocorrelation time altogether; a hybrid quantum-classical protocol retains a constant-size active block of qubits and replaces the Metropolis-Hastings acceptance step with an exact heat-bath draw, so that cluster updates of any size are rejection-free, and passes only classical information between updates, admitting parallel tempering and distributed execution across quantum processors. The circuit-depth scales more favorably with spatial volume than classical DQMC, at the price of a post-selection overhead determined exactly by the largest target probability --- polynomial for smooth distributions, exponential for sharply peaked ones. Finally, the reweighting estimator underlying the fermion sign problem maps exactly onto a quantum weak value, placing the exponential cost of sign-problematic DQMC in precise correspondence with the post-selection overhead of weak-value extraction.

</details>


### [257] [Floquet engineering of competing antiferromagnetism and $d$-wave superconductivity on the square lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28750)
*Zhaoyu Han,Subir Sachdev*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受驱动的Lieb-Hubbard量子模拟器，通过周期性调制Lieb晶格的电荷转移偏移，在方格子自旋-1/2费米模型中独立可调地实现排斥性在位相互作用和吸引性键相互作用，从而在中间耦合区域观察到反铁磁序与d波超导序的竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子模拟中，同时独立调控排斥性相互作用与吸引性相互作用（特别是键相互作用），并探索它们竞争产生的量子相，是当前量子模拟领域的一个关键挑战。作者希望通过周期驱动（Floquet）方案在一个简单晶格结构中实现这一目标。

Method: 利用Lieb晶格（具有d和p两种轨道），周期调制电荷转移偏移，使p轨道双占据与d轨道对在能量上近共振，同时使p轨道单占据保持非共振，从而形成合成"负U"中心。通过两次受控消去（controlled elimination）在约化后的纯d轨道方格子模型中产生紧凑的键吸引相互作用J，该相互作用与d轨道的Hubbard排斥U_d独立可调。采用均匀场（mean-field）计算分析半填充下的相图。

Result: 在中间耦合区域（J和U_d均与有效跃迁强度相当），均匀场计算发现半填充下存在相邻的反铁磁相和d波超导相，以及狭窄的共存区域，表明这两种序之间存在密切竞争。此外还指出了将形式化构造转化为光学晶格实验所需的支路制备、预热力学和高能带条件。

Conclusion: 该工作在不依赖微观p轨道吸引的前提下实现了方格子上的键吸引相互作用，并揭示了Floquet系统与电子-声子问题之间的结构相似性，为设计新型量子模拟协议提供了指导思路。

Abstract: We propose a driven Lieb--Hubbard quantum simulator whose prethermal dynamics realize a square-lattice spin-1/2 fermion model with independently tunable repulsive on-site and attractive bond interactions. Periodically modulating the charge-transfer offset between the site ($d$) and bond ($p$) orbitals of the Lieb lattice brings a $p$-orbital doublon energetically close to a pair in the $d$ manifold while keeping all $p$-orbital singlons off resonance, thereby creating a synthetic ``negative-$U$'' center on a Lieb-lattice bond site. Two controlled eliminations then generate a compact bond attraction in the reduced $d$-only model on the square lattice, despite the microscopic repulsion in the $p$ orbital. The resulting interaction $J$ is tunable independently of the Hubbard repulsion $U_d$ on the $d$ orbitals, while interference between photon-assisted paths provides access to an intermediate-coupling regime in which $J$ and $U_d$ are both comparable to the effective hopping. At half filling, a mean-field calculation in this regime finds adjacent antiferromagnetic and $d$-wave superconducting phases, as well as narrow coexistence regions, suggesting close competition between these orders. We discuss the branch-preparation, prethermal, and higher-band conditions required to translate the formal construction into an optical-lattice protocol. More broadly, our work identifies a structural similarity between Floquet systems and electron-phonon problems that may guide the design of novel quantum-simulation protocols.

</details>


### [258] [Phase stability, charge ordering, and charge-liquid formation in the Falicov-Kimball model on triangular and kagome lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28898)
*Ammar Nejati,Marvin Lenk*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文通过马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛模拟，系统研究了自旋无关Falicov-Kimball模型在二维非二分晶格（三角晶格和kagome晶格）上相图的稳定性。研究发现三角晶格上存在Anderson绝缘体、Mott绝缘体和电荷密度波（CDW）相，且在弱关联区与CDW之间存在随填充条件收缩的电荷液体区域；而kagome晶格上完全不存在CDW相，取而代之的是Mott型绝缘基态，并暗示可能存在零温量子相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的Falicov-Kimball模型研究多集中于二分晶格，而对非二分晶格（三角和kagome）中晶格几何与粒子-空穴对称性破缺如何影响关联电子系统相图的理解尚不充分。作者旨在系统探究这两种非二分晶格上该模型的相图稳定性，并考察微扰小次近邻跃迁及不同填充条件下的相变行为。

Method: 采用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）模拟方法，研究对象为二维三角晶格和kagome晶格上的自旋无关Falicov-Kimball模型。分别考察三种填充/化学势条件：固定化学势（μ_f=μ_c=U/2）、广义半填充（n̄_f=1/3, n̄_c=2/3）、常规半填充（n̄_f=n̄_c=1/2），并在每种条件下研究微扰性次近邻跃迁的影响。

Result: 在三角晶格上，所有填充条件下均观察到Anderson绝缘体、Mott绝缘体和CDW相，其中CDW由库仑相互作用介导的嵌套效应驱动。在弱关联区与CDW之间发现了电荷液体（charge-liquid）区域的证据，该区域具有竞争性的电荷有序波矢且缺乏普适标度行为，其范围从巨正则系综到常规半填充条件逐步缩小。在kagome晶格上，CDW相完全缺失，取而代之的是Mott型绝缘基态，并呈现出零温量子相变的迹象。

Conclusion: 研究确立了晶格几何结构和粒子-空穴对称性破缺在决定关联电子系统相图中的关键作用：三角晶格的嵌套特性支持CDW的形成，而kagome晶格的几何阻挫抑制了CDW并倾向Mott绝缘态。电荷液体区域的存在及其随填充条件的变化揭示了非二分晶格中关联电子系统丰富的量子相行为。

Abstract: We present a systematic investigation of the stability of the phase diagram of the spinless Falicov-Kimball model on two-dimensional non-bipartite lattices, the triangular and kagome lattices, using Markov-chain Monte Carlo simulations. Three filling or chemical potential conditions are examined: fixed chemical potentials ($μ_f = μ_c = U/2$), "generalized" half-filling ($\bar{n}_f = 1/3$, $\bar{n}_c = 2/3$), and conventional half-filling ($\bar{n}_f = \bar{n}_c = 1/2$). For each condition, the effects of a perturbatively small next-nearest-neighbor hopping are studied. On the triangular lattice, Anderson insulator, Mott insulator, and charge-density wave (CDW) phases are found in all cases, with the CDW driven by Coulomb-interaction-mediated nesting. Evidence for a charge-liquid regime is found in the region between the weakly correlated regime and the CDW, characterized by competing charge-ordering wavevectors and the absence of universal scaling behavior, whose extent shrinks progressively from the grand-canonical to the conventional half-filling condition. On the kagome lattice, the CDW phase is completely absent; instead, an insulating ground state, plausibly of the Mott type, is found, with signatures of a possible quantum phase transition at zero temperature. Our results establish the crucial role of lattice geometry and particle-hole symmetry breaking in shaping the phase diagram of correlated electron systems.

</details>


### [259] [Enhanced Diffusion from Twist-Angle Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29062)
*Nicole S Ticea,Yi-Ming Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究转角双层体系中转角无序对单粒子性质的影响，提出将转角视为相对位移的旋度（类比电磁场中的磁场与矢势关系），发现几何散射约束显著抑制了动量弛豫，并将问题映射到非线性sigma模型（NLSM）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 转角双层材料中微小且不受控的空间转角变化可能产生异乎寻常的后果——局部转角变化会引起母哈密顿量的空间关联性改变，进而影响输运和有序倾向等粗粒化性质，因此需要系统地理解这种无序效应。

Method: 采用与电动力学类比的方法，将转角角度建模为两层间相对位移的旋度（转角 = ∇×相对位移），以此构造转角无序的几何约束；随后通过分析几何散射约束对动量弛豫的影响，并将物理问题映射到非线性sigma模型（NLSM）进行定量研究。

Result: 发现转角结构所施加的几何散射约束相对于平均速率匹配的白噪声能显著抑制动量弛豫，并定量刻画了这一效应。问题可被映射为非线性sigma模型。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了转角无序对双层体系单粒子性质影响的理论框架，揭示了几何约束对散射行为的独特抑制作用，为理解转角材料中无序效应提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: We consider the effect of twist-angle disorder on the single-particle properties of twisted bilayer systems. These materials are a natural playground for studying how tiny, uncontrolled spatial variations may become unusually consequential; a local change in the twist geometry can give rise to spatially-correlated alterations of the parent Hamiltonian, which in turn affect the coarse-grained properties of the substrate such as transport and ordering tendencies. In analogy to electrodynamics, where the magnetic field is given by the curl of the vector potential, we model the twist angle as the curl of the relative displacement between the two bilayers. We find that geometric scattering constraints imposed here by the twist structure substantially suppress momentum relaxation relative to mean-rate-matched white noise. We quantify these effects and show that the problem can be mapped onto a non-linear sigma model (NLSM).

</details>


### [260] [Duality Between Twist-Angle Disorder and Non-Hermitian Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29064)
*Yi-Ming Wu,Nicole S. Ticea*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究了莫尔异质双层体系中扭转角仅在有限范围内保持均匀并发生远距离随机形变的模型，通过复本技巧证明无序平均产生非平凡的交变符号关联，并揭示了该模型与费米子耦合非厄米无序的对偶关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 莫尔异质双层体系中的扭转角无序是实验中不可避免的重要问题，但与传统无序有本质区别。现有研究缺乏对扭转角空间涨落（而非均匀无序）的系统理论框架，需要建立新的理论模型来理解扭转角无序对低能莫尔带的影响及其与传统无序的根本差异。

Method: 论文采用复本技巧（replica trick）对扭转角无序进行平均处理，建立局部扭转角遵循某种概率分布的统计模型；通过分析复本间的关联揭示无序的拓扑性质；并构造一个耦合适配非厄米无序的费米子对偶模型，证明两者在低能莫尔带中产生完全相同的关联。

Result: 发现无序平均后产生具有交替符号的非平凡复本间关联，该关联仅在每个局部畴域边界附近及特定方向上最大化；同时证明低能莫尔带中相同关联可由费米子与非厄米无序耦合的对偶模型产生，该模型可动态规避安德森局域化。

Conclusion: 该对偶关系为研究莫尔体系中扭转角无序提供了新视角，并揭示了扭转角无序与传统无序之间存在本质区别。

Abstract: We propose and study a model of moire heterobilayer systems where the twist angle remains uniform and well defined only within a finite range, but deforms randomly at larger distances. The local twist angle is then subject to a certain probability distribution that penalizes large fluctuations around its mean value. Within the framework of the replica trick, we show that disorder averaging produces a nontrivial correlation with alternating sign between replicas, maximized only close the boundary of each local domain and along certain directions. We show that for the low-energy moire bands, exactly the same correlation can be generated in a dual model where fermions are coupled to non-Hermitian disorder, which can evade Anderson localization dynamically. This duality provides a new perspective to investigate twist-angle disorder in moire systems, and unveils some of the essential differences between twist-angle disorder and conventional disorder.

</details>


### [261] [Real-space manifestation of ferroic multipoles in altermagnetic MnF$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29089)
*Iurii Kibalin,Dalila Bounoua,José A. Rodriguez Velamazán,Oscar Fabelo,Navid Qureshi,Quentin Faure,Philippe Bourges,Victor Balédent,Jian-Rui Soh,Jeffrey Rau,Paul McClarty,Arsen Gukasov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过极化中子衍射技术，首次在实空间直接重构了典型交错磁体MnF₂的三维磁化密度，揭示了氟配体上的有限磁矩及各向异性Mn磁化，证明了与对称性允许的交错磁rank-5磁多极子O₅₂（磁性三十二极子）一致的多极序存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错磁体（altermagnet）是一种在零自旋轨道耦合极限下产生的非传统自旋劈裂磁体，其核心特征是具有磁多极序参量，但迄今为止这些多极子在实空间中从未被直接观测到，因此迫切需要一种能够直接可视化这些隐藏磁多极子的实验手段。

Method: 利用极化中子衍射技术重构MnF₂的三维磁化密度。通过利用对称选择性磁反射，将占主导的球形Mn²⁺贡献与较弱的各向异性Mn磁化以及氟配体的共价自旋极化贡献分离开来，从而实现精确的自旋密度重构。

Result: 重构的自旋密度显示出有限氟离子磁矩以及各向异性的Mn磁化，后者与对称性允许的交错磁rank-5磁多极子O₅₂（磁性三十二极子）一致。这是交错磁体中铁性多极序的首次直接实空间证据。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了极化中子衍射作为探测量子材料中隐藏磁多极子的强大实验工具，为交错磁体领域提供了实空间层面的直接验证，也为进一步研究此类新型磁性材料的微观机制奠定了基础。

Abstract: Altermagnets are unconventional spin split magnets arising from the zero spin-orbit coupled limit. They host a magnetic multipolar order parameter yet direct real-space observation of these multipoles has remained elusive. Here we use polarized-neutron diffraction to reconstruct the three-dimensional magnetization density of the prototypical altermagnet MnF$_2$. By exploiting symmetry-selective magnetic reflections, we separate the dominant spherical Mn$^{2+}$ contribution from the much weaker anisotropic Mn magnetization and the covalent spin polarization of the fluorine ligands. The reconstructed spin density reveals a finite fluorine ion moment together with an anisotropic Mn magnetization consistent with the symmetry-allowed altermagnetic rank-5 magnetic multipole $O_{52}$(magnetic triacontadipole). These results provide direct real-space evidence of ferroic multipolar order in an altermagnet and establish polarized-neutron diffraction as a powerful probe of hidden magnetic multipoles in quantum materials.

</details>


### [262] [Electronic phase separation and emergence of a nondimerized insulating phase in VO$_2$ $(110)_{\mathit{R}}$ ultrathin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29116)
*S. Inoue,D. Shiga,R. Hayasaka,K. Ozawa,A. F. Santander-Syro,H. Kumigashira*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用原位光电子能谱和X射线吸收谱研究了VO₂/TiO₂(110)超薄膜中电子结构和V-V二聚化的厚度依赖性。研究发现，即使(110)取向不像(001)取向那样对V-V二聚化施加几何约束，1.5 nm以上的薄膜仍表现出温度驱动的金属-绝缘体转变，而1 nm时出现无V-V二聚化的绝缘相，表明降维增强的Mott不稳定性是VO₂超薄膜中非二聚化绝缘相出现的共同驱动力。但与(001)取向不同，(110)取向的非二聚化绝缘相与MIT相共存，其比例随厚度指数增长，有效临界厚度约为2.2 nm，说明V-V链的几何取向通过应变介导的相竞争决定了电子相分离的空间演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究VO₂超薄膜中电子结构和V-V二聚化随厚度的变化规律，特别是维度降低对Mott不稳定性及金属-绝缘体转变的影响，并通过对比不同晶面取向(001)与(110)来阐明几何取向在相分离演化中的作用。

Method: 采用原位光电子能谱(PES)和X射线吸收光谱(XAS)，对VO₂/TiO₂(110)超薄膜（厚度从1 nm至厚膜）进行系统性厚度依赖的电子结构表征，分析V-V二聚化随厚度的变化，并与已报道的(001)取向结果进行对比。

Result: (110)取向VO₂薄膜中，温度驱动的MIT相关光谱特征可保持至1.5 nm；在1 nm时出现无V-V二聚化的绝缘相。该非二聚化绝缘相与MIT相共存，其分数随厚度指数增加并在1 nm时占主导，有效临界厚度约为2.2 nm。

Conclusion: 降维增强的Mott不稳定性是VO₂超薄膜中非二聚化绝缘相出现的共同本质驱动力，与晶面取向无关；但V-V链的几何取向通过应变介导的相竞争决定电子相分离的空间分布演化方式。

Abstract: Using in situ photoemission spectroscopy and x-ray absorption spectroscopy, we investigated the thickness dependence of the electronic structure and V-V dimerization in VO$_2$/TiO$_2$ (110) ultrathin films, in which the one-dimensional V-V chains along the $c_{\mathit{R}}$ axis lie in the film plane. In VO$_2$ $(110)_{\mathit{R}}$ films, the reduction in dimensionality along the surface-normal direction is not expected to impose a geometric constraint on V-V dimerization, unlike in VO$_2$ $(001)_{\mathit{R}}$ films. Nevertheless, the characteristic spectral changes associated with the temperature-driven metal-insulator transition observed in thick films persist down to 1.5 nm, whereas at 1 nm an insulating electronic phase is observed without V-V dimerization. This behavior is highly similar to that reported for VO$_2$ $(001)_{\mathit{R}}$, suggesting that the enhancement of Mott instability resulting from reduced dimensionality is a common and essential driving force for the emergence of the nondimerized insulating phase in VO$_2$ ultrathin films. Meanwhile, unlike in VO$_2$ $(001)_{\mathit{R}}$, the nondimerized insulating phase in VO$_2$ $(110)_{\mathit{R}}$ coexists with the phase exhibiting the temperature-driven metal-insulator transition. Its fraction increases exponentially with decreasing thickness and becomes dominant at 1 nm. The corresponding effective critical thickness is estimated to be 2.2 nm. These results imply that the geometric orientation of the V-V chains dictates the spatial evolution of electronic phase separation via strain-mediated phase competition.

</details>


### [263] [Evolving charge order in the CDW state of AV$_3$Sb$_5$ metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29142)
*Anshu Kataria,Francis Pratt,Peter J. Baker,Stephen Cottrell,Miki Bonacci,Andrea Capa Salinas,Stephen D. Wilson,Zurab Guguchia,Samuele Sanna,Pietro Bonfà*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用回避交叉（ALC）μ子自旋谱学研究了AV₃Sb₅笼目金属（A = Cs, K）在超导相之前的电子态，发现除CDW转变外还存在第二个转变，表明CDW相内存在额外的电荷调制机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AV₃Sb₅笼目金属具有交织的电子与结构有序，但超导相之前的电子态细节仍不清楚，需要进一步阐明。

Method: 将此前对RbV₃Sb₅的ALC μ子自旋谱学方法扩展到A = Cs和K体系，通过ALC信号分析探测内部磁场及电荷分布变化。

Result: 在Cs和K体系中均观察到第二个转变，其来源不能仅归因于内部磁场，表明存在额外的电子机制微妙地改变V平面内的电荷分布。ALC结果表明CDW相内存在额外电荷调制：对A = Cs和Rb发生在T* < T_CDW，而对A = K则发生在T_CDW附近。

Conclusion: AV₃Sb₅体系在CDW相内普遍存在额外的电荷调制转变，其温度位置随A位元素而系统变化，为理解该材料体系中电子态与超导的关系提供了重要线索。

Abstract: AV$_3$Sb$_5$ kagome metals are characterized by intertwined electronic and structural orders, which motivated extensive studies in recent years. Yet the details of the electronic state preceding the superconducting phase remain poorly understood. Here we extend our previous investigation [Phys. Rev. Research 7, L032046 (2025)] of RbV$_3$Sb$_5$ using avoided level crossing (ALC) muon-spin spectroscopy to the $A$ = Cs and K systems. Consistent with our previous study, we identify a second transition whose origin cannot be attributed solely to an internal magnetic field, indicating the involvement of an additional electronic mechanism that subtly modifies the charge distribution within the V plane. In particular, the ALC results point towards an additional charge modulation taking place within the charge density wave (CDW) phase and occurring at $T^{*}<T_{CDW}$ for $A$ = Cs and Rb, or in the vicinity of $T_{CDW}$ for $A$ = K.

</details>


### [264] [Crystal electric field excitations and an effective-spin-$1/2$ ground doublet in the hyperkagome magnet Yb$_3$Sc$_2$Ga$_3$O$_{12}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29276)
*Tingjun Zhang,Zehao Wang,Douglas L. Abernathy,Rong-Zhu Lin,Steven J. Gomez Alvarado,Chien-Lung Huang,Pengcheng Dai*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用非弹性中子散射(INS)精确测定了稀土hyperkagome磁体Yb₃Sc₂Ga₃O₁₂中Yb³⁺的晶场(CEF)激发谱，在58、68和74 meV处观测到三个近乎无色散的磁激发，确定了Ising型基态g张量，建立了良好隔离的J_eff=1/2有效自由度，为构建有效交换哈密顿量奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前通过体测量（如磁化率、比热等）推断的晶场能级方案可能不准确，需要直接测量来确定Yb³⁺的单离子晶场基态与激发态，从而为理解该hyperkagome磁体的集体磁性提供可靠的微观单离子基础。

Method: 采用非弹性中子散射(INS)直接测量Yb₃Sc₂Ga₃O₁₂中Yb³⁺的晶场激发，并结合局部222(D₂)对称性约束的Stevens算子分析来重现激发能量和谱权重；采用两种独立的拟合协议验证结果的稳健性；并将得到的单离子模型用于重现磁化率、磁化强度及比热中Schottky异常随磁场的变化。

Result: 在58、68和74 meV处观测到三个近乎无色散的磁激发，对应从基态Kramers双重态到J=7/2多重态三个激发双重态的跃迁。Stevens算子分析得出Ising型基态g张量。第一激发双重态位于基态上方约58 meV，建立了良好隔离的J_eff=1/2有效自由度。直接测量的晶场谱显著修正了先前由体测量推断的能级方案，但保留了低能赝自旋描述。两种拟合协议给出了自洽的结果，单离子模型成功再现了磁化率、磁化强度和Schottky异常的场演化。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了Yb₃Sc₂Ga₃O₁₂的微观单离子晶场基态，为构建有效交换哈密顿量以及解读未来的低能集体激发测量提供了可靠基础。同时强调直接测量晶场谱的重要性，因为体测量推断的能级方案可能存在偏差。

Abstract: We use inelastic neutron scattering (INS) to determine the crystal electric field (CEF) excitations of Yb$^{3+}$ in the rare-earth hyperkagome magnet Yb$_3$Sc$_2$Ga$_3$O$_{12}$. Three nearly dispersionless magnetic excitations are observed near 58, 68, and 74~meV, corresponding to transitions from the ground-state Kramers doublet to the three excited doublets of the $J=7/2$ multiplet. A Stevens-operator analysis constrained by the local $222$ ($D_2$) symmetry reproduces the excitation energies and spectral weights and yields an Ising-type ground-state $g$ tensor. The first excited doublet lies approximately 58~meV above the ground state, establishing a well-isolated effective $J_{\mathrm{eff}}=1/2$ degree of freedom over the low-temperature regime relevant to collective magnetism. Notably, the directly measured CEF spectrum substantially revises the level scheme previously inferred from bulk measurements, while preserving the essential low-energy pseudospin description. Two independent fitting protocols give consistent excitation energies, ground-doublet wave functions, and $g$ tensors, despite the nonuniqueness of the individual CEF parameters. The resulting single-ion model also reproduces the characteristic susceptibility, magnetization, and field evolution of the Schottky anomaly in specific heat. These results establish the microscopic single-ion basis needed to construct an effective exchange Hamiltonian and to interpret future measurements of low-energy collective excitations in Yb$_3$Sc$_2$Ga$_3$O$_{12}$.

</details>


### [265] [Experimental observation of decoupled spin subsystems in decorated square kagomé lattice magnets of the nabokoite family](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29323)
*V. N. Glazkov,Ya V. Rebrov,M. A. Dubovitskii,M. M. Markina,A. F. Murtazoev,P. S. Berdonosov,A. N. Vasiliev*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过电子顺磁共振（EPR）研究发现了Nabokoite族化合物（Na/K/Cs/Rb）A₂Cu₃(SO₄)₃X中复杂交换网络发生"分裂"，仅部分铜自旋呈EPR活性并在Néel温度下形成磁有序，而其余EPR沉默自旋子系统可能表现出自旋液体行为，两者近乎退耦共存于同一化合物中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 方形Kagomé晶格（SKL）是研究二维几何阻挫的理想平台，而Nabokoite族化合物将其扩展到三维网络（2D SKL层被层间自旋点缀）。作者希望通过研究这类化合物中磁交换网络的复杂性，揭示其中可能存在的不寻常磁性行为。

Method: 使用绝对标定的电子顺磁共振（EPR）技术，并结合EPR吸收与静态磁化率的对比分析，定量测定EPR活性自旋的比例及其随温度的变化行为。

Result: EPR绝对标定显示Nabokoite中仅有部分铜自旋是EPR活性的，该活性自旋部分在Néel温度下发生磁有序；对比EPR吸收与静态磁化率发现，EPR沉默自旋子系统对总磁化率的贡献随降温而减小。

Conclusion: 在单一化合物中直接观察到磁有序与可能的自旋液体动力学的共存，挑战了装饰化阻挫晶格中统一交换网络的传统模型。

Abstract: The square kagomé lattice (SKL) offers a model platform for investigating geometric frustration in 2D systems. Nabokoite-family compounds \nabok{A}{X} (A=Na, K, Cs, Rb and X=Cl, Br) extend this physics to a 3D network, where 2D SKL layers are decorated by interlayer spins. Using electron paramagnetic resonance (EPR), we demonstrate a dramatic splitting of this complex exchange network into two virtually decoupled spin subsystems: absolute calibration of the electron paramagnetic resonance (EPR) absorption reveals that only a fraction of all copper spins in nabokoites is EPR-active and this fraction of the spins orders at the Néel point. Comparison of the EPR absorption and static susceptibility indicates that contribution of the EPR-silent spin subsystem to total magnetic susceptibility decreases on cooling. This direct observation of coexisting magnetic order and possible spin-liquid dynamics within a single compound challenges conventional models of unified exchange networks in decorated frustrated lattices.

</details>


### [266] [Collinear and noncollinear antiferromagnetic ordering in a highly frustrated decorated square kagomé lattice antiferromagnets of the nabokoite family](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29330)
*V. N. Glazkov,Ya V. Rebrov,M. A. Dubovitskii,M. M. Markina,K. V. Zakharov,A. F. Murtazoev,P. S. Berdonosov,A. N. Vasiliev*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文通过多频率电子自旋共振（ESR）和热力学测量（比热、磁化率、介电常数），系统研究了Nabokoite家族化合物ACu7(TeO4)(SO4)5X（A=Na,K,Rb,Cs; X=Cl,Br）的磁有序行为。研究发现轻碱金属（Na,K）和重碱金属（Rb,Cs）化合物的低温基态定性不同：重碱金属化合物呈常规共线反铁磁有序（易轴各向异性），而轻碱金属化合物先经历铁电相变（25-90K）解除阻挫，再通过两步相变（Tc1≈5-6K，Tc2≈3-4K）形成非共线磁有序，且表现出三个零场磁振子间隙和两个自旋重取向场。有限尺寸团簇模型表明，Nabokoite结构金字塔结构块的交换耦合参数临界接近不同量子基态之间的边界，这可能是轻/重碱金属化合物选择不同有序态的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nabokoite家族化合物包含阻挫的2D方形kagomé晶格层以及额外的层间磁性离子，提供了研究几何阻挫与磁有序竞争的独特平台。理解轻/重碱金属化合物之间截然不同的低温磁有序行为及其背后的物理机制（阻挫解除、铁电-磁耦合、量子临界性）是本研究的主要动机。

Method: 采用多频率电子自旋共振（ESR）光谱学以及热力学测量（比热、磁化率、介电常数）系统表征磁有序行为；结合有限尺寸团簇模型对Nabokoite结构的金字塔结构块进行建模，分析交换耦合参数与不同量子基态之间的关系。

Result: (1) 重碱金属（Rb,Cs）Nabokoite以常规共线反铁磁有序（易轴各向异性）为基态，有序态参数在子族内非常接近；(2) 轻碱金属（Na,K）Nabokoite先经历25-90K的铁电相变解除阻挫，再通过两步相变（Tc1≈5-6K，Tc2≈3-4K）发展出非共线磁有序，并观测到特征性非Larmor反铁磁共振模式；(3) 轻碱金属化合物表现出三个零场磁振子间隙和两个自旋重取向场，其数值在不同化合物间差异显著；(4) 团簇建模显示交换耦合参数接近不同量子基态之间的临界边界，这可能是选择不同有序态的关键。

Conclusion: Nabokoite家族中轻/重碱金属化合物的磁有序行为存在根本性差异：重碱金属化合物为常规共线反铁磁序，而轻碱金属化合物经历铁电过渡与两步相变形成非共线磁序。该差异归因于Nabokoite金字塔结构块交换耦合参数恰好处于不同量子基态（共线与非共线）的临界边界附近，这为理解几何阻挫体系中阻挫解除、多铁性与磁有序的耦合机制提供了重要物理洞察。

Abstract: Nabokoite family compounds ACu$_7$(TeO$_4$)(SO4)$_5$X (A=Na, K, Rb, Cs; X=Cl, Br) host frustrated 2D square kagom'{e} lattice layers decorated by additional inter-layer magnetic ions. We study magnetic order in nabokoites with multi-frequency electron spin resonance spectroscopy and thermodynamic measurement (specific heat, magnetization and dielectric permittivity). Our study reveals that the choice of the low-temperature ground state is qualitatively different in light-alkali-ion (K, Na) and heavy-alkali-ion (Rb, Cs) compounds. Heavy-alkali-ion nabokoites order in conventional collinear antiferromagnetic pattern with easy-axis anisotropy. The parameters of the ordered antiferromagnetic state are very close for all heavy-alkali-ion subfamily. Light-alkali-ion members of nabokoite family demonstrate much more complicated route to the ordered state: firstly, a ferroelectric transition at 25-90K lifts the frustration and thus pre-cooks the low-temperature ordering; secondly, an unusual noncollinear magnetic order develops via two-step phase transition with first transition temperature $T_{c1}\simeq 5-6$K and the second transition at $T_{c2}\simeq 3-4$K. Noncollinear order is evidenced by observation of characteristic non-Larmor antiferromagnetic resonance mode. Spin dynamics of light-alkali-ion nabokoites is characterized by three zero-field magnon gaps and two spin-reorientation fields, the values of magnon gaps and critical fields are quite different for different compounds. The finite-size cluster modeling of pyramidal structural block of nabokoite structure combined suggests that the critical closeness of the nabokoite exchange coupling parameters to the border-line between the different quantum ground state of pyramidal building block of nabokoite structure could be the clue to the choice of qualitatively different ordered state in light- and heavy-alkali-ion nabokoites.

</details>


### [267] [Magnetic Field-Tunable Repulsive Exciton-Exciton Interaction in the van der Waals Antiferromagnet NiPS$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29701)
*Kaiyang Huang,Jaena Park,Zhuo Yang,Je-Geun Park,Atsuhiko Miyata,Yoshimitsu Kohama,Yasuhiro H. Matsuda*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文通过脉冲磁场（最高178 T）下的磁光测量，研究了二维范德华晶体NiPS₃中两个超窄吸收峰（约1.5 eV）之间的相互作用，观察到两个激子峰之间显著的相互排斥和振子强度重新分布，构建了最小二能级相互作用模型并与实验数据进行了半定量对比，揭示了磁场可调的激子-激子耦合是NiPS₃的高场主导响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NiPS₃中两个超窄吸收峰具有显著的自旋依赖特性，被认为来源于自旋-轨道纠缠三重态到单重态的跃迁。一个关键的科学问题是：超高磁场是否会改变这两个峰之间由相互作用驱动的杂化行为。

Method: 在高达178 T的脉冲磁场下对NiPS₃进行系统的磁光测量；构建最小二能级相互作用模型，并与实验数据进行半定量对比。

Result: 观察到两个尖锐激子峰之间明显的相互排斥现象，伴随振子强度的重新分布；带边在实验分辨率内无检测到的磁场诱导位移。

Conclusion: 磁场可调的激子-激子耦合是NiPS₃的高场主导响应，该材料可作为探索磁性范德华绝缘体中强关联激子物理的新实验平台。

Abstract: Two ultra-narrow absorption peaks around 1.5 eV, which are widely believed to originate from a transition from a spin-orbital entangled triplet to a singlet state, in the two-dimensional van der Waals crystal NiPS$_3$, have attracted considerable attention because of their pronounced spin-dependent character. An interesting question is whether ultrahigh magnetic fields modify an interaction-driven hybridization between those two peaks. In this work we perform systematic magneto-optical measurements of NiPS$_3$ in pulsed magnetic fields of up to 178 T and observe a pronounced mutual repulsion between the two sharp exciton peaks accompanied by a redistribution of oscillator strength, while the band edge shows no detectable field-induced shift within our experimental resolution. We construct a minimal two-level interaction model and compare it semi-quantitatively with the experimental data. Our results reveal a magnetic-field-tunable exciton-exciton coupling as the dominant high-field response of NiPS$_3$, and clarify this material as a new experimental platform for exploring strongly correlated exciton physics in magnetic van der Waals insulators.

</details>


### [268] [Emergent Pair Density Wave and Incoherent Metallic State in a Strongly Correlated Doped System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29742)
*Soham Maiti,Nandan Pakhira,A. Taraphder*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文采用slave-rotor平均场框架研究反铁磁（AFM）与d波超导（SC）相互作用下态的动力学涌现机制，发现PDW（配对密度波）序在AFM-SC共存区动态涌现，并揭示了强关联效应对相图、临界点和谱权重的重要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强关联电子体系中反铁磁与d波超导之间的竞争与合作关系是高温超导研究的核心问题之一，传统平均场方法无法充分捕捉强关联效应，需要更先进的框架来揭示这些有序态之间的相互作用及其涌现现象。

Method: 采用slave-rotor平均场方法，将电子分解为电荷（rotor）和自旋（spinon）两个自由度，从而在平均场水平上引入超越常规处理方法的强关联效应。分析AFM与SC共存区中PDW序的涌现，并考察掺杂、温度依赖的序参量演化及rotor凝聚振幅φ（表征电荷相干性）的变化。

Result: PDW序在AFM-SC共存区动态涌现；强关联显著修改了AFM-SC共存区域并导致四临界点系统性移动；随掺杂和温度演化显示从相干金属到非相干金属的交叉行为；在AFM+(φ≠0)区域存在相干准粒子带与非相干Hubbard类激发的共存，而在AFM+(φ=0)区域仅存非相干谱特征；SC相具有与d波配对一致的节点准粒子。

Conclusion: 强关联效应通过电荷-自旋分离框架能捕获常规平均场方法无法描述的PDW动态涌现及相干-非相干交叉等物理现象，为理解高温超导体中AFM与SC共存区域的丰富物理提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamical emergence of a state driven by the interplay between antiferromagnetism (AFM) and singlet $d$-wave superconductivity (SC) within a slave-rotor mean-field framework. By decomposing the electron into charge and spin degrees of freedom, the formalism captures strong-correlation effects beyond conventional mean-field approaches. A PDW order is found to emerge dynamically in the coexistence region of AFM and SC. The AFM-SC coexistence region is significantly modified with correlation, leading to a systematic shift of the tetra-critical point. The doping and temperature evolution of the AFM, SC, and PDW order parameters, together with the rotor condensate amplitude $φ$, which characterizes charge coherence, shows a crossover from a coherent to an incoherent metal with the suppression of coherent quasi-particle spectral weight. Moreover, in the AFM + $(φ\neq 0)$ region, coherent quasi-particle bands coexist with incoherent Hubbard-like excitations, whereas only incoherent spectral features survive in the AFM + $(φ= 0)$ regime. The SC phase exhibits nodal quasi-particles consistent with $d$-wave pairing.

</details>


### [269] [Thermal Hall Signatures of Distinct Schwinger-Boson Flux Sectors on the Honeycomb Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29792)
*Daiki Sasamoto*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过有限温度Schwinger-boson平均场理论和适用于玻色子Bogoliubov-de Gennes系统的Kubo公式，研究了蜂巢晶格上受挫J1-J2海森堡模型在不同parton平均场通量扇区（0-通量、π-通量和手性π/2-通量）中的自旋子热霍尔电导率，发现三种通量扇区表现出不同的符号和温度依赖特征，从而为区分不同的通量结构提供了运输探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热输运是探测受阻磁性材料中电中性激发体的有效体相探针，但能否通过热输运区分同一自旋体系中不同的parton平均场结构仍然是一个未解决的问题。这一问题的关键在于确定热霍尔电导率能否作为不同通量扇区的区分标志。

Method: 采用有限温度Schwinger-boson平均场理论，对0-flux、π-flux和手性π/2-flux三个扇区进行自洽鞍点求解；利用玻色子Bogoliubov-de Gennes系统的Kubo公式计算内禀自旋子热霍尔电导率，并在具有次近邻Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用和垂直磁场的J1-J2海森堡模型中进行对比分析。此外，通过投影对称性分析（六重转动与时间反演的组合）研究零磁场下π/2-flux域的响应性质。

Result: 在三个分支均保持能隙的共同参数点上：0-flux响应为正，π-flux响应为负（在特定符号约定下），而π/2-flux响应随温度升高改变符号；同时证明六重转动与时间反演组合的投影对称性使零场下固定手性π/2-flux域的热霍尔响应消失。在平均场有效范围内，κ_xy/T的符号和温度依赖性可作为具有通量敏感性的运输特征。

Conclusion: κ_xy/T的符号和温度依赖行为能够区分蜂巢晶格上同一自旋体系的不同parton平均场通量扇区，热输运可以作为通量敏感的探测手段。这一结果为通过热霍尔效应对受阻磁性材料中的parton结构进行实验辨识提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Thermal transport offers a bulk probe of charge-neutral excitations in frustrated magnets. It remains challenging, however, to determine whether such transport can distinguish different parton mean-field structures of the same spin system. This question is particularly relevant on the honeycomb lattice, where distinct $0$-flux, $π$-flux, and chiral $π/2$-flux sectors have been proposed. In this paper, we compare self-consistent saddle-point solutions in these three sectors for a frustrated $J_1-J_2$ Heisenberg model with a next-nearest-neighbor Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction and a perpendicular magnetic field. Using finite-temperature Schwinger-boson mean-field theory and the Kubo formula for bosonic Bogoliubov-de Gennes systems, we evaluate the intrinsic spinon thermal Hall conductivity. At a common parameter point where all three branches remain gapped, the $0$-flux response is positive and the $π$-flux response is negative for $D>0$ and $h>0$ under the sign conventions used in this paper, whereas the $π/2$-flux response changes sign with increasing temperature. We further show that a projective symmetry combining a sixfold rotation with time reversal forces the zero-field response of a fixed chiral $π/2$-flux domain to vanish. Within the mean-field regime examined in this paper, the sign and temperature dependence of $κ_{xy}/T$ therefore provide a flux-sensitive transport signature.

</details>


### [270] [Symmetry-Enforced Topological Structures in Quantum Phase Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29915)
*Linhao Li,Yuan Yao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究有能隙系统中量子相图的拓扑结构，通过识别相图内不可收缩的S^1-族环来分类，并揭示了混合反常、半直积对称关系和非可逆对称算子等奇异性对称相互作用如何导致全新且此前无法实现的S^1-族分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期分类仅基于"独立"对称性，未能涵盖实际凝聚态系统中普遍存在的对称性之间复杂相互作用（如混合反常、自发破缺与非破缺对称间的半直积关系、非可逆算子对称），导致大量新颖的量子相未能被识别和分类。

Method: 通过分析有能隙系统中量子相图的拓扑结构，识别相图中不可收缩的S^1-族（环），并研究三种奇异的对称性相互作用如何影响S^1-族分类；进一步构建格点模型实现这些S^1-族，并显式展示其代数结构。

Result: 发现奇异的对称性相互作用（混合反常、半直积关系、非可逆对称算子）能产生基于"独立"对称性的传统分类无法获得的S^1-族分类，并通过格点实现明确展示了这些分类的新颖代数结构。

Conclusion: 对称性之间的复杂相互作用（而非独立对称性）在S^1-族分类中扮演关键角色，为有能隙系统量子相图的拓扑分类提供了更完整的理论框架。

Abstract: We study the topological structure of the quantum phase diagram of gapped systems by identifying the noncontractibility of loops, so-called $S^1$-families, within the gapped phase diagram in which many-body Hamiltonians can have nontrivial ground-state degeneracy. We manifest the role of symmetries in such $S^1$-family classifications by the exotic symmetry interplay: (i) nontrivial mixed anomalies, (ii) semi-direct product relation between the spontaneously broken and the unbroken symmetries, and (iii) symmetry with noninvertible operators. We find that such structures lead to the novel $S^1$-family classifications inaccessible by earlier classifications based on ``independent'' symmetries. Furthermore, we construct lattice realizations of these $S^1$-families and explicitly demonstrate their novel algebraic structures.

</details>


### [271] [Holographic Representations of Topological Quantum Criticality: Emergent Symmetry Approach around the Bott Clock](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30335)
*Fan Yang,Fei Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用涌现对称性的思想，为Bott时钟中费米子类的一系列拓扑量子临界点(tQCP)构建了全息表示，通过提升对称性构造(d+1)维晶格模型，并证明其d维边界在红外动力学上与原始d维tQCP等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑量子临界点是凝聚态物理中的重要课题，但长期以来缺乏系统的全息/体边界对应构造方法。作者希望利用涌现对称性这一概念，为Bott时钟中一大类费米子拓扑量子临界点建立统一的体边界对应框架，从而在不同维度和拓扑类之间建立联系。

Method: 作者从d维空间中被保护对称性G_p保护的拓扑相之间的tQCP出发，证明其存在涌现对称性U_EM。利用这一涌现对称性，将对称性提升为U_EM×G_p或U_EM⋊G_p等扩充对称群，从而构造(d+1)维晶格模型。具体做法是对d维相邻拓扑类的自由度进行倍增(约化维度翻倍)，使构造出的(d+1)维模型具有相邻拓扑类对应的扩大对称群（按Bott时钟逆时针方向）。然后分析这些(d+1)维能隙拓扑体模型的d维边界，验证其红外动力学与原始d维tQCP一致。

Result: 成功证明：Bott时钟中任意tQCP处均存在涌现对称性U_EM；通过自由度倍增构造的(d+1)维晶格模型精确具有扩大后的对称群，属于Bott时钟中逆时针相邻的拓扑类；这些(d+1)维模型的d维边界呈现与原始d维tQCP完全一致的红外动力学行为，但保留了较低（原始）的对称性。

Conclusion: 该工作为Bott时钟中一大类费米子拓扑量子临界点建立了系统的全息对应（体边界对应）构造方案，展示了涌现对称性作为桥梁，将不同维度下、不同拓扑类间的临界动力学联系起来的普遍原理。

Abstract: In this article, we apply the idea of emergent symmetries to construct holographic representations of a broad class of topological quantum critical points (tQCPs) that appear in fermionic classes in the Bott clock. We show explicitly that around the Bott clock, an emergent symmetry $U_{EM}$ exists at a tQCP between different symmetry protected gapped phases with protecting symmetry $G_p$ in $d$ spatial dimensions. This emergent symmetry can be used to construct a $(d+1)$ spatial dimensional lattice model with a properly upgraded symmetry such as $U_{EM}\times G_p$ or $U_{EM} \rtimes G_p$, etc. By doubling the degrees of freedom of the adjacent topological class in $d$-dimensions, we successfully show that the $(d+1)$-dimensional lattice models constructed in this way have the desired enlarged symmetry groups that precisely belong to the adjacent topological classes, counterclockwise around the Bott clock. Furthermore, $d$-dimensional boundaries of these $(d+1)$ dimensional lattice models of gapped topological classes are shown to exhibit identical infrared dynamics as the corresponding tQCPs in the $d$-dimensional adjacent topological phases in the Bott clock, with lower symmetries.

</details>


### [272] [Pseudospin Dynamics of Charge Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30381)
*Ping Tang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文从扩展Hubbard模型推导出有效的赝自旋模型，将电荷序映射为交错赝自旋序，证明其动力学由类似朗道-利夫希茨-吉尔伯特方程控制。外电场可直接控制交错赝自旋序，并在超过阈值场时通过失稳集体赝自旋激发驱动电荷序极性的相干翻转，建立了电荷赝自旋作为电荷序相干切换的微观动力学自由度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上电荷序被视为电子密度的静态调制，尽管实验已具备探测和操纵其非平衡演化的能力。与自旋有序系统相比，电荷序动力学及其在外驱动下控制的微观理论框架严重缺乏。

Method: 从扩展Hubbard模型出发，推导出有效的赝自旋模型，将电荷有序态映射为交错赝自旋序；电荷序动力学由此由类似于双亚晶格反铁磁体的Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert类方程所描述。

Result: 外电场可以直接控制交错赝自旋序；当电场超过阈值时，通过失稳集体赝自旋激发可驱动电荷序极性的相干翻转。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了电荷赝自旋作为电荷序相干切换与控制的微观动力学自由度，为电荷扇区提供了与自旋电子学中自旋序动力学框架相对应的等效体系。

Abstract: Charge order is conventionally viewed as a static modulation of the electronic density, despite growing experimental capabilities to probe and manipulate its nonequilibrium evolution. In contrast to spin-ordered systems, a microscopic framework for charge-order dynamics and its control under external driving remains largely underdeveloped. Here, starting from an extended Hubbard model, we derive an effective pseudospin model in which the charge-ordered state maps onto staggered pseudospin order. The resulting charge-order dynamics is governed by Landau--Lifshitz--Gilbert-like equations for the pseudospins, closely analogous to those of a bipartite antiferromagnet. We show that an external electric field directly controls the staggered pseudospin order and, above a threshold field, drives coherent reversal of the charge-order polarity by destabilizing collective pseudospin excitations. Our results establish the charge pseudospin as a microscopic dynamical degree of freedom for coherent switching and control of charge order, providing a charge-sector analogue of the well-established framework for spin-order dynamics in spintronics.

</details>


### [273] [Chiral Color Ice: Exact Local Handedness Constraints and Möbius Zero Modes in Frustrated Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30802)
*Péter Kránitz,Yasir Iqbal,Karlo Penc*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了自旋手性（handedness）可作为精确的局域量子约束，通过SU(2)不变的正半定哈密顿量实现。在四面体上构建手性单重态投影算符，其零能空间具有特定手性符号。对任意自旋，约束通过singlet-annihilation算符因式化为B†B形式，零空间由全局旋转的手性量子色冰态张成。相干态下约束退化为Möbius变换非线性条件，不同晶格连接方式产生截然不同的集体行为：角共享晶格保持指数级零能简并，边共享晶格支持亚维度平面/线零模，三角形构建则抑制非均匀相干形变并包含完整Anderson塔。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统冰规则约束作用于通量型物理量，对手性（handedness）不敏感。作者希望将手性本身作为精确的局域量子约束引入，且不选择自旋空间的特定轴，从而填补冰规则型约束无法体现手性的空白，并探索手性、非线性约束几何与量子简并度之间的关系。

Method: 构建SU(2)不变的正半定parent Hamiltonians：自旋1/2时局域项为手性四面体单重态上的秩1投影算符；任意自旋时通过singlet-annihilation算符B因式化为B†B，完全刻画其核。相干态表示下约束变为与S无关的非线性条件，三个自旋方向通过Möbius变换确定第四个，复合映射定义约束holonomy。在不同晶格拓扑（角共享、边共享、三角形覆盖）上连接同一局域约束。

Result: 角共享晶格的基态核呈指数级退化，下界超过传统冰基准；边共享晶格产生平面或线状的亚维度零模；三角形构建在零能处包含完整Anderson塔（四面体磁有序），同时抑制非均匀相干形变。两种不等价的三角形覆盖显示谐波零模计数不能决定量子核的大小。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个可处理的理论框架，将局域手性、非线性约束几何和量子简并度解耦，并直接关联到约束网络的连通性。这表明手性作为局域约束的可行性和丰富物理后果，为研究受挫磁体中手性有序与量子简并提供了新途径。

Abstract: Local constraints govern the low-energy physics of frustrated matter, but familiar ice-type rules constrain flux-like quantities and are insensitive to handedness. Here we show that handedness itself can be imposed as an exact local quantum constraint without selecting an axis in spin space. We construct positive-semidefinite, SU(2)-invariant parent Hamiltonians whose complete zero-energy space on a tetrahedron has a prescribed chirality sign, rather than selecting a particular chiral wave function. For spin-1/2 the local term is a rank-one projector onto a chiral tetrahedral singlet, while for arbitrary spin it factorizes as $B^\dagger B$ through a singlet-annihilation operator, with a completely characterized kernel given by the span of the globally rotated chiral color-ice states. For coherent states, the same zero-energy condition becomes an $S$-independent nonlinear constraint in which three spin directions determine the fourth through a Möbius transformation; compositions of these maps define constraint holonomies on extended lattices. Connecting the same local constraint in different ways produces qualitatively different collective regimes: corner-sharing lattices retain exponentially large quantum ground-state kernels, with rigorous lower bounds exceeding conventional ice benchmarks; edge-sharing lattices support subdimensional plane or line zero modes; while triangular constructions suppress nonuniform coherent deformations and contain the complete Anderson tower of tetrahedral magnetic order at exactly zero energy. Two inequivalent triangular coverings further show that harmonic zero-mode counting does not determine the size of the quantum kernel. These results establish a tractable setting in which local handedness, nonlinear constraint geometry, and quantum degeneracy can be disentangled and related directly to the connectivity of the constraint network.

</details>
